### [1] [Fractal Cross Product: Theory, Differentiable Implementation and Application to Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03755)
*Noaman Khan,Nihad Hadj Sahraoui,Samir Brahim Belhaouari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出广义分形叉积（FCP），将经典广义叉积推广为具有任意正次数 D 的非线性径向变形，并给出可微、无量纲的图像响应及非循环经验累积指数，用于多源图像融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典广义叉积的体积（Gram volume）在统一缩放下次数被框架的整数维数固定，无法刻画非整数维的分形结构；作者希望构造一种可指定非整数次数、同时保留正交性、交替性、旋转等变性与 D-齐次性的广义叉积，以捕捉自相似框架的分形尺度行为并用于图像融合。

Method: 以非线性径向变形构造广义分形叉积（FCP）：标量形式适用于任意环境维数 m≥k，规范定向的向量形式要求余维为一；在 D=k 时精确退化为经典广义叉积，保留正交性、交替性、旋转等变性与 D-齐次性，但一般非多线性。对精确自相似框架，构造满足相似维数处的尺度平衡律；进一步导出可微、无量纲的图像响应，并通过跨 patch 宽度对原始角度 Gram 响应做回归得到非循环的经验累积指数。

Result: Binary64 计算将有限框架恒等式恢复到舍入精度；光栅实验在三个分辨率下均恢复 Sierpiński 三角形维数值 1.5849625。五种子医学影像对比中，FCP 中心融合在 RIDBOE 随机划分与医院分离划分上分别将平均 AUC 从 0.7264 提升至 0.8135、从 0.5843 提升至 0.6765，在 FracAtlas 上从 0.7403 提升至 0.7475；FracAtlas 上平衡准确率从 0.6186 提升至 0.6721，精确率与敏感率的调和平均从 0.3669 提升至 0.4457。

Conclusion: 结果支持完整融合框架的实用性，但未能将 FCP 的贡献与其互补描述子及融合头的作用相分离，因此 FCP 的独立增益仍需进一步的消融实验验证。

Abstract: The magnitude of the generalized Euclidean cross product is a Gram volume whose degree under common scaling is fixed by the integer dimension of the spanning frame. We formulate a generalized Fractal Cross Product (FCP) as a nonlinear radial deformation with a prescribed positive degree $D$, which may be non-integer. The scalar construction applies in any ambient dimension $m\geq k$, while its canonically oriented vector form requires codimension one. It recovers the classical generalized cross product exactly at $D=k$ and retains orthogonality, alternation, rotation equivariance, and $D$-homogeneity, but is generally not multilinear. For exact self-similar frame systems, the construction also obeys a scale-balance law at the similarity dimension. We derive a differentiable, dimensionless image response and a non-circular empirical accumulation exponent obtained by regressing raw angular Gram responses across patch widths. Binary64 calculations recover the finite-frame identities to roundoff, while raster experiments recover the Sierpiński-triangle value 1.5849625 at three resolutions. In five-seed medical-imaging comparisons, FCP-centered fusion increased mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve from 0.7264 to 0.8135 and from 0.5843 to 0.6765 on the random and hospital-separated Retinal Image Database for Optic Nerve Evaluation partitions, respectively, and from 0.7403 to 0.7475 on FracAtlas. On FracAtlas, balanced accuracy increased from 0.6186 to 0.6721 and the harmonic mean of precision and sensitivity from 0.3669 to 0.4457. These results support the utility of the complete fusion framework, but do not isolate the effect of FCP from that of its complementary descriptors and fusion head.

</details>


### [2] [Do Motion Tokenizers for Co-Speech Gesture Generation Encode Gesture Semantics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03765)
*Varsha Suresh,Divij Jain,Jia Liu,M. Hamza Mughal,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过19个共语手势描述符探测重建训练的离散运动词表，发现几何与手性可从token嵌入中轻易解码，而运动类别仅弱可解码，表明重建目标不能保证手势语义被捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散运动tokenizer广泛用于共语手势生成，但尚不清楚这些码本中哪些运动属性（尤其是与手势语义相关的属性）是可恢复的。

Method: 对一个以重建为目标训练的词表进行探测，使用涵盖从原始运动到抽象交际功能的19个共语手势描述符进行解码分析。

Result: 几何和手性可 readily 从token嵌入中解码；运动类别虽然在使用上呈现系统性差异，但仅能被弱解码。重建质量与描述符可解码性之间存在差距。

Conclusion: 仅靠重建目标无法保证手势语义被捕获，基于此类属性的词表评估可指导更具语义性的运动tokenizer设计。

Abstract: Discrete motion tokenizers encode motion as atomic units and are widely used for co-speech gesture generation. It remains unclear which motion properties, especially those relevant to gesture semantics, are recoverable from these codebooks. We probe a reconstruction-trained codebook using 19 co-speech gesture descriptors spanning from raw motion to abstract communicative function. Results show that geometry and handedness are readily decodable from token embeddings, while motion category is only weakly decoded despite showing systematic differences in discrete code usage. This gap between reconstruction quality and descriptor decodability suggests that reconstruction objectives alone do not guarantee that gesture semantics are captured, and that evaluating codebooks on such properties can guide the design of more semantic motion tokenizers.

</details>


### [3] [LoRA Direction Extraction for Controllable Light Toggling in FLUX.1 Kontext](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03771)
*Petr Golenderov,Dmitry Mazyar,Natalia Sovpel,Alexander Aksenov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向流匹配扩散模型的微调方法，实现无需大规模数据集的真实人造光建模，用于可控室内图像编辑（开/关人造光源并保持场景几何、物体布局、材质与视觉身份）。方法将任务分解为两个独立形式，提出LoRA方向训练法提取扩散模型流场中LoRA适配器效应的纯方向，并设计专门损失函数保证逆变换的真实性，同时利用增量图精确调整光照颜色与温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控室内图像编辑中，人造光源开/关需在改变光照的同时保持场景几何、物体布局、材质及视觉身份不变，而现有人造光建模通常依赖大规模训练数据集，成本高且难以实现精细可控编辑。

Method: 1) 将任务分解为两个独立子问题；2) 提出LoRA方向训练法（LoRA Direction Training Method），在扩散模型流场中提取LoRA适配器效应的纯方向；3) 设计专门损失函数以保证逆变换的真实性；4) 利用所得增量图对照明颜色与温度进行更精细调节。

Result: 可实现无需大规模数据集的人造光建模与可控室内图像编辑；保持场景几何、物体布局、材质与视觉身份；增量图支持对光照颜色和温度的精确调整（摘要未给出定量指标）。

Conclusion: 该方法以流匹配扩散模型微调为核心，通过LoRA方向提取与专门损失设计，在有限数据条件下实现真实、可控的室内光照编辑，并可细化光照颜色与温度。

Abstract: We propose a fine-tuning method for flow-matching diffusion models aimed at realistic artificial light modeling without the need for a large training dataset. We address the task of controllable interior image editing, where the goal is to turn artificial light sources on or off while preserving the scene geometry, object placement, materials, and visual identity of the original image. To achieve this, we decompose the task into two independent formulations. We introduce the LoRA Direction Training Method, which extracts the pure direction of the LoRA adapter effect in the diffusion model flow field, and we also introduce specialized loss functions to ensure the realism of the inverse transformation. Additionally, the resulting increment map is used for more precise adjustment of the lighting color and temperature.

</details>


### [4] [Energy Variation in Training Modern Computer Vision Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03772)
*David Cortes,Carlos Juiz,Belen Bermejo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究实测7种现代视觉架构在ImageNet-10k上训练能耗，发现GFLOPs与能耗的Pearson相关为0.85，EfficientNet系列在精度与能效间取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习快速发展导致模型训练能耗显著上升，能效正成为日益重要的设计准则，但计算复杂度（GFLOPs）能否可靠预测实际能耗尚不明确，需要实证数据支撑架构选择。

Method: 在哥伦比亚BIOS中心的两块NVIDIA Tesla P100 GPU上，使用统一40轮基线配置，对MobileNetV3-Small、MobileNetV3-Large、EfficientNet-B0、EfficientNet-B1、ViT-B/32、ConvNeXt-Tiny、ViT-B/16七种架构，在ImageNet-1k分类任务的10,000张图像子集（ImageNet-10k）上训练；通过NVML直接记录能耗，并与各模型计算复杂度进行对比分析。

Result: GFLOPs与能耗（kWh）的Pearson相关系数为0.85，说明计算复杂度是强但非完美的能耗预测因子：GFLOPs相近的架构能耗差异可达3.1倍，源于其主导算子硬件效率的差异。EfficientNet变体在分类性能（Val Top-5最高97.15%）与能效（0.457–0.611 kWh）之间平衡最佳；Vision Transformer相对能耗最高，且在评测配置下分类性能较低。

Conclusion: GFLOPs可作为能耗的粗略代理指标，但不能替代实测；在能耗受限的计算环境中应优先考虑EfficientNet类架构，该结论为实际架构选型提供依据。

Abstract: The rapid growth of deep learning has substantially increased the energy consumption associated with model training, making energy efficiency an increasingly relevant design criterion. This study empirically measures the energy variation of training seven modern computer vision architectures, MobileNetV3-Small, MobileNetV3-Large, EfficientNet-B0, EfficientNet-B1, ViT-B/32, ConvNeXt-Tiny, and ViT-B/16 for the ImageNet-1k classification task, using a homogeneous 10,000-image subset (ImageNet-10k) and a uniform 40-epoch baseline configuration executed on two NVIDIA Tesla P100 GPUs at the Bioinformatics and Computational Biology Center of Colombia (BIOS). Energy was recorded directly via NVML and contrasted with the computational complexity of each model. The results show a Pearson correlation of 0.85 between floating-point operations (GFLOPs) and energy consumption in kWh, indicating that computational complexity is a strong but imperfect predictor of energy expenditure: architectures with comparable GFLOPs exhibited consumption differing by up to 3.1x due to differences in the hardware efficiency of their dominant operations. The EfficientNet variants offered the best balance between classification performance (Val Top-5 up to 97.15%) and energy efficiency (0.457-0.611 kWh), while Vision Transformers exhibited the highest relative energy consumption and lower classification performance under the evaluated configuration. These findings guide architecture selection in energy-constrained computing environments.

</details>


### [5] [What Do Verifiable Rewards Teach Video-Language Models About Time? A Controlled Multi-Model Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03792)
*Avyay Sadhu,Patrick Cooper*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）用于视频因果时序问答的研究中，四种开放模型在三种数据配方下的对比实验表明：验证式训练可显著提升域内准确率，但很大部分增益并非来自视觉理解；纯合成验证数据会严重损害域外性能，混入真实视频可消除该退化；所有配方均未使模型习得时序顺序grounding。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 已在语言模型推理上带来显著增益，且可验证视频基准使其适用于因果时序视频问答，但"RLVR 究竟让视频语言模型学到了关于时间的什么"尚不清楚。作者希望厘清验证式奖励带来的究竟是真正的时序理解，还是仅仅是基准准确率的提升，并考察合成数据与真实数据的差异及域外泛化风险。

Method: 对四个开放模型（Qwen3-VL-8B/4B、Qwen2.5-VL-7B、Gemma-3-12B）使用 GRPO 微调，设置三类数据配方：verified（合成 CLEVRER 问题，含精确答案与事件顺序奖励）、unverified（43,751 条真实网络视频上的自监督 pretext 任务）、以及二者 1:1 混合；另加一个 verified+real 组，在真实视频上补充 4,000 条可验证问题。每格在域内与域外真实视频（NExT-QA 时序压力集、MVBench 子集）上评估，并按帧顺序、打乱、无帧三种条件测试。

Result: (1) 验证式训练在域内带来大幅增益，且随基座能力增强而缩小（弱模型 +14~+19 分，最强模型 +6 分）。(2) 增益很大部分是非视觉的：无帧条件下的准确率提升几乎与有帧相当。(3) 纯验证式训练可严重损害域外准确率且训练中无任何征兆：Qwen3-VL-8B 在两个真实视频集上分别损失 26.7 与 25.2 分，而混合配方从未使模型显著退化；加入真实可验证问题后该损失消失（-2.3 分，处于基座噪声范围内），同时保留 +9.3 的域内增益，说明问题源于狭窄的纯合成数据而非验证本身。(4) 没有任何配方诱发时序顺序 grounding：41 次评估中有序与打乱的差距在 39 次中与零无异，两次为边缘差异，尽管存在事件顺序奖励。

Conclusion: 可验证奖励能提升基准准确率，却未带来看似对应的时序理解。作者建议：报告须包含无帧对照，并混入真实视频以防止域外性能退化。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) has produced large reasoning gains in language models, and verifiable video benchmarks make it applicable to causal-temporal video question answering. We study what RLVR teaches video-language models about time. We fine-tune four open models (Qwen3-VL-8B/4B, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, Gemma-3-12B) with group relative policy optimization under three data recipes: verified (synthetic CLEVRER questions with exact answer and event-order rewards), unverified (self-supervised pretext tasks over 43,751 real web videos), and a 1:1 mixture, plus a verified+real arm that adds 4,000 verifiable questions on real video. Each cell is evaluated in-domain and on out-of-domain real video (a NExT-QA temporal stress set and an MVBench subset), with frames in order, shuffled, and absent. (1) Verified training yields large in-domain gains that shrink as base competence grows (+14 to +19 points on weaker models; +6 on the strongest). (2) Much of the gain is non-visual: accuracy with no frames rises nearly as much as with frames. (3) Verified-only training can severely degrade out-of-domain accuracy with no sign during training: Qwen3-VL-8B loses 26.7 and 25.2 points on the two real-video sets, while the mixture never significantly degrades a model trained on it. Adding real verified questions removes that loss (-2.3 points, within noise of base) and keeps a +9.3 in-domain gain, so the cause is narrow synthetic-only data, not verification. (4) No recipe induces temporal-order grounding: across 41 evaluations the ordered-versus-shuffled gap is indistinguishable from zero in 39 and marginal in two, despite an event-order reward. Verifiable rewards improve benchmark accuracy without temporal understanding. Report no-frame controls, and mix in real video to guard against out-of-domain degradation.

</details>


### [6] [WAMJET: A Harness for World Action Model Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03797)
*Le Chen,Lixin Liu,Jan Schneider,Zeju Qiu,Simon Guist,Bernhard Schölkopf,Dieter Büchler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAMJET：面向世界动作模型推理加速的智能体化框架，通过编码智能体+可复用优化指导+测量验证工具，在六种WAM、三种编码智能体、两种GPU上实现最高9.95倍无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models（WAMs）借助预训练视频基础模型进行机器人操作，但大骨干网络与视频-动作联合预测导致推理开销高昂。现有加速技术虽多，却需针对每个模型和硬件平台进行大量工程化适配，成为部署瓶颈。

Method: 提出WAMJET——一个agentic harness，为编码智能体配备可复用的优化指导以及测量与验证工具。采用瓶颈驱动的工作流：智能体先剖析推理性能，修改目标代码，验证效果，并在瓶颈漂移时迭代精炼加速栈，同时保持动作质量。

Result: 实验覆盖6种WAM、3种编码智能体、2种GPU架构。相比上游实现最高获得9.95倍无损加速；近似与硬件感知优化进一步降低延迟，成功率保持可比。

Conclusion: 结果表明WAMJET能够为WAM部署生成有效的加速栈，缓解了逐模型、逐硬件的工程适配瓶颈。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) leverage pretrained video foundation models for robot manipulation, but their large backbones and video-action co-prediction are expensive. Although existing acceleration techniques offer many ways to reduce this cost, selecting and composing them requires substantial engineering for each model and hardware platform. To tackle this bottleneck, we present WAMJET, an agentic harness that accelerates WAM inference by equipping coding agents with reusable optimization guidance and measurement and validation tools. WAMJET follows a bottleneck-driven workflow where the agent profiles inference, modifies targeted code, validates effects, and iteratively refines the acceleration stack as bottlenecks shift, while preserving action quality. Experiments span six WAMs, three coding agents, and two GPU architectures. WAMJET achieves up to 9.95x lossless speedup over upstream implementations. Approximation and hardware-aware optimization yield additional latency reductions, with comparable success rates. The results show that WAMJET can produce effective acceleration stacks for WAM deployment.

</details>


### [7] [StepCAD: Mesh-to-CAD Code Generation via LLM Policy and Geometry-Guided Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03799)
*Ghadi Nehme,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 StepCAD 生成式优化方法，将状态条件 CAD 策略与几何引导搜索结合，从 3D 网格恢复可执行 CAD 程序，并发布 ARCADE-1.5M 数据集，在多个基准上取得 SOTA 几何重建精度，相对 IoU 最高提升 87.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从 3D 网格恢复可执行 CAD 程序具有挑战性，原因在于 CAD 构建的组合性、离散建模选择与连续参数之间的交互。现有学习类方法多为单次完整预测程序，且主要依赖 sketch-extrude 表示，限制了操作多样性以及重建过程中修正几何误差的机会。

Method: StepCAD：一种生成式优化方法，将状态条件 CAD 策略与几何引导搜索相结合。给定输入网格，策略以目标几何与中间几何为条件预测构建动作，并通过 IoU 引导的树搜索以局部编辑方式精炼所得程序。同时构建 ARCADE-1.5M 大规模数据集，包含 150 万条可执行 CAD 程序，覆盖多样操作、最长 150+ 计步操作序列及 1250 万中间状态-动作转移。

Result: 在多个 CAD 重建基准上，StepCAD 取得 SOTA 几何重建精度并保持高有效率，相对 IoU 相比最强基线最高提升 87.2%，在复杂形状上增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 通过状态条件策略与几何引导搜索的组合式优化，配合大规模多样化程序数据，可有效提升从网格恢复 CAD 程序的精度与有效性，尤其在复杂形状上优势明显。

Abstract: Recovering executable CAD programs from 3D meshes is challenging due to the compositional nature of CAD construction and the interaction between discrete modeling choices and continuous parameters. Many learning-based methods predict complete programs in a single pass and rely predominantly on sketch-extrude representations, limiting operation diversity and opportunities to correct geometric errors during reconstruction. We introduce StepCAD, a generative optimization approach that combines a state-conditioned CAD policy with geometry-guided search. Given an input mesh, the policy predicts construction actions conditioned on both target and intermediate geometry, and an IoU-guided tree search refines the resulting program through local edits. We also introduce ARCADE-1.5M, a large-scale dataset of 1.5M executable CAD programs spanning diverse operations, sequences with a maximum length of 150+ counted operations, and 12.5M intermediate state-action transitions. Experiments across multiple CAD reconstruction benchmarks show that StepCAD achieves state-of-the-art geometric reconstruction accuracy with consistently high validity, yielding up to 87.2% relative IoU improvement over the strongest evaluated baseline, with particularly large gains on complex shapes. Project page: https://ghadinehme.com/stepcad.github.io/

</details>


### [8] [Beyond Token Accuracy: Prioritizing What Matters for Visual Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03822)
*Zhicheng Liu,Zhouxiang Zhao,Chenliang Wu,Zhaohui Yang,Zhaoyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdapToC：面向重建质量的自适应视觉token通信框架，通过发送端自适应选择器联合建模图像内容、信道状态与通信预算，动态决定传输token数量并施加分级保护；接收端采用自适应MaskGIT，区分可靠性、保留高置信观测、纠正受损token并迭代重建缺失内容。在通信开销匹配下，峰值平均PSNR较最强静态基线提升4.20 dB，达到所评估方法中的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token通信方法多采用静态策略，无法同时适应图像内容与信道条件；且其token级效用目标与图像重建质量并不一致，导致资源分配未必带来重建质量提升。

Method: 1）发送端自适应选择器：联合建模图像内容、信道状态与通信预算，进行实例级资源分配，动态确定token数量，并按token重要性与当前信道条件分配不同保护等级。2）接收端自适应MaskGIT接收器：将信道可靠性纳入上下文token建模，区分不同可靠性等级的token，保留高置信观测、纠正可能受损token，并基于已接收证据与全局视觉上下文迭代重建缺失内容。3）三要素协同设计：token数量、不等保护与可靠性感知恢复共同服务于图像级重建质量。

Result: 在通信开销匹配的条件下，峰值平均PSNR相较最强静态基线提升4.20 dB；在所评估的视觉token通信方法中取得最优性能。

Conclusion: 以图像重建质量为优化导向、面向实例自适应联合设计token数量、不等保护与可靠性感知恢复，可显著优于静态token通信策略，为多模态基础模型下的视觉token通信提供有效范式。

Abstract: Tokens have become a unified interface for multimodal foundation models, making visual-token communication a natural paradigm for efficient image delivery. However, existing methods typically rely on static policies that cannot jointly adapt to image content and channel conditions. Moreover, their token-level utility objectives do not necessarily translate into improved image reconstruction quality. In this paper, we propose AdapToC, an adaptive, reconstruction-oriented visual-token communication framework. At the transmitter, an adaptive selector jointly models image content, channel state, and communication budget to perform instance-wise resource allocation. Rather than using a fixed token rate and protection policy, it dynamically determines how many tokens should be transmitted and assigns different protection levels according to token importance and current channel conditions. At the receiver, an adaptive MaskGIT receiver incorporates channel reliability into contextual token modeling. It distinguishes tokens with different reliability levels, preserves high-confidence observations, corrects potentially corrupted tokens, and iteratively reconstructs missing content from the received evidence and global visual context. By co-designing token quantity, unequal protection, and reliability-aware recovery for image-level reconstruction quality, AdapToC achieves a peak mean PSNR gain of 4.20 dB over the strongest static baseline under matched communication costs and state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated visual-token communication methods.

</details>


### [9] [Are We Measuring Anticipation? Auditing Privileged Information in Procedural Video Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03826)
*Mahsa Mohammadi,Sareh Rowlands*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文审计了评估协议本身所"授权"的推断，而非仅预测模型。以程序性动作预判为案例，揭示一种评估效度失效模式：协议在时间因果、无经典目标泄漏的前提下，仍提供了特权中间表征，导致所声称能力与实际测量构念不匹配，并提出四条件审计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数常被用来支持关于模型能力的结论。但现有评估只关注预测模型，忽略了协议本身携带的额外信息。作者希望指出，即便协议满足时间因果性与无目标泄漏，也可能通过提供"特权中间表征"而高估模型实际能力，从而造成能力声称与测量构念的错配。动机是建立更严格的评估效度审计方法。

Method: 以程序性动作预判为受控案例，提出"特权信息差距"概念，并相对于指定的非预言恢复管线进行定义。设计多项干预实验：在Breakfast上对比匹配的recognizer-history与GT-history两种机制；采用固定检查点的2×2干预隔离测试时预言对比；进行匹配的无视频来源探针；以及边界独立的查询/历史控制。另在五个评估设置上检验差距异质性，并在50 Salads上做长时预判复现。

Result: Breakfast上recognizer-history与GT-history得分分别为30.3%与62.3%，Δ_PH(R_BA)=+32.0，95% Student-t区间[+24.1, +39.8]。固定检查点2×2干预隔离出+14.6个百分点的测试时预言对比；匹配的无视频来源探针产生+26.8个百分点差距；边界独立的查询/历史控制仍保留+19.4个百分点差距。五个评估设置中差距呈异质性；50 Salads长时预判复现提供谨慎支持。

Conclusion: 评估协议的推断授权存在系统性效度问题：时间因果与无目标泄漏不足以保证构念效度。作者提出的四条件审计可复用，用于识别特权信息差距，并提示需重新审视基准分数所支持的能力声称。

Abstract: Benchmark scores license claims about the capabilities being evaluated. We audit the inference licensed by an evaluation protocol, rather than the predictive model alone. Using procedural action anticipation as a controlled case study, we study a broader evaluation-validity failure mode: a protocol can remain temporally causal and free of classical target leakage while still supplying a privileged intermediate representation. The failure is not merely optimistic accuracy, but a mismatch between the capability claimed and the construct actually measured.
  On Breakfast, matched recognizer-history and GT-history regimes score 30.3% and 62.3% (Delta_PH(R_BA) = +32.0, 95% Student-t interval [+24.1, +39.8]). A fixed-checkpoint 2 x 2 intervention isolates a +14.6-point test-time oracle contrast; a matched no-video provenance probe yields a +26.8-point gap; and a boundary-independent query/history control retains a +19.4-point gap. We refer to this discrepancy as a privileged-information gap, defined relative to a specified non-oracle recovery pipeline, and propose a reusable four-condition audit. Across five evaluation settings the gap is heterogeneous; a standard 50 Salads long-term-anticipation re-implementation provides cautious support beyond the audit-specific next-action construction.

</details>


### [10] [Streaming Multi-Track Timeline Control for 3D Human Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03873)
*Yangsong Zhang,Anujith Muraleedharan,Rikhat Akizhanov,Gül Varol,Fabio Pizzati,Ivan Laptev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出流式多轨时间线控制任务与TimelineControl方法，使模型能在动作持续进行时响应新到达的指令，并支持时间重叠的多动作并行生成；同时构建了TimelineMotion数据集，实验验证了语义对齐与时间遵循性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动人体动作生成方法大多假设所有指令在生成前已完整给出，无法满足交互式应用需求——如边走边接电话时需要在新指令到达时继续原有动作。已有工作分别处理流式生成或同步组合，但未将“流式指令到达”与“独立计时且时间重叠的多动作”显式结合。

Method: 提出TimelineControl：1) 采用区间感知条件（interval-aware conditioning）保留指令的时间信息；2) 引入因果的部分结构化表示（causal part-structured representations）；3) 设计部位感知去噪（part-aware denoising），协调身体不同区域上并发动作的生成。同时构建TimelineMotion数据集，包含重叠指令区间与身体部位标注。

Result: 在TimelineMotion与MTT数据集上，相比所评估的流式基线（包括在同一数据上重训练的模型），在语义对齐和时间遵循性上均有提升；消融实验与人工评估验证了设计有效性，并补充了空间条件与类人执行演示。

Conclusion: TimelineControl能够在流式指令到达场景下同时处理独立计时、时间重叠的多动作生成，显著改善语义对齐与时间遵循性；代码、数据与模型将公开。

Abstract: Text-driven human motion generation has advanced substantially, yet most methods assume instructions are available before synthesis. Interactive applications require responding to new instructions while continuing ongoing actions, such as answering a phone while walking. Existing approaches address streaming generation or simultaneous composition without explicitly combining streaming instruction arrival with independently timed, overlapping actions. We introduce streaming multi-track timeline control and propose TimelineControl to incorporate new instructions alongside ongoing actions. Interval-aware conditioning preserves instruction timing, while causal part-structured representations and part-aware denoising coordinate concurrent actions across body regions. We also construct TimelineMotion, a dataset with overlapping instruction intervals and body-part annotations. Experiments on TimelineMotion and MTT demonstrate improved semantic alignment and temporal adherence over evaluated streaming baselines, including models retrained on the same data. Ablations and human evaluations validate our design, complemented by spatial conditioning and humanoid execution demonstrations. Our code, data and models will become publicly available.

</details>


### [11] [DABACO: A Multi-Camera Dataset and Benchmark for Screen Localization and Pointing Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03928)
*Óscar Gómez-Cárdenes,José Gil Marichal-Hernández,Juan Manuel Martín-Doñas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出DABACO数据集，面向低成本嵌入式设备的屏幕定位与指向估计算法开发与评估，包含多传感器视频序列、系统化标注流程及开源评估工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法开发与评估缺乏真实数据：合成采集难以复现光学畸变、卷帘快门、运动模糊和显示处理等物理采集特性；且多数现有数据集仅提供静态图像，无法评估连续指向任务。DABACO旨在填补这一资源空白。

Method: 数据集包含多种低成本相机传感器（单色全局快门、彩色卷帘快门模块）在嵌入式平台（Raspberry Pi 4B、ESP32-S3）上采集的视频序列。标注流程结合临时视觉水印、光流跟踪、人工验证与标记去除，并同时发布原始带标记采集、无标记图像、显式角点标注和修改掩码以支持审计与重建偏差研究。另发布开源评估工具包，含两个参考基线：基于边缘与轮廓几何的经典屏幕检测流水线，以及未做数据集特定训练的现成YOLOv8闭词汇分割模型。

Result: 在通用计算机上使用IoU、角点误差和指向误差评估两个基线，建立了面向未来嵌入式实现的初始参考结果。

Conclusion: DABACO弥补了现有资源缺口，旨在支持新型低成本屏幕定位与指向系统的开发与评估。

Abstract: Screen localization and pointing estimation are key to low-cost interactive devices. Yet developing and evaluating these algorithms requires realistic data: synthetic captures cannot fully reproduce the optical distortion, rolling shutter, motion blur, and display processing of a physical acquisition, and most existing datasets provide static images rather than the video needed to assess continuous pointing. In this paper, we introduce the DABACO Dataset. Developed within the DABACO (Dispositivo Apuntador de BAjo COste, or Low-Cost Pointing Device) project, this dataset supports the development and evaluation of screen detection and camera-based pointing algorithms for embedded systems. It comprises video sequences captured with multiple low-cost camera sensors, including monochrome global-shutter and color rolling-shutter modules, on embedded platforms such as the Raspberry Pi 4B and ESP32-S3.
  We present a systematic annotation pipeline combining temporary visual watermarking, optical-flow tracking, manual verification, and marker removal. Both the original marked captures and the marker-free images, together with explicit corner annotations and modification masks, are released to support auditing and the study of potential reconstruction bias.
  In addition, we release an open-source evaluation toolkit with two reference baselines: a classical screen-detection pipeline based on edge and contour geometry, and a closed-vocabulary, off-the-shelf YOLOv8 segmentation model evaluated without dataset-specific training. Both are evaluated on a general-purpose computer using Intersection over Union, corner error, and pointing error, establishing initial reference results for future embedded implementations. Overall, DABACO addresses a gap in existing resources and is intended to support the development and evaluation of new low-cost screen-localization and pointing systems.

</details>


### [12] [Dynamic Time Step Prediction in Inverse Heat Dissipation for Blur-Like Image Restoration Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03942)
*Cap Dang Xuan Kiet,Tat-Jen Cham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对扩散模型图像复原中固定反演时间步与高斯噪声不匹配退化类型的问题，提出逆热耗散模型（IHDM）进行渐进模糊扩散，并用时间预测器自适应估计反演起始时间步，在多个复原任务上取得SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散模型的图像复原通常依赖扩散反演保留退化图像信息。但现有方法存在两个问题：(1) 反演到预先设定的中间时间步，而该固定步长并不理想——严重退化图像需要更早的起始时间步，轻微退化则不需要；(2) DDIM类模型通过添加高斯噪声破坏原始信号，与模糊、雾霾、低光等模糊型退化的本质不匹配。

Method: 提出两个解决方案：(1) 采用替代的扩散过程——逆热耗散模型（Inverse Heat Dissipation Model），通过逐步模糊数据点来扩散输入图像，替代加高斯噪声的方式；(2) 提出时间预测器（time predictor），估计反演所用的起始时间步，使模型能够自适应退化严重程度。

Result: 在标准基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在定量与定性评估中均达到SOTA性能，并对多种复原任务具有出色的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 以渐进模糊的逆热耗散扩散过程替代高斯加噪，并结合自适应时间预测器确定反演起始步，可有效缓解固定时间步与退化性质不匹配的问题，提升扩散反演式图像复原的性能与泛化性。

Abstract: When using diffusion models to target image restoration problems, diffusion inversion is typically employed to retain relevant image information from the degraded images. Instead of inverting back to the initial time step (i.e., T), many methods invert to a pre-determined intermediate time step, in order to better preserve information from degraded source images. However, a pre-determined time step for inversion is not ideal for reconstruction, as a severely degraded image requires an earlier starting time step than a mildly degraded one. In addition, DDIM-based models corrupt the original signal by adding Gaussian noise, which can be mismatched to the nature of blur-like degradations, such as blur, haze, and low-light. To address these problems, we propose two solutions: (1) we adopt an alternative diffusion process, called the Inverse Heat Dissipation Model, that diffuses the input image by gradually blurring a data point (2) we propose to implement a time predictor to estimate the starting time step for the inversion, with the model learning to adapt to the degradation severity. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both quantitative and qualitative evaluations, with excellent generalization to many restoration tasks.

</details>


### [13] [FADE: Frame-Aware Diffusion-Transformer-based Multi-Concept Erasure for Video Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03980)
*Yuchen Li,Kaiyuan Deng,Chaoran Feng,Zhenyu Tang,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FADE多概念视频擦除框架：先用闭式键/值联合编辑抑制所有目标概念，再用按帧索引与去噪时间步门控的逐概念低秩适配器消除残留逐帧泄漏，并以硬负样本训练和相似度软路由组合各适配器。16个概念擦除下物体基准残差准确率降至4.9%（最强基线15.5%），VBench仅下降0.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V扩散模型概念擦除存在两个未决问题：（1）帧无关的抑制策略会残留个别帧，导致被擦除概念在孤立帧中重新出现，而片段级平均指标掩盖了这种"帧重激活"现象；（2）评估通常一次只针对单一目标概念或类别，缺乏多概念同时擦除的场景验证。

Method: FADE分两阶段：（1）对预训练T2V模型施加联合闭式键/值（key/value）编辑，一次性抑制全部目标概念；（2）训练逐概念的帧感知低秩适配器（LoRA），其注入强度由帧索引和去噪时间步共同门控，以清除残余的逐帧泄漏。每个适配器以其他目标概念的提示作为硬负样本训练，使不同适配器中的概念特异成分保持良好分离；推理时用基于相似度的软路由器按提示组合各适配器。

Result: 在单个Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B骨干上擦除16个概念（物体、艺术风格、裸露内容），FADE将物体基准的残差准确率降至4.9%，而八种基线中最强者为15.5%；同时VBench平均分与未编辑模型相差不超过0.9%。在VLM评判与盲测人类研究中排序保持一致；对混合多个被擦除概念的提示、30个同时擦除的名人身份，以及Wan2.1-T2V-14B、CogVideoX-2B、HunyuanVideo-1.5等骨干，相对最强基线的优势均可迁移。

Conclusion: FADE是一个可扩展的多概念T2V视频反学习框架，同时解决了帧重激活泄漏与多概念同时擦除两大问题；在保持生成质量（VBench）的前提下，显著优于现有八种基线，并具备跨提示组合、跨大概念数量与跨模型骨干的良好泛化性。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) diffusion models can reproduce copyrighted, violent, or explicit content, which motivates concept erasure: removing designated concepts from a pretrained model while preserving its behavior on everything else. Existing T2V erasure methods leave two problems open. Their frame-agnostic suppression can leave isolated frames in which an erased concept resurfaces, a frame-reactivation gap that clip-level averages obscure; and they are usually evaluated with one target concept or category at a time. We propose Frame-Aware Diffusion Erasure (FADE), a multi-concept video unlearning framework. FADE first applies a joint closed-form key/value edit that suppresses all target concepts, then trains per-concept frame-aware low-rank adapters whose strength is gated by the frame index and the denoising timestep to remove residual per-frame leakage. Each adapter is trained with the other targets' prompts as hard negatives, which keeps the concept-specific components of different adapters well separated, and a similarity-based soft router combines the adapters according to the prompt. With 16 concepts (objects, artistic styles, and nudity) erased from a single Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B backbone, FADE reduces the residual accuracy on the object benchmark to 4.9%, against 15.5% for the strongest of eight baselines, while keeping the VBench average within 0.9% of the unedited model. The ranking is unchanged under a VLM judge and a blinded human study, and the advantage over the strongest baseline carries over to prompts that combine several erased concepts, to 30 simultaneously erased celebrity identities, and to Wan2.1-T2V-14B, CogVideoX-2B, and HunyuanVideo-1.5.

</details>


### [14] [Masked Privileged-Information Distillation for Multimodal Skin Lesion Classification Under Missing Clinical Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03991)
*Anirban Barua,Md Mahir Abrar Khan,Ayman Iktidar,Md. Sajjatul Islam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出特权信息蒸馏框架：在完整元数据上训练的多模态教师监督一个9.2倍更小的学生模型，学生训练时临床字段按组随机掩码，实现元数据可用性全覆盖下的稳健皮肤病变分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态皮肤病变分类依赖临床图像与患者元数据，但部署时元数据往往仅部分可得，且资源受限场景要求计算高效。训练时元数据完整与部署时不完整的落差，以及模型紧凑性需求，构成主要挑战。

Method: 采用特权信息蒸馏（privileged-information distillation）：教师在完整元数据上训练，学生小9.2倍并以随机掩码临床字段训练。字段按整组掩码，每样本掩码率取自U(0,1)，使单次训练覆盖完整元数据可用范围。融合采用残差方式，元数据以门控校正形式叠加在无条件图像基线上。

Result: 在PAD-UFES-20上，掩码训练下的蒸馏在各元数据可用水平均优于交叉熵训练，平均提升+4.7个百分点（无掩码仅+1.8）。掩码学生从元数据完整到缺失仅损失7.8平衡准确率点，而同一学生在完整元数据训练下损失36.6点。Grad-CAM显示元数据撤除时学生注意力仍大体集中于病变区域。

Conclusion: 掩码训练与特权信息蒸馏相结合，可在元数据缺失时实现性能的优雅退化，且不依赖蒸馏本身；所得紧凑模型适用于临床元数据常不完整的即时诊疗场景。

Abstract: Multimodal skin lesion classification combines clinical images with patient metadata to improve diagnostic accuracy. However, complete metadata available during training may be only partially accessible at deployment, and resource-constrained settings additionally require computational efficiency. We address these challenges with a privileged-information distillation framework in which a multimodal teacher trained on complete metadata supervises a 9.2x smaller student trained with randomly masked clinical fields. Clinical fields are masked as whole groups at a per-sample rate drawn from U(0,1), so one training run covers the full metadata availability range. Fusion is residual, with metadata added as a gated correction to an unconditional image base. On the PAD-UFES-20 dataset, distillation under masked training improves balanced accuracy over cross-entropy training at every availability level, by an average of +4.7 points versus +1.8 points without masking. The masked student loses only 7.8 balanced-accuracy points as metadata decreases from complete to absent, compared with 36.6 points for the same student trained on complete metadata, highlighting the role of masked training in graceful degradation beyond distillation alone. Grad-CAM visualizations further show that the masked student's attention generally remains lesion-centered as metadata is withdrawn. The resulting compact model targets point-of-care settings, where clinical metadata is often incomplete.

</details>


### [15] [Verifier-Guided Synthetic Augmentation for 3D Human Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04006)
*Yuexuan Wu,Yang Xiang,Hamid Laga,Dip Das,Anuj Srivastava,Zhengwu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出验证器引导的数据增强框架，用全局与局部PCA生成候选并筛选，重训扩散模型，在DFAUST上提高生成多样性与合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D人体生成受限于训练数据多样性：保守模型仅贴近已有样本，探索性模型则常违反基本身体比例，需兼顾多样性扩大与解剖合理性。

Method: 框架采用全局与模式局部PCA生成低成本候选样本；利用弹性配准提供的模态结构与稠密解剖对应关系，基于对应关系导出的骨骼比例与身体部位几何进行筛选（无需逐候选拟合身体模型）；将接受的候选用于重训扩散模型。

Result: 盲测人工研究支持验证器作为保守门控；在4,498个配准DFAUST表面数据上，分布式PCA增强达86.32%接受率、最高CP-AUC（0.871）与最高EAUC（0.752），EAUC较仅真实样本与自增强扩散提升32%。

Conclusion: 模式局部、验证器引导的候选生成能在保持与标定人体测量高度一致的同时，拓宽扩散生成的范围。

Abstract: Limited training data diversity constrains generative modeling of 3D human bodies: conservative models remain close to observed examples, whereas exploratory models often violate basic body proportions. We introduce a verifier-guided augmentation framework that uses global and mode-local PCA to generate inexpensive candidates, screens them using correspondence-derived skeletal proportions and body-part geometry, and retrains a diffusion model on accepted candidates. Elastic registration provides both the modal structure used by distributed PCA and the dense anatomical correspondence needed for scalable screening without per-candidate body-model fitting. A blinded human study supports the verifier as a conservative gatekeeper, favoring verifier-accepted over rejected outputs. We evaluate full-pool verifier acceptance separately from the coverage and departure of accepted samples and combine them through EAUC. On 4,498 registered DFAUST surfaces, distributed-PCA augmentation achieves 86.32% acceptance, the highest CP-AUC (0.871), and the highest EAUC (0.752), improving EAUC by 32% over real-only and self-augmented diffusion. These results show that mode-local, verifier-guided proposals broaden diffusion generation while maintaining high agreement with calibrated body measurements.

</details>


### [16] [VolS-GS: Relightable Gaussian Splatting with Volumetric Subsurface Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04007)
*Junyeong Ahn,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VolS-GS 将高斯场景的空间支撑作为可微有限体积传输求解器的域，通过求解器在物体内部传播光线，从而在 OLAT 捕获数据上重建并渲染新光照与新视角下的物体，在三个基准上持续提升重光照质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可重光照高斯泼溅方法通常在每个图元上独立建模外观，难以表达非局部效应，尤其是次表面散射（光从物体一处进入、从另一处射出）这类跨位置的光传输现象。作者希望不局限于用神经网络或局部核来近似该效应，而是让光真正在物体内部传播。

Method: 1) 以高斯场景的空间支撑作为可微有限体积传输求解器的域，使光在物体内部传播；2) 用一个小型网络为每个高斯预测散射与吸收系数，求解器据此场重新分配入射光；3) 系数由图像拟合而非实测获得，求解器提供的是聚合逐图元外观的"传输形状"路径，而非材料测量；4) 阴影项由可见性、透射率以及求解产生的散射共同预测，并引入正则项抑制阴影项判定为未受光区域的镜面高光，防止阴影与镜面项压制其他分量。

Result: 在三个 OLAT 基准上的实验表明，VolS-GS 在留出光照与视角上的重光照质量均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 用可微传输求解器替代逐图元的局部外观建模，可有效表达次表面散射等非局部效应，并配合阴影与镜面项的耦合设计及正则化，稳定提升新光照、新视角下的重光照效果。

Abstract: We present VolS-GS, a relightable Gaussian splatting framework that reconstructs objects from one-light-at-a-time (OLAT) captures and renders them under novel lighting and viewpoints. Relightable Gaussian Splatting methods typically model appearance independently at each primitive, which makes non-local effects difficult to represent. This limitation is particularly apparent for subsurface scattering, where light entering the object at one location can emerge at another. Rather than modeling this effect solely with a neural network or a local kernel at each primitive, we use the spatial support of the Gaussian scene as the domain of a differentiable finite-volume transport solver, so that light can propagate through the object's interior. A small network predicts scattering and absorption coefficients for each Gaussian, and the solve redistributes incident light through the resulting field. The coefficients are fit to images rather than measured, so the solve supplies a transport-shaped path for aggregating per-primitive appearance, not a measurement of the material. To keep the learned shadow and specular terms from taking over the other components, our shadow term is predicted from visibility together with the transmittances and the scattering the solve produces, and a regularizer suppresses specular highlights in regions the shadow term predicts to be unlit. Experiments on three OLAT benchmarks show that VolS-GS consistently improves relighting quality on held-out lights and views.

</details>


### [17] [PhysMamba: Selective State Space Models as Learned Articulated Body Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04014)
*Haochuan Zhang,Sinisa Todorovic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个基于选择性状态空间模型(SSM)的铰接体模拟器PhysMamba，从位置、旋转与关节动作历史预测下一帧全身状态（无需速度输入），在精度与推理效率间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有铰接体模拟器多依赖RNN等架构，且通常需要速度输入；作者希望探索选择性状态空间模型在铰接体动力学预测中的可行性，并评估不同观测条件与训练协议下的表现及实时部署潜力。

Method: 基于Mamba2构建PhysMamba，以位置、旋转、关节动作历史为输入，输出下一帧全身状态，不使用速度输入；对比四种架构，在部分/全观测输入及三种训练协议下评测，并采用CUDA graph编译优化推理。

Result: 从头训练的rollout协议使Mamba2在部分观测下取得较强短中期精度（s10=43 mm，2/50发散）；两阶段教师式rollout协议稳定了GRU但对Mamba2失效；经CUDA graph编译，Mamba2在H100上达0.107 ms/帧（9,334 FPS），为GRU未编译吞吐的1.1倍以内，仅给30 Hz HMR流程增加不足1%延迟。

Conclusion: PhysMamba验证了SSM在铰接体模拟中的有效性，可在保持高精度的同时实现高吞吐与低延迟，具备作为可微物理模块集成到基于视频的网格恢复流程中的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce the first learned articulated body simulator based on a selective state space model (SSM), called PhysMamba. PhysMamba predicts next-frame full-body state from position, rotation, and joint-action history, without velocity inputs. We compare four architectures under partial- and full-observation inputs and three training protocols. The from-scratch rollout training protocol gives Mamba2 strong short- and mid-horizon accuracy under partial observation (s10 = 43 mm, 2/50 diverged), while the two-stage teacher-based rollout protocol stabilizes GRU but fails for Mamba2. With CUDA graph compilation, Mamba2 reaches 0.107 ms per frame (9,334 FPS) on an H100 GPU, within 1.1$\times$ of GRU's un-compiled throughput, adding under 1% latency to a 30 Hz HMR pipeline and enabling integration as a differentiable physics module for video-based mesh recovery.

</details>


### [18] [ClasSAE: Class-Aligned Sparse Autoencoders via Differentiable Feature-Class Affinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04020)
*Jakub Stępień,Marcin Mazur,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出ClasSAE，通过在训练中引入可微top-k特征-类别亲和矩阵，使稀疏自编码器同时实现特征分类标注与类别可分表示，无需事后探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）从无监督表示分解工具，逐渐被用于去学习、偏见缓解、概念编辑等主动干预任务。这类编辑与引导方法的核心难题是可靠地将特征匹配到目标概念，而现有方法多在已训练、冻结的字典上计算事后（post-hoc）得分，导致特征选择与概念对齐分离、依赖事后处理。

Method: 提出ClasSAE：在SAE训练中对可训练的特征-类别亲和矩阵施加可微top-k算子，并为每个特征设置预算，将每个样本选中的特征与其被训练代表的类别耦合。由于梯度通过活跃特征的选择而非仅通过其幅值回传，编码器与亲和矩阵协同自适应，而非分阶段拟合。框架内提出三种稀疏性实现变体，整体性能相当、权衡略有差异。

Result: 在ImageNet上使用CLIP ViT-L/14嵌入：学习到的亲和矩阵与在留出数据上独立估计的事后特征-类别矩阵高度一致；仅凭编码器与亲和矩阵即可直接进行类别预测，无需单独拟合分类器；类别对齐更强的编码器在Targeted Probe Perturbation评估中呈现更好的分离度。

Conclusion: ClasSAE将类别分配与类别可分表示学习统一到训练过程中，生成既类别可分又类别标注的字典，免去事后探测；同时支持直接类别预测并提升引导/编辑相关评估中的特征分离性能，为SAE的主动干预应用提供更可靠的类别-特征匹配机制。代码已开源。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) began as an unsupervised tool for decomposing neural representations into sparse, interpretable features, and are increasingly used not only for passive analysis but also for active interventions such as unlearning, bias mitigation, and concept editing. A central challenge for these editing and steering methods is reliably matching features to target concepts; most current approaches address this by computing post-hoc scores over an already-trained, frozen dictionary. We instead introduce ClasSAE, a novel method that both automatically assigns classes to features and guides the encoder toward class-separable representations during training. Specifically, we apply a differentiable top-$k$ operator to a trainable feature--class affinity matrix with per-feature budgets, coupling the features selected for each sample to the classes they are trained to represent. Because gradients flow through the selection of active features rather than only through their magnitudes, the encoder and the affinity matrix co-adapt rather than being fit in separate stages. The result is a dictionary that is both class-separable and class-annotated, with no need for post-hoc probing. We propose three variants for enforcing sparsity within this framework, which achieve comparable overall performance with slightly different trade-offs. Using CLIP ViT-L/14 embeddings on ImageNet, we show that the learned affinity matrix agrees closely with an independently estimated post-hoc feature-class matrix computed on held-out data. The model also supports direct class prediction from the encoder and affinity matrix alone, without a separately fitted classifier, and its more class-aligned encoder yields improved separation in Targeted Probe Perturbation evaluations. https://github.com/St0pien/ClasSAE.

</details>


### [19] [Learning Subject-Specific Anatomical Representations via Manifold Expansion: Application to Accelerated Multi-Contrast MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04028)
*Ruimin Feng,Wanyu Bian,Albert Jang,Zachary Stewart,Fang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MAX框架，从单幅全采样参考对比图像中学习与对比无关的解剖表示，用以重建加速多对比MRI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床MRI常规采集同一解剖部位的多种对比加权图像以互补表征组织。但现有加速MRI方法通常独立重建各对比，未充分利用共享的解剖信息。

Method: MAX（MAnifold eXpansion）为受试者特异性框架：以解剖保持的强度增广扩展多对比流形；采用解耦隐式神经表示，用共享空间坐标建模解剖、空间不变坐标建模对比外观；固定学到的解剖表示，将对比表示适配欠采样目标数据，再进行展开式精修。

Result: 脑MRI R=8、膝MRI R=6时，MAX在所有任务上取得最高平均PSNR和SSIM，脑部两个对比的PSNR较最强基线提升超过1 dB；能更忠实恢复细微解剖与病理结构，并对对比间运动、参考与目标对比的结构异质性及测量噪声保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: MAX为加速MRI中利用高质量参考扫描提供了通用策略，并有望推广至其他参考辅助的MRI逆问题。

Abstract: Clinical MRI routinely acquires multiple contrast-weighted images of the same anatomy for complementary tissue characterization. However, current accelerated MRI methods typically reconstruct each contrast independently, without fully exploiting shared anatomical information. This work aims to learn anatomical representations invariant to contrast-dependent appearance for reconstruction of accelerated multi-contrast MRI. We propose MAX (MAnifold eXpansion), a subject-specific framework that learns anatomical representations from a single fully sampled reference contrast. To address the under-constrained separation of shared anatomy and contrast-dependent components from a single image, MAX expands the multi-contrast manifold using anatomy-preserving intensity augmentations. A disentangled implicit neural representation models augmented samples using shared spatial coordinates for anatomy and spatially invariant coordinates for contrast appearance. The learned anatomical representation is then fixed, with the contrast representation adapted to the undersampled target data, followed by unrolled refinement. Theoretical analyses further provide insight into the disentangled representation learning and explain how the learned anatomical representation improves the target contrast reconstruction. At R = 8 for brain MRI and R = 6 for knee MRI, MAX achieves the highest mean PSNR and SSIM across all tasks, improving PSNR by more than 1 dB over the strongest baseline for both brain contrasts. MAX more faithfully recovers subtle anatomical and pathological structures and remains robust to inter-contrast motion, structural heterogeneity between reference and target contrasts, and measurement noise. Therefore, MAX provides a general strategy for leveraging high-quality reference scans in accelerated MRI and has the potential to be extended to other reference-assisted MRI inverse problems.

</details>


### [20] [MAGEFormer: Learning Metric-Consistent Representations for Anisotropic CT Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04036)
*Jiaying Li,Paolo Remagnino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MAGEFormer，将物理度量约束嵌入表示学习，解决CT各向异性导致的位置编码失配与重采样精度下降问题，在BTCV和FLARE 22上取得最优边界精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT在体数据分割中表现优异，但其各向同性欧氏晶格假设与临床CT的各向异性采集相冲突，导致体素索引与真实解剖度量失配、以及各向同性重采样带来的精度损失。

Method: MAGEFormer为几何校准框架，包含三个模块：Metric-Adaptive Spatial Embedding (MASE)——依据体素间距校准位置频率；Geometry-Constrained Attention (GCA)——抑制物理上不合理的特征相关性；Geometric View Voting (GVV)——在推理阶段降低离散化偏差。

Result: 在BTCV与FLARE 22两个多器官腹部CT基准、统一协议下与强CNN及Transformer基线对比，MAGEFormer边界精度最优：BTCV HD95为10.58 mm，FLARE 22 HD95为3.40 mm，Dice也获得一致提升。

Conclusion: 对于各向异性CT分割，几何感知的内部校准比仅依赖传统各向同性预处理更有效。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) have shown strong performance in volumetric segmentation, but their effectiveness on clinical CT is limited by an isotropic Euclidean lattice assumption. This conflicts with anisotropic CT acquisition, leading to two key issues: (1) a metric mismatch between voxel indices and physical anatomy, and (2) accuracy degradation from isotropic resampling. To address this, we propose MAGEFormer, a geometry-calibrated framework that embeds physical metric constraints directly into representation learning. Our method introduces Metric-Adaptive Spatial Embedding (MASE) to calibrate positional frequencies using voxel spacing, Geometry-Constrained Attention (GCA) to suppress physically implausible feature correlations, and Geometric View Voting (GVV) to reduce discretization bias during inference. We evaluate MAGEFormer on two multi-organ abdominal CT benchmarks, BTCV and FLARE 22, under a unified protocol against strong CNN and Transformer-based baselines. MAGEFormer achieves the strongest boundary accuracy among the compared methods, with 10.58 mm HD95 on BTCV and 3.40 mm HD95 on FLARE 22, and shows consistent gains in Dice under the same protocol. These results show that geometry-aware internal calibration is more effective than relying on conventional isotropic preprocessing alone for anisotropic CT segmentation.

</details>


### [21] [Dynamic Quadtree Tokenization and Transformer for Adaptive Mesh PDE Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04044)
*Yilin Zhuang,Noah Zambrano,Karthik Duraisamy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAMRViT 是一种基于平衡四叉树和三维旋转位置编码的视觉 Transformer，可原生处理多级自适应网格加密（AMR）数据，实现无固定 token 预算的自适应分辨率与稳定长时序推演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT 的二次注意力开销导致空间分辨率与推演时长之间存在权衡；对于激波、反应前沿、材料界面等细尺度偏微分方程问题，细粒度特征仅占据小而演化的区域。现有神经代理模型缺乏动态调整分辨率的机制，且固定预算的自适应 token 化方法受限于预设 token 数，无法全程自适应拓扑变化。

Method: 1) 以平衡四叉树 token 化输入，采用小波启发的细化准则；2) 用三维旋转位置编码统一编码位置与细化层级；3) 推理阶段在单元空间重网格化以保证稳定的长时序推演；4) 提出多尺度变体，保留每个叶节点在其原始源分辨率，使模型跨分辨率层级学习；5) 不预设 token 数量，支持自回归推演中完全自适应的拓扑，原生 token 化多级 AMR 数据。

Result: 在均匀网格基准上，均匀块 WAMRViT 以显著更少的 token 超过最细块均匀 ViT 的最细层感兴趣区域 VRMSE。多尺度变体在两个基准上均取得最低的首步全场 RMSE 与 VRMSE，并提升长时序下推演平均的全场与细化区域精度。并行重网格化下，端到端推演开销介于最细块与 token 数近似匹配的较粗块 ViT 之间。在细特征无法被均匀网格基线原生表示的复杂 AMR 燃烧问题上，WAMRViT 直接作用于自适应单元，在相同 Transformer 容量下大幅降低最细层误差。

Conclusion: WAMRViT 是首个原生 token 化多级 AMR 数据的机器学习代理模型，通过平衡四叉树、3D 旋转位置编码和单元空间重网格化，缓解了分辨率与推演时长的权衡，在均匀网格与真实 AMR 问题上均优于基线，同时保持可控的计算开销。

Abstract: The quadratic attention cost of Vision Transformers (ViTs) forces a trade-off between spatial resolution and rollout horizon, particularly for fine-scale PDEs where shocks, reaction fronts, and material interfaces occupy small, evolving regions of the domain. Conventional neural surrogates also lack mechanisms to adapt resolution dynamically. We propose WAMRViT, a ViT that tokenizes inputs as balanced quadtrees using a wavelet-inspired refinement criterion, jointly encodes position and refinement level with 3D rotary positional embeddings, and regrids in cell space during inference for stable long-horizon rollouts. A multi-scale variant retains each leaf at its native source resolution and lets the model learn across resolution levels. Unlike fixed-budget adaptive-tokenization methods, WAMRViT imposes no predetermined token count and supports fully adaptive topology throughout autoregressive rollout. To our knowledge, it is the first machine-learning surrogate to natively tokenize multi-level Adaptive Mesh Refinement (AMR) data. On uniform-grid benchmarks, uniform-patch WAMRViT improves finest-level region-of-interest VRMSE over a finest-patch uniform ViT while using substantially fewer tokens. The multi-scale variant achieves the lowest first-step full-field RMSE and VRMSE on both benchmarks and improves rollout-averaged full-field and refined-region accuracy at long horizons. With parallelized regridding, its end-to-end rollout cost lies between finest-patch and approximately token-matched coarser-patch ViTs. On a complex AMR combustion problem whose finest features cannot be represented natively by the evaluated uniform-grid baselines, WAMRViT operates directly on adaptive cells and substantially reduces finest-level error at matched transformer capacity. Code: https://github.com/tonyzyl/wamrvit

</details>


### [22] [ASD-FEAT: A Multi-Modal Infant Video-Derived Dataset for Early ASD Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04051)
*Sidrah Liaqat,Halil Helvaci,Sen-Ching Cheung,Chongruo Wu,Dongjie Chen,Chen Nee Chuah,Sally Ozonoff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出ASD-FEAT多模态纵向数据集，覆盖0-36月龄婴儿-成人互动视频，并构建自动化流水线实现76.2%的ASD分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）的早期精准筛查是及时干预的前提，对改善认知与行为结局至关重要。然而现有ASD行为数据集在纵向覆盖、重复会话、临床验证结果、专家标注与隐私保护特征等方面难以同时满足需求。

Method: 构建ASD-FEAT数据集，源自婴儿-成人互动视频，具备纵向覆盖（婴儿期至36个月）、重复互动会话、临床验证发育结果、专家帧级行为标注及隐私友好的多模态特征。整合专家定义特征与深度学习特征：面部与眼部关键点、面部动作单元、注视方向、头部姿态、梅尔频谱音频表示及光流。基于机器学习构建端到端ASD风险识别流水线，并引入"访视内伙伴对比"信号，对考察者导向与家长导向的社交行为进行对比。

Result: 自动化流水线ASD分类准确率76.2%，AUROC为0.82；基于人工标注行为的分类器准确率81.3%，AUROC为0.88。加入访视内伙伴对比信号后，全自动的"注视面部+微笑"与"注视面部+发声"配置的Matthews相关系数分别提升至0.49与0.45，超过人工编码单伙伴基线的0.42。

Conclusion: ASD-FEAT在规模上首次联合提供纵向覆盖、重复会话、临床验证结果、专家标注与隐私保护多模态特征；其自动化流水线与伙伴对比信号可有效识别ASD风险，部分配置已超越人工编码基线，为ASD早期筛查与行为标记研究提供了有价值的数据与方法支撑。

Abstract: Accurate early screening for Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) is a precursor to timely intervention, which is critical for improving cognitive and behavioral outcomes. We present ASD-FEAT (ASD - Feature Extraction And Tracking), a multimodal dataset derived from video recordings of infant-adult interaction sessions. The key contribution of ASD-FEAT is the combination of longitudinal coverage from infancy through 36 months, repeated interaction sessions, clinically validated developmental outcomes, expert frame-level behavioral annotations, and privacy-conscious multimodal feature representations. To the best of our knowledge, existing ASD behavioral datasets do not jointly provide these characteristics at comparable scale. To demonstrate the utility of ASD-FEAT, we use it to evaluate a computer-vision-based end-to-end pipeline relying on machine learning techniques to automatically identify ASD risk. ASD-FEAT integrates both expert-defined and deep-learned features, including face and eye landmarks, facial action units, gaze direction, head position, mel-spectrogram audio representations, and optical flow, to identify behavioral markers of social interaction. Our automated pipeline achieves an ASD classification accuracy of 76.2% and an Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic (AUROC) of 0.82, compared to classifiers trained on manually labeled behaviors, which yielded 81.3% accuracy and an AUROC of 0.88. We further introduce a within-visit partner contrast: a per-visit signal contrasting examiner-directed and parent-directed social behavior which, when added to the classifier, lifts the fully automated Look Face + Smile and Look Face + Vocal configurations to Matthews correlations of 0.49 and 0.45 respectively, exceeding the human-coded single-partner baseline of 0.42.

</details>


### [23] [A Theory of Shape Reconstruction from Heat Conduction and Shading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04052)
*Akihiko Oharazawa,Sriram Narayanan,Mani Ramanagopal,Srinivasa G. Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 结合明暗线索与热传导线索，提出新理论以消除单图形状恢复中的局部形状歧义，且不依赖光滑等先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单幅Lambertian图像形状恢复存在固有歧义：光源已知时有锥歧义，光源未知时更严重；基于热传导的形状方法虽能估计内蕴的形状Laplacian，但从Laplacian恢复法向又存在局部凸/凹二值歧义。

Method: 提出新理论，融合明暗（shading）线索与热传导线索，在同时满足明暗约束与Laplacian约束的条件下求解，以消除局部形状歧义（排除少数退化情形），且不依赖光滑性等先验，并支持光源未知情形。

Result: 通过复杂形状的仿真验证理论，分析噪声下的性能，并在真实物体（复杂形状与材质、含变化反照率）的含噪单段热视频上进行评估。

Conclusion: 明暗与热传导线索的联合约束可在不依赖先验、甚至光源未知的情况下解决局部形状歧义，仿真与真实热视频实验均表明方法有效。

Abstract: Shape from shading using a single image of a Lam- bertian surface is inherently ambiguous. When the light source direction is known, the surface normal estimation has a cone- ambiguity, which worsens when the source is unknown. Recently, shape from heat conduction has emerged as an approach that leverages heat transport equations to estimate the Shape Lapla- cian operator, an intrinsic measure of shape. However, deriving surface normals from the Laplacian operator encounters a local binary convex/concave ambiguity. Our contribution introduces a novel theory to resolve these local shape ambiguities (excluding a few degeneracies) without relying on priors like smoothness, by combining the cues from shading and heat conduction. Our method ensures the mathematical constraints of both shading and the Laplacian are satisfied simultaneously, even with an unknown light source. We validate our theory through simulations of complex shapes and analyze its performance in the presence of noise, as well as on a noisy single thermal video of real-world objects with complex shapes and material properties, including varying albedo.

</details>


### [24] [Evaluating Zone-Guided Front Extraction for Glacier Calving-Front Delineation in SAR Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04066)
*Chhaya Kulkarni,Emam Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在CaFFe数据集上对比"直接预测崩解前沿"与"语义分区引导提取前沿"两类方案，使用U-Net、DeepLabV3+、SegFormer-B0。结果表明分区标签确实包含前沿边界信息，但从模型预测分区中提取的前沿质量仍较差，分区分割得分不能替代前沿级评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR影像中崩解前沿是冰、海洋与岩石/地形之间细薄且常模糊的边界，自动描绘困难。CaFFe同时提供二值前沿掩膜与语义分区掩膜，作者希望探究分区级监督能否支撑前沿恢复，并检验分区分割性能是否能代表前沿提取效果。

Method: 在同一CaFFe边界框裁剪设置下，比较两种范式：直接设定中模型预测二值崩解前沿掩膜；分区引导设定中模型先预测四类语义分区，再从预测的冰—海边界提取前沿。采用U-Net、DeepLabV3+、SegFormer-B0三种网络，同时评估分区级与前沿级性能，加入真值分区边界检查，并考察在传感器特异与冰川特异子集上的轻量测试时自适应。

Result: 分区标签包含有用的前沿边界信息：从真值分区提取前沿获得最低平均距离误差。但从模型预测分区提取的前沿仍较弱，即使分区分割得分处于中等水平；测试时自适应也未能稳定改善分区引导的前沿恢复效果。

Conclusion: 分区分割性能不应被视为前沿级评估的替代品；要有效利用分区标签，可能需要边界感知训练、标签融合或显式的前沿监督。

Abstract: Automatic calving-front delineation from synthetic aperture radar imagery is challenging because the front is a thin and often ambiguous boundary between glacier ice, ocean, and surrounding rock or terrain. The CAlving Fronts and where to Find thEm (CaFFe) dataset provides both binary calving-front masks and broader semantic zone masks, making it possible to study whether zone-level supervision can support front recovery. In this paper, we compare direct front prediction with zone-guided front extraction using U-Net, DeepLabV3+, and SegFormer-B0 under the same bounding-box-cropped CaFFe setting. In the direct setting, models predict the binary calving-front mask. In the zone-guided setting, the model first predicts four semantic zone classes, and the front is then extracted from the predicted glacier-ocean boundary. We evaluate both zone-level and front-level performance, include a ground-truth-zone boundary check, and examine lightweight test-time adaptation on sensor-specific and glacier-specific subsets. The results show that zone labels contain useful front-boundary information: extracting the front from ground-truth zones gives the lowest mean distance error. However, fronts extracted from model-predicted zones remain weak, even when zone segmentation scores are moderate. Test-time adaptation also does not consistently improve zone-guided front recovery. These results indicate that zone segmentation performance should not be treated as a substitute for front-level evaluation and that effective use of zone labels may require boundary-aware training, label fusion, or explicit front supervision.

</details>


### [25] [Why Convolution Still Matters: Evaluating Inductive Biases in Cryospheric Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04073)
*Chhaya Kulkarni,Emam Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在格陵兰冰盖基准数据集上以统一协议对比AlexNet、ConvNeXt-Tiny、Swin-Tiny与CoAtNet-0，发现AlexNet准确率与宏平均F1最高，ConvNeXt-Tiny宏平均AUC最高，混合架构提升稀有类召回，模型复杂度并不必然带来可靠性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力类深度学习在遥感图像分类中广泛采用，但在冰圈影像上其收益不明：该场景地表状态以细粒度纹理为主且存在类别不平衡。作者旨在检验现代注意力与混合架构能否提升逐类可靠性。

Method: 复用一个此前被认为偏向CNN的格陵兰冰盖图像基准数据集，在完全一致的训练与评估协议下，对经典CNN（AlexNet）、现代CNN（ConvNeXt-Tiny）、纯注意力模型（Swin-Tiny）及卷积-注意力混合模型（CoAtNet-0）进行受控对比，并开展逐类分析。

Result: AlexNet取得最高准确率与最佳宏平均F1（平衡性能最强）；ConvNeXt-Tiny宏平均AUC最高，说明类可分性强但最终决策质量一致性较弱。逐类分析显示混合架构提升了稀有且结构独特地表类的召回率，而卷积模型在纹理主导类别上更可靠。

Conclusion: 架构归纳偏置需与冰圈数据特性相匹配；模型复杂度增加并不必然转化为冰盖地表分类可靠性的提升。

Abstract: Recent advances in attention-based deep learning have motivated their adoption for remote sensing image classification; however, their benefits for cryospheric imagery, where surface states are dominated by fine-grained textures and class imbalance, remain unclear. In this work, we revisit a benchmark Greenland Ice Sheet image dataset, previously shown to favor convolutional neural networks (CNNs), to examine whether modern attention-based and hybrid architectures improve class-wise reliability. We conduct a controlled comparison between a classical CNN (AlexNet), a modern CNN (ConvNeXt-Tiny), a pure attention-based model (Swin-Tiny), and a hybrid convolution-attention model (CoAtNet-0) under identical training and evaluation protocols. Results show that AlexNet achieves the highest accuracy and the strongest balanced performance as measured by macro-averaged F1, while ConvNeXt-Tiny exhibits the highest macro-averaged AUC, indicating strong class separability but less consistent final decision quality. Class-wise analysis reveals that hybrid architectures improve recall for rare and structurally distinct surface classes, whereas convolutional models remain more reliable for texture-dominated categories. These findings highlight the importance of aligning architectural inductive bias with cryospheric data characteristics and suggest that increased model complexity does not necessarily translate to improved reliability for ice-sheet surface classification.

</details>


### [26] [VCURF: Virtual Camera-based Uncertainty of Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04076)
*Liyan Chen,Nathaniel Burgdorfer,Philippos Mordohai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VCURF，一种通过虚拟相机渲染不一致性度量像素级不确定性的黑盒方法，适用于 NeRF 与 3DGS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射场（NeRF、Gaussian Splatting）在新视角合成上进展迅速，渲染结果虽具吸引力但并非无误差，因此需要可靠的像素级不确定性量化。

Method: VCURF 将辐射场模型视为黑盒，仅要求其能按需渲染颜色与深度；在目标视角附近采样虚拟相机，度量这些渲染结果之间的不一致性，以此估计像素级不确定性。该方法无需修改即可适用于 NeRF 和 GS 模型。

Result: 在多种数据集、辐射场模型与基线组合上的实验证明 VCURF 在像素级不确定性估计方面有效。

Conclusion: VCURF 是一种通用、免修改的像素级不确定性估计方法，且研究结论对当前文献中主流的视角选择（view selection）方式提出了质疑。

Abstract: Radiance fields, implemented with either implicit (NeRF) or explicit (Gaussian Splatting) representations, are advancing the state of the art in novel view synthesis at a rapid pace. Even though the rendered views they generate are often compelling, they are not free of errors. In this paper, we propose a new approach for pixel-wise uncertainty quantification based on measuring the inconsistencies among renderings by the radiance field model in virtual cameras sampled near the target viewpoint. We named our approach VCURF for Virtual Camera-based Uncertainty of Radiance Fields. VCURF treats the radiance field model as a black box, only assuming that it is capable of rendering color and depth on demand. This property makes our approach applicable to both NeRF and GS models without any modification. Our experiments on a combination of datasets, radiance field models and baselines demonstrate VCURF's effectiveness in pixel-wise uncertainty estimation. We conclude the paper with findings that question the way view selection is tackled by the majority of the current literature.

</details>


### [27] [Dependable AI-Assisted Engineering: A Formal Framework for AI Participation and Assurance in Safety-Critical Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04084)
*Puxue Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个在工作流单元层面分配AI参与度和保证等级的形式化框架，并在一个已执行的17单元机翼梁结构分析工作流中实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI能产出工程制品，但仅凭"生成"无法决定这些制品是否或如何进入安全关键工作流。需要一种细粒度的方法来分配AI的参与程度及其对应的保证义务。

Method: 构建形式化框架，以工作流单元为粒度。每个单元配有"参与度—保证"记录（工程需求、经批准的操作形式化、适用机制、回退方案、证据义务与适用保证、部署就绪状态）。框架区分六类安排：确定性验证、统计校准准入、AI辅助的授权人工判断、对AI产出物的授权人工裁决、保留的确定性工具路径、显式不参与；这些安排承载不同种类的保证而非同一尺度上的等级。框架还分离形式化保真度与验证器可靠性，提供分阶段分类与就绪程序，并在重复总体假设下推导门控AI辅助单元与现有流程的比较条件。

Result: 在17单元机翼梁结构分析工作流中应用：AI生成的CAD程序通过全部23项确定性检查并首次即被准入；应力数值虽有利，但因预先声明的网格收敛证据不足而未能通过应力准则，转由工程判断处理。

Conclusion: 案例展示了单元层面的选择性AI参与和显式证据处理；作者明确不对工作流级可靠性、认证、结构安全性或生产率作任何声明。

Abstract: Generative AI can produce engineering artefacts, but generation alone does not determine whether or how those artefacts should enter safety-critical workflows. This paper develops a formal framework for assigning AI participation and assurance at the level of individual workflow units. Each unit has a participation and assurance record covering its engineering requirement, an approved operational formalization where applicable, the applicable mechanism, fallback where applicable, evidence obligations and the applicable guarantee, plus a deployment-readiness status. The framework distinguishes deterministic verification, statistically calibrated admission, authorized human judgement supported by AI advice, authorized human adjudication of AI-produced artefacts, retained deterministic tool paths and explicit non-participation; these arrangements carry different kinds of guarantee rather than levels on a common scale. The framework also separates formalization fidelity from verifier soundness, provides a staged classification and readiness procedure, and derives conditions for comparing a gated AI-assisted unit with an incumbent process under recurring-population assumptions. We instantiate and apply the framework in an executed 17-unit wing-spar structural-analysis workflow combining deterministically gated AI-generated CAD, retained deterministic computation and human judgement. The AI-generated CAD program passed all 23 deterministic checks and was admitted at the first attempt. Favourable stress magnitudes did not suffice to pass the stress criteria where the predeclared mesh-convergence evidence was insufficient; those criteria were instead referred to engineering judgement. The case demonstrates selective AI participation and explicit evidence handling at unit level; no claim is made of workflow-level dependability, certification, structural safety or productivity.

</details>


### [28] [UniBRep: Learning Unified Geometry and Topology for Image-conditioned B-Rep Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04092)
*Haiyang Ying,Allen Tu,Jiaye Wu,Tom Goldstein,Matthias Zwicker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniBRep 是一个以几何为先的图像条件 B-rep 生成框架，借助预训练 image-to-3D 模型产生带特征的网格作为统一中间表示，再通过双解码分支与几何/特征引导的构建流程，用 CAD 内核组装显式 B-rep，从而在保证几何精度的同时恢复有效拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张图像生成 B-rep 需要同时满足三项要求：几何忠实重建、拓扑有效、以及支持复杂形状。已有方法往往依赖预设面数限制或难以兼顾几何与拓扑，因此需要一种能突破面数上限、随形状复杂度自适应扩展的方法。

Method: 以几何为先（geometry-first）的框架：将预训练 image-to-3D 模型适配为生成带特征的网格（feature mesh）作为统一中间表示。网格表面提供几何脚手架，空间对齐的学习特征编码面分离线索以支持拓扑恢复。采用双解码分支分别生成几何与面分离特征；随后通过几何与特征引导的构建管线拟合参数化曲面、恢复边界曲线与连接关系，并利用 CAD 内核组装显式 B-rep。

Result: 在标准 DeepCAD 基准上，UniBRep 对 80.49% 的输入生成有效 B-rep，并将 face Chamfer 距离从 CADDreamer 的 0.1096 降至 0.0345；在匹配比较中，所有报告指标均优于 HoLa 公开 demo。进一步实验表明其可扩展到超出标准 30 面范围的高复杂度形状，能泛化到 CAD 训练分布之外的物体，并可定性迁移到真实照片。

Conclusion: 通过从网格区域恢复拓扑，UniBRep 避免了预设架构面数限制，使面数可随形状复杂度扩展，在几何精度、拓扑有效性与复杂形状泛化上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Generating a boundary representation (B-rep) conditioned on a single image requires faithful reconstruction of geometry, valid topology, and support for complex shapes. We present UniBRep, a geometry-first framework that adapts a pretrained image-to-3D model to generate a feature mesh as a unified intermediate representation. Its surface provides a geometric scaffold, while spatially aligned learned features encode face-separation cues for topology recovery. Dual decoder branches generate the geometry and face-separation features; a geometry- and feature-guided construction pipeline then fits parametric surfaces, recovers boundary curves and connectivity, and assembles an explicit B-rep using a CAD kernel. Recovering topology from mesh regions avoids predefined architectural face-count limits, allowing face count to scale with shape complexity. On the standard DeepCAD benchmark, UniBRep produces valid B-reps for 80.49\% of inputs and reduces face Chamfer distance from 0.1096 to 0.0345 relative to CADDreamer. In a matched comparison, UniBRep also outperforms the HoLa public demo across all reported metrics. Further evaluations demonstrate scalability to high-complexity shapes beyond the standard 30-face range, generalization to objects outside the CAD training distribution, and qualitative transfer to real photographs.

</details>


### [29] [Scaling 3D Visual Grounding in Abdominal CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04095)
*Sam Church,Danyal Maqbool,Joshua D. Warner,Andrew Voter,Junjie Hu,Meghan G. Lubner,Tyler J. Bradshaw*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出自动化流程，将放射科日常PACS中的2D标注转化为大规模"短语-掩膜-体积"三元组监督，用于3D CT视觉定位；在单机构59K腹部CT上生成105K样本，并构建两个基准与LocusCT模型，命中率显著优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D CT视觉定位可提升放射工作流，但训练需要大量配对的短语与区域标注，人工由放射科医生逐例标注成本极高。作者认为这种监督在常规报告过程中已被隐式产生（医生在关键图像上放置测距、箭头等2D标注），但此前未被利用。

Method: 构建自动化流水线：首先通过元数据匹配将每个临床标注关联到报告中对应的发现；再用可提示的3D分割模型将2D标注（如测距、箭头）转化为体素级掩膜，从而无需额外放射科标注即可生成短语-掩膜-体积数据集。并在此数据上训练3D视觉定位模型LocusCT。

Result: 应用于单一机构PACS，从59K腹部CT检查生成105K短语-掩膜-体积三元组。提出LocusBench-Onc（240例肿瘤学）与LocusBench-ED（260例急诊，覆盖阑尾炎、血肿、疝等13类）两个经放射科医生复核的基准。LocusCT在LocusBench-Onc命中率0.725、LocusBench-ED命中率0.773，显著优于对比模型。

Conclusion: 常规PACS标注是一种可扩展且此前未被使用的3D视觉定位监督来源，能低成本构建大规模数据集并训练出性能优异的3D定位模型。

Abstract: Visual grounding models can enhance radiology workflows by linking report findings to image regions. This is particularly valuable for 3D CT, where findings often occupy a tiny fraction of the volume. Training 3D grounding models requires large sets of paired phrases and regions, and building such datasets is expensive, requiring radiologists to annotate images by hand. We posit that this supervision is already created implicitly during routine reporting, as radiologists frequently place 2D annotations (e.g., distance measurement, arrows) on key images to make measurements and to support report interpretation. We introduce an automated pipeline that converts these routine clinical annotations into large-scale phrase-region supervision for 3D visual grounding. The pipeline links each annotation to the corresponding finding in the report through metadata matching, then uses a promptable 3D segmentation model to convert the 2D annotation into a volumetric mask. This produces phrase-mask-volume datasets without requiring additional radiologist annotation. Applied to a single institution's clinical picture archiving and communication system (PACS), our approach generated 105K phrase-mask-volume triplets from 59K abdominal CT exams. We also introduce two abdominal CT grounding benchmarks, LocusBench-Onc and LocusBench-ED, which comprise 240 oncology and 260 emergency-department radiologist-reviewed phrase-mask-volume triplets, respectively, with the latter spanning 13 distinct categories such as appendicitis, hematoma, and hernia. We further introduce LocusCT, a 3D visual grounding model trained on this dataset, which achieves hit rates of 0.725 on LocusBench-Onc and 0.773 on LocusBench-ED, substantially outperforming comparator models. These results show that routine PACS annotations are a scalable, previously unused source of supervision for 3D visual grounding.

</details>


### [30] [Where Does the Semantic Gain Come From? A Reproduction and Extension of Semantic Knowledge-driven Contrastive Learning for Long-Tailed Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04104)
*Sushrut Ghimire*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文复现了SKCL方法，发现其宣称的增益主要来自基线选择与训练轮数差异，语义图本身贡献有限，并指出该图与BCL模型的混淆结构高度重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SKCL论文宣称在CIFAR-100-LT上比BCL高出2.01个点，但代码与类别描述未公开，结果无法直接验证；作者希望通过独立复现,厘清SKCL真实增益的来源。

Method: 在同一框架下重新实现SKCL、BCL与ConCutMix，与公开基线代码校对，并对每种配置使用三个随机种子；进一步将SKCL嵌入作者自己的ConCutMix代码中，在300与400轮不同训练预算下对比；分析BCL模型自身最混淆类别与语义图top-2邻居的重叠率。

Result: 两个基线复现误差在1.5个点内，但按论文描述实现的SKCL比BCL低0.56个点；在ConCutMix代码中训练300轮达53.69，仅比发表值低0.33；相同预算下语义图只带来+0.23，而多训练100轮带来+1.04；BCL模型与语义图top-2邻居重叠41.2%（随机为2.0%）。结合CutMix分支提升1.06点，自适应图分别提升+0.34与+0.28，但均在种子噪声范围内。

Conclusion: SKCL的宣称增益可由ConCutMix的基线优势和额外训练轮数解释，而非语义图驱动；由于BCL本身的混淆结构与语义邻居高度重合，图在该基准上增益有限。作者建议对语义驱动的对比学习方法进行更严格的复现与消融评估。

Abstract: Semantic Knowledge-driven Contrastive Learning (SKCL) uses a language model to decide which classes are related, and pulls each image towards the prototypes of its semantic neighbours. On CIFAR-100-LT (beta = 100) it reports 54.02% top-1 accuracy, 2.01 points above Balanced Contrastive Learning (BCL), the method it builds on. The code and the class descriptions are not public. I reimplement SKCL, BCL and ConCutMix in one framework, check it against the public baseline code, and run every configuration with three seeds. The two baselines reproduce within 1.5 points, but SKCL built on BCL, as the paper describes it, ends up 0.56 points below BCL. To find out why, I add SKCL to the authors' own ConCutMix code. Trained for the paper's 300 epochs, it reaches 53.69, only 0.33 below the published number. At the same budget, however, the semantic graph adds just 0.23 points over ConCutMix, while training ConCutMix for 100 more epochs adds 1.04. Together with ConCutMix's published lead over BCL (1.15), this explains the claimed gain. A BCL model that never sees the graph already shares 41.2% of the graph's top-2 neighbours with its own most-confused classes (2.0% by chance), which shows why the graph adds so little on these benchmarks. I also test several changes to SKCL. Combining it with the CutMix branch improves it by 1.06 points, and an adaptive version of the graph improves it slightly (+0.34 and +0.28 in two codebases), although these gains are within seed noise.

</details>


### [31] [From Sight to Foresight: Predictive Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04139)
*Feiran Wang,Xiaoqi Wang,Ziwei Li,Wenbin He,Yan Yan,Liu Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialMind，一个度量尺度的视觉语言模型（VLM），用于空间推理与未来预测。它通过度量深度适配器将空间推理锚定到真实世界尺度，并用渐进状态链建立当前状态与观测动态，作为未来预测的基础。作者还构建了可扩展数据引擎、SpatialMind-30K数据集与SpatialMind-2K基准，覆盖驾驶与日常第一视角场景，包含三个层级共八项任务。实验表明SpatialMind在自建基准上显著优于通用与空间专用模型，并在VSI-Bench、OSI-Bench、VLM4D上取得有竞争力的零样本表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测未来空间状态对动态环境中的避障与及时决策至关重要。然而现有视觉语言模型与空间推理基准主要关注已观测场景，对超出观测区间的预测性空间推理探索不足。

Method: 提出SpatialMind度量尺度VLM：度量深度适配器将空间推理锚定到真实世界尺度；渐进状态链将当前空间状态与观测动态作为未来预测的基础。给定视频前缀，模型预测已观测与未见未来帧中的距离、运动方向和空间关系。构建可扩展数据引擎，将实体描述锚定到度量几何以生成问答对与状态监督，并据此构建SpatialMind-30K数据集和SpatialMind-2K基准。

Result: 在SpatialMind-2K基准上，SpatialMind显著优于通用模型与空间专用模型；在VSI-Bench、OSI-Bench和VLM4D上取得有竞争力的零样本性能。基准覆盖驾驶与日常第一视角场景，包含当前状态理解、观测动态理解、未来预测三个层级共八项任务。

Conclusion: SpatialMind将空间推理从观测场景拓展到未来预测，并通过度量尺度建模与状态链设计提升预测性空间推理能力，配套数据集与基准为该方向提供了训练与评估基础。

Abstract: Predicting future spatial states supports collision avoidance and timely decision-making in dynamic environments. However, existing vision-language models (VLMs) and benchmarks for spatial reasoning primarily focus on observed scenes, leaving predictive spatial reasoning beyond the observed interval underexplored. To this end, we introduce SpatialMind, a metric-scale VLM for spatial reasoning and future prediction. Its metric depth adapter anchors spatial reasoning to real-world scale, while its progressive state chain establishes current spatial states and observed dynamics as the foundation for future prediction. Given a video prefix, SpatialMind predicts distances, motion directions, and spatial relations in both observed and unseen future frames. For training and evaluation, we build a scalable data engine that grounds entity descriptions in metric geometry to generate question-answer pairs and state supervision. Using this engine, we construct the SpatialMind-30K dataset and the SpatialMind-2K benchmark, both covering driving and everyday egocentric scenes. The benchmark spans eight tasks across three levels: current-state understanding, observed-dynamics understanding, and future prediction. Experiments show that SpatialMind substantially outperforms both general and spatially specialized models on our benchmark while achieving competitive zero-shot performance on VSI-Bench, OSI-Bench, and VLM4D.

</details>


### [32] [Watermarks and Fingerprints as Soft Bindings for Content Provenance: An Open-Licence Benchmark for Images, Audio and Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04151)
*Seyedmahdi Kazempourradi,Ramtin Mojtahedi,Behrang Mohseni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在同一协议下系统基准测试了水印与指纹两类软绑定技术，覆盖图像、音频、视频，评估感知质量、鲁棒性、误报与成本，揭示两族方法在许可、误报与失败模式上的关键差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C2PA等内容来源标准在清单被剥离后需依赖软绑定恢复来源；水印与指纹两族方法各有宣称但缺乏统一、可复现的公平比较，尤其在开源许可、负样本校准误报率等方面的可比性不足。

Method: 在统一协议下，仅使用经许可审计的开放模型与公开媒体，误配率在留出负样本上校准并用源级bootstrap给出置信区间。水印：12种方法的25种图像/7种音频/7种视频配置，评估感知质量、鲁棒性、误报与成本；指纹：35种方法，注册库最大98,985张图像，含部分编辑与对抗攻击。

Result: 图像/视频水印PixelSeal最佳，音频AudioSeal最佳；但所有TrustMark变体的纠错检测器在未标记图像上触发率5.9%–15.3%，验证器应检验期望载荷。指纹中，非商用数据训练的复制检测器在配对误配率1e-7下检出率最高75.5%，宽松许可最佳的DINOv2为65.0%；误配主要由商品目录近副本导致，几何验证未带来检测提升。两族失败模式不同：并集覆盖图像副本69%，指纹覆盖更多音频查询，期望密钥水印验证覆盖更多视频查询。水印使62.0%图像的ISCC代码越过匹配阈值，颜色转换导致x86与ARM主机间水印比特变化。

Conclusion: 水印与指纹是互补而非互替的软绑定方案，选择需结合模态、许可与误报约束；验证应检验期望载荷而非仅检测存在性，且须考虑平台相关色彩转换对水印稳定性的影响。

Abstract: Content-provenance standards such as C2PA let a platform recover a stripped manifest through a soft binding: an invisible watermark read from the content, or a fingerprint looked up in a registry. We benchmarked both families under one protocol, restricted to openly available models whose licences we audited, on public media, with false-match rates calibrated on held-out negatives and source-level bootstrap intervals for performance estimates. For watermarking we evaluated 25 image, 7 audio and 7 video configurations from 12 methods on perceptual quality, robustness, false positives and cost; for fingerprinting, 35 methods on registries of up to 98,985 images, partial edits and adversarial attacks. PixelSeal gave the best balance for image and video watermarks and AudioSeal for audio, but the error-correcting detector of every TrustMark variant fired on 5.9 to 15.3% of unmarked images, so a verifier should test the expected payload. Among fingerprints, copy detectors trained on non-commercial data detected up to 75.5% of transformed images at a pair-level false-match rate of $10^{-7}$ and DINOv2, the best permissively licensed method, 65.0%; near-copies in a product catalogue dominated the false matches, and no detection improvement from geometric verification was observed under a matched calibration false-binding constraint in the evaluated image pipelines. On the same attacked copies the two families failed differently: the union of watermark and fingerprint successes covered 69% of image copies; fingerprints covered more audio queries, whereas expected-key watermark verification covered more video queries. Embedding a watermark moved the ISCC code of 62.0% of images past its match threshold, and platform-dependent colour conversion changed watermark bits between x86 and ARM hosts.

</details>


### [33] [Kepler4D: Controllable Future Video Generation via 4D Scene State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04152)
*Feiran Wang,Bin Duan,Junyi Wu,Gaowen Liu,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Kepler4D：通过显式4D场景状态演化实现单目视频的未来视频生成，将物体运动预测与外观合成解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型需在视觉观测之外保持场景结构、预测动态物体的未来演化，现有方法在对象运动可控性与场景一致性上存在不足。

Method: 给定单目视频，构建含背景几何、物体运动历史、粗空间支撑与语义上下文的共享3D表示；Chain-of-Motion用视觉语言模型基于观测运动选择结构化速度/航向决策，并判定是否对物体中心高度设定下界；确定性rollout将决策转为未来轨迹以供检查与编辑；再将演化代理渲染为几何控制信号，驱动预训练视频生成器，分离粗物体运动与外观/关节合成。

Result: 在真实世界视频上的实验表明，Kepler4D可实现可控的物体运动与合理的未来rollout，同时保持场景一致性。

Conclusion: 显式4D场景状态演化是提升视频世界模型可控性与一致性的有效途径。

Abstract: Video world models aim to preserve scene structure and predict how dynamic objects evolve beyond visual observations. We present Kepler4D, a framework for future video generation through explicit 4D scene state evolution. Given a monocular video, Kepler4D constructs a shared 3D representation of background geometry, object motion histories, coarse spatial supports, and semantic context. Chain-of-Motion summarizes observed motion and uses a vision-language model to select structured speed and heading decisions and decide whether to bound object-center height from below. A deterministic rollout converts these decisions into future object trajectories for inspection and editing before synthesis. We render the evolving proxies into geometric controls for a pretrained video generator, separating coarse object motion from the synthesis of appearance and articulation. Experiments on real-world videos demonstrate that Kepler4D enables controllable object motion and plausible future rollout while preserving scene consistency.

</details>


### [34] [Diagnosis-Conditioned Spatial Gating and Decoder-Level Supervised Contrastive Learning for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04159)
*Md Mustafizur Rahman,Mylene C. Q. Farias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出位置感知门控PAG与解码器级监督对比损失DSCL，提升放射报告生成的临床准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诊断驱动方法虽可改善报告生成，但其预测结果无法直接修改解码器的视觉patch特征，且全局门控对所有空间位置施加相同调制，导致发现级不准确。

Method: PAG利用预测的发现表征在无区域监督下对视觉patch进行空间调制；DSCL在解码器层用共享阳性发现（而非实例身份）构建对比表示。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR上，PAG+DSCL将CE F1从匹配全局门控基线的0.484提升至0.502，PAG与DSCL单独分别为0.491和0.495；在IU X-Ray上无需微调即达0.226 CE F1，高于PromptMRG的0.211。

Conclusion: 位置感知的视觉调制与基于发现的对比学习可有效提升放射报告生成的临床效能，并具备跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Radiology report generation models can produce fluent text while still containing finding-level inaccuracies. Diagnosis-driven methods improve generation by conditioning on predicted findings, but these predictions do not directly modify the visual patch features provided to the decoder, and global gating applies the same modulation across spatial locations. We introduce a Position-Aware Gate (PAG) that uses predicted finding representations to modulate visual patches spatially without region supervision. We also propose a Decoder-level Supervised Contrastive Loss (DSCL) that structures decoder representations using shared positive findings rather than instance identity. On MIMIC-CXR, PAG+DSCL improves clinical efficacy (CE) F1 from 0.484 to 0.502 over a matched global-gate reference, while PAG and DSCL individually reach 0.491 and 0.495. Without additional fine-tuning, the combined model achieves 0.226 CE F1 on IU X-Ray, compared with 0.211 reported by PromptMRG.

</details>


### [35] [Referring Multi-Object Tracking in Moving-Camera Videos via Global Motion Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04185)
*Hsin-Chen Pai,Jyun-Kai Wang,Yi-Cheng Peng,Wei-Ta Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过估计车载视角视频中的相机运动来提取帧间残差运动，并将运动匹配程度与现有RMOT方法的预测结果进行后期融合，从而提升指代多目标跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多目标跟踪需求中许多涉及物体的运动方式而非外观，但在运动相机拍摄的视频中，相机运动会导致静止车辆看似移动、运动车辆位移却不明显，而现有RMOT方法虽将运动与文本关联，却未显式去除相机引起的运动干扰。
