FLASHSWIN restores position information as window-local learnable 2D RoPE, making large windows and dense token grids both affordable and accurate. At matched scale, FLASHSWIN-T outperforms Swin variants. With dense tokens and wide windows ($p=2,M=32$), the same Tiny model reaches $84.1\%$ ImageNet-1K, $44.1$ COCO box AP, and $47.28$ ADE20K mIoU---gains of $+1.3$, $+5.1$, and $+1.82$ over SwinV2-T at $M=16$, respectively. At fixed $M=32$, halving the patch size yields roughly $3\times$ larger gains in boundary quality than in mIoU.

</details>


### [68] [COMPASS: Comet Object Measurement Pipeline with Automated Selection and Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04680)
*Jack Roberts,Canya Lu,Alexis Michelle Lawson,Kaitlyn Holden,Gerald S. Wilkinson,Anne M. Bronikowski,Ritambhara Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COMPASS：基于深度学习的彗星实验图像自动化分析流程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 彗星实验（单细胞凝胶电泳）广泛用于单细胞水平DNA损伤定量，但图像分析多依赖人工判读或半自动软件，劳动强度大、可重复性差，且对图像质量和彗星形态敏感。

Method: COMPASS将基于深度学习的彗星分割与损伤测量、自动化彗星筛选相结合，构建端到端图像分析流程；并以Python实现，开源发布，提供预训练权重与使用示例。

Result: 实现了在减少人工投入的同时输出标准化的DNA损伤测量结果，具备稳健的检测能力，并可通过透明化筛选与可选人工复核提升可重复性。

Conclusion: COMPASS为彗星实验图像分析提供了自动化、开源且可复现的解决方案。

Abstract: Summary: The single-cell gel electrophoresis ('comet') assay is a widely used technique for quantifying DNA damage at the individual cell level. However, image analysis often relies on manual inspection or semi-automated software, which can be labor-intensive, difficult to reproduce, and sensitive to image quality and comet morphology. COMPASS automates comet assay image analysis by combining deep learning-based comet segmentation with damage measurement and automated comet selection. The pipeline produces standardized DNA damage measurements while offering robust detection, reducing manual effort and improving reproducibility through transparent selection and optional manual review.
  Availability and implementation: COMPASS is implemented in Python and is freely available at https://github.com/ rsinghlab/COMPASS. Installation instructions, pretrained weights, and example usage are provided in the repository.
  Contact: jack_roberts2@brown.edu, ritsingh@illinois.edu
  Supplementary information: Available onlime upon publication.

</details>


### [69] [Decouple, Purify and Unite: Semantic-Structural Prototype Learning for Federated Medical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04700)
*Xingyue Zhao,Wenke Huang,Linghao Zhuang,Yanzhou Su,Zhifeng Wang,Haoyu Zhao,Mengfan Li,Junjun He,Tao Tan,Dakai Jin,Le Lu,Mang Ye,Qiang Yang,Ming Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FedBCS+，一种面向医学图像分割的联邦学习方法，通过频域风格重校准、解耦上下文原型对齐与风格净化语义原型聚合，缓解特征异质性，在五个异构数据集上取得最高平均 Dice。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可在不共享数据的前提下训练全局模型，但医学场景中扫描仪或协议差异导致的特征异质性仍是难点。现有基于表征的方法存在两点局限：一是上下文表征学习不完整，单层或耦合表征忽略多层级结构线索，并将区域语义与边界细节纠缠；二是逐层风格与聚合偏差，中间层的域特有风格差异会劣化原型，而忽视客户端分布偏移的聚合会进一步放大偏差。

Method: FedBCS+ 采用三项关键设计：1）频率域风格重校准（FSR），在原型构建中解耦内容与风格表征，提取风格净化后的原型；2）解耦上下文原型对齐（DCPA），显式将多层级特征解耦为语义原型与结构原型，分别对齐区域语义和细粒度解剖结构；3）风格净化语义原型聚合（S2PA），度量各客户端净化原型与全局共识的偏差，自适应地向上表征不足的客户端重新加权聚合，以降低共识偏差。

Result: 在覆盖组织病理学、MRI、超声和结肠镜的五个异构医学分割基准上，FedBCS+ 在所比较方法中取得最高平均 Dice。收敛性分析进一步刻画了聚合与对齐如何影响优化界。

Conclusion: FedBCS+ 通过风格净化与解耦式上下文对齐，有效缓解特征异质性带来的原型劣化与聚合偏差，在多种医学分割任务上表现优异，并从优化界角度给出了理论解释。

Abstract: Federated learning enables medical institutions to train a global model without sharing data, yet feature heterogeneity from diverse scanners or protocols remains challenging. Existing representation-based methods face two limitations: 1) Incomplete Contextual Representation Learning: single-layer or coupled representations overlook multi-level structural cues and entangle regional semantics with boundary details. 2) Layerwise Style and Aggregation Biases: domain-specific style discrepancies across intermediate layers degrade prototypes, while aggregation that overlooks client distribution shifts can further amplify bias. We propose FedBCS+, federated decoupled contextual alignment with style-purified aggregation. We employ Frequency-domain Style Recalibration (FSR) in prototype construction to decouple content-style representations and extract style-purified prototypes. Built upon these purified features, Decoupled Contextual Prototype Alignment (DCPA) explicitly decouples multi-level features into semantic and structural prototypes and aligns regional semantics and fine-grained anatomical structures separately. Style-purified Semantic Prototype Aggregation (S2PA) measures each client's purified prototype divergence from the global consensus and adaptively reweights aggregation toward under-represented clients to reduce consensus bias. On five heterogeneous medical segmentation benchmarks spanning histopathology, MRI, ultrasound, and colonoscopy, FedBCS+ achieves the highest mean Dice among the compared methods. A convergence analysis further characterizes how aggregation and alignment affect the optimization bound.

</details>


### [70] [Knossos and Ariadne: Benchmarking and Learning Complete Diagram Topology Extraction with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04721)
*Bangwei Guo,Xujiang Zhao,Shengyu Chen,Yanchi Liu,Wei Cheng,Xi Zhu,Guoning Zhang,Dimitris N. Metaxas,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究"图到图拓扑抽取"任务，提出大规模基准Knossos与结构化框架Ariadne，将任务分解为节点清单抽取与源条件边预测，在开源VLM上显著提升完整拓扑抽取性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型虽能识别结构图元素并推理其内容，但完整图拓扑（全部实体及全部关系）的抽取相对未被充分探索，且缺乏大规模监督数据与系统评测手段。

Method: 1) 构建Knossos基准：覆盖6个领域的19,200张图、245,179个节点、439,740条边，采用符号化生成流程保证渲染图与完整拓扑注释、关系类型及连接线几何的精确对齐；2) 提出Ariadne框架：将完整拓扑抽取分解为节点清单抽取与源条件边预测两个子任务；3) 在Knossos上训练并评测多种VLM方法，与一步式抽取进行对照。

Result: 在Knossos上训练可显著提升小型开源VLM的完整拓扑抽取能力；在同等监督下Ariadne优于一步式抽取，平均Edge F1在所有评测方法中最高；两种骨干变体在真实外部基准上均优于未适配的对应模型。

Conclusion: 结构化分解（节点清单+源条件边预测）对完整图拓扑抽取有额外增益，Knossos提供了可扩展的监督训练与系统评测基础，代码与基准已公开。

Abstract: Structural diagrams are widely used to represent complex systems and relational information across scientific, engineering, procedural, and spatial domains. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) have become increasingly capable of recognizing diagram elements and reasoning about their content, while complete diagram topology extraction remains comparatively underexplored. In this paper, we study diagram-to-graph topology extraction: extracting all diagram entities and the complete relations among them. To enable large-scale supervised training and systematic evaluation of this task, we introduce Knossos, a benchmark of 19,200 diagrams across six diverse domains, with 245,179 nodes and 439,740 edges. Its symbolic generation process provides exact alignment between rendered diagrams and annotations of complete topology, relation types, and connector geometry. To address the modeling challenge of complete topology extraction, we also present Ariadne, a structured framework that decomposes the task into node inventory extraction and source-conditioned edge prediction. Extensive experiments show that training on Knossos substantially improves complete topology extraction in smaller open-source VLMs. Ariadne further improves over one-step extraction under matched supervision, demonstrating the additional benefit of structured decomposition. It achieves the highest average Edge F1 among the evaluated methods on Knossos, while both backbone variants also improve over their unadapted counterparts on the real-world external benchmark. Code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/bangwayne/knossos_Ariadne_Public.

</details>


### [71] [NAMVIS: Next-Scale Autoregressive Multi-View Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04722)
*Ramil Khafizov,Ilya Statsenko,Ruslan Rakhimov,Artem Komarichev,Peter Wonka,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NAMVIS是一个无扩散的稀疏视角多视图合成框架，将多视图生成重构为几何条件化的"下一尺度自回归"，通过少量由粗到细的尺度步骤预测离散视觉token，在性能和速度上均优于扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的稀疏视角新视角合成方法受限于迭代去噪过程，导致多视图生成的推理成本高昂，亟需更高效的替代范式。

Method: 提出NAMVIS框架，将多视图图像合成重构为几何条件化的下一尺度自回归：不通过重复去噪，而是用少量由粗到细的尺度步骤预测离散视觉token，每个尺度内及跨目标视图的token并行采样。同时提出多尺度投影位姿编码（Multi-scale Projective Pose Encoding），在每个分辨率下将源与目标相机变换注入目标视图自注意力和源到目标的交叉注意力，并结合全局条件与密集几何感知交叉注意力。

Result: 在Objaverse、GSO和OmniObject3D上，NAMVIS在PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS指标上均优于扩散基线，且在相同评测设置下运行速度比所评估的扩散基线快3倍以上。

Conclusion: 结果表明，几何条件化的下一尺度自回归是稀疏视角多视图合成中一个有前景且高效的扩散替代方案。

Abstract: Sparse-view novel view synthesis is a central problem in 3D content creation, but diffusion-based approaches remain limited by iterative denoising, making multi-view generation expensive at inference time. We introduce NAMVIS, a diffusion-free framework that reformulates multi-view image synthesis as geometry-conditioned next-scale autoregression. Instead of generating target views through repeated denoising, NAMVIS predicts discrete visual tokens through a small number of coarse-to-fine scale steps, while sampling all tokens within each scale and across target views in parallel. To anchor this generation process to explicit camera geometry, we propose Multi-scale Projective Pose Encoding, which injects source and target camera transformations into both target-view self-attention and source-to-target cross-attention at every resolution. NAMVIS further combines global conditioning with dense geometry-aware cross-attention, enabling the model to preserve source-view appearance while maintaining target-view consistency. Across Objaverse, GSO, and OmniObject3D, NAMVIS outperforms diffusion-based baselines in PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS, while running over 3 times faster than the evaluated diffusion baselines under the same evaluation setting. These results suggest that geometry-conditioned next-scale autoregression is a promising and efficient alternative to diffusion for sparse-view multi-view synthesis. Additional qualitative results, videos, and resources are available at https://corl-team.github.io/namvis/

</details>


### [72] [Investigating Spatiotemporal Redundancy in Video Transformer for Collision Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04727)
*Xiaoshan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以VideoMAEv2-Base在Nexar碰撞预测数据集上为对象，分析了视频Transformer在工人-设备接近监测中的冗余结构：MLP结构化容量冗余与token流的时空冗余。基于“时间冗余远大于空间冗余”的发现，采用时间token合并与重要性引导的MLP单元剪枝，在精度基本不变的前提下将计算量从356.99降至178.50 GFLOPs、时延从12.69降至7.21 ms（1.76倍加速），并保持相当的判别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频Transformer在碰撞预测中精度高但计算开销大，与移动机器人低时延推理需求以及建筑领域低碳计算目标之间存在矛盾。因此需要明确网络内部哪些计算是冗余的，以及这些冗余能否在不显著损害预测性能的情况下被去除。

Method: 在Nexar碰撞预测数据集上使用VideoMAEv2-Base，先用线性探针考察碰撞相关信息随网络深度的演化，再分别研究两类冗余：(1) MLP中的结构化容量冗余，采用重要性引导的单元保留与随机保留对照；(2) token流中的时空冗余，通过相邻时间token相似度与空间token相似度对比，并据此实施时间token合并。

Result: 线性探针显示，可解释运动线索（流幅值、looming、接近/远离）在中间层最易获取，而碰撞标签判别能力向末层增强。token冗余具有轴向特异性：后层相邻时间token相似度达0.970，空间相似度降至0.297，即时间冗余显著高于空间冗余。据此进行时间token合并，主干计算由356.99降至178.50 GFLOPs，每clip时延由12.69降至7.21 ms，实现1.76倍加速，mAP仅由0.7478变为0.7443。保留50% MLP单元时AUC为0.753，而等量随机保留仅为0.529；剪枝会先改变分数校准，随后判别排序才崩溃。

Conclusion: 视频Transformer的冗余具有明确的层次性和轴向不对称性：时间token压缩与神经元剪枝可在基本不损失预测性能的前提下显著降低计算与时延，为通过定向时间token压缩和神经元剪枝实现更快算法提供了新路径，同时有助于满足移动机器人低时延推理与低碳计算需求。

Abstract: In worker-equipment proximity monitoring, video transformers are widely used for collision anticipation and have demonstrated strong performance. However, their accuracy comes with substantial computational demands, creating a tension with the need for low-latency inference on mobile robots and the pursuit of lower-carbon computation in construction. To address this, this study investigates where computation within an established video transformer is redundant and whether that redundancy can be removed without materially degrading predictive performance. Using VideoMAEv2-Base on the Nexar Collision Prediction dataset, we first examine how collision-relevant information evolves across network depth and then investigate two complementary forms of redundancy: structured capacity redundancy in multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) and spatiotemporal redundancy in the token stream. Linear probes show that interpretable motion cues, including flow magnitude, looming, and approach versus retreat, are most accessible at intermediate layers, whereas collision-label discrimination strengthens toward the final layer. Token redundancy is axis-specific: adjacent temporal-token similarity reaches 0.970 in later layers, while spatial similarity falls to 0.297, indicating substantially greater redundancy across time than across space. Exploiting this asymmetry, temporal token merging reduces backbone computation from 356.99 to 178.50 GFLOPs and latency from 12.69 to 7.21 ms per clip, a 1.76x speedup, while mean average precision changes only from 0.7478 to 0.7443. Importance-guided retention of 50% of MLP units preserves an AUC of 0.753, compared with 0.529 under matched random retention, and reveals that pruning alters score calibration before discriminative ranking collapses. These findings establish a new pathway for pursuing faster algorithms through targeted temporal token compression and neuron pruning.

</details>


### [73] [Probabilistic Pedestrian Forecasts from a Handheld Phone: World-Frame Heat Maps, Visual-Inertial Height Drift, and Evaluation without Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04736)
*Danial Safaei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该系统仅用单目相机与VIO，将行人检测并投影到地面、在重力对齐度量帧中跟踪，并用小型U-Net预测未来数秒的逐步概率图；在SDD与EgoTraj-Bench上显著优于恒速高斯基线，但在手持视频的预注册评估中优势减半，且自一致性评分存在解释局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手持手机用户希望预知附近行人未来几秒的位置，而现有方案需要地面真值、多相机或稳定平台。本文旨在验证仅靠单目相机与视觉惯性里程计（VIO）能否在手机移动、地面静止的条件下实现可用的行人轨迹预测。

Method: 检测行人后通过射线-平面求交将其"抬升"到地面，在重力对齐的度量帧中跟踪；以小型U-Net在鸟瞰（SDD）与第一人称（EgoTraj-Bench）轨迹上以负对数似然（NLL）损失训练，输出逐步概率图。用低通滤波后的高度约束相机离地高度以抑制VIO垂直漂移与用户楼层变化引起的尺度失真。

Result: 在手持ADVIO数据上，垂直VIO漂移与自身楼层变化会无声地重缩放单目地面位置（某片段90秒内达87%，另一片段最后31%轨迹全部丢失），固定离地高度可避免该问题但在扶梯上滞后。SDD与EgoTraj-Bench测试集上，4.8秒处NLL较拟合恒速高斯分别降低1.51与1.37 nats。七个留出片段的预注册评估中，1.2/2.4/4.8秒NLL分别低0.17/0.23/0.47 nats（95%区间不含零），但降幅不足开发集的一半；按片段重采样则1.2与2.4秒区间含零，双方重新校准后网络仅在1.2秒显著更优。

Conclusion: 单目+VIO的行人预测在自采手持数据上可行，但增益小于基准数据集；由于缺乏行人真值，只能以跟踪器后续原始测量作自一致性评分，该评分对重采样单元、校准方式与位姿来源高度敏感，其可证明与不可证明的边界需谨慎解读。

Abstract: A pedestrian with a phone could be shown where nearby people will be in the next few seconds, if the forecast stays on the ground while the phone moves, is calibrated, and needs only a monocular camera and visual-inertial odometry (VIO). We build and evaluate such a system. People are detected, lifted onto the floor by ray-plane intersection, tracked in a gravity-aligned metric frame, and forecast as per-step probability maps by a small U-Net trained with a negative log-likelihood (NLL) loss on bird's-eye (SDD) and first-person (EgoTraj-Bench) trajectories. On handheld ADVIO recordings, vertical VIO drift and the user's own changes of level silently rescale monocular ground positions (by 87% within 90 s on one clip; on another, all tracks are lost for the last 31% of the clip); keeping the camera's height above the floor constant under a low-pass-filtered altitude avoids this, though it lags on escalators. On the SDD and EgoTraj-Bench test splits, the final forecaster lowers the NLL at 4.8 s by 1.51 and 1.37 nats relative to a fitted constant-velocity Gaussian. Lacking ground truth for people in handheld video, we score forecasts against the tracker's own later raw measurements. In an internally pre-registered evaluation on seven held-out clips, the final forecaster's NLL is lower than the benchmark-fitted baseline's at 1.2, 2.4 and 4.8 s (by 0.17, 0.23 and 0.47 nats; 95% intervals over people exclude zero), but by less than half as much as on the development clips. Exploratory analyses cut both ways: resampling clips instead of people widens the intervals to include zero at 1.2 and 2.4 s, and once both forecasters are recalibrated on the development clips the network is significantly better only at 1.2 s; but two clips run with ADVIO's reference poses favour the network much more when re-run with the phone's own poses. We discuss what such self-consistency scores can and cannot show.

</details>


### [74] [ThyCLIPNet: A BiomedCLIP-Guided Lightweight Attention-Enhanced DeepLabV3+ Framework for Robust Thyroid Nodule Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04743)
*Tasnim Jahan,Md Easin Arafat,Swakkhar Shatabda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ThyCLIPNet：一种轻量级语义引导混合编解码框架，将BiomedCLIP视觉编码器的全局生物医学语义引入多尺度CNN分割流程，实现高效鲁棒的甲状腺超声分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺超声分割面临低对比度、斑点噪声和边界模糊等挑战；现有方法多依赖资源密集架构，或缺乏多尺度特征与全局生物医学视觉引导的显式融合。

Method: 编码器采用MobileNetV2结合高效通道注意力，并以空洞空间金字塔池化和自定义卷积块注意力模块增强瓶颈特征；解码器结合分层跳跃连接、轻量注意力精修，以及BiomedCLIP引导的门控融合路径，将纯视觉全局生物医学嵌入投影到解码器特征空间，通过语义-局部融合和空间门控进行选择性整合。首次仅用BiomedCLIP视觉编码器（无文本提示）提供图像级全局语义引导。

Result: 在TG3K、TN3K、DDTI、PKTN上Dice分别为96.22%、87.58%、84.73%、80.70%；IoU为92.72%、77.91%、73.51%、67.64%；HD95为3.75、16.38、18.23、10.86；参数量8.55M，FLOPs 22.99G。

Conclusion: 结果支持将全局生物医学语义引导与轻量多尺度CNN表示相结合，可实现鲁棒且计算高效的甲状腺超声分割。

Abstract: Accurate thyroid ultrasound segmentation is often challenged by low contrast, speckle noise, and unclear boundaries. Although recent methods have improved segmentation accuracy, many rely on resource-intensive architectures or lack explicit integration of multiscale features with global biomedical visual guidance. In this paper, we introduce ThyCLIPNet, a lightweight semantic-guided hybrid encoder-decoder framework that integrates BiomedCLIP-derived biomedical semantic guidance into a lightweight multi-scale CNN segmentation pipeline. The encoder integrates MobileNetV2 with efficient channel attention, while atrous spatial pyramid pooling and a custom convolutional block attention module enrich bottleneck features. The decoder combines hierarchical skip connections and lightweight attention refinement with a BiomedCLIP-guided gated fusion pathway that projects vision-only global biomedical embeddings into decoder feature space and selectively integrates them through semantic-local fusion and spatial gating. To the best of our knowledge, ThyCLIPNet is among the first lightweight thyroid ultrasound segmentation frameworks to use BiomedCLIP's vision encoder alone for image-only global semantic guidance without text prompting. Experiments on TG3K, TN3K, DDTI, and PKTN achieve dice similarity coefficients of 96.22%, 87.58%, 84.73%, and 80.70%; intersection over union scores of 92.72%, 77.91%, 73.51%, and 67.64%; and 95th-percentile hausdorff distances of 3.75, 16.38, 18.23, and 10.86, respectively. ThyCLIPNet uses 8.55M parameters and 22.99G FLOPs. Overall, the results support integrating global biomedical semantic guidance with lightweight multi-scale CNN representations for robust and computationally efficient thyroid ultrasound segmentation. Source code: https://github.com/Tasnim-Jahan/ThyCLIPNet. [Abstract shortened for arXiv. See PDF for full abstract.]

</details>


### [75] [ARISE: Adaptive Agentic Reasoning with Image-grounded Self-Evaluation for Interpretable IBD Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04777)
*Pronoma Banerjee,Anuva Shah,Jason Wu,Md. Masudur Rahman,Sanjay Mohanty,Satya Kurada,Juan P. Wachs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARISE是一个自主规划框架，将少样本医学图像理解建模为序贯智能体工作流，用于炎症性肠病的多模态诊断，在胶囊内镜和肠道超声两个队列上提升了诊断性能并增强了可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 炎症性肠病（IBD）需频繁的影像学评估，但无线胶囊内镜（WCE）和肠道超声等模态的解读高度依赖专家经验。视觉语言模型（VLMs）在多模态医学图像分析中潜力显著，但其临床应用受限于领域推理能力不足、易产生幻觉、细粒度诊断的高质量训练数据稀缺以及可解释性有限。

Method: 提出ARISE（Adaptive Agentic Reasoning with Image-grounding Self-Evaluation），一个自主规划框架，将少样本医学图像理解建模为序贯智能体工作流。ARISE将智能体执行结构化为透明的5阶段流程：假设生成、图像锚定的证据总结、证据条件化的精炼、符号化验证和最终诊断。

Result: ARISE应用于两个独立患者队列的IBD评估：用于克罗恩病的无线胶囊内镜（WCE）图像和用于溃疡性结肠炎的B超数据。与基线VLMs相比，ARISE持续提升诊断性能，同时揭示推理成功或失败之处。

Conclusion: ARISE为临床决策支持提供了更可解释的依据，并支持更现实的部署，同时暴露了推理的成功与失败环节。

Abstract: Inflammatory bowel disease (IBD) requires frequent imaging-based assessment, yet interpretation of modalities such as wireless capsule endoscopy (WCE) and intestinal ultrasound remains heavily dependent on specialist expertise. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate significant potential in multimodal medical image analysis, but their clinical adoption is hindered by their insufficient domain-specific reasoning, susceptibility to hallucination, scarcity of high quality training data in fine-grained diagnostics and limited interpretability. We introduce ARISE (Adaptive Agentic Reasoning with Image-grounded Self-Evaluation), an autonomous planning framework that models few-shot medical image understanding as a sequential agentic workflow. ARISE structures agent execution into a transparent 5-stage workflow: hypothesis generation, image-grounded evidence summarization, evidence-conditioned refinement, symbolic verification, and final diagnosis. We apply ARISE to IBD assessment across two independent patient cohorts: wireless capsule endoscopy (WCE) images for Crohn's disease and B-mode ultrasound data for ulcerative colitis. ARISE consistently improves diagnostic performance over baseline VLMs while exposing where reasoning succeeds or fails, providing a more interpretable basis for clinical decision support and realistic deployment.

</details>


### [76] [Super-Resolution in The Right Latent Space: A Frozen Vision-Foundation Substrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04781)
*Wanzhou Lei,Cuifeng Sheng,Yanjin He,Maohua Li,Hua Yuan,Per-Olof Persson,Hanlin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出在冻结DINOv3-L的融合隐空间中执行真实世界超分，训练415M解码器（RAESR）完成单步映射与像素重建，在多个基准上取得最优保真度-感知平衡，速度约37ms/512×512。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超分方法在重建导向的隐空间或像素空间中实现退化嵌入到流形的映射，但这些空间并非合适基底：低质图像嵌入离流形过远，映射困难且代价高；同时缺乏语义信息，难以导航到流形上的忠实点，重退化时产生严重幻觉。因此需要寻找更合适的隐空间。

Method: 作者主张在冻结DINOv3-L的23层融合特征隐空间中进行超分。该空间使退化图像嵌入靠近高质图像流形，并包含从像素记录到退化鲁棒语义的层级信息。在此基础上训练一个415M参数解码器，采用重建与对抗目标，将退化嵌入单步映射回并解码到像素空间，得到模型RAESR。作者还以相同流程替换VAE隐空间作为对照。

Result: RAESR在RealSR、DRealSR、LSDIR和DIV2K-Val上，在保真度-感知权衡方面优于现有基于对抗和扩散的恢复器；单张H20 GPU上处理512×512图像约37ms。将基底换成VAE隐空间后，在相同训练流程下所有指标均落后。

Conclusion: 在合适的隐空间（如DINOv3-L融合层特征）中进行超分，比在重建导向隐空间或像素空间中更有效，可同时提升保真度与感知质量并降低计算成本；基底选择对超分性能至关重要。

Abstract: In an image latent space, the embeddings of high-resolution, natural, and sharp images form a manifold. Degradation of high-resolution images pushes their embeddings off this manifold. Real-world super-resolution (SR) then becomes the task of mapping the degraded embedding back onto this manifold --- not anywhere on the manifold, but to the point that preserves what the input still carries, both its semantics and pixel details. Every published method implements this mapping in a reconstruction-oriented latent space or pixel space. We claim these spaces are the wrong substrates for SR. Low-resolution and degraded images are embedded far from the manifold, making the mapping difficult and expensive. The lack of semantic information in these substrates also makes it difficult to navigate to the faithful point on the manifold, causing severe hallucination when degradation is heavy. Thus, restoring in a suitable latent space is crucial to the SR task. We show that the latent space of 23 fused layers of a frozen DINOv3-L is one such space that makes the SR task easier. Degraded images are embedded near the manifold. Moreover, this substrate contains a hierarchy of information, from pixel record to degradation robust semantics, guiding the model to find the faithful point on the manifold. On this substrate, a 415M decoder is trained under reconstruction and adversarial objectives to map the degraded embeddings back and decode to pixel space in one pass. The resulting model, RAESR, attains the best fidelity--perception trade-off among state-of-the-art adversarial and diffusion-based restorers on RealSR, DRealSR, LSDIR and DIV2K-Val, at 37 ms per 512 by 512 image on a single H20 GPU. Swapping the substrate for a VAE latent under an identical recipe loses on every metric.

</details>


### [77] [Lollypop: Camera-to-Motion-Capture Calibration Verification with a Reference Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04785)
*Tianyi Liu,Kevin Harris,Mihika Dave,Kun He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Lollypop——一种将 ArUco 基准与动捕标记星座耦合的参考靶标，用于相机-动捕标定的独立验证，通过比较动捕点投影与基准中心检测结果来量化标定偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机-动捕标定是机器人与AR/VR等领域中将动捕作为真值的前提，但标定在部署后可能发生漂移，而标定残差与人工目视检查对独立验证的支撑有限。

Method: 设计 Lollypop 基准靶标，将 ArUco 视觉基准与动捕标记星座刚性耦合，使视觉中心与被跟踪质心代表同一物理点；给定候选标定，将动捕点投影到图像中，度量其与检测到的基准中心之间的不一致度。

Result: 实验显示标称误差达亚像素级；对受控外参扰动具有敏感性；在一段示例性混合操作序列中误差随操作过程增大。

Conclusion: 该靶标可作为一种低成本的独立标定验证手段，在无需外部真值的情况下暴露标定漂移。

Abstract: Camera-to-motion-capture (mocap) calibration is essential for using mocap as ground truth in robotics, AR/VR, and other computer vision tasks. However, the calibration can drift after deployment, while calibration residuals and visual inspection provide limited independent verification. We present Lollypop, a fiducial-mocap reference target for independent calibration verification. The target couples an ArUco fiducial with a mocap marker constellation so the visual center and tracked centroid represent the same physical point. Given a candidate calibration, verification projects the mocap point into the image and measures its disagreement with the detected fiducial center. Experiments show sub-pixel nominal error, sensitivity to controlled extrinsic perturbations, and increasing error during an illustrative mixed-handling sequence.

</details>


### [78] [Active-DiNTS: Active Differentiable Network Topology Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04787)
*Gean Trindade Pereira,Thierry Urruty,Muriel Visani,André C. P. L. F. de Carvalho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出Active-DiNTS，将基于池的主动学习嵌入双层可微拓扑搜索中，联合进行架构发现与标签筛选，在单GPU、少量标注数据条件下于3D医学分割任务上超越DiNTS、C2FNAS与nnU-Net。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经架构搜索（NAS）虽优于人工网络设计，但应用于3D医学图像分割时受限于两大成本：高昂的标注预算与多GPU集群需求。因此需要一种在现实约束下同时降低标注与算力开销的方案。

Method: 提出Active-DiNTS（主动可微网络拓扑搜索）：在双层可微拓扑搜索中嵌入基于池的主动学习，联合进行架构发现与标签筛选。每轮查询用三种不确定性信号（熵、方差、标准差）对未标注MRI体数据排序，仅将排名靠前的送交标注；新标签同时驱动两个互锁阶段——外循环更新网络权重，内循环优化U-Net式主干的宏/微观拓扑。设置三种主动学习模式（仅权重、仅拓扑、联合）以揭示速度-精度权衡。

Result: 在MSD Task01 BrainTumour基准上，Active-DiNTS在Dice上超越DiNTS、C2FNAS和nnU-Net：Edema约提升10个百分点，Non-Enhancing core提升约5个百分点；仅用单GPU和部分标注数据。发现的架构比基线更稠密、FLOP更高，但可训练参数量与峰值内存仍具竞争力；最快搜索变体在0.25 GPU-day内完成，比八GPU的DiNTS搜索快27倍以上。

Conclusion: 将可微NAS与主动数据获取相结合，是在现实约束下实现高精度3D分割的实用方案。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has proved to be a strong alternative to manual network design, but applying it to 3D medical image segmentation is limited by two well-known costs, large annotation budgets and multi-GPU clusters. Thus, this paper introduces Active-DiNTS (Active Differentiable Network Topology Search), an approach that embeds pool-based Active Learning (AL) into a bi-level differentiable topology search to perform architecture discovery and label curation jointly. At each query round, unlabeled MRI volumes are ranked by one of three uncertainty signals (Entropy, Variance, or Standard Deviation), and only the top-ranked volumes are sent to an oracle for annotation. The new labels feed two interlocked stages. Network weights are updated in an outer loop, while the macro/micro topology of a U-Net-style backbone is refined in an inner loop. Three AL regimes (weights-only, topology-only, joint) expose the speed-accuracy trade-off. Evaluations on the Medical Segmentation Decathlon (MSD) Task01 BrainTumour benchmark showed that Active-DiNTS surpasses DiNTS, C2FNAS, and nnU-Net in Dice, with gains of about 10 percentage points on Edema and 5 points on Non-Enhancing core, on a single GPU and using a fraction of the labeled volumes. The discovered architectures are denser and more FLOP-heavy than prior baselines, but remain competitive in trainable parameters and peak memory; the fastest search variant finishes in under 0.25 GPU-days, over 27x faster than the eight-GPU DiNTS search. Together, these results indicate that pairing differentiable NAS with active data acquisition is a practical recipe for accurate 3D segmentation under realistic constraints.

</details>


### [79] [MOXIE: Discovering Alternative Explanations for Biomedical Image Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04814)
*Abiha Tahsin Chowdhury,Rahul Dubey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOXIE：一种进化多目标框架，用NSGA-II搜索保留分类置信度的最小图像片段子集，返回从紧凑到高保真的Pareto前沿解释，在BloodMNIST与HAM10000上超越LIME。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于片段（如LIME）的解释方法对每个预测仅返回单一解释，且依赖固定的图像分割，掩盖了两个事实：同一预测可由多种片段集合支持；分割方式本身决定了可发现的解释。因此需要提供多备选解释并考察分割的影响。

Method: 提出MOXIE（Multi-Objective eXplanation Imaging Engine）进化框架，寻找在尽可能少保留图像内容的前提下维持分类器置信度的片段子集。采用NSGA-II，并结合四种分割方法（SLIC、Felzenszwalb、Watershed、Voronoi），在BloodMNIST和HAM10000数据集上使用与LIME相同的评估预算进行评测。

Result: MOXIE在每张图像上的超体积（hypervolume）均高于LIME。LIME的解释看似合理，但仅展示其高亮片段时分类器置信度会崩溃。MOXIE的Pareto前沿揭示了维持模型置信度所需的图像比例以及哪些上下文区域产生影响。分割方式显著影响评估：当以片段数量计数时，片段大小不均的分割方法显得最紧凑。

Conclusion: 备选解释相比单一解释能更完整地刻画模型决策，并且评估结果对分割方法高度敏感，凸显多目标、多分割解释框架的价值。

Abstract: Segment-based explanation methods such as LIME return a single explanation for each prediction, computed from one fixed image segmentation. This hides two important facts: a prediction can be supported by many different sets of image segments, and the segmentation itself shapes which explanations can be found. We introduce MOXIE (Multi-Objective eXplanation Imaging Engine), an evolutionary framework that searches for segment subsets that preserve the classifier's confidence while keeping as little of the image as possible. Instead of one explanation, MOXIE returns a Pareto front of alternative explanations that range from compact to highly faithful. We evaluate MOXIE with NSGA-II and four segmentation methods (SLIC, Felzenszwalb, Watershed and Voronoi) on BloodMNIST and HAM10000 datasets, using the same evaluation budget as LIME. Results show that MOXIE achieves a higher hypervolume than LIME on every image. LIME's explanations often appear convincing, yet the classifier's confidence collapses when only the highlighted segments are shown. MOXIE's fronts reveal how much of the image is needed to preserve the model's confidence and which contextual regions influence it. We also find that segmentation strongly affects evaluation: methods with unequal segment sizes appear most compact when segments are counted. These results show that alternative explanations provide a more complete view of a model's decision than a single explanation.

</details>


### [80] [DriftSR: One-Step Real-World Image Super-Resolution via Distribution Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04819)
*Wei Zhu,Kai Zhang,Yu Zheng,Zhaopeng Yang,Lei Luo,Yong Guo,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriftSR：一步式真实图像超分辨率框架，在预训练扩散模型的冻结中间表示空间中进行分布漂移，仅优化一步生成器，无需蒸馏分支或对抗判别器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步式Real-ISR推理高效，但要恢复真实且感知丰富的细节往往依赖分数蒸馏或对抗学习，这会引入额外可训练组件并使优化更复杂。作者希望在不增加这些组件的前提下利用预训练扩散先验。

Method: 通过分布漂移利用预训练扩散先验；在冻结的扩散模型中间表示空间中进行漂移，不引入任务专用特征编码器。提出Spatial Feature Drifting，将空间特征而非整幅图像作为分布样本，实现更密集的分布对齐监督；提出Structure-Modulated Guidance，依据与LQ输入的局部结构一致性自适应精炼漂移引导。最终仅优化一步生成器。

Result: 在三个真实世界基准上的大量实验表明，DriftSR在高效一步推理下实现了高质量的超分辨率重建。

Conclusion: DriftSR证明可通过冻结扩散表示空间中的分布漂移实现一步式Real-ISR，避免辅助蒸馏分支与对抗判别器，兼顾高质量重建与高效推理。

Abstract: One-step real-world image super-resolution (Real-ISR) offers efficient inference, but recovering realistic and perceptually rich details often relies on score distillation or adversarial learning, introducing additional trainable components and making optimization more cumbersome. To this end, we propose DriftSR, a one-step Real-ISR framework that leverages pretrained diffusion priors through distribution drifting. Specifically, we perform drifting in the frozen intermediate representation space of a pretrained diffusion model, without introducing an additional task-specific feature encoder. Building on this space, we introduce Spatial Feature Drifting, which treats spatial features rather than entire images as distributional samples, enabling denser supervision for distribution alignment. To mitigate structural deviations, we further introduce Structure-Modulated Guidance, which adaptively refines drifting guidance according to local structural consistency with the LQ input. Consequently, DriftSR optimizes only the one-step generator, without auxiliary distillation branches or adversarial discriminators. Extensive experiments on three real-world benchmarks demonstrate that DriftSR delivers high-quality super-resolution reconstruction with efficient one-step inference.

</details>


### [81] [RSure-Agent: Reliable Use of Tool Observations for Remote Sensing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04836)
*Fuyuan Liu,Nayu Liu,Wenhao Yu,Peijin Wang,Yingchao Feng,Fanglong Yao,Liang Wan,Wei Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析遥感智能体工具观测的不确定性，提出RSure-Agent框架验证观测并抑制误差传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感智能体依赖感知、测量与栅格分析工具，其关于地物的判断与定量结果存在显著不确定性，即使工具成功执行也可能错误，并被接受后传播导致任务失败。作者通过分析1,229条执行轨迹，量化这一问题的普遍性与严重程度。

Method: 提出RSure-Agent框架，包含可验证观测协议，要求工具返回过程证据供智能体验证；并从离线任务反馈构建任务-工具可靠性先验，总结各工具配置在不同任务类型上的历史表现。结合过程证据与先验，智能体决定接受、要求补充证据或拒绝该观测。

Result: 在三类遥感智能体基准上，至少88.1%的任务依赖工具观测，其中至少22.7%含错误观测，且至少82.0%受影响的最终答案被错误传播。RSure-Agent将误差传播率相对基线降低21.3至25.9个百分点；在CHOICE-420上，11种骨干模型平均准确率提升5.71个百分点；在EarthBench上，工具调用率相对Earth-Agent降低25.9%。

Conclusion: 通过可验证证据与可靠性先验的联合验证机制，RSure-Agent有效缓解遥感智能体工具观测错误传播，提升任务准确率并降低工具调用开销。

Abstract: Remote sensing agents rely on perception, measurement, and raster analysis tools to solve Earth observation tasks. We refer to their judgments and quantitative results about ground objects as tool observations. However, these observations are subject to substantial uncertainty and may be incorrect even when the tools execute successfully. When agents accept incorrect observations, the errors can propagate through subsequent reasoning and cause task failure. We analyze 1,229 execution trajectories across three remote sensing agent benchmarks. On each benchmark, at least 88.1% of tasks depend on tool observations. Among these tasks, at least 22.7% contain incorrect observations despite successful tool execution. These errors propagate to the final answer in at least 82.0% of affected tasks on each benchmark. To address this problem, we propose RSure-Agent, a framework for verifying tool observations and limiting error propagation. We introduce a verifiable observation protocol that requires tools to return process evidence for the agent to verify their observations. We also construct a task-tool reliability prior from offline task feedback. The prior summarizes each tool configuration's past performance across task types and provides a task-specific reference for verification. Using process evidence and this prior, RSure-Agent decides whether to accept an observation, request additional evidence, or reject it. We evaluate RSure-Agent on EarthBench, ThinkGeo, TerraLogic, and CHOICE-420. Across the three agent benchmarks, RSure-Agent reduces the error propagation rate by 21.3 to 25.9 percentage points relative to the base configuration with verification and the prior disabled. On CHOICE-420, it improves overall accuracy over direct answering by 5.71 percentage points on average across 11 backbone models. On EarthBench, it reduces the tool-call ratio by 25.9% relative to Earth-Agent.

</details>


### [82] [One Tile, Multiple Instances: Rethinking MIL for Sparse Diagnostic Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04853)
*Runsheng Liu,Cheng Jin,Hao Jiang,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DI-MIL通过在每个瓦片内聚类冻结基础模型的密集空间token，将单个瓦片分解为多个独立加权实例，从而解耦编码上下文与实例粒度；作为免训练的后编码模块，它在细胞病理学WSI分类中显著提升多种聚合器性能且无额外图像提取开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督WSI分类中，特征提取器通常将每个图像瓦片压缩为单一全局嵌入，导致滑窗级聚合器被限制在粗瓦片尺度上，细粒度的亚瓦片证据被注意力机制掩盖。在细胞病理学等诊断信号稀疏的场景下，这种粗粒度聚合容易稀释微小但关键的证据，因此需要一种能在不改变主干和聚合架构的前提下提升实例粒度的方法。

Method: 提出DI-MIL框架，通过分解实例将编码上下文与实例粒度解耦：对每个瓦片，利用冻结基础模型提取密集空间token并在瓦片内聚类，将单个瓦片转换为多个可独立加权的实例嵌入。DI-MIL作为免训练的后编码模块，可直接接入现有流水线，无需重新编码或修改下游架构。

Result: 在细胞病理学场景下，跨4个数据集、3个冻结基础模型和2种基于注意力的聚合器进行评估，DI-MIL在72项指标级比较中提升了67项，在细胞病理学专用主干下最大平均增益达3.64个百分点。与7个代表性MIL基线对比，DI-MIL搭配ACMIL在36项主干-数据集-指标比较中33项取得最高平均性能。消融实验表明，直接采用更小瓦片会使提取瓦片数最多膨胀43.3倍且性能非单调，而DI-MIL在零额外图像提取开销下取得最强总体结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明实例构造是MIL中一个正交的设计维度，DI-MIL为稀疏诊断证据场景提供了一种高性价比的解决方案。

Abstract: In weakly supervised Whole Slide Image (WSI) classification, feature extractors typically compress each image tile into a single global embedding. Consequently, slide-level aggregators are restricted to this coarse tile scale, concealing fine-grained sub-tile evidence from the attention mechanism. We introduce DI-MIL, a framework that decouples encoding context from instance granularity through decomposed instances. By clustering dense spatial tokens from a frozen foundation model within each tile, DI-MIL converts a single tile into multiple independently weighted instance embeddings. As a training-free post-encoding module, DI-MIL integrates seamlessly into existing pipelines without requiring re-encoding or downstream architectural modifications. We evaluate DI-MIL on cytopathology, a challenging testbed where sparse diagnostic signals are easily diluted within standard tiles. Across four datasets, three frozen foundation models, and two attention-based aggregators, DI-MIL demonstrates consistent efficacy, improving 67 of 72 metric-level comparisons, with the largest mean gains reaching 3.64 points under cytopathology-specific backbones. In a broader comparison against seven representative MIL baselines, DI-MIL paired with ACMIL achieves highest mean performance in 33 of 36 backbone-dataset-metric comparisons. Ablations show that direct smaller tiling inflates the extracted tile count by up to 43.3$\times$ with non-monotonic performance, whereas DI-MIL incurs zero additional image-extraction overhead while achieving the strongest overall results. These results establish instance construction as an orthogonal design dimension in MIL, supporting DI-MIL as a cost-efficient solution under sparse diagnostic evidence.

</details>


### [83] [Enhancing Long-Video VLM Embeddings with Query-Aware Streaming Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04864)
*Haozhe Chi,Song Jin,Yang Jin,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QASLR后训练框架，通过流式潜在推理在固定嵌入维度下跨片段累积长视频证据，HourVideo检索Hit@1由54.2/57.4提升至70.7/72.8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频嵌入需在有限视觉token预算下捕获稀疏的查询相关证据。均匀采样易遗漏数分钟至数小时视频中的短暂事件，而在单上下文中编码更多帧又会显著增加显存与计算开销。

Method: QASLR选取有界帧集并恢复其时序，逐片段经视觉-语言骨干处理；一组紧凑的持久think token对每个片段特征做交叉注意力，embed token在每次更新后读出归一化表示，从而在多骨干调用间整合证据而无需共享单一上下文。训练结合最终对比学习、逐步对比监督与最终嵌入自蒸馏：中间监督训练部分视频读出用于检索，自蒸馏将其正则化向最终表示对齐。查询相关选择生成查询条件表示用于候选集打分与重排，查询无关选择支持可复用语料索引。

Result: 在完整训练配方下，2B与8B Qwen3-VL-Embedding骨干的HourVideo检索Hit@1分别从54.2提升至70.7、从57.4提升至72.8；增益延伸至所评估的时刻检索与视频问答任务，且流式头可迁移至第二个Qwen系列嵌入骨干。

Conclusion: 结果支持将流式潜在聚合作为把长视频证据整合进固定维度表示的有效途径。

Abstract: Long-video embedding requires capturing sparse query-relevant evidence under a limited visual-token budget. Uniform sampling can miss brief events in videos spanning minutes or hours, whereas encoding more frames in a single context increases memory and computation. We introduce \textbf{Query-Aware Streaming Latent Reasoning} (QASLR), a post-training framework that accumulates evidence across clips while keeping the embedding size fixed. QASLR selects a bounded set of frames, restores their temporal order, and processes them clip by clip with a vision-language backbone. A compact set of persistent think tokens cross-attends to each clip's features, while an embed token reads out a normalized representation after every update. This design integrates evidence across multiple backbone calls without requiring all selected frames to share a single context. Training combines final contrastive learning, step-wise contrastive supervision, and final-embedding self-distillation. Intermediate supervision trains partial-video readouts for retrieval, while self-distillation regularizes them toward the final representation. Query-aware selection produces query-conditioned representations for candidate-set scoring and reranking, whereas query-independent selection enables reusable corpus indexing. Under the full training recipe, HourVideo retrieval Hit@1 increases from 54.2 to 70.7 and from 57.4 to 72.8 for 2B and 8B Qwen3-VL-Embedding backbones, respectively. Gains extend to the evaluated moment-retrieval and video-QA tasks, and the streaming head transfers to a second Qwen-family embedding backbone. These results support streaming latent aggregation as an effective approach to integrating long-video evidence into fixed-dimensional representations.

</details>


### [84] [Reflection-Robust 6DoF Object Tracking with Light Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04883)
*Nikolai Goncharov,Donald G. Dansereau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于光场的反射鲁棒6DoF跟踪器，将镜面反射的视角依赖外观作为位姿线索，在完全反射物体上保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6DoF跟踪器假设物体外观在序列中稳定，但反射表面会镜像环境导致外观随视角变化，该假设失效。

Method: 逐帧在抗反射条件下恢复深度，反投影为点云并估计表面法线；将视角依赖外观分解为漫反射反照率与所反射的环境贴图，得到可重光照的表面光场；由粗初始化出发用恢复的环境贴图重光照，并在光度损失上优化位姿。随物体运动镜像新场景区域，环境贴图逐渐补全，信号随时间增强。

Result: 构建了由机器人操作基准重渲染的光场跟踪数据集，含四个受控反射率等级，并配有模拟消费级RGB-D传感器在光泽表面失效的深度；另在两段实拍光场序列上评测。方法在漫反射物体上略逊于最强基线，但在完全反射物体上是唯一保持精度的，所有基线均退化。

Conclusion: 将反射这一干扰转化为位姿线索，通过可重光照表面光场与光度优化实现反射鲁棒跟踪，在完全反射条件下优于所有基线。

Abstract: Tracking the 6DoF pose of a moving rigid object is fundamental to robotics and autonomous driving, but existing trackers assume that object appearance is stable across a sequence, an assumption that breaks down on reflective surfaces whose appearance changes as they mirror the environment. We introduce a light field based reflection-robust 6DoF tracker that turns this apparent nuisance into a pose cue. Per frame, our method recovers depth robustly against reflections, back-projects it into a point cloud, and estimates surface normals. It then decomposes the object's view-dependent appearance into a diffuse albedo and the environment map it reflects, resulting in a relightable surface light field. Starting from a coarse initialization, we relight it by the recovered environment map and optimize the pose on the photometric loss. Because a moving object mirrors new parts of the scene, the environment map fills in as the sequence proceeds, sharpening this signal over time. To evaluate this approach, we introduce a light field tracking dataset re-rendered from a robotic manipulation benchmark at four controlled reflectivity levels, each paired with simulated depth that reproduces how consumer RGB-D sensors fail on shiny surfaces. Additionally, we evaluate on two captured light field sequences. Our method trails the strongest baselines on diffuse objects and is the only one that holds its accuracy on fully reflective objects, where every baseline degrades.

</details>


### [85] [PWM: Personalized World Models with Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04920)
*Zhexin Lou,Guancheng Lu,Zeyu Zhang,Yi Zhang,Yang Zhao,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PWM框架，通过在线强化学习将预训练世界模型定制到指定场景视频，并构建PWM-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练世界模型虽能生成多样环境，但用户往往希望探索由自己视频指定的特定场景，这要求模型学习该场景的视觉身份，同时保持动作条件生成的质量。

Method: PWM利用支持轨迹及其控制信号对当前策略采样的续帧提供奖励反馈。在GRPO实例中，采用组相对优化更新紧凑的LoRA适配器，使用涵盖场景外观、视觉连续性和运动的统一奖励，并以基础策略锚定正则化冻结的Yume-5B骨干的预训练生成先验。相同流程同时应用于真实与渲染环境，另用DiffusionNFT实现替代的奖励引导优化。

Result: PWM-Bench包含150个覆盖室内、室外和游戏场景的定制任务，并在留出续帧上配对评估。GRPO与DiffusionNFT实例分别在71.3%和65.3%的评估场景中优于原生Yume，三个领域均有正向平均增益。GRPO在匹配SFT对比中于每个领域获得更高平均定制增益和更好的平均图像质量分数，同时帧级视觉质量接近预训练模型。

Conclusion: PWM通过在线强化学习与LoRA适配，实现了对预训练世界模型的场景级个性化定制，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升定制效果。

Abstract: Pretrained world models can generate diverse environments, yet users often want to explore a particular scene specified by their own video. This requires learning the scene's visual identity while retaining the quality of action-conditioned generation. We introduce Personalized World Models (PWM), a framework for customizing interactive world models from short scene videos through online reinforcement learning. In PWM, the support trajectory and its associated controls provide reward feedback on continuations sampled from the current policy. In the GRPO instantiation, group-relative optimization updates a compact LoRA adapter using a unified reward for scene appearance, visual continuity, and motion, while base-policy anchoring regularizes changes to the pretrained generation prior of a frozen Yume-5B backbone. The same adaptation procedure is applied across real and rendered environments. We also instantiate PWM with DiffusionNFT as an alternative reward-guided optimization method for learning the scene-specific adapter. We also introduce PWM-Bench, comprising 150 customization tasks across Indoor, Outdoor, and Gaming, with paired evaluation on held-out continuations. The GRPO and DiffusionNFT instantiations of PWM improve customization over native Yume in 71.3% and 65.3% of the evaluated scenes, respectively, with positive mean gains across all three domains. For the GRPO instantiation, matched SFT comparisons further demonstrate higher mean customization gains and better mean image-quality scores in every domain, while retaining frame-level visual quality close to the pretrained model.

</details>


### [86] [TRACE: Time-Adaptive Residual Attention Control with Content-Style Decomposition for Training-Free Diffusion Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04922)
*Duc Khoan Le,Kim Ngoc Tran,Minh Nhat Le,Thanh An Tran,Viet Toan Nguyen,Khanh An Lay,Tran Thai Son,Hoang Pham Minh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练扩散风格迁移框架TRACE，通过时间自适应残差注意力控制与内容-风格分解，缓解风格保真度、内容保持与内容泄漏之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练扩散风格迁移方法难以兼顾风格保真度、内容保持与内容泄漏：直接注入风格会无意迁移风格图中的语义内容，而固定引导调度忽略了扩散采样过程随时间与状态变化的特性。

Method: TRACE首先离线进行基于CLIP的子空间分析，从成对数据中分离内容方向与风格方向；推理时从风格参考中移除内容相关成分、从内容参考中移除风格相关成分以降低泄漏；随后通过残差交叉注意力注入风格信息，并采用不确定性感知引导在每个去噪步自适应调整引导信号。

Result: 在风格化与保持之间取得良好权衡：相比最优控制基线，风格保真度显著提升（CSD +17.28，SRA +34.10）；相比风格化方法，更好地保持内容结构（DINO +12.80，CLIP-I +5.52，LPIPS -8.19），并在DCL上将方向性语义泄漏降低29.5%。

Conclusion: TRACE作为一种免训练框架，通过内容-风格分解与时间自适应残差注意力引导，有效缓解了风格迁移中的内容泄漏与保真度-保持权衡问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reference-guided style transfer aims to preserve the semantic structure of a content image while transferring the visual appearance of a style reference. Recent diffusion-based methods achieve impressive stylization quality by exploiting strong pretrained generative priors. However, training-free approaches still face a difficult trade-off among style fidelity, content preservation, and content leakage. Direct style injection may unintentionally transfer semantic content from the style image, while fixed guidance schedules often ignore the time- and state-dependent nature of diffusion sampling. To address these limitations, we propose TRACE, a training-free diffusion style transfer framework with Time-adaptive Residual Attention Control and Content-Style Decomposition. TRACE first performs offline CLIP-based subspace analysis to separate content and style directions from paired data. During inference, it removes content-related components from the style reference and style-related components from the content reference to reduce leakage. It then injects style information through residual cross-attention and applies uncertainty-aware guidance to adapt the guidance signal at each denoising step. Experiments show that TRACE achieves a favorable trade-off between stylization and preservation. Compared with optimal-control-based baselines, TRACE substantially improves style fidelity (+17.28 CSD and +34.10 SRA). While, compared with stylization methods, it better preserves content structure (+12.80 DINO, +5.52 CLIP-I, and -8.19 LPIPS) and reduces directional semantic leakage by 29.5% in DCL. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/pixelchemy-research/TRACE.

</details>


### [87] [Robust Tensor Completion via Reflective Convolution Nuclear Norm Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04979)
*Weiguo Zhou,Feng Zhang,Wenjin Qin,Jianwen Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出反射卷积核范数最小化（RCNNM），用端点不重复反射替代循环平移构造卷积低秩模型，实现含稀疏误差张量的精确恢复与高效优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒张量补全需从部分观测且含稀疏大误差的数据中恢复多维信号。现有卷积低秩模型通过循环延拓构造平移副本，对有限非周期数据会引入人工的环绕邻域，边界处建模失真。

Method: 以端点不重复反射替代循环移位，构建反射卷积核范数最小化；所得提升具有非均匀元素重数并满足加权Gram恒等式，支撑恢复分析与优化；采用两块ADMM，含闭式逐元素张量更新，奇异值阈值通过较小的右Gram矩阵实现。

Result: 在随机采样与稀疏污染下，证明高概率精确恢复原张量与稀疏误差，并具界稠密扰动下的稳定性；在合成张量、BSDS彩色图像和CAVE多光谱图像上，RCNNM稳定优于循环提升对应方法，边界PSNR平均提升达3.26 dB，全局重建质量保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 反射卷积低秩建模有效消除循环延拓的环绕伪影，理论保证与实验结果一致，尤其改善图像边界重建，为鲁棒张量补全提供了更优的卷积低秩范式。

Abstract: Robust tensor completion recovers multidimensional data from partial observations corrupted by sparse gross errors. Existing convolutional low-rank models typically construct translated copies using circular continuation, which introduces artificial wrap-around neighborhoods for finite nonperiodic data. We propose reflective convolution nuclear norm minimization (RCNNM), which replaces circular shifts with endpoint-nonrepeating reflection. The resulting lifting has nonuniform entry multiplicities and satisfies a weighted Gram identity that supports both the recovery analysis and the optimization method. Under random sampling and sparse corruption, we establish high-probability exact recovery of the underlying tensor and sparse errors, together with stability under bounded dense perturbations. We further develop a two-block ADMM with a closed-form entrywise tensor update, while singular-value thresholding is implemented through the smaller right Gram matrix. Experiments on synthetic tensors, BSDS color images, and CAVE multispectral images show that RCNNM consistently improves over its circular-lifting counterpart, with the clearest gains near image boundaries. In particular, average boundary-PSNR improvements reach 3.26 dB while global reconstruction quality remains competitive.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [Fine-Grained Emotion Classification from Mobile App Reviews: An Empirical Study with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03802)
*Quim Motger,Carlota Catot,Marc Oriol*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何用大语言模型对移动应用评论进行细粒度多标签情感分类，并比较编码器微调、解码器提示与不平衡缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度情感分类可支撑情感感知的问题优先级排序和面向特征的反馈分析，但应用评论的自动细粒度情感抽取研究不足，且面临严重类别不平衡。

Method: 基于既有标注框架与Plutchik分类体系的人工标注真值，比较编码器在多标签与二分类集成下的微调、解码器零样本/少样本提示（开源与闭源模型），并测试损失重加权、重采样、生成式数据增强等不平衡缓解策略；合成评论生成器与提示策略通过内在增强效用排序选择。

Result: 基线中微调编码器远逊于最佳解码器少样本提示（macro-F1 0.642；多标签0.387，二分类集成0.450）；将最佳多标签编码器与生成式数据增强及正类加权损失结合后缩小差距（+0.204），推理延迟最多低三个数量级，且最稀有情感提升最大（从未检出到最高+0.501 F1）。

Conclusion: 大语言模型使应用评论的细粒度多标签情感分类在需求工程流水线中可行，但macro-F1仅为中等水平，最佳方案与缓解策略依赖骨干模型和建模形式；作者公开实验流水线、合成语料与微调检查点。

Abstract: Context: Fine-grained emotion classification of mobile app reviews enables requirements engineering activities that go beyond polarity-based opinion mining, including emotionally informed issue prioritisation and feature-oriented feedback analysis. However, automatic fine-grained emotion extraction from app reviews remains understudied. Objectives: Building on a previously published annotation framework and human-labelled ground truth adapted from Plutchik's taxonomy, this paper investigates how large language models can be leveraged for automatic multi-label emotion classification under severe class imbalance. Methods: We compare encoder-only fine-tuning under multi-label and binary-ensemble formulations, decoder-only zero- and few-shot prompting across open-source and proprietary models, and a catalogue of imbalance mitigation strategies (loss reweighting, resampling, generative data augmentation), with the synthetic-review generator and prompting strategy selected via an intrinsic augmentation-utility ranking. Results: Fine-tuned encoders trail the best decoder-only few-shot prompting (macro-F1 0.642) by a wide margin at baseline (multi-label: 0.387; binary ensemble: 0.450); pairing the best multi-label encoder with generative data augmentation and positive-weighted loss closes most of this gap (+0.204) at up to three orders of magnitude lower inference latency than the decoders, with the largest gains on the rarest emotions, from undetected to gains of up to +0.501 F1. Conclusion: Large language models make fine-grained, multi-label emotion classification of app reviews feasible for requirements engineering pipelines, with modest macro-F1, and the best formulation and mitigation strategy are backbone- and formulation-dependent. We release the experimental pipeline, synthetic corpora, and fine-tuned checkpoints for replication and reuse.

</details>


### [89] [Same Output, Different Gold: Measuring How Reference Choice Moves a Multilingual Benchmark Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03825)
*Parth Kulshreshtha,Shivali Dalmia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 基准分数的研究几乎只关注被评系统输出，而忽略参照标准本身。本文利用六语言PII基准中留存的标注记录（两套独立标注、发布版聚合金标、专家复审金标），在固定系统输出、更换参照标准的条件下测量分数变化，发现更换参照可使F1变动4.95与2.00分，最差语言达7.55分，且会改变输出间的比较结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基准评测的方法学讨论几乎全部集中在被评系统输出这一侧，默认参照金标是稳定、无歧义的。但金标由标注与聚合流程产生，存在未公开的聚合默认规则与标注者间差异，可能导致分数本身不可复现、系统间比较不稳定。作者因此主张把参照标准也作为变量来测量。

Method: 采用固定系统输出、交换参照标准的对照设计。数据为六语言PII标注基准留存的完整标注记录，包含两位独立标注者的标注、作为金标发布的聚合结果，以及由独立专家对样本重新标注得到的复审金标。在此基础上计算同一输出在不同参照下的F1差异、参照与系统间的配对交互效应，并做多重检验校正，最后提出可推广的参照敏感度区间计算方法。

Result: 更换参照标准后，一个输出的F1移动4.95分（95% CI [3.23, 6.27]），另一输出移动2.00分（95% CI [0.26, 3.37]），最差语言达7.55分。输出间的比较同样受影响：参照与系统的配对交互为+2.95分（CI [+1.97, +3.87]），经多重检验校正后依然显著，并直接改变了某一语言中的优劣差距。根源是一处未公开的聚合默认设置：当裁决未触发时通常只保留一位标注者，使发布金标成为被评输出的部分复制。

Conclusion: 参照标准是基准分数中未被测量的重要不确定性来源，其影响足以改变绝对分数与系统间排序。作者提出"参照敏感度区间"这一指标，给出从任意留存标注记录计算该区间的方法，并主张该指标应与分数并列报告，作为基准评测的常规组成部分。

Abstract: A benchmark score compares a system output against a reference, and methodological attention falls almost entirely on the first term. We measure the second. The retained annotation record of a six-language benchmark for personally identifiable information contains two independent annotator labellings, the aggregate shipped as gold, and a reviewer gold from independent expert re-annotation of a sample. Using it, we hold the scored output fixed and exchange the reference. The score moves by 4.95 F1 points for one output and 2.00 for the other (95% CIs [3.23, 6.27] and [0.26, 3.37]), and by 7.55 in the worst language. The comparison between two outputs moves as well: the paired interaction between reference and system is +2.95 points (CI [+1.97, +3.87]), survives correction for multiple testing, and changes one language's margin outright. The cause is an undocumented aggregation default that usually kept one annotator when adjudication did not fire, making the shipped reference a partial copy of an output being scored. We report the resulting reference-sensitivity band, show how to compute one from any retained annotation record, and argue that the quantity belongs beside the score.

</details>


### [90] [The Score Is Not the Structure: Brain Alignment and Cross-Lingual Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03827)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文质疑“相似度分数”作为模型与大脑或跨语言共享结构证据的有效性：在结构缺失或测量工具失效时，分数仍会显现，导致结论被高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者常以相似度分数来支持“模型与大脑共享结构”或“跨语言共享结构”的主张，但作者追问：当共享结构根本不存在、或测量工具本身失效时，这个分数究竟说明了什么。

Method: 检验两个场景：（1）跨语言语法可接受性探针的迁移实验，分析探针自身在17种语言上的表现及272个语言对的统计单位问题；（2）训练语言模型匹配人脑响应，并设置“被破坏的目标”作为对照，以分离出真正与大脑相关的增益，同时考察沿语言自身方向操控的效果。

Result: 跨语言探针在17种语言中有4种表现处于随机水平，导致272个语言对中有64个用失效工具评分；剔除这些语言后关系强度减半，但它们恰是最远距离的语言，设计无法分离两种效应。统计单位影响显著：272对独立时p=0.0006，以17种语言为单位时p=0.155。脑相似度从0.10升至0.34（上限0.54），但用被破坏目标训练的模型仍达0.31，真正特异于大脑的增益仅0.028–0.068；无模型时被破坏目标与真实目标的距离已达0.204，构成下限。沿语言自身方向操控有效（17种语言中16种比随机方向高6.2），但不随语言距离变化。

Conclusion: 在询问某种对应关系是否有帮助之前，应先评估：若该对应关系不存在，分数还能剩下多少。报告的相似度分数很大程度可能来自测量工具失效、统计单位误用及非特异性基线，而非真实的共享结构。

Abstract: Researchers often support the claim that a model shares structure with the brain, or across languages, by reporting a similarity score. We ask what such a score reads when the shared structure is absent, or when the tool that measures it does not work. We check two settings, and in both the score is not what it appears. First, a probe trained to tell grammatical from ungrammatical sentences in one language transfers worse to more distant languages, the usual evidence for shared structure. But the probe itself gets worse along the same axis: in four of seventeen languages it performs at chance, so 64 of 272 language pairs are scored with a tool that does not work. Dropping those languages halves the strength of the relationship, but they are also the most distant, and this design cannot separate the two effects. Counting matters too: the same data give p = 0.0006 when the 272 pairs are treated as independent and p = 0.155 when the seventeen languages are, which is the correct unit. Second, training a language model to match human brain responses raises its similarity score from 0.10 to 0.34, against a ceiling of 0.54. A model trained on a target whose correspondence to the brain was destroyed still scores 0.31, so only 0.028 to 0.068 of the rise is specific to the brain. With no model at all, a destroyed target already sits at 0.204 from the real one, a floor that tracks the target's rank divided by the number of sentences. Finally, steering a language along its own direction works (+6.2 over a random direction in sixteen of seventeen languages) yet shows no effect that varies with language distance. Before asking whether a correspondence helps, ask how much of the score would survive without it.

</details>


### [91] [OncoNoteBERT: A Foundation Representation Model for Natural Language Processing of Real-World Outpatient Oncology Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03829)
*Wuraola Oyewusi,Eliana Vasquez Osorio,Goran Nenadic,Gareth Price*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究基于英国门诊肿瘤病历构建肿瘤专科BERT编码器，发现继续预训练（OncoNote-RadBERT）语料拟合最优，而定制分词从头训练（OncoNoteBERT）分词效率与掩码词预测更佳，二者优势互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界门诊肿瘤病历包含专科术语、肿瘤分期表达、治疗方案、毒性描述及机构特有去标识标记，通用生物医学或邻近临床语言模型难以高效表示，需评估肿瘤专科编码器的构建策略。

Method: 基于英国门诊肿瘤语料（21,564例肺癌与头颈癌患者的290,026份病历），比较外部编码器RadBERT、PathologyBERT与两种本地策略：继续掩码语言模型预训练得到的OncoNote-RadBERT，以及使用肿瘤WordPiece分词器从头训练的OncoNoteBERT。评估手段包括验证集掩码语言建模损失与困惑度、分词碎片化指标、临床术语分词、掩码词探针及探索性表征分析。

Result: 零样本评估中外部编码器拟合较差（RadBERT困惑度113.04，PathologyBERT 2035.03），继续预训练拟合最优（OncoNote-RadBERT 2.10）；OncoNoteBERT困惑度2.83，但分词效率最高，子词生育率更低、归一化序列长度更短；掩码词探针中OncoNoteBERT在13项中12项给出临床可接受预测，OncoNote-RadBERT为7项。语料级拟合与掩码词表现的差异部分源于分词碎片化而非学习到的语义。两种本地模型均将机构占位符表示为单一可学习token。

Conclusion: 继续适配与定制分词提供互补收益，在将邻近领域编码器应用于肿瘤NLP之前，表征层设计至关重要。

Abstract: Real-world outpatient oncology notes contain specialised terminology, tumour staging expressions, treatment names, toxicity descriptions, and institution-specific de-identification markers that may not be represented efficiently by general biomedical or adjacent clinical language models. We developed and evaluated oncology-specific BERT-style encoders using a governed UK outpatient oncology corpus comprising 290,026 notes from 21,564 patients treated for lung and head-and-neck cancer. We compared RadBERT and PathologyBERT with two local strategies: OncoNote-RadBERT, produced by continued masked language model pretraining, and OncoNoteBERT, trained from scratch with an oncology WordPiece tokenizer. Models were evaluated using masked language modelling loss and perplexity on the validation set, tokenizer fragmentation metrics, clinical term tokenisation, masked-token probes, and exploratory representation analysis. Both external encoders fit the oncology corpus poorly in zero-shot evaluation (perplexity 113.04 for RadBERT; 2035.03 for PathologyBERT), while continued pretraining produced the strongest fit (2.10 for OncoNote-RadBERT). OncoNoteBERT achieved perplexity 2.83 but produced the most efficient tokenisation, with lower subword fertility and shorter normalised sequence length. It also returned a clinically acceptable prediction for 12 of 13 masked-token probes, compared with 7 of 13 for OncoNote-RadBERT. This divergence between corpus-level fit and masked-token performance was partly attributable to tokenizer fragmentation rather than learned semantics alone. Both locally developed models represented the institutional placeholder as a single learnable token. These findings show that continued adaptation and bespoke tokenisation provide complementary benefits, and that representation-layer design matters before adjacent-domain encoders are applied to oncology NLP.

</details>


### [92] [SYNLAT: Syntax-Aligned Text-Latent Compression for Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03839)
*Yifeng Zhao,Hongjun Yu,Shibo Wang,Yunjiao Zhou,Zixiao Zhu,Zhipeng Ning,Kezhi Mao,Junlang Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 SynLat，用句法对齐单元（SAU）对齐压缩边界，通过答案条件教师与压缩条件学生实现文本-隐空间混合 CoT 压缩，在更强压缩下优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维链（CoT）轨迹带来显著输出 token 成本，预算受限时压缩必须保留答案关键信息，因此压缩边界位置至关重要。token 级与固定长度边界会切碎短语、公式、局部推导等连贯片段，而步级边界又会把需要不同压缩动作的内容捆在一起。

Method: SynLat 是一个文本-隐空间（text-latent）CoT 框架，将压缩边界与非重叠的句法对齐单元（SAU）对齐。答案条件教师（Teacher）为单一压缩条件学生（Student）构造渐进式 KEEP/LATENT 目标；推理时学生仅凭问题和指定压缩等级即可生成混合推理内容。

Result: 在两个 Qwen3 学生规模、Standard-CoT 与 Long-CoT 两组、三种压缩等级下，SynLat 在全部 12 个任务-组聚合中达到或超过最强基线，按所报告 achieved-CR 选择协议在 11 组中严格领先。Qwen3-8B/14B 上 MEDIUM 提升 3.6/2.6 分，HIGH 提升 7.0/5.5 分，压缩越强优势越大，尤其在 Long-CoT 组。

Conclusion: 以句法结构对齐压缩边界可缓解 token/固定长度与步级边界的缺陷，在受限预算下更有效保留答案关键信息，并在强压缩与长 CoT 场景中取得更显著收益。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought (CoT) traces impose substantial output-token costs. Under constrained budgets, compression must preserve answer-critical information, making boundary placement central. Token-level and fixed-length boundaries can fragment coherent spans such as phrases, formulas, and local derivations, whereas step-level boundaries can bind content requiring different compression actions. We introduce SynLat, a text-latent CoT framework that aligns compression boundaries with syntactic structure through non-overlapping Syntax-Aligned Units (SAUs). An answer-conditioned Teacher constructs progressive KEEP/LATENT targets for a single compression-conditioned Student, which generates mixed reasoning from only the question and requested compression level at inference. Across two Qwen3 Student scales, Standard-CoT and Long-CoT groups, and three compression levels, SynLat matches or exceeds the strongest evaluated baseline in all 12 task-group aggregates and strictly leads in 11 under the reported achieved-CR selection protocol. Overall gains reach 3.6/2.6 points at MEDIUM and 7.0/5.5 points at HIGH for Qwen3-8B/14B, with larger advantages under stronger compression, particularly on Long-CoT groups.

</details>


### [93] [When Evidence Changes: Evaluating Memory Repair and Re-reading in Language-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03902)
*Wenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"证据修订"评测，在公开ICU病历的用药与问题列表任务上，比较重读与记忆（缓存、重建、图局部修复）在撤销/替换/对照事件下的表现。短记录下局部修复修订token少5-10倍，但所有记忆流水线总成本至少为全量重读的两倍；长记录下记忆平均累计成本在2-14次使用后低于全量重读，但源过滤重读始终最便宜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当支持智能体推导事实的文档被撤销或替换时，是应修复记忆还是重新读取当前证据？需明确记忆在证据修订后的真实成本，并与源过滤重读进行全流水线对比。

Method: 在公开ICU病历的用药与问题列表任务上构建证据修订评测。在撤销、替换与对照事件下，对两个7B模型比较：全量重读、源过滤重读，与带缓存、重建、图局部修复的记忆方案。记忆不检索、以完整形式提供，成本计入摄取、修订与每次使用。

Result: 短记录下，局部修复的修订token比重建少5-10倍，但在留出条件下所有记忆流水线成本至少为全量重读的两倍。在小规模预设定开发扫描中，加入任务无关文档使记录扩展到约10,000 token；该长度下记忆的平均累计成本在2-14次使用后低于全量重读，部分归因于抽取被截断，而源过滤重读仍最便宜。替换研究中四项主要验证性检验均未达统计显著。

Conclusion: 证据修订后智能体记忆的成本，必须在完整流水线上与源过滤重读进行对照评估，单看修订环节的token节省会产生误导。

Abstract: When documents supporting an agent's derived facts are revoked or replaced, should it repair memory or re-read current evidence? We introduce an evidence-revision evaluation on medication- and problem-list tasks from public ICU records. Under revocation, replacement and control events, we compare full and source-filtered re-reading with caching, rebuilding and graph-local repair across two 7B models. Memory is supplied in full without retrieval, and costs include ingest, revision and every use. On short records, local repair uses 5-10$\times$ fewer revision tokens than rebuilding, yet every memory pipeline costs at least twice full re-reading in held-out conditions. In a small pre-specified development sweep, adding task-ineligible documents extended records to about 10,000 tokens; at that length, memory's mean cumulative cost fell below full re-reading's after 2-14 uses, partly through truncated extraction, while source-filtered re-reading remained cheapest. In the replacement study, none of the four primary confirmatory tests reached statistical significance. These results show why the cost of agent memory after evidence revision must be assessed against source-filtered re-reading over the full pipeline.

</details>


### [94] [General Decision Models: Benchmarking and Insights Beyond Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03935)
*Feiyu Duan,Jiayu Lin,Jia Wang,Jun Xiang,Jialiang Wu,Xinnong Zhang,Hanqi Yan,Siyuan Wang,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出双语基准JEVal（11257个实例、36个数据集、10个应用领域），系统评估25个通用决策模型与生成式LLM，揭示通用决策模型在证据可推断场景具竞争力但在专家知识与不确定性估计上薄弱，并在多步交互与社会模拟中暴露系统性可靠性问题；同时提出InnerJev-4B/27B，通过推理到读出自我蒸馏实现单步首token决策，其中27B性能与Jev相当且约0.1秒响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用决策模型（如Jev）近期被视为LLM在结构化判断与选择任务上的高效替代方案，但其可靠决策的边界以及在决策组合成更大系统后的行为变化尚不清楚，缺乏系统性的评估基准与实证分析。

Method: 构建双语基准JEVal，涵盖10个应用领域、36个数据集、11257个实例；评估25个模型配置，覆盖通用决策模型与生成式LLM；在静态判断、长时程多步交互（τ-bench）和大规模社会模拟三类场景下进行对比；并提出InnerJev-4B与InnerJev-27B，采用Reasoning-to-Readout Self-Distillation将开放权重LLM的推理内化为单次前向传播的首token决策。

Result: (1) 通用决策模型在证据可推断的决策上最具竞争力，但在需要专家知识或忠实不确定性估计时变弱，常能识别最可能结果却大幅高估其概率；(2) 在长时程多步交互系统中，快速局部决策的优势被系统级可靠性失效抵消，τ-bench上更快决策缩短中位episode时间但降低任务成功率，误差沿长轨迹累积；(3) 在大规模社会模拟中，决策模型以显著更低推理成本接近强生成式LLM的个体响应预测水平，但在用户画像建模上更弱，且聚合估计误差与系统性偏差更大。InnerJev-27B在JEVal上与Jev持平，典型查询响应约0.1秒。

Conclusion: 通用决策模型适合证据驱动的局部判断，但在专家知识、不确定性校准、长时程系统可靠性和社会模拟聚合准确性方面存在明显短板；通过将LLM推理蒸馏为单步首token决策，InnerJev可在保持性能的同时大幅提升推理效率，为高效决策模型的实际部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: General decision models, such as Jev, have recently emerged as efficient alternatives to LLMs for structured judgment and selection. But what kinds of decisions can these models reliably make, and how does their behavior change when individual decisions are composed into larger systems? To study this, we introduce JEVal, a bilingual benchmark comprising 11,257 instances from 36 datasets across 10 application domains, and evaluate 25 model configurations spanning general decision models and generative LLMs. Our results show that (1) general decision models are most competitive when decisions can be resolved from available evidence, but weaken when they require specialist knowledge or faithful uncertainty estimation: they can often identify the most likely outcome while substantially overstating its probability. (2) In more dynamic and realistic systems involving long-horizon, multi-step interactions, the advantages of fast local decision making are offset by reliability failures at the system level. on $τ$-bench, faster local decisions reduce median episode time but lower task success as decision errors accumulate over long trajectories. (3) In large-scale social simulation, decision models approach strong generative LLMs on individual response prediction at substantially lower inference cost, yet remain weaker in user profiling and exhibit larger aggregate estimation errors and systematic bias. Finally, we propose InnerJev-4B and InnerJev-27B, which internalize an open-weight LLM's own reasoning into a single-pass first-token decision through Reasoning-to-Readout Self-Distillation, with InnerJev-27B performing on par with Jev on JEVal while answering a typical query in about 0.1 s.

</details>


### [95] [A Step Towards Forgetting: Optimiser History and the Loss of Answer Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03940)
*Valeria Ruscio,Seth Nabarro,Keiran Thompson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究优化器记忆（动量）在微调中导致语言模型遗忘的机制：将旧任务损失分解为"旧答案间的混淆"与"概率向答案集外的泄漏"，发现答案质量持续下降而旧答案判别力往往提升，即模型对仍能区分的答案更不愿输出；Adam的累积历史偏向泄漏，当前梯度则抵抗泄漏，且有害贡献主要来自新任务的较旧梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调时，即使当前梯度方向是"保护"已学答案的概率，模型仍会降低对这些答案的赋值概率。既有研究多关注当前梯度与损失曲面，忽视了带动量的优化器内部状态（历史梯度记忆）本身可能驱动遗忘。作者因此希望厘清"优化器记忆"在遗忘中的独立作用及其来源与结构。

Method: 1) 将旧任务损失分解为两部分：旧答案之间的混淆（判别力）与分配到答案集之外的泄漏（answer mass）。2) 在三个语言模型家族上跟踪这两项随训练的变化。3) 对Adam更新做分解，区分累积历史与当前梯度的相反贡献。4) 按梯度"年龄"分解历史，定位有害贡献来自新任务的旧梯度还是近期梯度。5) 通过重置动量并匹配初始更新范数的干预实验验证历史对保持率的因果作用；6) 沿有限步更新的积分分析，用局部投影与沿历史的曲率解释损失突增事件。

Result: 在三个模型家族中，旧答案的质量（answer mass）一致下降，而旧答案间的判别力通常改善，说明模型变得不愿输出自己仍能正确区分的答案。Adam更新分解显示相反的两股力量：累积历史推动泄漏，当前梯度抵抗泄漏。按梯度年龄分解表明，有害贡献主要来自新任务的较旧梯度，近期梯度反而保护旧答案；历史效应的变化主要由其相对于旧任务梯度的方向决定。重置动量并匹配初始更新范数的干预实验证明历史确实影响保持率，且效果依赖状态；在更长的Adam续训中，最终旧任务损失更低，主要通过恢复答案质量实现。有限步更新的积分分析显示，多数采样到的大幅损失上升可由局部投影解释，而沿历史的曲率会放大部分事件。

Conclusion: 优化器的记忆（动量累积的历史梯度）本身可以侵蚀已学行为，即使当前梯度在方向上试图保护这些行为。遗忘不只是当前梯度或数据分布的问题，而是优化器内部状态与当前梯度交互的产物。理解并针对这种历史效应（如动量重置或按年龄调制历史）可能为缓解微调遗忘提供新的干预点。

Abstract: During fine-tuning, a language model can assign less probability to previously learned answers even when the current gradient acts to preserve that probability. With momentum, each update also carries gradients computed at earlier model states, and these stored contributions can push the model in the opposite direction. We investigate how this optimiser memory contributes to forgetting by separating old-task loss into confusion among its answers and leakage of probability outside the answer set. Across three language-model families, answer mass consistently declines while discrimination among old answers usually improves: the model becomes less likely to produce answers that it can still distinguish correctly. Decomposing Adam updates reveals opposing contributions to this loss of answer mass. Over training, accumulated history favours leakage, while the current gradient opposes it. Resolving history by age shows that the harmful contributions come mainly from older gradients of the new task, whereas recent gradients tend to protect the old answers. Changes in history's effect are dominated by its orientation relative to the old-task gradient. Interventions that reset momentum while matching the initial update norm establish that stored history affects retention, with state-dependent immediate effects and lower final old-task loss over longer Adam continuations, mainly through recovered answer mass. Finally, integration along finite updates shows that most sampled large loss increases are captured by local projections, while curvature along history amplifies some events. Together, these findings reveal how an optimiser's memory can erode learned behaviour even as its current gradient acts to preserve it.

</details>


### [96] [BAIBAICHUCHU at the NTCIR-19 FinArg-3 Task: When Is Maximum Possible Profit Predictable from Investor Text?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03962)
*Zong-Han Bai,Po-Yen Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文报告了BAIBAICHUCHU团队在NTCIR-19 FinArg-3社交媒体子任务中的排名系统：三路集成在开发集达0.734，但官方提交仅0.517，事后审计发现判官对看跌帖误用long-only规则以及验证集存在语义捷径，并提出方向性文本、波动率与时间跨度、成对边际的分解框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NTCIR-19 FinArg-3要求按最大可能利润（MPP）对中文投资者帖子排序，而MPP是stance-aware（方向感知）的；团队希望构建稳健的排序系统，并解释开发集与官方提交之间显著的性能落差（0.734 vs 0.517），进而厘清金融社媒排序中真正可迁移、可预测的成分。

Method: 三路集成：词法特征、FinArg-2预微调的MacBERT排序器、LLM判官；提交12个run。事后做审计修正判官的long-only规则（涉及87条官方预测中的28条）。理论/实证分解：将排序拆为方向性文本、波动率与horizon、成对margin；用running-extremum模型预测σ√T标度，并在2026年7月独立采集的502条价格对齐帖子上事后检验；以发帖前20日波动率按帖子特定观察horizon缩放（σ_pre,20√N_i）构建MPP代理（截断horizon），用Spearman ρ与ticker-cluster自助置信区间评估，并检验成对排序正确率与条件增量贡献。

Result: 开发集三路集成0.734（post-grouped），最佳官方run仅0.517，12个提交run介于0.4598–0.5402，26个一致三路对为0.500。修正long-only后改变87条官方预测中的28条但准确率不变，反而使开发准确率从0.680降至0.622，显示regime-specific语义捷径提升了验证拟合。σ_pre,20√N_i与截断horizon MPP代理相关ρ=0.320（ticker-cluster 95% CI [0.133,0.466]），正确排序61.4%的不等结果对；迁移文本与7月结果近乎不相关（ρ=0.055），在此分数与stance条件下增量有限。开发集可靠性随标注MPP差距扩大从约0.60升至0.93。较早的ERAI结果0.6207排除了"普遍不可预测"的简单解释。

Conclusion: MPP排序的可预测性来自市场regime、历史波动率与horizon而非帖文本语义；验证集上的高表现可能源于regime-specific语义捷径而非真实泛化。评估应同时考虑文本、市场regime、历史波动率、horizon与成对组成，并需警惕stance-aware规则误用与pair composition对指标的影响。

Abstract: The BAIBAICHUCHU team participated in the Social Media Subtask of NTCIR-19 FinArg-3, ranking Chinese investor posts by Maximum Possible Profit (MPP). A three-track ensemble of lexical features, a FinArg-2-pre-finetuned MacBERT ranker, and an LLM judge reaches 0.734 in post-grouped development evaluation, but our best official run scores 0.517. All twelve submitted runs lie between 0.4598 and 0.5402, and our 26 unanimous three-track pairs score 0.500.
  A post-hoc audit finds that the submitted judge applied a long-only rule to bearish posts although MPP is stance-aware. Correcting it changes 28 of 87 official predictions without changing accuracy, yet lowers development accuracy from 0.680 to 0.622: a regime-specific semantic shortcut improved validation fit.
  We decompose ranking into directional text, volatility and horizon, and pairwise margin. A running-extremum model predicts $σ\sqrt{T}$ scaling, observed ex post in 502 price-aligned posts from a separate July 2026 collection. Pre-posting volatility scaled by the post-specific observation horizon, $σ_{\mathrm{pre},20}\sqrt{N_i}$, is associated with a later truncated-horizon MPP proxy (Spearman $ρ=0.320$; ticker-cluster 95% CI [0.133,0.466]) and correctly orders 61.4% of unequal-outcome pairs. Transferred text is nearly uncorrelated with the July outcome ($ρ=0.055$) and adds little conditional on this score and stance. Development reliability rises from about 0.60 to 0.93 as the labeled MPP gap widens. An earlier ERAI result of 0.6207 rules out universal unpredictability as a simple explanation. Evaluation should jointly consider text, market regime, historical volatility, horizon, and pair composition.

</details>


### [97] [Do Language Models Need a Trainable Input Embedding Table? Fixed Minimal Token Codes at 1.7B-Class Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04002)
*A. Bochkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文检验语言模型是否必须使用可训练的token专用输入嵌入，通过对比可学习嵌入表、规范16位token-ID编码与GF(2)固定可逆重编码三种输入接口，发现固定编码模型仍能获得实质性语言建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究Transformer语言模型的强大能力是否依赖于token专用的独立可调输入向量参数化，即区分架构必要性（architectural necessity）与经验效用（empirical utility），并考察在固定token身份输入下能否学到实质能力。

Method: 训练三个从零开始的decoder-only语言模型，采用相同tokenizer、上下文骨干、非绑定输出头架构与训练配方，目标预算为每模型1000亿预测token。三种输入接口分别为：可学习嵌入表、规范16位token-ID编码、GF(2)上的一个固定可逆重编码；固定编码直接重复至模型宽度，不额外增加可训练输入投影。

Result: 两种固定编码模型均获得实质能力：规范编码达到52.40% HellaSwag归一化准确率、70.51% PIQA准确率、42.75% LAMBADA准确率。可学习输入对照在HellaSwag和LAMBADA等若干评测上表现更好，因此结果确立的是可行性而非性能持平。固定接口移除了1.007亿可训练参数，得到1.711B参数模型，但参数减少并非核心结论。

Conclusion: 这些单次运行实验表明：独立可训练的token专用输入向量并非观察到能力的必要条件，可将架构必要性同经验效用区分开。固定身份接口还为研究不可变输入下游的表示学习提供了受控环境，但不用于确定特定能力在何处定位。

Abstract: A trainable input embedding table assigns each vocabulary item an independently adjustable vector. We investigate whether this token-specific parameterization is required for substantial language-modeling capability, or whether a shared Transformer can learn from fixed token identities. We compare three decoder-only language models trained from scratch with the same tokenizer, contextual backbone, untied output-head architecture, and training recipe, with a target budget of 100 billion prediction tokens per model. Their input interfaces are a learned table, canonical 16-bit token-ID codes, and one fixed invertible recoding over GF(2). The fixed codes are repeated to model width without an additional trainable input projection. Both fixed-code models acquire substantial capabilities: canonical codes achieve 52.40\% HellaSwag normalized accuracy, 70.51\% PIQA accuracy, and 42.75\% LAMBADA accuracy. The learned-input control performs better on several evaluations, including HellaSwag and LAMBADA, so these results establish viability rather than performance parity. The fixed interfaces remove 100.7 million trainable parameters, yielding 1.711B-parameter models, but parameter reduction is not the central result. These single-run experiments distinguish architectural necessity from empirical utility: independently trainable token-specific input vectors are not required for the observed capabilities. A fixed identity interface also provides a controlled setting for studying representation learning downstream of an immutable input, without establishing where particular capabilities are localized.

</details>


### [98] [Periscope: Extending Frozen Language Models Beyond Their Context Window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04047)
*Mohamed Eltahir,Anas Obayd,Raed Rashid,Abdulrahman Alghamdi,Abdulrahman Mousa,Abdallah Ahmed,Tanveer Hussain,Naeemullah Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Periscope,一种免训练的推理方法。它将长文本切分为 N 个块,按 K×K 网格排列,让冻结的模型仅对 K 个连续块的局部片段和 K 个跨越全文的跨步片段回答同一问题,通过答案的 log-odds 打分,将长文本的"读取"分解。该方法在亚平方级成本下实现对长上下文的证据定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型以单次二次复杂度的前向传播读取长文本,受限于上下文窗口,且在达到窗口前精度就随长度下降。作者希望探究:在有限候选集的决策场景(哪篇文档相关、哪个选项被支持、哪段是证据)中,读取过程能否被分解,从而避免昂贵的长文本单次读取。

Method: Periscope 将 N 个文本块排布在 K×K 网格(K=⌈√N⌉),向冻结模型提出对 K 个连续块的局部片段和 K 个采样全文的跨步片段的同一问题,读取每个答案在某一 token 处的 log-odds。每个答案取局部与跨步得分中的最优值,并对每个块用其两个片段打分,从而无需额外成本得到证据图,其峰值即为答案背后的块。每个探针约 √(sc) 个 token(s 为文本长度,c 为块大小),因此窗口 W 可覆盖 W²/c 个 token,成本为 s^1.5。

Result: 在 LongBench v2 上,仅读取证据图排名最高的 K 个块(9k token)即可匹配同模型 32k 至 1M 窗口的最佳读取;在 InfiniteBench(上下文中位数为 150k token)上领先最佳窗口读取 5 分。同一证据图在 BRIGHT 长文档语料上取得六种方法中最佳的 NDCG@10。每次调用仅缓存一个探针,故 27B 模型可在单张 80GB GPU 上读取 4.5M token 上下文,而单次前向传播需 296GB 缓存。

Conclusion: 长文本读取可以被分解为对局部与跨步片段的并行探针,证据图可替代完整的长读取。该方法使长上下文所需的 GPU 只需能容纳模型本身,而无需容纳全文,大幅降低显存需求,同时保持甚至超越窗口读取的准确率。

Abstract: A language model reads long text in one quadratic forward pass, stops at the context window, and loses accuracy with length before reaching it. We ask whether the read can be factorized when deciding over a finite set: which document is relevant, which option is supported, which passage is the evidence. Periscope, a training-free inference method, arranges the $N$ chunks of a text on a $K{\times}K$ grid with $K{=}\lceil\sqrt{N}\rceil$ and asks a frozen model the same question about $K$ local spans of consecutive chunks and $K$ strided spans that sample the whole text, reading the log-odds of every answer at one token. Each answer takes its best local and strided score, and scoring every chunk by its two spans gives an evidence map at no further cost, whose peak is the chunk behind the answer. Every probe is about $\sqrt{sc}$ tokens for a text of $s$ tokens and chunk size $c$, so a window of $W$ tokens reaches $W^{2}/c$ tokens at $s^{1.5}$ cost. The map replaces the long read. On LongBench v2, reading only the $K$ chunks the map ranks highest, 9k tokens, matches the same model's best window read across windows from 32k to 1M tokens, and on InfiniteBench, where the median context is 150k tokens, it leads the best window read by 5 points. The same map ranks BRIGHT's long-document corpora with the best NDCG@10 of six methods. Each call caches only one probe, so a 27B model reads 4.5M-token contexts on one 80GB GPU, where a single pass would need 296GB of cache. A long read then needs a GPU that holds the model, not one that holds the text.

</details>


### [99] [IdeaScientist: Orchestrating Agents for Grounded Scientific Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04074)
*Jiarui Liu,Renjie Tao,Yiwei Liao,Chuanyang Jin,Kai Sun,Xiao Yang,Xinyuan Zhang,Xilun Chen,Zhuangqun Huang,Lechen Zhang,Yongjin Yang,Yinghui He,Weihao Xuan,Rakesh Wanga,Anuj Kumar,Mona T. Diab,Wen-tau Yih,Xin Luna Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IdeaScientist 将研究构思拆解为“发现空白—创新—报告撰写”三个角色并用强化学习训练，配合 2.77M 条分解式研究想法的 Svalbard Idea Vault 语料库，在受限时间文献条件下评测，27B 开源模型上显著超越最强开源基线，甚至优于部分闭源大模型方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化科研虽进展迅速，但生成有前景且论证扎实的研究方案仍是核心难题。作者将“研究构思”单独列为任务，基于一个直觉：某一领域的挑战常可由另一领域中解决过类似挑战的机制来应对，即跨领域类比迁移是高质量想法的重要来源。

Method: 提出 IdeaScientist，将构思分解为 gap finding（发现空白）、innovation（创新）、report writing（报告撰写）三个角色，并分别用强化学习训练。各角色负责识别相关工作局限、从类比问题设定中抽取解法直觉，并发展为完整研究提案。同时构建 Svalbard Idea Vault，包含 2.77M 条分解式研究想法，用于检索、训练与时间可控评测。

Result: 评测限制仅访问截止日期前的文献，并衡量所提方向与人类研究者后续 15K 篇论文所探索方向的一致程度。在 Qwen3.6-27B 上，IdeaScientist 相对最强开源自动科研基线提升 14.0%，主要来自新颖性增益；在该 27B 开源骨干上，还分别超过使用 Claude-4.8-Opus 的 Claude Code SDK 与使用 GPT-5.4 的 Codex SDK，最高达 5.9%。

Conclusion: 将研究构思显式建模为可分解、可强化学习训练的角色化流程，并辅以跨领域检索语料与时间可控评测，可有效提升自动科研想法的质量与新颖性，使较小开源模型也能在构思任务上超越依赖强闭源模型的通用智能体方案。
