Abstract: The deployment of Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents in critical domains must be preceded with a pipeline to evaluate the alignment of the RL agent with complex multi-objective specifications and robustness under real-world environmental drift. However, to protect intellectual property, the RL agent may be delivered for evaluation as opaque executable or remote API, which makes traditional evaluation techniques based on the internals of the policies infeasible. To address this gap, CORE-RL: Confidence-Oriented Reliability Evaluation of black box RL policy is proposed in this paper. The CORE-RL pipeline introduces a Unified Reliability Metric that formally integrates early task termination and safety constraint violations, preventing unsafe policies from masking failures through premature episode halts. By subjecting the policy to a noise certification envelope of perceptual noise, actuation noise and change in environment dynamics, the pipeline computes the finite-sample Clopper-Pearson bounds on unified reliability metric and Hoeffdings' lower bound on reward and safety cost. The pipeline then defines safe operational design domain to report high-confidence certificates for safety and expected performance. Experiments on continuous control tasks demonstrate the CORE-RL pipeline's ability to automatically reject non-compliant policies and map the safe Operational Design Domain (ODD) of safety-aware policies. Thus CORE-RL provides an evaluation framework towards a quantitative, transparent and reproducible, statistical rationale necessary to safely evaluate, compare, and deploy black box RL solutions.

</details>


### [232] [Asking Earns Nothing: Scoring the Decision to Act in BFCL Multi-Turn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04429)
*Yangze Liu,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示 BFCL 多轮榜单中"应当提问"类别存在评测缺陷：应提问回合金标轨迹为空、评分器跳过、脚本用户无法作答，导致提问无收益、猜测无成本；作者用配对控制实验构造同回合"完整/缺失"对照，证明官方分数随"倾向行动"而非正确决策移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在信息不足时应先提问而非直接行动，BFCL 多轮榜单的四个类别中有两个正是围绕"应当提问"的回合设计。然而作者发现该基准的评分器完全忽略这些回合：金标轨迹为空、检查器跳过、脚本化用户无法作答，于是提问得不到任何分数、乱猜在该回合也不受惩罚、而提问两次反而会丢失该项。这意味着榜单在关键决策点上并未真正测量其所宣称的能力，需要一个能直接测量"该不该行动"这一决策的对照实验。

Method: 作者利用基准自身蕴含的对照设计：一个"应当提问"条目就是把基础条目的某一回合删掉一条信息，因此同一请求在同一回合索引出现两次，一次信息完整、一次缺失——前者应执行改变世界的调用，后者应提问。对每一对只评一个决策（模型在该回合是否尝试了改变世界的调用），直接从存储轨迹读取，不使用 LLM 评判。在 223 对构成该决策的样本上评测七个模型，并进行提示扰动实验：给 gpt-5.4 增加一句"不要提问"，给 gemma-4-31B-it 增加一句"先提问"。

Result: 在 223 对上，gpt-5.4 在完整回合有 83.4% 尝试调用、在信息缺失回合有 78.0% 抑制调用，决策准确率 80.7%，为七个模型最高；但同一批条目上官方分数将其排第六，并把在此处仅居中游的模型排第一。加入"不要提问"提示后，gpt-5.4 在配对两侧都更倾向行动，决策准确率无可检测变化，而其官方分数在两类 should-ask 类别及基础孪生条目上提升 13.5 至 23.5 分；相反，提示 gemma-4-31B-it"先提问"使其决策提升 4.5 分却未获得任何官方分数增益。结论是官方分数随"倾向行动"移动，而非随决策质量移动。

Conclusion: BFCL 多轮的 should-ask 评分并未衡量正确的提问决策，而是奖励"更倾向行动"的行为倾向，因此其排名与真实决策能力脱节。作者公开发布配对样本、一个无需 API key、可运行于任意 BFCL 输出目录的回合级评分器，以及 31 条人工核验的坏条目，以支持更可靠的基准评测。

Abstract: An agent that lacks the information it needs should ask rather than act, and the task definitions of agent leaderboards say so. BFCL multi-turn builds two of its four categories around a turn on which the model is supposed to ask, and its scorer never looks at that turn: the gold trajectory there is empty, the checker skips it, and the scripted user cannot answer, so asking earns nothing, guessing costs nothing on that turn, and asking twice loses the item. The benchmark also contains the control experiment for that decision. A should-ask item is a base item with one piece of information removed from one turn, so the same request appears twice at the same turn index, once complete and once not: on the first the model should make the call that changes the world, on the second it should ask. We score one decision per pair, whether the model attempted a world-changing call on that turn, read off the stored trajectories with no LLM judge; acting always and asking always both score 50. On the 223 pairs that pose this decision, gpt-5.4 attempts the call on 83.4% of the complete turns and holds back on 78.0% of the incomplete ones, the best decision accuracy of seven models at 80.7%; on the same items the official score ranks it sixth and puts first a model that lands in the middle here. One added line telling gpt-5.4 not to ask pushes it toward acting on both sides of the pair, so its decision accuracy shows no detectable change, while its official score rises by 13.5 to 23.5 points on the two should-ask categories and on the base twins; the opposite line, telling gemma-4-31B-it to ask first, improves its decision by 4.5 points and gains no official score. The score moves with the push toward action, not with the decision. We release the pairs, a turn-level scorer that runs on any BFCL output directory without an API key, and 31 manually verified bad items.

</details>


### [233] [MERCI Cards: An LLM Evaluation and Deployment Framework for High-Stakes Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04430)
*Aparna Komarla,Annalisa Szymanski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个加权多目标优化的数学框架，用于迭代式LLM评估与部署，并以刑事司法AI系统为例，通过MERCI Cards指导系统在部署迭代中的改进、定位薄弱环节并引导用户进行验证与纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被越来越多地部署于高风险专业工作流，工程师与研究者需要系统化、有原理支撑的协议来跟踪、监测并改进模型在部署周期中的表现。

Method: 提出一个用于迭代式LLM评估与部署的数学框架，将高风险、资源受限场景下的LLM集成形式化为加权多目标优化，涵盖模型选择、评分标准(rubric)设计、评估与部署等环节。

Result: 以刑事司法领域的AI系统为应用案例，展示了MERCI Cards在部署迭代中引导系统改进、将开发者注意力导向表现不佳区域、并促使用户聚焦于对LLM输出的验证与纠错方面的效果。

Conclusion: 该框架为高风险场景下的LLM迭代评估与部署提供了原则性协议，MERCI Cards可作为实用工具支撑持续改进与人工监督。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly deployed in high-stakes professional workflows, engineers and researchers require principled protocols to systematically track, monitor, and improve model performance across deployment cycles. We present a mathematical framework for iterative LLM evaluation and deployment, and demonstrate its application to AI systems used in criminal justice. Our framework formalizes LLM integration in high-stakes, high-risk, and resource-constrained domains across model selection, rubric design, evaluations and deployment via a weighted multi-objective optimization. We demonstrate that MERCI Cards can guide improvements of the system across deployment iterations, direct developer attention toward under-performing areas, and focus user attention on validation and error-correction in the LLM's outputs.

</details>


### [234] [What Does a Harness Buy? Tokens, Mostly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04433)
*Yangze Liu,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在SWE-bench Verified上以5个模型×3个生产级harness（Claude Code、mini-SWE-agent、OpenCode）做重复运行校准后发现：harness本身对分数的漂移大多落在重跑噪声内，唯一稳健的harness效应是一项损失（OpenCode最多低9分）；harness真正决定的是成本，同模型同任务下每任务成本可差3倍，差异由首次调用时system prompt与工具schema的开销决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级harness每日发版，厂商宣称harness改动带来通过率提升，排行榜也随意混用不同harness，但鲜有人测量"固定模型、只换harness"时分数究竟变化多少。作者希望把harness效应从运行噪声中分离出来，并给出harness比较所需的统计分辨率。

Method: 将5个模型分别接入3个生产级harness（Claude Code、mini-SWE-agent、OpenCode），在SWE-bench Verified上评测；对同一配置重复运行以校准"什么都不变、只重跑"带来的分数波动。分析规模包括447任务池（2个模型）与45任务困难子集（5个模型）；同时用统一价目表比较每任务成本，并拆解成本结构（首次调用preamble、步数放大、每步增长、每调用工具输出、缓存输入价格）。

Result: 在447任务、两个模型上，最重的Claude Code与最轻的mini-SWE-agent差距在5分以内、属等价。在45任务困难子集、5个模型上，换harness带来的任务翻转率与同harness重跑同为13%，且某次运行中获胜的任务并非下次仍获胜。唯一超过噪声的harness效应是负向的：OpenCode在大池上最多落后9分，其中一个模型约一半差距来自输出上限被截断的运行。成本方面，同模型、同任务、同价目表下每任务成本跨harness最多差3倍，差距在首次调用即由system prompt与工具schema的preamble决定，并随步数放大；每步增长与每调用工具输出差异小得多，缓存输入价格只缩放账单、不改变排序。重跑数据还给出分辨率：在当前不一致率下，45任务仅半数情况下能检出13分差距、对无差距的检验功效80%；447任务可分辨5分，仍比许多harness改动宣称的增益更粗。

Conclusion: harness对分数的真实影响常被重跑噪声掩盖，除OpenCode的负向差距外多数"harness收益"难以被稳健检出；harness的核心可测量效应在于成本，且主要由首次调用的prompt与工具schema开销决定。现有评测规模（45任务）不足以支撑常见的harness比较，447任务也仅能分辨5分，故harness相关声明需按此分辨率谨慎解读。

Abstract: A coding agent is a language model wrapped in a harness: the system prompt, the tool set, and the context management that turn a chat model into something that can work inside a repository. Production harnesses ship releases daily, vendors advertise pass-rate gains from harness changes, and leaderboards mix harnesses freely. What is rarely measured is how much the harness itself moves the score when the model is held fixed. We run five models through three production harnesses, Claude Code, mini-SWE-agent, and OpenCode, on SWE-bench Verified, and rerun the same configurations to calibrate how much a score moves when nothing changes but the run. On 447 tasks and the two models we ran there, Claude Code and mini-SWE-agent, the heaviest and the lightest harness, are equivalent within five points. On a 45-task hard subset and five models, swapping the harness flips as many tasks as rerunning the same harness, 13% in both cases, and the tasks a harness wins in one run are not the tasks it wins in the next. The one harness effect that clears the noise is a loss, not a gain: OpenCode trails by up to 9 points on the large pool, and on one model half of that gap sits in runs its output cap cut short. What the harness does decide is the bill. With the same model, the same tasks, and one price list, cost per task differs by up to 3x across harnesses. The gap is set at the first call, by the preamble of system prompt and tool schemas each harness sends with every step, and scaled by the number of steps; per-step growth and per-call tool output differ far less. The provider's price for cached input scales the bill and does not reorder it. The rerun data also give the resolution a harness comparison needs: at the discordance we observe, 45 tasks catch a 13-point gap only half the time and no gap with 80% power, and 447 tasks resolve 5 points, still coarser than the gains many harness changes claim.

</details>


### [235] [CRAFT: An Agentic Spreadsheet Form Filling System with Template Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04437)
*Leyao Gu,Yingjie Xiong,Zirui Tang,Jiangtao Zhou,Yeye He,Chunwei Liu,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CRAFT是一个模板感知的表格填写智能体框架，通过将反思式验证与受限局部修复相连接，提升电子表格填写的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子表格填写需整合外部证据、将值定位到精确单元格并保持不规则模板结构；早期编辑错误会覆盖标签或使字段错位，进而破坏后续决策。

Method: 提出CRAFT框架：不将反思视为自由形式的整体重写，而是将检测到的错误定位到具体表格区域，必要时恢复被破坏的模板状态，并在后续编辑前重新定位可写槽位；其中Rectangle-Aware Slot Grounder（RASG）负责提出可写单元格，标签-槽位提示与保护区域约束后续编辑。

Result: 提出FormFillBench基准，含327个表单，分Instruction-Only与Multi-File两个赛道；相比最强基线，CRAFT在两条赛道上成对准确率分别提升8.51与23.38个百分点；组件移除实验支持结构裁决与槽位重定位作用，且在第二个骨干模型下仍保持相对优势。

Conclusion: 模板感知的反射验证与受限局部修复机制能有效提升表格填写性能，代码与基准已开源。

Abstract: Spreadsheet form filling requires agents to consolidate external evidence, ground values to precise cells, and preserve irregular template structure. Errors in early edits can overwrite labels or misalign fields, undermining later decisions. We propose CRAFT, a template-aware agent framework that connects reflective validation to constrained local repair. Instead of treating reflection as a free-form request to regenerate the workbook, CRAFT grounds detected errors to spreadsheet regions, restores corrupted template state when necessary, and re-grounds plausible writable slots before subsequent edits. A Rectangle-Aware Slot Grounder (RASG) proposes writable cells, while label-slot hints and protected regions constrain subsequent edits. We introduce FormFillBench, with 327 forms across Instruction-Only and Multi-File tracks. Compared with the strongest baselines, CRAFT improves pair accuracy by 8.51 and 23.38 percentage points on these tracks, respectively. Component-removal experiments support structural adjudication and slot re-grounding within the pipeline, and the framework retains its relative advantage among the methods evaluated with a second backbone. The code and benchmark FormFillBench are available at https://github.com/Glllllly/CRAFT.

</details>


### [236] [RAGrasp: Geometry-Semantic Template Retrieval and Grasp Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04438)
*Shenzhe Zhu,Chengxiao He,Jan Harder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAGrasp 提出一种检索增强的平面平行夹爪抓取流水线，仅依赖少量本地采集的带抓取标注 RGB-D 模板，实现新部署场景下的免端到端重训练抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取预测器多依赖大规模公开或合成数据训练，任务专用性强，迁移到新部署时需端到端重训练，且与目标工作空间的相机、机器人、夹爪及传感条件不匹配。

Method: 以部署相机/机器人/夹爪在工作空间采集的观测构建模板记忆；用自监督 DINOv2 视觉特征结合外观与深度线索提示 SAM2 分割查询物体；通过两阶段几何-语义检索级联选择模板，并以置信度门控在两种抓取迁移估计器间选择；迁移抓取经掩码支撑与轮廓接触约束细化，再进行标定的 2D-3D 转换。

Result: 真实世界试验中，已知物体抓取 20/20 成功，未知物体 19/20 成功。

Conclusion: 在评估设置内，RAGrasp 证明了基于有限本地标注的部署特定抓取自适应能力，并对测试的视角与光照变化具有容忍性。

Abstract: We present RAGrasp, a retrieval-augmented pipeline for planar parallel-jaw grasping from a compact set of locally collected, grasp-annotated RGB-D (color and depth) templates. Unlike task-specific predictors trained primarily on large public or synthetic grasp datasets, RAGrasp requires no end-to-end retraining for a new deployment.Its template memory is constructed from observations collected with the deployment camera, robot, and gripper in the target workspace, thereby aligning stored examples with the local sensing and embodiment conditions. The system uses self-supervised DINOv2 visual fea- tures together with appearance and depth cues to prompt the Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2), which isolates the query object. A two-stage geometry-semantic retrieval cascade then selects a template, and a confidence gate chooses one of two grasp- transfer estimators. The transferred grasp is refined using mask- support and silhouette-contact constraints before calibrated 2D- to-3D conversion. In real-world trials, RAGrasp achieves 20/20 successful grasps on seen objects and 19/20 on unseen objects. Within the evaluated setting, the results demonstrate deployment- specific grasp adaptation from limited local annotation and tolerance to the tested viewpoint and illumination changes.

</details>


### [237] [Causally Fair Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04444)
*Patrik Okanovic,Torsten Hoefler,Drago Plecko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CFG（Causally Fair Generation with LLMs），一种在 LLM 生成过程中基于因果推断消除人口统计差异的方法，可选择性移除用户指定的因果效应并保留业务必要性路径，并在适当因果假设下提供形式化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 的输出日益影响重大决策，引发对其加剧人口统计差异的担忧。纯统计方法即使拥有无限数据也无法将观测差异归因于生成机制，而因果推断可提供原则性的公平评估基础。此外，LLM 生成中查询可能涉及多个因果相关变量，每个变量既是结果又可能是其他输出的原因，且提示信息不必遵循拓扑或时间顺序，因此亟需能分析并选择性移除此类灵活生成过程中差异的方法。

Method: CFG 提取相关概念，将生成锚定于参考人群与因果图，并移除用户选定的因果效应；同时允许保留对任务效用而言可正当化的路径（法律文献中的"业务必要性"）。

Result: 在消除适配人群模型中所有歧视性因果效应的情形下，于适当的因果假设下为该方法提供了形式化保证；并使用四个 LLM 在三个真实场景（基于人群数据及一个具有已知因果真值的合成数据集）上进行了评估。

Conclusion: CFG 为 LLM 生成中的因果公平性提供了可选择性移除差异、保留业务必要性路径并具形式化保证的框架，且在真实与合成数据设置中得到验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate, complete, and transform information in settings where their outputs can shape consequential decisions, raising concerns about their impact on demographic disparities. In this context, causal inference provides a principled basis for assessing fairness, because it attributes observed disparities to the mechanisms that generated them, which a purely statistical approach cannot do even with infinite data. In LLM generation, a query may request several causally related variables, each of which is both an outcome of interest and a possible cause of other outputs, and the information supplied in the prompt need not follow a topological or a temporal order. This calls for methods that can analyze and selectively remove disparities from such a flexible generation process. In this paper we introduce Causally Fair Generation with LLMs (CFG, for short). CFG extracts relevant concepts, grounds generation in a reference population and causal diagram, and removes user-selected causal effects. CFG also allows pathways deemed justifiable for the task's utility to be retained, which is known in legal literature as business necessity. Further, we provide formal guarantees for our method when eliminating all discriminatory causal effects in the adapted population model, under appropriate causal assumptions. We evaluate CFG with four LLMs in three real-world settings based on population data and on a synthetic dataset with a known causal ground truth.

</details>


### [238] [Understanding Generative AI Use in Programming MOOCs: The Role of Course Context and Learner Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04447)
*Marina Lepp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过两门爱沙尼亚语编程MOOC的课后问卷，考察学习者对生成式AI的使用情况，发现AI采用率普遍较高且与人口学特征无关，而课程情境（时长与负荷）显著影响使用频率与用途。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具（如ChatGPT与代码补全助手）日益普及，学习者在学习活动中如何整合这些工具尚不清楚，尤其在参与者背景多样的MOOC情境中更缺乏实证证据。

Method: 选取两门时长、工作量、主题复杂度与作业量各异爱沙尼亚语编程MOOC（About Programming：4周、约26小时、n=187；Introduction to Programming：8周、约78小时、n=182），基于课后问卷数据，采用非参数统计方法分析自报的AI采用率、使用频率与使用目的，并比较不同课程与学习者背景间的差异；另进行探索性分析考察AI使用与学习相关结果的关系。

Result: 两门MOOC中GenAI采用率均较高，且在性别、年龄、教育水平与先前编程经验上无统计显著差异；较长、内容更繁重的MOOC参与者报告的使用频率显著更高，更倾向将GenAI用于调试与创意生成，用于代码解释与调试的使用频率也更高；探索性分析发现AI使用与学习相关结果之间关系有限。

Conclusion: 课程情境可能比学习者个体特征在塑造GenAI使用方式上发挥更大作用，研究结果为编程教育中的教学设计与学习指导提供了启示。

Abstract: The increasing availability of generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) tools, such as ChatGPT and code-completion assistants, raises questions about how learners integrate these tools into learning activities, particularly in MOOCs that attract diverse participant populations. This study examines the use of GenAI in two programming MOOCs taught in Estonian that differ in duration, workload, topic complexity, and assignment volume: About Programming (4 weeks, 26 expected hours, n = 187) and Introduction to Programming (8 weeks, 78 expected hours, n = 182). Post-course questionnaire data were analyzed using non-parametric statistical methods to examine self-reported adoption, usage frequency, and purposes of GenAI use across courses and learner backgrounds. The results show that GenAI adoption was widespread in both MOOCs, with no statistically significant differences by gender, age, education level, or prior programming experience. However, participants in the longer, more extensive MOOC reported significantly higher usage frequency and were more likely to use GenAI for debugging and idea generation. Reported usage frequency for code explanation and debugging was also higher in the longer course. Exploratory analyses found limited relationships between GenAI use and learning-related outcomes. The findings suggest that course context may play a greater role than learner characteristics in shaping how GenAI tools are used. These results provide implications for instructional design and guidance in programming education.

</details>


### [239] [Trinity: Self-Evolving Vision-Language Models with a Self-Verifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04469)
*Youngwan Lee,Yong-Ju Lee,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trinity提出让单一VLM同时扮演提问者、求解者与自验证者（策略自身的EMA副本），用自验证代替答案一致性作为奖励信号，仅用无标注图像即可自进化，提升Qwen3-VL-8B的视觉数学与科学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化VLM方法由Questioner出题、Solver作答，但奖励主要依赖多次采样答案之间的一致性。一致性是"真值"的弱代理：它无法判断问题是否真正基于图像、参考答�是否正确，也无法识别自信的多数是否同错。因此需要一个无需标注与外部评判者的可靠验证机制。

Method: Trinity让一个VLM承担三种角色：Questioner、Solver和Verifier。Verifier是模型策略自身的指数移动平均（EMA）副本，即自验证者。Verifier在生成问题转为监督信号前先筛查其图像基础性（image grounding）与答案正确性；对Solver的推理依据图像进行打分；并在参考答�与强Solver共识发生冲突时进行裁决，若共识正确则修正参考答�并惩罚Questioner。

Result: 仅使用无标注图像训练，Trinity在Qwen3-VL-8B上提升了数学视觉推理与含生物学内容的科学基准表现，例如SciVQR生物学子集提升8.6分，MathVerse提升12.8分；奖励动态表现出健康自博弈课程应有的特征。

Conclusion: 结果表明，自进化多模态智能体可以仅凭无标注科学图像强化其科学推理能力，而模型自身即可担任验证者。

Abstract: Self-evolving vision-language models (VLMs), a form of self-improvement in which a model generates its own training data from unlabeled images, are a promising route toward agents that expand their reasoning capability in an unsupervised manner, without relying on ever-larger annotation budgets. Existing methods pair a Questioner that proposes problems with a Solver that answers them, but reward both roles mainly by agreement among sampled answers. Agreement is a weak proxy for truth: it cannot tell whether a question is grounded in the image, whether the proposed reference answer is right, or whether a confident majority is wrong in the same way. We present Trinity, in which one VLM plays three roles, Questioner, Solver, and Verifier, and the Verifier is a self-verifier: an exponential moving average (EMA) of the policy itself, requiring neither labels nor an external judge. The Verifier screens every generated question for image grounding and answer correctness before it becomes supervision, scores Solver reasoning against the image, and adjudicates disputes between the reference answer and a strong Solver consensus, correcting the reference and penalizing the Questioner when the consensus is right. Trained on images alone, Trinity improves Qwen3-VL-8B on mathematical visual reasoning and on science benchmarks with biology content, for example, +8.6 points on the biology split of SciVQR and +12.8 on MathVerse, and its reward dynamics behave as a healthy self-play curriculum should. These results suggest that a self-evolving multimodal agent can strengthen its scientific reasoning from unlabeled scientific images alone, with the model itself serving as the verifier.

</details>


### [240] [Reactivating Alignment: Defending LLMs from Jailbreaks via Intention-Aware Input-Output Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04470)
*Luoyu Chen,Weiqi Wang,Chenhan Zhang,Zhiyi Tian,Shui Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SENTINEL：一种即插即用的生成时越狱防御，将缓解重构为意图抽取问题，通过匹配输入-输出语义对齐区域提取意图子序列并评估危害性，在 HarmBench 上将越狱成功率降至约 5% 且保持低过度拒答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御主要依赖输入扰动或有害输出抑制，很少建模恶意意图所在位置，导致防护脆弱且过度拒答严重。

Method: 基于指令微调 LLM 具有强输入-输出语义一致性的洞察：匹配语义对齐的输入-输出区域以提取揭示意图的子序列，利用拒绝方向投影对这些子序列打分以估计危害性，并在必要时中止生成。

Result: 在多个 LLM 上的 HarmBench 实验显示，SENTINEL 将越狱成功率降至接近 5%，同时保持较低的过度拒答；并对自适应攻击表现出鲁棒性。机制解释表明，SENTINEL 将越狱特征从对齐盲区重新分配到已对齐区域。

Conclusion: SENTINEL 通过意图抽取的生成时防御，在低过度拒答下有效降低越狱成功率，并具备对自适应攻击的鲁棒性与可解释的机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that conceal harmful intent within complex adversarial prompts. Existing defenses primarily rely on input perturbation or harmful-output suppression, but they rarely model where malicious intent resides, resulting in brittle protection and excessive over-refusal.
  We propose SENTINEL, a plug-and-play, generation-time jailbreak defense that reframes mitigation as an intent extraction problem. Our key insight is that instruction-tuned LLMs exhibit strong input--output semantic consistency: regardless of jailbreak complexity, generated outputs tend to align with the attacker's true intent. SENTINEL exploits this property by matching semantically aligned input--output regions to extract intention-revealing subsequences, scores these subsequences using refusal-direction projections to estimate harmfulness, and halts generation when necessary. Experiments on HarmBench across multiple LLMs show that SENTINEL reduces jailbreak success rates to close to 5\% while maintaining low over-refusal. We further demonstrate robustness to adaptive attacks and provide a mechanistic interpretation: SENTINEL re-distributes jailbreak features from alignment blind spots to aligned regions.

</details>


### [241] [Semantic Causal-Factor Inference from Aviation Incident Narratives Using A Variational Autoencoder with Cosine-Similarity-Based Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04472)
*AZIIDA NANYONGA1,HASSAN WASSWA,UGUR TURHAN,KEITH FRANCIS JOINER,GRAHAM WILD*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出语义因果因子推断框架，结合自然语言处理与变分自编码器（VAE），学习航空事故调查叙事与专家报告的可能原因之间的映射关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空事故调查产生大量非结构化文本，其中包含与事故原因及促成因素相关的证据；但由于因果证据分散在冗长复杂的调查叙事中，自动提取此类信息十分困难。

Method: 将调查叙事及其对应可能原因转化为数值表示；编码器将叙事表示映射到概率潜空间；解码器估计对应可能原因的表示，并以结合Kullback-Leibler散度与基于余弦相似度的语义重构目标进行训练。

Result: 基于2005至2020年美国国家运输安全委员会20,919份最终调查报告进行评估；在留出测试集上，预测与专家报告的可能原因表示达到平均余弦相似度0.786（SD=0.120），并产生与报告中因果信息相关的可解释术语。

Conclusion: 结果证明概率潜表示学习在AI辅助从航空安全叙事中提取因果信息方面具有潜力，同时仍以专家调查作为正式因果判定的基础。

Abstract: Aviation accident and incident investigations generate extensive unstructured textual information containing evidence relevant to the causes and contributing factors of safety occurrences. Automatically extracting such information is challenging because causal evidence may be distributed across long and complex investigation narratives. This study proposes a semantic causal-factor inference framework combining natural language processing with a variational autoencoder (VAE) to learn the relationship between aviation investigation narratives and expert-reported probable causes. Investigation narratives and their corresponding probable causes are transformed into numerical representations, after which the encoder maps narrative representations to a probabilistic latent space. The decoder estimates representations of the corresponding probable causes and is trained using an objective that combines Kullback-Leibler divergence with cosine-similarity-based semantic reconstruction. The framework was evaluated using 20,919 finalized U.S. National Transportation Safety Board investigation reports from 2005 to 2020. On the held-out test set, the predicted and expert-reported probable-cause representations achieved a mean cosine similarity of 0.786 (SD = 0.120). The predicted representations also yielded interpretable terms associated with causal information in the reports. The results demonstrate the potential of probabilistic latent representation learning for AI-assisted extraction of causal information from aviation safety narratives while retaining expert investigation as the basis for formal causal determination.

</details>


### [242] [InferOpt: Constrained Multi-Objective Search for LLM Inference Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04473)
*Qi Chen,Yingying Cheng,Zhaoyi Sun,Li Zhou,Fan Zhang,Jie Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出InferOpt框架，将LLM推理配置（KV保留、MoE专家数等）建模为带约束的多目标黑盒优化问题，统一覆盖28维连续KV空间与26维离散MoE空间，并在两个空间上均优于Random Search、NSGA-II与MOTPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理服务需设置众多推理期参数（如逐层KV保留量、逐层专家数），而现有实践依赖机制特定的启发式方法，只能给出单一工作点，无法应对逐层搜索空间的可扩展性问题。

Method: 将推理配置重铸为带约束的多目标黑盒优化问题，构建可复用搜索框架InferOpt：仅需变量边界、确定性资源代价与评估钩子；在冻结的采样代理集上搜索，调用模型前剔除超预算候选，自适应收紧预算，并在全规模数据集上重新验证Pareto代表点。

Result: 一条流水线同时覆盖28维连续KV空间（Qwen2.5-7B）与26维离散MoE空间（DeepSeek-V2-Lite）。KV方面，prefill后剪枝使16K缓存减少64.4%、TPOT降低22.9–48.5%，搜索得到的逐层预算相比匹配的均匀预算在Full KV参考点上提升7.3%与14.0%；MoE方面，搜索得到的top-k调度剔除43.0%的token–expert对，与默认值差距在0.59个百分点内，优于匹配预算基线。

Conclusion: InferOpt在KV与MoE两个空间上均领先于Random Search、NSGA-II和MOTPE：KV上取得最佳代理超体积与最低保留率，MoE上在最低专家数下最接近未压缩参考。

Abstract: Serving an LLM means setting dozens of inference-time knobs, from per-layer KV retention to per-layer expert counts. Practice sets them with mechanism-specific heuristics that return a single operating point and do not scale to layer-wise search spaces. We recast inference configuration as constrained multi-objective black-box optimization and build InferOpt, a reusable search framework that requires only variable bounds, a deterministic resource cost, and an evaluation hook. InferOpt searches on a frozen sampled proxy set, rejects over-budget candidates before any model call, tightens the budget adaptively, and re-validates Pareto representatives on full-scale dataset. One pipeline covers a 28-dimensional continuous KV space (Qwen2.5-7B) and a 26-dimensional discrete MoE space (DeepSeek-V2-Lite). On KV, post-prefill pruning cuts the 16K cache by 64.4% and TPOT by 22.9--48.5%, and the searched layer-wise budget by InferOpt beats a matched uniform budget by 7.3% and 14.0% of the Full KV reference points. On MoE, a searched top-k schedule by InferOpt removes 43.0% of routed token--expert pairs while staying within 0.59 points of the default, closer than the matched-budget baselines. Against Random Search, NSGA-II, and MOTPE, InferOpt leads on both spaces, taking the best proxy hypervolume and the lowest retention on KV and staying closest to the uncompressed reference at the lowest experts on MoE.

</details>


### [243] [VCLMU: Mechanism-Centric Virtual Cell World Modeling for Perturbation Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04475)
*Yuwei Miao,Azim Dehghani Amirabad,Scott Oloff,Junzhou Huang,Tianyu Cui,Rui Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以潜在机制单元(LMU)为核心的虚拟细胞世界模型VCLMU，将基因扰动视为对潜在细胞状态的"动作"，通过两阶段预训练预测转录组响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测细胞对遗传扰动的响应是虚拟细胞的核心能力，也是计算建模生物干预的关键一步。现有模型大多直接将未扰动分子谱与扰动映射到观测结果，未显式刻画干预所诱导的潜在细胞状态转变，缺乏机制层面的可解释性。

Method: 提出机制中心(multi-mechanism)的虚拟细胞世界模型：将细胞状态表示为一组Latent Mechanism Units（LMU），每个LMU由基于多模态生物学证据的可复用身份(identity)与观测特异的state组成；遗传扰动被建模为对潜在状态的动作，先诱发机制特异的随机状态转移，再解码出转录响应。模型采用两阶段预训练：先在大约20万条伪bulk扰动谱上预训练，再在基因对齐的单细胞扰动数据上训练。

Result: 在六个扰动不相交(perturbation-disjoint)的基准上，VCLMU在扰动特异性响应恢复上持续优于强基线，同时保持有竞争力的全局响应精度；通过对扰动响应与LMU基因集之间的富集分析，显示LMU捕获了结构化、可解释的生物学响应程序。

Conclusion: 结果支持"机制层面的潜在状态转移"作为虚拟细胞建模的有效范式，能够同时实现对生物干预下细胞响应的预测与解释。

Abstract: Predicting cellular responses to genetic perturbations is a central capability for virtual cells and a key step toward computational modeling of biological interventions. Most existing models directly map an unperturbed molecular profile and perturba- tion to the resulting observation without explicitly representing the latent cellular transition induced by the intervention. We introduce a mechanism-centric virtual cell world model that represents cellular state as a set of Latent Mechanism Units (LMUs) and treats genetic perturbations as actions on these latent states. Each LMU combines a reusable identity grounded in multimodal biological evidence with an observation-specific state, allowing a perturbation to induce mechanism- specific stochastic transitions before decoding the resulting transcriptional response. We train VCLMU through two-stage pretraining, first on around 200K pseudo-bulk perturbation profiles and then on gene-aligned single-cell perturbation data. Across six perturbation-disjoint benchmarks, VCLMU consistently improves perturbation- specific response recovery over strong baselines while maintaining competitive global response accuracy. We further analyze learned LMUs through enrichment between perturbation responses and LMU gene sets and show that they capture structured biological response programs. These results support mechanism-level latent state transition as a useful formulation for virtual cell models that aim to predict and interpret cellular responses to biological interventions.

</details>


### [244] [ManifoldCache: Training-Free Diffusion Acceleration via Constraint Manifold Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04510)
*Prashant Pandey,Devineni Sri Venkatraya Chowdary,Brejesh Lall*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文将八类结构化科学扩散模型统一为约束流形扩散模型（CMDMs），并首次提出免训练、免数据的加速器 ManifoldCache，利用条件得分正交分解给出安全缓存边界与时序策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化科学生成（医学体数据、分子构象、蛋白骨架、晶体结构、多视图3D场景）需满足物理/化学/生物硬约束，扩散模型推理需数百至上千次神经函数评估，速度过慢。现有四类加速方法（量化、剪枝、快速ODE求解器、特征缓存）在该类问题上均失效：量化在高维体算子上内存耗尽，剪枝破坏约束保真度，快速ODE求解导致轨迹偏离约束流形，特征缓存对约束几何不敏感并在高噪声区引发模式混淆。

Method: 将目标分布支撑于外部约束映射定义的流形上，统一建模为CMDMs。核心洞察：条件得分可正交分解为法向分量（强制约束满足）与切向分量（在流形内导航）。据此证明噪声调度中点是尖锐的安全缓存边界——此前缓存误差有界，此后缓存则保证严格正比例的轨迹发生模式混淆；并证明在安全阶段内更深网络块可获得可证明更大的认证缓存步长（源于得分分解经块雅可比传播）。

Result: ManifoldCache 为免训练、免校准数据的加速器，零训练开销；给出安全缓存边界（噪声调度中点）与深层块更大缓存步长的理论保证；此前方法（量化、剪枝、快速ODE求解器、特征缓存）在CMDMs上均失败，本方法在安全阶段有可证明有界误差，越过边界则出现模式混淆。

Conclusion: 通过约束流形的得分正交分解，ManifoldCache 首次为CMDMs提供从第一性原理设计的免训练加速方案，在保证约束保真度与样本质量的同时降低推理成本，并揭示了缓存安全性与网络深度、噪声阶段的理论关系。

Abstract: Diffusion models for structured scientific generation must produce samples satisfying hard geometric constraints imposed by physics, chemistry, or biology, yet inference in these settings is prohibitively slow, demanding hundreds to thousands of neural-function evaluations per sample. We unify eight state-of-the-art models spanning medical volumetrics, molecular conformations, protein backbone design, crystal structure prediction, and multi-view 3D scenes under a single abstraction, Constraint-Manifold Diffusion Models (CMDMs), in which the target distribution is supported on a manifold defined by an externally specified constraint map. All existing acceleration families fail on this class: quantization exhausts memory on high-dimensional volumetric operators; pruning breaks constraint fidelity; fast ODE solvers allow trajectories to drift off the constraint manifold; and feature-caching heuristics are blind to constraint geometry, inducing mode confusion in the high-noise regime. We introduce ManifoldCache, the first training-free, data-free accelerator designed from first principles for CMDMs. The key insight is that the conditional score decomposes orthogonally into a normal component, which enforces constraint satisfaction, and a tangential component, which navigates within the manifold. Exploiting this structure, we prove that the noise-schedule midpoint is a sharp safe-caching boundary: caching before it incurs provably bounded error, while caching after it guarantees a strictly positive fraction of trajectories suffer mode confusion, a gap that persists up to the boundary. We further prove that deeper network blocks admit provably larger certified cache strides within the safe phase, as a consequence of the score decomposition propagating through block Jacobians. The resulting schedule requires no calibration data, along with zero training overhead.

</details>


### [245] [Towards Credible Agent-Based Policy Simulations: Disentangling Opportunities and Preferences in a Financial Inclusion Case Study of Egypt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04515)
*Alba Aguilera,Georgina Curto,Nardine Osman,Ahmed Al-Awah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于能力方法（Capability Approach）的通用建模框架，用于构建可信的政策模拟；并以埃及金融包容性为例，实现了一个基于异质性主体（个人与企业）的 agent-based model（ABM），通过两阶段拟合真实数据，区分制度/社会障碍与主体偏好的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向政策制定的 agent-based model 的可信性（credibility）至关重要：模拟不仅要复现目标场景及其核心动态，还要证明其假设、参数与输出具有经验依据，且在预期用途下足够准确。本文旨在应对这一挑战，提升政策模拟的可信性与实用性，并强化模型、真实目标系统与利益相关者之间的关系。

Method: 提出一个与能力方法（Capability Approach）对齐的通用建模框架，依赖数据与领域专家知识构建可信政策模拟；将其实例化用于研究埃及金融包容性社会问题。模型基于 agent-based model，表示异质性个人与企业，其行为依据金融状态、障碍、机会与偏好。模型分两阶段拟合真实数据：（1）初始化，构建具有代表性的合成人口；（2）校准，估计行为参数。通过固定决定主体机会的可行性参数（feasibility parameters），并跨不同人群组校准偏好参数（preference parameters），区分制度与社会障碍的作用，以及主体动机与优先级的作用。

Result: 该校准阶段提供了关于观察到的金融包容缺口驱动因素的透明且分组的假设；这些缺口可进一步被分析为主体的机会（opportunities）与已实现结果（realised outcomes）之间的差距，为政策制定提供高度相关的洞见。本文是提升政策模拟可信性与实用性的一步。

Conclusion: 通过能力方法对齐的建模框架与两阶段（初始化+校准）数据拟合流程，能够透明地区分制度/社会障碍与主体偏好对金融包容缺口的影响，从而增强模型可信性及其对政策制定的支持能力。代码已公开。

Abstract: Credibility is a central topic for agent-based models intended to support policy-making. Simulations must not only represent the target scenarios and their core dynamics but also demonstrate that their assumptions, parameters, and outputs are empirically grounded and sufficiently accurate for their intended use. This paper addresses this challenge by presenting a general modelling framework, aligned with the Capability Approach, for building credible policy simulations that rely on data and domain-expert knowledge. It then demonstrates how it can be contextualised and implemented to study the social challenge of financial inclusion in Egypt, building on an agent-based model that represents heterogeneous individuals and firms behaving according to their financial states, barriers, opportunities, and preferences. The model is fitted to real-world data in two stages, initialisation and calibration, which respectively build representative synthetic populations and estimate behavioural parameters. By fixing the feasibility parameters, which determine agents' opportunities, and calibrating preference parameters across different population groups, we are able to distinguish and analyse the role of institutional and social barriers in the system, as well as the role of agents' motivations and priorities. This calibration stage provides transparent and group-specific hypotheses about the drivers of observed financial-inclusion gaps, which can further be analysed as gaps between agents' opportunities and realised outcomes, a very relevant insight for policy-making. This paper is thus a step towards improving the credibility and usefulness of policy simulations, strengthening the relationship between the model, the real target system, and the stakeholders who will use it. The code is available at: \url{https://www.comses.net/codebase-release/df8383cb-f49b-4f09-8cce-73603b59adcc/}.

</details>


### [246] [EvoCast: Reliable Autonomous Research Agents for Iterative Forecasting Architecture Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04517)
*Kaipeng Xu,Xianli Yan,Yan Wang,Xiang Liu,Shan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoCast是一个全自主研究智能体系统，通过“认知-权限分离”设计实现时序预测架构的迭代演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度时序预测模型多样化，但适配具体任务仍需专家在模型选择、机制诊断、架构设计、实现与评估上大量投入；现有AutoML受限于预定义搜索空间，通用LLM研究智能体缺乏对实验协议与模型晋级流程的可靠控制。

Method: 建立并诊断任务特定基线（执行机制消融）；结合数据特征、诊断结果、历史轮次与失败记录生成有证据支撑的研究方向；核心为“认知-权限分离”：开放式假设生成与代码实现交由LLM智能体，源码编辑边界、规范化评估与晋级决策由确定性程序权限控制；实验结果累积为证据指导后续迭代。

Result: EvoCast能以更高实现成功率和更低的智能体侧token/时间开销完成复杂架构修改；在三个真实预测案例中，所得任务特定架构优于所选基线、强预测模型及智能体基线。

Conclusion: 通过认知与权限分离，EvoCast实现了可靠、低成本的自主架构演化，能够自动产出优于现有基线的任务特定时序预测架构。

Abstract: Deep time-series forecasting models have rapidly diversified, yet adapting them to a specific task still requires extensive expert effort in model selection, mechanism diagnosis, architecture design, implementation, and evaluation. Existing AutoML methods are constrained by predefined search spaces, while general-purpose LLM research agents lack reliable control over experimental protocols and model promotion. We introduce EvoCast, a fully autonomous research-agent system for iterative forecasting architecture evolution. EvoCast first establishes and diagnoses a task-specific baseline through executed mechanism ablations, then generates evidence-grounded research directions from dataset characteristics, diagnostic results, prior rounds, and failure records. Its central design, cognition-authority separation, assigns open-ended hypothesis generation and code implementation to LLM agents, while deterministic program authorities control source-edit boundaries, canonical evaluation, and promotion decisions. Experimental outcomes are accumulated as evidence to guide subsequent rounds. Results show that EvoCast completes complex architecture modifications with higher implementation success and lower agent-side token/time cost, and develops task-specific architectures that outperform selected baselines, strong forecasting models, and agent baselines in three real-world forecasting cases. The code is available at https://github.com/18e0-x/EvoCast.

</details>


### [247] [Action-Consequence Alignment for Reliable Planning and Self-Improving in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04539)
*Jinping Wang1,Zhiqiang Gao,Xiantong Zhen,Ling Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出低预测误差不等于可靠规划：世界模型会把与记录目标物理偏差更大的邻近动作赋予更低预测误差，形成"预测-规划错配"。作者提出动作后果对齐（ACA）训练目标，通过惩罚局部搜索替代动作相对真实动作的预测误差优势来弥补该缺口，实验证明ACA提升规划性能并降低真实目标误差，ACA引导的数据采集也优于随机局部采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受神经科学"自我挠痒"实验启发——干扰运动-感觉对应关系会增加预测不匹配——作者质疑潜世界模型是否保留了类似的"动作-后果对应"关系。核心动机在于：仅靠低预测误差无法保证可靠规划，需要检验并修正这种预测与规划之间的错配。

Method: 提出动作后果对齐（Action Consequence Alignment, ACA）训练目标：在前向预测之外，惩罚局部搜索得到的替代动作相对于真实动作的预测误差优势，不增加任何额外模型组件，训练期间也无需额外环境交互。同一原则还可用于指导自改进式的额外数据采集。

Result: 结果显示，邻近替代动作虽可取得更低预测误差，却产生与记录目标物理偏差更大的结果；若以该未来为目标，模型会给更不准确的动作更低代价。实验中，ACA在多样环境与评估设置下提升规划性能并降低真实目标误差；ACA引导的数据采集优于随机局部采样。

Conclusion: 动作后果对齐可作为连接预测学习与可靠规划的实用原则，有效弥合预测误差与规划目标之间的错配。

Abstract: Latent world models learn to predict observed transitions, yet low prediction error alone does not guarantee reliable planning. Inspired by self tickling experiments in neuroscience showing that disrupting motor sensory correspondence increases prediction mismatch, we examine whether learned world models preserve an analogous action consequence correspondence.The results show nearby alternatives can receive lower prediction errors despite producing physical outcomes farther from the recorded target. With that future treated as a goal, this reveals a concrete prediction planning mismatch: the model assigns a lower cost to an action that achieves the target less accurately. To mitigate this gap, we introduce Action Consequence Alignment (ACA), a training objective that complements forward prediction by penalizing the prediction error advantage of locally searched alternatives over factual actions without additional model components or environment interactions during training. The same principle can also guide additional data collection for self improvement. We demonstrate that across diverse environments and evaluation settings, ACA improves planning performance and reduces real goal error, while ACA guided data collection outperforms random local sampling. These results support action consequence alignment as a practical principle for bridging predictive learning and reliable planning.

</details>


### [248] [Autonomous Structuring of Radiology Reports Across Modalities at Archive Scale Using an Open-Weight Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04541)
*Friedrich Puttkammer,Fabian Drexel,Marlene Fritzsche,Era Stambollxhiu,Miriam Kumpf,Lena Schmitzer,Lea Schumann,Lina Xu,Johannes Moll,Jannik Lübberstedt,Zeineb Ben Chaaben,Anirudh Narayanan,Hartmut Häntze,Renato Cuocolo,Antonios Billis,Alexander Löser,Jawed Nawabi,Marcus R. Makowski,Cosmin I. Bercea,Shahrooz Faghihroohi,Lisa C. Adams,Keno K. Bressem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发并评估一个开源权重LLM流水线，可在无人工监督下将整个自由文本放射报告档案转为结构化报告，内容保真度高，多区域报告是模板错误的主要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射报告自由文本难以直接用于结构化分析与利用，需要一种无需人工干预、可规模化处理整个报告档案的结构化方案，并验证其准确性、质控与效率。

Method: 回顾性研究：在一个中心开发含150个分层模板的流水线，并在第二中心2010—2025年报告上测试。开源权重模型gpt-oss-120B经三步受约束解码选择模板，并在单个本地GPU上填充模板。以914份随机抽样报告（五种模态）的专家标签评估模板选择；由五名住院医师逐字段校正920份X线与CT报告评估结构化质量。随后处理第二中心完整档案。比例以Wilson 95%置信区间报告。

Result: 最优模板集匹配74.4%（680/914；95% CI 71.5%–77.1%），合适模板集82.3%（752/914；95% CI 79.7%–84.6%），单区域报告达87.7%、多区域报告54.1%。输出与校正参考的宏观语义文本相似度：X线0.95、CT 0.97；住院医师对24,638个字段中88.7%未修改；无支持内容在1.0%和1.5%报告中被标记。完整档案2,186,982份报告中96.5%获得结构化输出，共2,401,544份结构化报告，单GPU速度1,258份/小时。

Conclusion: 开源权重LLM流水线可在无人工监督下以高内容保真度结构化完整的全模态报告档案；多区域报告仍是模板错误的主要来源。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate an open-weight large language model (LLM) pipeline that converts an entire archive of free-text radiology reports into structured reports without human oversight. Materials and Methods: In this retrospective study, a pipeline with 150 hierarchically organized templates was developed at one center and tested at a second center on reports from 2010 to 2025. The open-weight model gpt-oss-120B selects the template in three constrained-decoding steps and fills it on one local graphics processing unit. Template selection was scored against expert labels on 914 randomly sampled reports of five modalities, structuring quality on 920 radiography and CT reports corrected field by field by five residents. The pipeline then processed the complete archive of the second center. Proportions are reported with Wilson 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Results: An optimal template set was selected for 74.4% of reports (680 of 914; 95% CI: 71.5%, 77.1%) and an appropriate set for 82.3% (752 of 914; 95% CI: 79.7%, 84.6%), 87.7% for single-region and 54.1% for multi-region reports. Macro semantic textual similarity between output and corrected reference was 0.95 for radiography and 0.97 for CT, residents left 88.7% of 24,638 fields unchanged, and unsupported content was flagged in 1.0% and 1.5% of reports. Of 2,186,982 archive reports, 96.5% received structured output, 2,401,544 structured reports, at 1,258 reports per hour on one graphics processing unit. Conclusion: An open-weight LLM pipeline structured a complete multimodality report archive without human oversight with high content fidelity. Multi-region reports remained the main source of template errors.

</details>


### [249] [Decide, Ask, or Defer: Clinical LLMs under Incomplete Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04542)
*Mingzhan Yang,Weili Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DECIDE/ASK/DEFER 三分类框架及盲化评测协议，在 200 个匹配临床证据状态上评测 Qwen、Gemini、GPT，发现模型在相同证据下动作选择差异显著，且分离"信息获取"与"临床转诊"可揭示二元弃权所掩盖的行为，但并未带来一致更优的决策策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床大模型不仅需给出诊断，还需判断现有证据是否足以支持自主决策。现有二元 DECIDE/ABSTAIN 范式将不同的非决策状态混为一谈，且未显式评估信息获取行为，掩盖了模型在"提问"与"转诊"上的差异。

Method: 提出 DECIDE/ASK/DEFER 三分类表述，并设计盲化协议，防止模型利用证据完整性元数据。基于 DDXPlus 构建 200 个匹配的临床证据状态，评测 Qwen、Gemini 与 GPT；对 Qwen 进一步开展匹配的目标信息 vs 随机信息分析，并与其匹配的 DECIDE/ABSTAIN 基线对比。

Result: 相同证据下模型动作选择差异显著，200 个状态中有 137 个出现分歧。对 Qwen，目标信息相比随机信息更能改变后续自主决策的可能性，而非诊断正确性。与其匹配的 DECIDE/ABSTAIN 基线相比，三分类策略呈现安全性—自主性权衡：挽救部分错误的自主决策，但也移除了部分正确的自主决策。

Conclusion: 将信息获取与临床转诊分离，能够暴露二元弃权所隐藏的行为模式，但并未产生一致改进的决策策略，说明临床 LLM 的自主性设计需在该权衡下谨慎取舍。

Abstract: Clinical LLMs must decide not only what diagnosis to produce, but also whether the available evidence is sufficient for autonomous decision making. Binary DECIDE/ABSTAIN formulations merge distinct non decision states and do not explicitly evaluate information acquisition. We introduce a DECIDE/ASK/DEFER formulation together with a blinded protocol that prevents models from using evidence completeness metadata. We evaluate Qwen, Gemini, and GPT on 200 matched clinical evidence states constructed from DDXPlus. The models show substantial differences in action selection under identical evidence, with disagreement in 137 of 200 states. For Qwen, a matched targeted versus random analysis shows that selected information changes the likelihood of a subsequent autonomous decision more clearly than diagnostic correctness. Its matched DECIDE/ABSTAIN baseline further reveals a safety autonomy tradeoff: the three action policy rescues some erroneous autonomous decisions but also removes some correct autonomous deci sions. These results show that separating information acquisition from clinician deferral exposes behavior that binary abstention hides, without yielding a uniformly improved decision policy.

</details>


### [250] [Label Agreement Does Not Measure Authorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04544)
*Amir Sabbaghziarani,Bradley Thomas Baker,Theodore J. LaGrow,Sergey Plis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文构建并审计了一个用于标签本体与元数据协调的智能体LLM流水线，发现总体指标健康却掩盖了授权、完整性和证据敏感性三类失败；作者提出将三者分开度量并配以对照，将承诺门控于结果，使失败可见且可路由给人工处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体LLM流水线普遍以标签一致性（label agreement）作为核心评估指标，但该指标仅衡量提议标签是否匹配参考标签，无法反映智能体是否有权提出该标签、输出是否完整可执行、标签是否随证据变化而改变。团队在实践中发现聚合分数正常但流水线持续失败，因此希望揭示这些被掩盖的失败模式。

Method: 在COBRE与FBIRN两个来自不同联盟的精神分裂症与对照神经影像队列上，将三类属性分开测量：(1)授权性（agent是否有资格提议该标签）；(2)输出完整性（如JSON解析成功但仍缺必填字段）；(3)证据敏感性（改变决定性证据后标签是否随之变化）。每项度量均配以对照条件，例如向智能体展示上游提议、交换行顺序等，并将最终承诺门控于度量结果。

Result: 三类度量彼此分离，且均与标签一致性分离：展示上游提议后标签一致性仅从0.857微升至0.870，而所选动作的一致性从0.409翻倍至0.830；可解析为JSON的输出在一个模型10%、另一个模型33%的案例中仍缺失必填字段；重放首个答案的智能体在原案例上得分完美，但在改变决定性证据后得分为零；下游一个未被任何指标报告的行序错误抹去了大部分诊断信号。

Conclusion: 应分别度量授权性、输出完整性与证据敏感性，并为每项配置对照，将承诺门控于度量结果，从而使失败可见并易于路由给人工。该方法并不能防止失败；此外参考标签由规则推导，与之一致仅代表一致性而非正确性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Many groups now delegate label ontology and metadata harmonization to agentic LLM pipelines. We built one and audited it. Our aggregate scores looked healthy, but the pipeline kept failing in ways they did not show, so we set out to find what they hid. Label agreement asks whether a proposed label matches a reference. It does not ask whether the agent was entitled to propose it, whether the output was complete enough to act on, or whether the label moved when the evidence moved. We measured those three separately on COBRE and FBIRN, two schizophrenia and control neuroimaging cohorts from different consortia, and they come apart, from label agreement and from each other. Showing the agent an upstream proposal barely moves label agreement, 0.857 to 0.870, while agreement on the chosen action doubles, 0.409 to 0.830. Output that parses as JSON still drops a required field on 10% of one model's cases and 33% of the other's. And an agent that replays its first answer scores perfectly on original cases and zero once we change the evidence that decides them. Downstream, a row-order error that none of these metrics reports erases most of the diagnostic signal. So we measure these properties apart, pair each with a control, and gate commitment on the result, which makes failures visible and easy to route to a person. None of this prevents failure. Our reference labels are rule-derived, so agreement with them means consistency, not correctness. Code is available at https://github.com/amir-sbg/Label-Agreement-Does-Not-Measure-Authorization.

</details>


### [251] [$\mathrm{TRIZ}^{a}$: Guiding Agent Evolution from Pattern Recognition to Solution Invention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04555)
*Wenyin Liu,Yiheng Huang,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRIZ^a研发自动化范式，将TRIZ发明理论与LLM智能体进化搜索结合，在网络安全数据集上取得F1提升但未通过冻结晋升门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机或无类型变异缺乏结构化、可解释的解生成方向，需要将TRIZ发明理论的理论化构思与LLM驱动的智能体进化搜索结合，实现可解释、可追溯的研发自动化。

Method: TRIZ^a整合TRIZ 40条发明原理与矛盾矩阵，提供结构化可解释的解生成方向；以冻结参考契约下的功能信息（FI）结合TRIZ理想度，在可通约的信息尺度上度量有用与有害功能；用硬门将晋升与指标改进分离。

Result: 在PowerDuck GOOSE、CICIoT2023、CIC-DDoS2019上前期实验绝对F1提升分别为+2.88、+4.23、+0.15个百分点；完成的45项CICIoT2023战役将macro-F1从0.8325提至0.8483，但未通过冻结晋升门。

Conclusion: TRIZ^a在对抗性快速演化的网络安全域验证可行，所有结果从矛盾识别、TRIZ原理选择到代码变换、评估指标与晋升决策均保持可追溯，但指标提升不等同于晋升通过。

Abstract: We propose $\mathrm{TRIZ}^{a}$ (TRIZ exponentiated by an agent), a general R\&D automation paradigm that combines TRIZ inventive theory with LLM-driven agent evolutionary search. TRIZ's 40 inventive principles and contradiction matrix provide structured, explainable directions for solution generation, replacing random or untyped mutation with theory-guided ideation. Functional information (FI), operationalized under a frozen reference contract, is combined with TRIZ Ideality to measure useful and harmful function on a commensurable information scale, while hard gates keep promotion distinct from metric improvement. We validate $\mathrm{TRIZ}^{a}$ in cybersecurity--an adversarial and rapidly evolving domain--on PowerDuck GOOSE, CICIoT2023, and CIC-DDoS2019. Under paired-rerun protocols with protocol fingerprinting and hard-gate validation, the legacy experiments yield absolute F1 improvements of $+2.88$, $+4.23$, and $+0.15$ percentage points, respectively. A completed 45-activity CICIoT2023 campaign further increases macro-F1 from $0.8325$ to $0.8483$, but does not pass its frozen promotion gate. Every result remains traceable from contradiction identification and TRIZ principle selection to code transformation, evaluation metrics, and promotion decision.

</details>


### [252] [Recursive Improvement of a Differentiable Scientific Software Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04561)
*Pengcheng Hou,Xiaojun Tan,Sihan Hu,Ruisi Wang,Shuo Chen,Lei Wang,Youjin Deng,Kun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向可微科学软件的智能体驱动演化环境，统一微分接口+需求驱动开发+独立质量评估，跨20个物理化学生物建模包验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微编程将科学计算与基于梯度的推断、学习与设计相连接，但跨异构软件生态扩展需大量实现导数、对接接口与质量评估的专门工作；AI编码智能体可加速这一转化，但需识别科研需求并评估实现是否满足需求。

Method: 构建智能体驱动演化的可微科学软件环境，贯通需求识别、开发与质量评估：统一微分接口暴露可复用导数规则并与既有数值例程并列；科研需求引导开发；通过独立导数校验、工作流测试与性能评估验收；已验证软件、科研程序与测试成为后续研究的共享资源。

Result: 跨20个涵盖物理、化学、生物建模的包构建并验证自动微分扩展；研究工作流展示跨任务复用；基准显示梯度评估与完整参数估计相较有限差分具计算节省；科研驱动修订使原不支持的工作流可微、修正科学可观测量导数并消除冗余计算；量子控制与热设计研究依据物理评估修正目标，在复用既有导数的同时改进设计。

Conclusion: 为在既有科学软件中扩展可微编程、并围绕共享计算生态的递归改进来组织AI智能体提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Differentiable programming connects scientific computation with gradient-based inference, learning and design. Extending these capabilities across a heterogeneous software ecosystem requires specialized effort to implement derivatives, integrate interfaces and evaluate quality. AI coding agents can accelerate this transformation, but translating their capabilities into useful scientific software requires identifying research needs and evaluating how well implementations meet them. We present an environment for agent-driven evolution of differentiable scientific software that connects demand identification, development and quality evaluation. A unified differentiation interface exposes reusable derivative rules alongside existing numerical routines, allowing research tasks to share these capabilities. Research requirements guide development, with implementations assessed through independent derivative checks, workflow tests and performance evaluation. Validated software, research programs and tests become shared resources for subsequent studies. We construct and validate automatic differentiation extensions across 20 packages spanning physical, chemical and biological modeling, with research workflows demonstrating reuse across tasks. Benchmarks demonstrate computational savings over finite differences in gradient evaluation and complete parameter estimation. Research-driven revisions make previously unsupported workflows differentiable, correct derivatives of scientific observables and eliminate redundant computation. Quantum-control and thermal-design studies revise objectives in response to physical evaluation, improving designs while reusing existing derivatives. This work provides a practical approach to expanding differentiable programming across established scientific software and organizing AI agents around the recursive improvement of a shared computational ecosystem.

</details>


### [253] [Strong Helps Weak: Directional Cross-Modal Alignment Transfer in Multi-modal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04580)
*Hoigi Seo,Byung Hyun Lee,Minjun Kim,Dohyun Mah,Jongho Lee,Se Young Chun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了跨模态模型合并中的非对称现象：将数据丰富的源模态MLLM合并进数据稀缺的目标模态MLLM可显著提升后者在其自身基准上的表现，并据此提出DCAT框架，通过闭式权重空间解实现跨模态对齐迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型提升某一模态能力通常需大量模态专属数据训练，成本高昂；而模型合并对数据稀缺、单样本体积大或领域特定的模态（如音频、视频）往往不可行，因缺乏同模态模型变体。

Method: 作者刻画了跨模态合并的非对称现象，并通过理论与实证分析表明增益源于更强的供体模态增强了模态专属token与文本token间的对齐。据此推导出与对齐量单调相关、且与下游性能强相关的互信息下界，提出方向性跨模态对齐迁移（DCAT）框架，将强源（供体）模态的文本对齐迁移至弱目标（接收者）模态，并给出仅需小规模校准集即可计算的闭式权重空间解，无需进一步微调。

Result: DCAT在目标模态性能上优于现有模型合并方法，无需额外微调即可提升目标模态表现。

Conclusion: 跨模态对齐可从强模态向弱模态定向迁移，且该对齐增强目标具备闭式权重解；DCAT为跨模态对齐迁移提供了一条高效路径。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong modality understanding by pairing a large language model (LLM) with an encoder for a target modality such as vision, video, or audio. However, improving an MLLM's capability for a given modality typically requires additional training on large modality-specific datasets, incurring substantial data collection and compute costs. Model merging offers an alternative, but it is often infeasible for data-scarce, large per-sample size, or domain-specific modalities (\textit{e.g.}, audio and video), where same-modality model variants are rarely available. In this work, we characterize an intriguing asymmetric phenomenon: merging a well-aligned, data-rich source-modality MLLM into a data-scarce target-modality MLLM substantially improves the target on its own benchmarks. Our theoretical and empirical analyses show that this gain stems from enhanced alignment between modality-specific and textual tokens, induced by the stronger donor modality. Specifically, we derive a mutual-information lower bound that is monotonic in alignment-related quantities and strongly correlated with downstream MLLM performance. Building on this principle, we propose Directional Cross-modal Alignment Transfer (DCAT), a novel framework that transfers textual alignment from a strong, well-aligned source (donor) modality to a weak target (recipient) modality, boosting target-modality performance without further fine-tuning. We further show that the alignment-enhancing objective admits a closed-form weight-space solution computed from only a small calibration set. DCAT outperforms existing model-merging methods, offering an efficient path toward cross-modal alignment transfer. Project page with code is available at \url{https://seohoiki3215.github.io/DCAT_project_page}

</details>


### [254] [Bounds, Decompositions and Null Behaviour of KRATOS: A Mathematical Specification of a Recognition-Comparability Diagnostic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04592)
*Maria Dolores Gonzalez,Alberto Barbado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对KRATOS这一群组结构型文献计量诊断工具给出完整数学规范，并推导其解释性质，包括识别对齐得分、参与—识别因子的组成上限与Fréchet型界、对数分解、不变性及置换零模型下矩等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KRATOS通过比较固定分析群组上文档分布（参与）与引用权重分布（识别）进行诊断，但缺乏完整数学规范与解释性质，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 数学推导：给出各分量界与等号条件；证明识别对齐得分仅通过群组平均引用率与语料均值的比率依赖引用数据；推导参与—识别因子组成上限为到均匀参考的总变差距离的仿射函数；建立基于该上限与参与加权识别得分的Fréchet型界；给出复合指数的精确对数分解、主识别得分与倒数对称识别得分的序关系、不变性，以及全局与分层置换零模型下识别比率的闭式一阶和二阶矩；并用脚本数值验证。

Result: 识别对齐得分依赖引用数据的比率形式；组成上限是总变差距离的仿射函数；因子满足Fréchet型界；得到复合指数的精确对数分解；确立两类识别得分的序关系与不变性；给出置换零模型下识别比率的闭式一阶、二阶矩；所有结果经数值验证。

Conclusion: 这些结果解释了复合排序为何对小群组、重尾引用分布和元数据重分配敏感。KRATOS规范仅涉及测量结构，不定义认知变化或正义的度量。

Abstract: KRATOS is a group-structured bibliometric diagnostic that compares the distribution of documents (participation) with the distribution of citation weight (recognition) over a fixed, finite universe of analytical groups. This note gives its complete mathematical specification and derives the properties that govern its interpretation. Beyond bounds and equality conditions for each component, we show that the recognition-alignment score depends on citation data only through ratios of group mean citation rates to the corpus mean; that the composition ceiling of the participation--recognition factor is an affine function of the total variation distance to the uniform reference; and that the factor itself satisfies Fréchet-type bounds in terms of this ceiling and a participation-weighted recognition score. We establish an exact logarithmic decomposition of the composite index, an ordering between the primary and a reciprocal-symmetric recognition score, invariance properties, and closed-form first and second moments of the recognition ratios under global and stratified permutation nulls. These results explain why composite orderings can be sensitive to small groups, heavy-tailed citation distributions and metadata reassignment. All results are verified numerically with an accompanying script. The specification concerns measurement structure only; it does not define a measure of epistemic change or justice.

</details>


### [255] [LatentIndex: Cross-Layer Sharing with Layer-Specific Selection for Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04635)
*Zhaohui Wang,Zhixin Pan,Fanxu Meng,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LatentIndex 将 MLA 的潜在共享思想扩展到稀疏注意力 indexer 层之间：每个层组从首层隐状态构建共享潜在缓存，各层仍独立打分选 token，从而在减少 indexer 缓存与重复选择开销的同时保持层内独立选择能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力虽降低了核心注意力计算，但其 indexer 仍带来重复的 token 选择工作和逐层 key 缓存存储。跨层复用选中的索引可减少开销，但会让多层被迫使用同一 token 集合，限制各层选择灵活性。

Method: 提出 LatentIndex，将 MLA 的潜在共享原则扩展到 indexer 层。每个层组用首层隐状态构建共享潜在缓存，层特定打分实现独立 token 选择；把 key 解码器吸收进 query，直接对共享缓存打分而无需重建历史逐层 key。提供免训练校准，并探索训练感知实现；另设层级选择（HS）变体，由 anchor 提出共享候选集、followers 独立精化，以平衡质量与计算。

Result: 四层共享下，LatentIndex 在 DeepSeek-V3.2 上将逻辑 indexer 缓存存储减少 61.1%。在 DeepSeek-V3.2 和 GLM-5 上，免训练 LatentIndex 相比 IndexCache 将 head-wise attention-mass recall 最高提升 3.28 个百分点，同时 RULER 和 LongBench 表现接近原生 DSA。HS 在 8K–128K 上下文上实现 2.30–2.72 倍于 DSA 的解码 indexer 加速，并保留大部分 recall。

Conclusion: LatentIndex 为跨层索引提供了新视角：共享连续表示而非离散选择，可实现高效复用，同时保留层特定的 token 选择。

Abstract: Sparse attention reduces core-attention computation, but its indexers still incur repeated selection work and per-layer key-cache storage. Reusing selected indices across layers reduces this overhead but constrains multiple layers to the same token set. We introduce LatentIndex, which extends the latent-sharing principle of Multi-head Latent Attention across indexer layers. Each layer group constructs a shared latent cache from its first layer's hidden states, while layer-specific scoring enables independent token selection. Absorbing key decoders into queries enables direct scoring of the shared cache without reconstructing historical per-layer keys. We develop training-free calibration and investigate a training-aware instantiation of this principle. To balance quality and computation, a hierarchical selection (HS) variant lets followers independently refine a shared candidate set proposed by the anchor. With four-layer sharing, LatentIndex reduces logical indexer-cache storage by 61.1% on DeepSeek-V3.2. Across DeepSeek-V3.2 and GLM-5, training-free LatentIndex improves head-wise attention-mass recall over IndexCache by up to 3.28 percentage points while maintaining RULER and LongBench performance close to native DSA. HS further achieves 2.30-2.72 times decode indexer speedups over DSA across 8K-128K contexts, retaining most of LatentIndex's recall. LatentIndex offers a new perspective on cross-layer indexing: sharing continuous representations rather than discrete selections enables efficient reuse while preserving layer-specific token selection.

</details>


### [256] [SEIS: Self-Evolving Inference Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04646)
*Zhen Xu,Jingyu Liu,Zongze Li,Tahseen Rabbani,Ce Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SEIS（自进化推理系统），用智能体自进化方法端到端优化整个推理引擎，在 H100 上服务 Qwen3-0.6B 时吞吐量达原始 mini-sglang 的 3.27 倍，并击败 vLLM、TensorRT-LLM、SGLang 等 SOTA 引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理系统的速度直接决定语言模型的服务成本与效率，具有重要实用价值。但以往工作多聚焦于系统内局部组件（如 kernel、内存）的优化，缺乏对整个系统端到端的整体优化视角。

Method: 提出 SEIS（Self-Evolving Inference Systems），采用智能体自进化、无人工干预的方式，通过迭代会话继承历史经验并进行代码修改，自动优化整个 mini-sglang 引擎；同时从数值差异与下游任务精度两方面验证优化后引擎的正确性。

Result: 在 H100 上服务 Qwen3-0.6B，优化后引擎吞吐量达原始 mini-sglang 的 3.27 倍，并在单请求负载下超越 vLLM、TensorRT-LLM、SGLang 等 SOTA 引擎；代码与会话历史表明加速源于对整个引擎的重设计，且继承早期会话优于独立尝试。

Conclusion: 智能体自进化能够对复杂系统进行端到端优化。由于评估也需随引擎演化，让智能体自动进化评估方法是有前景的下一步。

Abstract: Inference systems determine how fast and how cheaply language models can be served, so making them faster has direct practical value. However, prior work focuses mostly on optimizing certain parts such as kernels or memory within the large system. In this work, we take a holistic approach and apply agentic self-evolution to optimize the whole system end-to-end. Our SEIS (Self-Evolving Inference Systems) autonomously optimizes the entire mini-sglang engine without human intervention through iterative sessions with inherited experiences and code changes. Serving Qwen3-0.6B on H100, the resulting engine reaches 3.27X the throughput of the original mini-sglang implementation and beats SOTA engines like vLLM, TensorRT-LLM, and SGLang in the single-request workload. The correctness of the optimized inference engine by SEIS is tested in terms of numerical difference and downstream accuracy on math and long-context retrieval tasks. The code and session histories show that the speedup comes from redesigning the whole engine and that building on earlier sessions beats independent attempts. These results suggest that agentic self-evolution can optimize a complex system end-to-end. The evaluation also has to evolve with the engine, and letting agents evolve it is a natural next step.

</details>


### [257] [PermVLA: Factorization Order as a Regularizer for VLA Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04659)
*Yanqiao Chen,Yuhan Rui,Dongsheng Hou,Zijie Nie,Yutong Wan,Qi Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文识别出VLA策略中动作块建模的分解顺序是一种被忽视的正则化选择，提出因果锚定置换（CAP），通过从同一专家动作块采样不同揭示顺序、构造条件集增强的辅助目标，在不增加演示数据的情况下提升策略泛化能力，并在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus及跨数据集CALVIN上取得一致增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略通常以固定的从左到右（LTR）分解方式学习动作块，但同一专家轨迹分布可支持多种合法的链式法则分解。现有做法只依赖单一时间顺序前缀进行teacher forcing，容易过度依赖该固定分解，作者认为分解顺序是一种被忽视的正则化手段。

Method: 提出因果锚定置换（CAP）：在保持可调时间前缀的前提下采样动作揭示顺序，用辅助目标训练同一共享策略，使其能从同一专家块的已知子集预测其余动作，部署时仍采用确定性LTR控制。该机制称为条件集增强，即从单个专家块生成多个条件预测问题而不增加演示。另有诊断指标：衡量同一块在两种揭示顺序下联合对数似然的期望平方差。

Result: 受控实验显示CAP在LIBERO与LIBERO-Plus上持续优于标准LTR训练，跨数据集CALVIN评估中亦呈现相同优势；诊断指标验证CAP训练使策略在不同揭示顺序间内化了一致性。作者还将该思路扩展到扩散动作生成器。

Conclusion: 采样得到的子集条件辅助目标可作为构建VLA正则化器的一般配方，分解顺序正则化（如CAP）能有效缓解固定LTR分解的偏差，且可推广至扩散式动作生成等更广泛框架。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies commonly learn action chunks through a fixed left-to-right (LTR) factorization, although the same expert trajectory distribution admits many valid chain-rule factorizations. We identify factorization order as an overlooked regularization choice and introduce causally anchored permutation (CAP), which samples action reveal orders with a tunable chronological prefix. Its auxiliary objective trains one shared policy to predict actions from different known subsets of the same expert chunk, while deployment retains deterministic LTR control. We call this conditional-set augmentation: it creates multiple conditional prediction problems from one expert chunk without adding demonstrations. This discourages reliance on the single chronological prefix used by ordinary teacher forcing. Controlled experiments show that CAP consistently outperforms standard LTR training on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus, with the same advantage appearing in cross-dataset CALVIN evaluation. A diagnostic that measures the expected squared difference between a chunk's joint log likelihood under two reveal orders verifies that CAP training internalizes agreement across reveal orders. These findings position sampled subset-conditioned auxiliary objectives as a general recipe for constructing VLA regularizers, illustrated by an extension to diffusion action generators.

</details>


### [258] [Efficient Neural Surrogates for Linear Radiation Transport on the Lattice and Hohlraum benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04665)
*Carmelo Gonzales,Steffen Schotthöfer,Cory D. Hauck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文对两种参数匹配的神经代理架构（Transolver 与 BSMS-MGN）在二维线性辐射输运方程标准基准上进行了端到端基准测试，并通过消融揭示归纳偏置偏好高度依赖架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性辐射输运方程（RTE）是高维相空间仿真，工程精度下计算成本高昂，导致设计优化、不确定性量化、参数扫描等外层工作流受预算限制。神经代理可通过摊销仿真成本缓解瓶颈，但使代理在某一输运问题上准确的架构选择与工程归纳偏置难以跨模型族迁移。

Method: 基准测试两种参数匹配的神经代理架构：物理注意力模型 Transolver 与多尺度图网络 Bi-Stride Multi-Scale MeshGraphNet（BSMS-MGN），作为二维线性 RTE 最终时刻粒子浓度的端到端近似；在经典 Lattice 与 Hohlraum 基准上评估；并针对傅里叶特征与区域加权训练损失进行消融实验。

Result: 消融实验显示，傅里叶特征与区域加权损失的偏好强烈依赖架构，表明物理信息代理工作流中的常见设计选择需按架构重新审视，而不能跨模型族直接套用；下游效用取决于逐 QoI（量级）敏感性，而非单一全场级评分。

Conclusion: 神经代理的归纳偏置与设计选择需针对具体架构定制，评估应以逐 QoI 敏感性为准。作者公开训练配方、训练数据与评估管线，以支持复现、向相关输运问题迁移，以及在更大外层仿真工作流中作为摊销前向模型组件进行评估。

Abstract: Linear radiation transport equations (RTEs) form the simulation foundations underpinning design and analysis tasks in nuclear engineering, inertial confinement fusion, medical imaging, and astrophysics, but resolving the high-dimensional phase space at engineering fidelity remains expensive enough that outer-loop workflows, such as design optimization, uncertainty quantification, and parameter sweeps, are routinely budget-bound on traditional solvers. Neural surrogates promise to relax this bottleneck by amortizing simulation cost across thousands of downstream queries, but the architectural choices and engineered inductive biases that make a surrogate accurate on one transport problem do not transfer straightforwardly across model families. We benchmark two parameter-matched neural surrogate architectures, the physics-attention Transolver and the multi-scale graph network Bi-Stride Multi-Scale MeshGraphNet (BSMS-MGN), as end-to-end approximations of the final-time particle concentration for the two-dimensional linear RTE on the canonical Lattice and Hohlraum benchmarks. An ablation across Fourier features and region-weighted training loss exposes strongly architecture-dependent inductive-bias preferences, indicating that design choices common to physics-informed surrogate workflows must be revisited per architecture rather than imported across model families, and that downstream utility depends on per-QoI sensitivity rather than a single field-level score. The model training recipe, training data, and evaluation pipeline are released alongside this paper to support reproduction, transfer to related transport problems, and evaluation as amortized forward-model components in larger outer-loop simulation workflows.

</details>


### [259] [A Tropical Geometry View of Forgetting: A Per-Unit Projector for Knowledge-Preserving Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04670)
*Yuyang Zhang,Xiaoyin Chen,Chunlin Ren,Qihuang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文用ReLU层的热带几何刻画微调中的灾难性遗忘，提出门控感知的逐单元投影器，实现无回放且更省预算的持续学习保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调语言模型会破坏其原有能力（灾难性遗忘）。现有无回放投影方法（如Adam-NSCL、GPM）在每一行更新中禁用层的同一共享输入子空间，忽略了ReLU单元的门控（开/闭）结构，约束过粗。

Method: 用热带几何分析ReLU层：数据空间中单元边界是热带超曲面，其胞腔与zonotope上顶点对偶；权重空间中每个旧token是一个超平面，token切出多面体（保持各token同侧的权重闭包）。给出精确恒等式：层输出平方变化分解为胞内项、开-闭项与闭-开项，前两项仅依赖各单元激发的token。由此构造门控感知的逐单元投影器，并给出预算分离定理以量化精确保护代价。

Result: 预算分离定理表明：精确保护单单元需付出其自身开放token秩的代价，而共享子空间每行至少付出这些token并集的秩。OPT-1.3b上96%的(token,unit)对为闭；在9-60个约束方向/行的六个匹配预算下，遗忘均少于Adam-NSCL，差距从1.1倍扩大到4.3倍；以1/5.5的方向数在GPM能量阈值下将Adam-NSCL遗忘减半。OPT-6.7b上匹配预算下遗忘相当而学习更多。开/闭划分是关键变量，开放token在18/18个种子对上优于随机、符号盲与反门控token集。在剪枝修复中，基于导数的局部误差模型对“开启”对完全盲：门控加权目标的最优解可离开多面体，其闭式解在OPT-1.3b上比不修复差1.94 nats，凸单边惩罚可约束逃逸。

Conclusion: 开/闭token划分是决定投影保护效果的核心变量；门控感知的逐单元投影器在更少约束预算下优于共享子空间方法，并能诊断剪枝修复中导数型局部模型的失效，凸单边惩罚可限制其逃逸。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a language model on new text degrades what it already does. Replay-free projectors such as Adam-NSCL and GPM forbid one shared subspace of a layer's inputs in every row of the update. The tropical geometry of a ReLU layer shows why this is too coarse. In data space, the units' walls are tropical hypersurfaces whose cells are dual to the upper vertices of a zonotope; in weight space, each old token is a hyperplane, and the tokens cut out a polyhedron, the closure of the weights that keep every token on its side. An exact identity joins the two pictures: the squared change of the layer's output under any weight change splits into in-cell, open-to-closed and closed-to-open terms, and the first two live on the tokens each unit fires on. The identity names a gate-aware per-unit projector, and a budget-separation theorem prices exact protection: it costs a unit the rank of its own open tokens, while a shared subspace pays at least the rank of their union in every row. On OPT-1.3b, where 96% of (token, unit) pairs are closed, the projector forgets less than Adam-NSCL at all six matched budgets from 9 to 60 constrained directions per row, the gap widening from $1.1\times$ to $4.3\times$; with 1/5.5 of the directions it halves the forgetting of Adam-NSCL at GPM's energy threshold. On OPT-6.7b, it matches Adam-NSCL's forgetting at matched budget while learning more. As the theory predicts, the open/closed partition is the operative variable: open tokens beat random, sign-blind and anti-gate token sets on 18 of 18 seed-pairs. In pruning repair, every derivative-based local model of the output error at the dense weights is blind to pairs that open: the minimisers of the gate-weighted objective can leave the polyhedron, the objective's closed-form solution is 1.94 nats worse than no repair on OPT-1.3b, and a convex one-sided penalty bounds the escape.

</details>


### [260] [MASBench: Benchmarking LLM-based Multi-Agent Collaboration under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04672)
*Qizhi Chu,Zekai Yu,Sijie Wen,Yang Liu,Chen Qian,Cheng Yang,Chuan Shi,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MASBench——一个面向部分可观测约束的多智能体协作基准，通过推理、调度、博弈三类渐进任务，系统评估协议、记忆、路由三种协作机制，并提供性能分数、通信成本与成本效益等确定性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统效能不仅取决于单个智能体，还取决于协作机制的设计与组织；而真实协作通常为部分可观测，现有基准多假设全局可观测，缺乏对协作机制的系统评估支持。

Method: 构建MASBench基准，在部分可观测约束下组织三类渐进任务（Reasoning、Scheduling、Game），对应评估三种代表性协作机制（Protocol、Memory、Routing），并设计确定性评价指标（性能分数、通信成本、成本效益）。

Result: 在多种LLM骨干模型与机制配置下开展实验，刻画协作效果与通信开销，为有效MAS设计提供经验性指导；代码已开源。

Conclusion: MASBench填补了部分可观测条件下多智能体协作机制系统评估的空白，可作为MAS设计的实证参考与基准工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have progressively evolved into the core of autonomous agents. Building on this progress, LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) coordinate multiple agents into a synergistic team to accomplish complex tasks that exceed the capabilities of individual agents. The effectiveness of such systems depends not only on the agents themselves, but also on how collaboration mechanisms are designed and organized. Note that real-world collaboration is typically partially observable, where each agent can only access partial information about the environment due to physical or privacy-related constraints. However, many existing multi-agent benchmarks assume global observability, and leave limited support for systematically evaluating collaboration mechanisms. To bridge this gap, we introduce MASBench, a multi-agent collaboration benchmark designed under partially observable constraints. It is organized into three progressive task categories: Reasoning, Scheduling, and Game. Through this structure, we progressively evaluate three representative collaboration mechanisms: Protocol, Memory, and Routing. MASBench further provides deterministic evaluation metrics, including performance score, communication cost, and cost effectiveness, to characterize both collaboration outcomes and communication overhead. Experiments across diverse LLM backbones and mechanism configurations offer empirical guidance for effective MAS design. Code is available at: https://github.com/BUPT-GAMMA/MASBench

</details>


### [261] [When Debate Helps: Proposal Supply and Verification-Aware Readout in Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04686)
*Zihao Zhao,Tunyu Zhang,Haizhou Shi,Yusong Zhao,Xinxi Zhang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出多智能体辩论超越简单多数投票需满足两个独立机制：提案供给须产出正确答案（可恢复余量），读出端须在投票失效时识别正确答案（潜变量验证辩论LVD）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论虽能提升推理，却常常无法超越简单多数投票；作者认为原因在于以往工作未区分两个必要条件，即正确提案是否已被供给、以及当投票错过正确答案时读出机制能否识别它。

Method: 形式化"可恢复余量"刻画"存在正确提案但多数答案错误"的情形；提出潜变量验证辩论（LVD）会计模型，使候选提案在最终生成前获得答案特定的验证证据，并通过受控固定提案干预以等价同伴支持单位估计其潜变量效应；同时用带标签与无标签覆盖目标构建神经丛（neural-thicket）智能体社会以改善提案供给，并在两种骨干网络与预算匹配的推理基准上进行重复随机评估及轮次级对照。

Result: 受控干预显示正确证据在提案供给固定时改变答案概率与生成决策；覆盖度选择的智能体社会增加了互补提案供给并提升总体准确率；轮次级对照表明交互的增益超过将同一最终器直接应用于初始提案。

Conclusion: 提案覆盖度与真值敏感的证据使用是辩论超越投票的两个互补条件。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate can improve reasoning, yet often fails to beat simple majority voting. We argue that successful debate requires two distinct mechanisms: proposal supply must surface a correct answer, and readout must identify that answer when voting misses it. We formalize the first requirement through recoverable headroom, which measures cases where a correct proposal is available but the majority answer is wrong. For the second, we develop Latent Verification Debate (LVD), an accounting model in which candidate proposals receive answer-specific verification evidence before final generation. Controlled fixed-proposal interventions estimate this latent effect in equivalent peer-support units and show that correct evidence changes answer probabilities and generated decisions while proposal supply remains fixed. To improve proposal supply, we construct societies from neural-thicket agents using labeled and label-free coverage objectives. Across two backbones and matched-budget reasoning benchmarks, coverage-selected societies increase complementary proposal supply and improve aggregate accuracy in repeated stochastic evaluations. Round-level controls further show that interaction provides gains beyond applying the same finalizer directly to the initial proposals. These results identify proposal coverage and truth-sensitive evidence use as complementary conditions for debate to outperform voting. Code is available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/when-debate-helps.

</details>


### [262] [RAGStress: A controlled benchmark for evaluating retrieval-augmented generation under knowledge-base degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04691)
*Shiqi Yang,Jiekai Ma,Gaoyuan Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RAGStress 基准，用于在系统化知识库损坏条件下压力测试 RAG 系统的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 评估通常隐含假设知识库（KB）是干净的，对真实场景中 KB 退化时 RAG 系统的行为刻画不足。

Method: 构建 RAGStress 受控评估基准：四种自然主义损坏类型（事实损坏、数字笔误、相关性投毒、矛盾注入）× 三个严重程度（轻微、中等、明显）；采用单 KB、元数据过滤的实验设计，基于 57 个 MMLU 科目、182,546 篇文档，共完成 52,500 次模型-问题-条件评估。

Result: RAGStress 揭示：干净检索会掩盖鲁棒性差异；语义保真类损坏比信号效用扰动危害更大；无检索准确率无法预测损坏检索下的鲁棒性；混合 KB 准确率不应视为最坏情况鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RAGStress 是面向 KB 损坏下 RAG 鲁棒性的受控压力测试工具，而非通用模型排行榜；作者明确了其预期用途、可支持结论与局限，并提供生成脚本、损坏提示、元数据模式与评估代码等工件包。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is typically evaluated under the implicit assumption that the underlying knowledge base (KB) is clean, leaving the behaviour of RAG systems under realistic KB degradation poorly characterised. We introduce RAGStress, a controlled evaluation benchmark for stress-testing RAG systems under systematic KB corruption. The benchmark pairs four naturalistic corruption types (factual corruption, numeric typo, relevance poisoning, and contradiction injection) with three severity levels (subtle, moderate, and obvious) over a single-KB, metadata-filtered experimental design built from 57 MMLU subjects and 182,546 documents. Across 52,500 model-question-condition evaluations, RAGStress reveals that clean retrieval can mask robustness differences, semantic-fidelity corruptions are substantially more harmful than signal-utility perturbations, no-retrieval accuracy does not predict corrupted-retrieval robustness, and mixed-KB accuracy should not be treated as worst-case robustness. We document the benchmark's intended use, supported claims, and limitations, and provide an artifact bundle including generation scripts, corruption prompts, metadata schema, and evaluation code. RAGStress is intended as a controlled stress test for RAG robustness under KB corruption, not as a general model leaderboard.

</details>


### [263] [Penumbra: Sample-Efficient Adversarial Search for Regulatory Obligations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04693)
*Anthony Rhodes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Penumbra：一种样本高效的对抗搜索方法，用于在金融、医疗、法律等领域的合规红队测试中，定位"最小编辑距离即在合规边界翻转"的相邻响应对，以覆盖（义务×失败模式）的失败面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融、医疗、法律等高风险领域，违规不具有明显的词法特征，且带来实际惩罚；判断某个"遗漏"是否重要、某个"披露"是否充分，取决于响应遗漏了什么。探测此类义务需要找到与合规翻转仅差一处最小编辑的响应，而每次探测成本为一次生成加两次裁定，因此监管红队测试的约束瓶颈是样本效率而非数量。

Method: 提出 Penumbra 对抗搜索：从一个经核验的锚点出发，在逐步扩大的编辑预算下行走，直到由一个双评估者委员会改变裁定，并输出跨越该变化的两个相邻响应。采用自适应分配，目标为失败面的覆盖，即每个候选所解决的、互不相同的（义务×失败模式）单元数。

Result: 在预算匹配条件下，自适应分配在 59% 的候选上达到均匀分配满预算的覆盖；在记录数相等时，其覆盖的失败模式是朴素枚举的 1.43 倍，且增益局限于其所针对的维度。在金融咨询宪章的 60 项筛查义务上，Penumbra 返回 144 对响应，每对均为被委员会置于边界两侧的合规与违例响应，仅相差数词；而直接要求模型同时生成两类文本时，两者内容几乎无重叠。在临床分诊宪章的 18 项义务上复现该结果：49 对，紧致度相同，且困难模式一致。

Conclusion: 此类响应对揭示了某项义务的自身条文在何处停止作出判定，这正是部署于该义务下的智能体必须被测试的内容；而该搜索找到它们的成本随边界规模而非文本规模扩展。

Abstract: Agents are entering finance, healthcare and law, sectors where a violation leaves no lexical signature and carries real penalties. Whether an omission is material, or a disclosure sufficient, depends on what the response left out. Probing such an obligation means finding responses one minimal edit from flipping compliance, and every probe costs a generation and two adjudications, so the binding constraint on regulatory red-teaming is sample efficiency, not volume. We introduce Penumbra, an adversarial search that walks from a verified anchor under an expanding edit budget until a two-evaluator committee changes its verdict, and emits the two adjacent responses that straddle the change. Allocation is adaptive, and the objective is coverage of the defeat surface: distinct (obligation x defeat mode) cells resolved per candidate. At matched budget, adaptive allocation reaches uniform allocation's full-budget coverage on 59% of the candidates; at equal records it covers 1.43x the defeat modes of naive enumeration, and the gain is confined to the axis it targets. On 60 screened obligations of a financial advisory constitution, Penumbra returns 144 pairs, each a compliant and a violating response that the committee places on opposite sides of the boundary, differing by a handful of words where a model asked for both directly produces texts sharing almost nothing. A second constitution, for clinical triage, reproduces this on 18 obligations: 49 pairs at the same tightness, with the same modes hardest.
