Pairs like these show where an obligation's own terms stop deciding, which is what an agent deployed under it must be tested against, and the search finds them at a cost that scales with the boundary, not the text.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [264] [Bayes-Sufficient Compression Is Not Enough: How Does Communication Help Multi-Agent Systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03769)
*Yi Xie,Zhanke Zhou,Yi Fan,Yong Ge,Bo Han,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"接收者相对有界协调"框架，将消息效用建模为接收者收益减去协议税，刻画了压缩与原始上下文、发送者升级的适用条件，并通过六项基准验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统中，发送者拥有宽泛上下文而执行者仅有局部视图。现有研究缺乏对"何时短消息有用、何时原始上下文更优、何时更强发送者有益"的系统性理论刻画。

Method: 提出receiver-relative bounded coordination框架，将消息效用表示为接收者增益减协议税；用Bayes充分压缩反例说明有界执行者无法利用表面形式；用三阶段分解（外化、吸收、动作闭包）分析误差残留；在单交叉条件下给出发送者升级的接收者负载阈值。

Result: 在六个基准上，同一Qwen协议将ContextBench联合准确率从0.633提升至0.775，但使ToolSandbox从0.889降至0.653；固定消息重放显示即使正确恢复工件仍存在闭包失败。

Conclusion: 这些结果支持一个推理时选择器，在所评估的通信体制下改善了准确率-成本前沿。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems pair a sender with broad context and an executor with a limited local view. We study when a short message improves the executor's next decision, when raw context is preferable, and when a stronger sender helps. Our framework, \emph{receiver-relative bounded coordination}, expresses message utility as receiver gain minus protocol tax. Compression beats raw context when tax savings exceed losses from omitted information and decoder mismatch. Even \emph{Bayes-sufficient} compression can fail when a bounded executor cannot use its surface form. A three-stage decomposition separates externalization, absorption, and \emph{action closure}, explaining how errors remain after the correct content reaches the receiver. Under a single-crossing condition, sender upgrades help above a receiver-burden threshold. Across six benchmarks, the same Qwen protocol raises ContextBench joint accuracy from $0.633$ to $0.775$ but lowers ToolSandbox from $0.889$ to $0.653$. Fixed-message replay reveals closure failures despite correct artifact recovery. These results guide an inference-time selector that improves the accuracy-cost frontier on the evaluated communication regimes.

</details>


### [265] [Least Squares for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03812)
*Weiu-qiou Ciang,Yuzhou Hong,Sherry Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对比了"预测目标"与"回归下一潜变量"两种表示学习目标：标量目标+线性解码器下二者等价（各向同性约束仅为重缩放）；但在四维序列的固定低秩拟合中，秩1向量拟合把质量放在重复坐标上，预测误差（2.794）远大于预测程序（0.992），秩2时二者一致；迭代一步系数0.803会使开环误差从0.992升至2.700。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测的评分对象是未来的序列值，而LeNEPA式表示损失却回归下一潜向量，二者虽共享同一瓶颈却属于不同的最小二乘问题。作者希望厘清这两种程序何时等价、何时分歧，并揭示固定秩表示拟合对最终预测质量的隐性影响。

Method: 将两个程序形式化：预测程序最小化解码潜变量在待报告坐标上的误差；对照程序在固定秩下拟合整个下一向量，随后冻结编码器并接上预测头。以标量目标+线性解码器作理论分析，并在一个后三维为相同自回归的四维序列上做秩1与秩2的实证对比。

Result: 标量目标+线性解码器下，任一秩≥1的潜表示均等价于普通最小二乘，各向同性约束仅相当于重缩放（解码器重拟合后预测不变）；四维序列秩1向量拟合将0.9998的质量放在重复坐标上，对剩余信号仅给出边际方差2.794的预测，而预测程序将质量1放在信号上，匹配新息方差0.992；秩2时向量拟合获得第二方向，两程序一致；迭代拟合的一步系数0.803使开环误差由一步0.992增至八步2.700。

Conclusion: 回归下一潜变量的表示损失与实际预测目标并不天然一致：低秩下前者的潜空间可能被无关的重复方向占据，导致预测退化，需提高秩或直接针对报告坐标优化；同时一步精度不能外推到多步开环，误差随迭代显著放大。

Abstract: A time-series forecast is scored on a future value of the series. A representation loss that regresses the next latent, as in LeNEPA, is a different least-squares problem on the same bottleneck. We write both programs down. The forecast program minimizes the error of a decoded latent on the coordinate that will be reported. For a scalar target and a linear decoder, every latent rank of at least one matches ordinary least squares, and an isotropy constraint is only a rescaling: after the decoder is refit, the forecast does not move. The other program fits the whole next vector at a fixed rank, then freezes the encoder and attaches a head. On a four-dimensional series whose last three coordinates are the same autoregression, that rank-1 fit puts mass $0.9998$ on the repeated coordinate and forecasts the remaining signal at the marginal variance $2.794$. The forecast program puts mass $1$ on the signal and matches the innovation variance $0.992$. Rank $2$ gives the vector fit a second direction, and the two programs agree. Iterating the fitted one-step coefficient $0.803$ raises the open-loop error from $0.992$ at one step to $2.700$ at eight steps.

</details>


### [266] [Memory-State Critic for Asymmetric Actor-Critic with Application to Vision-Based Pursuit-Evasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03830)
*Arthur Louette,Alejandro Sánchez Roncero,Gaspard Lambrechts,Pascal Leroy,Julien Hansen,Petter Ögren,Damien Ernst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 memory-state critic：仅以状态与策略自身记忆作为critic输入，即可well-defined且给出无偏策略梯度，无需再建第二个recurrent history近似器，也无需把损失反传到策略记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POMDP中最优策略依赖观测与动作历史；非对称actor-critic在训练时可用真状态等额外信息，但仅以状态为条件的critic定义不良、策略梯度有偏。既有解法history-state critic需额外recurrent近似history，成本高。

Method: 将critic条件化为状态+策略自身记忆（策略据以选动作的历史内部表示），称memory-state critic；论证其well-defined且梯度无偏，并指出无需将critic损失反传进该记忆。在两个竞技场类型的双四旋翼视觉追逃环境中评估，追捕者为学习agent，逃避者按episode从固定启发式行为池采样。

Result: memory-state critic优于history-state critic且收敛更快；相比state-only critic保持无偏且略有优势；在wall竞技场中，actor history携带privileged state所不包含的信息。

Conclusion: 以策略记忆+状态condition critic足以无偏，省去第二个recurrent history近似器及对策略记忆的反传，为部分可观测下非对称学习提供更简洁有效的方案。

Abstract: In partially observable Markov decision processes, the optimal policy generally depends on the history of observations and past actions. Asymmetric actor-critic methods have become popular to learn such policies when additional information, such as the true state of the environment, is available during training. The critic, which is not needed at execution, is given access to the state. A critic conditioned on the state alone is generally ill-defined and yields biased policy gradients. Conditioning on the state and the history, the history-state critic restores both. In this paper, we show that conditioning the critic on the state and the policy's own memory, i.e., the internal representation of the history through which the policy selects its actions, is already well-defined and gives unbiased policy gradients, removing the need for a second recurrent approximator of the history. We call it the memory state critic. It follows that a critic based on the policy's memory need not backpropagate its loss into that memory, even though the memory is a lossy encoding of the history. We evaluate the memory-state critic in a vision-based pursuit-evasion environment between two quadrotors across two arena types. The pursuer is the learning agent, and the evader is sampled per episode from a fixed pool of heuristic behaviours. The results show that the memory-state critic outperforms the history-state critic and converges faster. In addition to being unbiased compared to the state-only critic, it maintains a slight edge in the wall arena, where the actor's history carries information that the privileged state alone does not.

</details>


### [267] [Where Does Jagged Competence Come From?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03831)
*Ioannis Tsiokos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在由两层构成的任务中，学习上层理论B会使网络仅获得"参差不齐"的下层理论A：B需要处A可读性高（槽位1约97-98%），不需要处低（其他槽位5-10%），且即便显式训练A也仅近似正确；这种不均匀的习得、访问与保持部分解释了网络在B上的参差不齐表现，部分失败仍无法解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统常表现出"参差不齐的能力"（jagged competence）：平均误差低但在特定输入上失败。作者希望在一个由两个已知层次构成的任务中，定位这种参差性的来源——即探究当网络学习建立在下层理论之上的上层理论时，其对下层理论的习得情况。

Method: 构建两层任务：下层A从记录计算五个逐槽位求和；上层B使用槽位1的和与从先前棋盘继承的模式来预测下一棋盘的类别，因此B是A的严格扩展。用小型循环网络训练B，借助探针可读性与监督输出，对照精确真值测量网络对A的习得。并进行匹配实验：将已学习的A连接到B、比较one-hot与数值输入、单次B更新对精确A的影响（阻断B梯度与否）等。

Result: 学习B得到的是A的"参差"版本：B需要处（槽位1）线性探针可读性97-98%，不需要处（其他槽位）降至5-10%；类别误读集中在槽位1的截断值附近；显式训练A也仅近似正确。网络在B上呈现参差能力：自然KL低于3×10⁻⁴比特，而在构造历史上最大law TV约0.25。匹配实验显示好的A也不够：学得的A接到B使误读减少1.3-17倍；同一精确A作one-hot输入误读更少（0-2）而作数值输入更多（10-317）；一次B更新会使精确A变不精确（除非阻断B梯度），且精确A仍残留部分B失败。

Conclusion: 在该任务中，下层理论在习得、访问与保持上的不均匀性，部分解释了学习其上层理论的网络的参差能力；但仍有部分失败无法解释。这一现象是否存在于更大系统中，有待进一步测试。

Abstract: Capable systems often show jagged competence: low average error alongside failures on particular inputs. We ask where it comes from in a task built from two known layers. A lower layer A computes five per-slot sums from records; an upper layer B uses the slot-1 sum and a mode carried over from earlier boards to predict the next board's category, so B cannot be computed from the current A alone: B is a strict extension of A. We train small recurrent networks on B and measure, against exact ground truth, what they acquire of A. The central finding is that learning B gives the network a jagged version of A, measured through probe readability and supervised outputs. It is readable where B needs it (slot 1, 97-98% by a linear probe) and becomes less readable where B does not (the other slots, 5-10%); category misreads concentrate near the slot-1 cutoffs; and when A is trained explicitly it comes out only approximately right. The networks show jagged competence in B: natural KL below $3\times10^{-4}$ bits coexists with a maximum law TV of about 0.25 on constructed histories. Matched experiments show that even a good A is not enough: connecting a learned A to B cuts misreads 1.3 to 17-fold, the same exact A gives fewer misreads as one-hot inputs (0-2) than as numerical inputs (10-317), one B update makes an exact A inexact unless the B gradient is blocked, and exact A still leaves some B failures. In this task, uneven acquisition, access and preservation of the lower theory explain part of the jagged competence of a network that learns the theory built on it; some failures remain unexplained. Whether the same holds in larger systems is a test to run.

</details>


### [268] [LLM-enhanced spatio-temporal learning for grid-level docked bike sharing demand prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03834)
*Xuxilu Zhang,Francesc Soriguera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM驱动的"语义冲击波"机制，将城市非结构化文本转化为量化时空扰动场，并注入零膨胀自适应时空图卷积网络（ZI-ASTGCN）用于共享单车短期需求预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享单车短期需求预测受时空非平稳性困扰，且难以将非结构化外部文本纳入数值化流程。传统方法依赖历史流量序列和固定图结构，当异常社会事件扰动正常出行模式时，预测精度受限。

Method: 用LLM驱动语义冲击波机制，把市政活动安排、本地新闻、交通公告等自由文本转为量化时空扰动场；LLM为每个事件抽取三个具有物理可解释性的参数——强度、空间影响半径、时间滞后，据此构建高斯衰减场并注入ZI-ASTGCN。针对网格级测量高度稀疏的问题，模型将双分支输出头与多任务零膨胀损失耦合，联合训练门控概率与条件流量强度。

Result: 在巴塞罗那Bicing运营数据集上的实验表明，ZI-ASTGCN优于已有神经基线，在高需求时段增益尤为显著，验证了物理驱动的语义信号在时空出行预测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 将LLM提取的物理可解释语义扰动信号与零膨胀时空图卷积网络结合，可有效缓解异常事件导致的预测精度下降，为时空出行需求预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Short-term bike-sharing demand forecasting is complicated by spatial-temporal non-stationarity and the practical difficulty of incorporating unstructured external text into numerical pipelines. Conventional approaches rely on historical flow sequences and fixed graph structures, thereby constraining their accuracy when anomalous social events perturb normal travel patterns. We propose a forecasting framework in which a Large Language Model (LLM) drives a semantic shockwave mechanism that converts free-form urban text, such as municipal event schedules, local news, and transit bulletins, into quantified spatial-temporal perturbation fields. The LLM extracts three physically interpretable parameters per event (intensity, spatial reach, and temporal lag), from which Gaussian decay fields are constructed and injected into a Zero-Inflated Adaptive Spatio-Temporal Graph Convolutional Network (ZI-ASTGCN). To handle the pronounced sparsity of grid-level measurements, the model couples a dual-branch output head with a multi-task zero-inflated loss that jointly trains a gating probability and a conditional flow intensity. Experiments on the operational Barcelona Bicing dataset show that ZI-ASTGCN outperforms established neural baselines, with particularly strong gains during high-demand periods, validating the utility of physics-grounded semantic signals in spatial-temporal mobility forecasting.

</details>


### [269] [KVE-KD: Key Visual Evidence-Guided Knowledge Distillation for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03842)
*Jianbin Zhang,Xin Sun,Shanwen Wang,Wei Ye,Susanto Rahardja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 KVE-KD 框架，通过教师模型识别任务相关的关键视觉证据，实现聚焦式视觉特征蒸馏，在六个基准上超越现有跨模态蒸馏方法且无推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型知识蒸馏方法通常对所有视觉 token 施加统一监督，或采用静态 token 选择策略，导致任务相关线索与背景噪声混淆，损害跨模态推理性能。

Method: KVE-KD 以最后一个预生成文本 token 作为统一语义锚点，通过迭代移除视觉 token 并分析锚点表示变化来定位目标跨模态融合层；在该层内，依据锚点条件化的注意力分布并结合归一化熵对视觉 token 排序，选出最具信息量的关键视觉证据，用以引导聚焦式视觉特征蒸馏，使学生对齐教师的任务相关视觉表示并抑制无关背景。

Result: 在六个基准上的大量实验表明，KVE-KD 优于当前最先进的跨模态蒸馏方法，在需要复杂推理和细粒度视觉理解的任务上提升尤为显著，且未引入任何推理时开销。

Conclusion: 通过动态识别并聚焦任务相关的关键视觉证据，KVE-KD 有效缓解了统一监督与静态选择带来的噪声干扰问题，实现了高效且无推理代价的跨模态知识蒸馏。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation is crucial for deploying vision-language models on resource-constrained devices. However, existing methods typically impose uniform supervision across visual tokens or rely on static token selection, which confuses task-relevant cues with background noise and degrades cross-modal reasoning. To address this limitation, we propose Key Visual Evidence-guided Knowledge Distillation (KVE-KD), a framework that dynamically focuses feature distillation on task-relevant visual tokens identified by the teacher model. Specifically, KVE-KD appoints the final pre-generation textual token as a unified semantic anchor and identifies the target cross-modal fusion layer by analyzing changes in the anchor representation through iterative visual-token contribution removal. Within this layer, KVE-KD ranks visual tokens via the anchor-conditioned attention distribution and selects the most informative visual tokens as key visual evidence with normalized entropy. The key visual evidence subsequently guides focused visual feature distillation, making the student align closely with the teacher's task-relevant visual representations while suppressing irrelevant background information. Extensive experiments on six benchmarks demonstrate that KVE-KD outperforms state-of-the-art cross-modal distillation methods, with particularly pronounced gains on tasks requiring complex reasoning and fine-grained visual understanding. Importantly, these improvements are achieved without introducing any inference-time overhead. The source code is available at https://github.com/zhangjianbin07/KVE-KD.

</details>


### [270] [BAT-NO: A Boundary-Condition-Aware Transformer Neural Operator for Crashworthiness Prediction of Vehicle Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03854)
*Haoran Li,Yingxue Zhao,Haosu Zhou,Mustapha Ziane,Pierre Culiere,Tobias Pfaff,Nan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出边界条件感知的Transformer神经算子（BAT-NO），用于在几何与边界条件变化下自回归预测瞬态位移场和碰撞响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真有限元仿真精度高但成本大，限制迭代设计；现有深度学习代理模型多在单一规定边界条件下开发，难以泛化到边界条件变化的情形。

Method: 提出BAT-NO，结合循环网格处理与潜在网格傅里叶算子处理；通过混合局部—全局机制将边界条件信息传递到潜在网格；采用切片注意力建模物理相关区域间交互，并利用直接边界到网格投影保留局部空间结构；以B柱仿真框架评估几何、冲击位置与速度、支撑刚度变化下的泛化能力。

Result: 在shape-only、shape-and-loading、shape-loading-boundary验证集上，BAT-NO均取得最低的平均末步节点欧氏位移误差；最难情形下较次优模型降低32.6%。超参调优使验证误差从0.451降至0.269 mm，300个未见测试仿真误差为0.267 mm。基于注意力的标量解码器联合预测六条响应轨迹，平均相对误差2.46%，多数导出碰撞指标中位误差低于3%。

Conclusion: 显式的局部与全局边界条件表示可提升扩展构件级设计空间下的碰撞预测性能。

Abstract: High-fidelity finite-element simulations provide accurate crashworthiness predictions, but their cost limits iterative design exploration. Deep learning surrogates can reduce this cost, but many component-level models are developed under a single prescribed boundary condition, limiting generalisation to boundary variations. This work proposes a Boundary-Condition-Aware Transformer Neural Operator (BAT-NO) for autoregressive prediction of transient displacement fields and scalar crashworthiness responses under variations in geometry and boundary conditions. A B-pillar simulation framework evaluates generalisation across variations in geometry, impact position and velocity, and support stiffness. BAT-NO combines recurrent mesh processing with latent-grid Fourier operator processing. Boundary-condition information is transferred to the latent grid through a hybrid local--global mechanism. Slice-based attention models interactions among physically related regions, while direct boundary-to-grid projection preserves local spatial structure. Across the validation sets for the shape-only, shape-and-loading, and shape-loading-boundary cases, BAT-NO achieves the lowest mean final-step mean nodal Euclidean displacement error among the evaluated baselines. In the most challenging case, it reduces the mean error by 32.6% relative to the second-best model. Hyperparameter tuning reduces the validation error from 0.451 to 0.269 mm, with a comparable error of 0.267 mm on 300 unseen test simulations sampled within the investigated design space. An attention-based scalar decoder jointly predicts six response trajectories with a mean relative error of 2.46%. Most derived crashworthiness indicators have median errors below 3%. These results show that explicit local and global boundary-condition representations improve crashworthiness prediction over expanded component-level design spaces.

</details>


### [271] [Retrieval-Centric Deep Learning in Growing Nonparametric Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03858)
*Maximilian Schlegel,Rajai Nasser,Seijin Kobayashi,Yanick Schimpf,Oliver Sieberling,Robert Obryk,Kazuki Irie,João Sacramento,Johannes von Oswald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"检索中心深度学习"(RCDL)范式，为核化注意力层推导了基于泛函梯度的学习规则，弥补了将线性注意力扩展至更强注意力函数时的优化理论空白，并在图像分类与师生学习任务上验证了其性能与学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习将任意规模的训练数据压缩进固定大小的权重矩阵，而Irie等人提出的观点认为：深层网络中任意线性层（经梯度下降训练）可等价表示为对训练数据点的线性注意力(LA)，故可用存储键值对并检索重组的"增长型神经网络"替代固定权重。然而，将LA替换为更强大的注意力函数并非易事——直接把LA情形下的学习规则套用到高级核函数上，并不能得到有原则(principled)的优化方法。本文正是为填补这一理论与方法空白而展开。

Method: 作者基于泛函梯度(functional gradient)为核化注意力层推导学习规则，涵盖径向基函数(RBF)核与softmax类核，从而建立起有原则的"检索中心深度学习"(RCDL)范式；此外，将神经网络对偶形式中的LA替换为更高级的LA变体（MesaNet/DeltaNet），以建立与现有优化器的形式联系。

Result: (1) 在图像分类和合成师生学习任务上，RCDL展现出有前景的性能与学习效率；(2) 将NN对偶形式中的LA替换为MesaNet/DeltaNet等高级LA变体后，与近期针对传统固定规模NN提出的优化器建立了形式上的联系，为深度学习优化提供了新视角。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个有原则的RCDL范式，为核化注意力层给出了基于泛函梯度的学习规则，实证验证了其有效性与学习效率，并揭示出高级线性注意力变体与传统NN优化器之间的形式联系，为检索式增长型神经网络与深度学习优化提供了新的理论视角与方法基础。

Abstract: We investigate a general-purpose layer for deep learning that, instead of compressing arbitrary-size training data into fixed-size weight matrices, stores a new pair of key-value representations for every data point during training, and retrieves and recombines these representations through an attention mechanism at inference time - resulting in a growing neural net (NN). While Irie et al. (arXiv:2202.05798) have put forward this perspective from the classic duality expressing any linear layer in a deep NN trained by gradient descent as linear attention (LA) over the training data points, replacing LA by more powerful attention functions, as they suggest, turns out to be non-trivial: we show that naively applying learning rules from the LA case to advanced kernels does not lead to principled optimization. Here we fill this gap and develop functional gradient-based learning rules for kernelized attention layers, based on radial basis function (RBF) and softmax-like kernels - establishing the principled "retrieval-centric deep learning" (RCDL) paradigm. Empirically, we demonstrate the promising performance and learning-efficiency of RCDL on image classification and synthetic teacher-student learning tasks. Moreover, we show that replacing LA in the dual form of NNs by advanced LA variants, namely MesaNet/DeltaNet, yields a formal connection to recently proposed optimizers for conventional fixed-size NNs, offering a novel perspective on deep learning optimization.

</details>


### [272] [Reinforcement Learning on the Discrete Composition Channel of a Crystal Generator: Validated Gains and Reward Hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03880)
*Pawan Prakash,Philipp Höllmer,Addis Fuhr,Peter Hirschfeld,P. Ganesh,Stefano Martiniani,Richard Hennig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作用GRPO强化学习对齐基于随机插值与离散流匹配的晶体生成模型，通过直接作用于原子类型转移似然的策略梯度实现黑箱奖励优化，将mSUN产率从13.4%提升至45.5%，并揭示奖励利用风险与基准指标缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体材料的逆向设计是计算材料发现的长期目标，但现有生成模型仅被训练去匹配结构数据库的分布，其训练目标并不指向特定设计目标（如目标性质），因此需要将生成模型与黑箱奖励函数对齐。

Method: 采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）对齐基于随机插值和离散流匹配的生成模型，原子类型由离散流生成，广义GRPO的策略梯度直接作用于原子类型转移的似然；设计提升亚稳、独特且新颖结构（mSUN）产率的奖励函数，并用显式防护机制抑制奖励利用。

Result: mSUN产率从预训练模型的13.4%提升至强化模型的45.5%（社区基准评估）；该奖励同样提升了基于潜在去噪扩散模型的强化学习框架性能；发现直接强化原子类型转移似然会导致奖励利用，需要显式防护；同时暴露社区指标的缺陷——不同堆积方式的单元素结构被计为mSUN从而虚高指标。

Conclusion: 稳定性声明取决于其参考凸包；作者按参考相数量对全部结果进行分组报告，并主张基准测试也应采用同样的做法。

Abstract: Inverse materials design is a long-standing goal of computational materials discovery. Generative models for crystalline materials are typically trained to match the distribution of a structure database, while nothing in their training objective points them at specific design goals such as targeted properties. We use group-relative policy optimization (GRPO) to align a generative model based on stochastic interpolants and discrete flow matching with general black-box reward functions through reinforcement learning. Atom types are generated by a discrete flow and the policy gradient of our generalization of GRPO directly acts on the likelihoods of the atom-type transitions, which differentiates our work from previous reinforcement-learning approaches for diffusion and flow-based generative models of crystalline materials. We introduce a reward function that raises the yield of metastable, unique and novel structures (mSUN) from 13.4% for the pretrained model to 45.5% for the reinforced model, as evaluated by a community benchmark. Our reward also improves the performance of a reinforcement learning framework for crystalline materials based on latent denoising diffusion models. At the same time, we find that directly reinforcing atom-type transition likelihoods enables reward exploitation that has to be prevented with explicit guards. The same analysis also exposes a gap in the community metric. Single-element structures in distinct packings are counted as metastable, unique and novel materials and inflate mSUN without yielding any new compounds. A stability claim is only as good as its reference hull. We report every result split by the number of reference phases behind it and argue that benchmarks should do the same.

</details>


### [273] [AdaEva: Accelerating LLM-Driven Algorithm Design with Adaptive Partial Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03896)
*Tai Nguyen,Fei Liu,Phong Le,Carola Doerr,Nguyen Dang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 AdaEva，一种即插即用的自适应部分评估框架，在 LLM 驱动的自动算法设计（LLM4AD）中逐步扩大候选算法的评估实例子集并及时淘汰劣质候选，以降低评估成本。基于逐次减半（AdaEva-S）和统计竞速（AdaEva-R）两种实例化机制，在多个 LLM4AD 框架、LLM 主干与优化领域上验证其搜索效率与泛化优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM4AD 中评估生成算法的计算开销过大；现有做法通常需将每个候选算法在完整训练实例集上评估后才能判断其竞争力，而候选评估本质上是对共享实例池的性能聚合，这种逐实例结构为部分评估提供了空间。

Method: 提出 AdaEva 自适应部分评估框架：随着证据累积，逐步在更大的实例子集上评估候选，并淘汰不具潜力的候选。该框架即插即用，不改变底层 LLM4AD 流程与单实例评估器，且无需实例难度的先验知识；分别以逐次减半（AdaEva-S）和统计竞速（AdaEva-R）实现。

Result: 在三个代表性 LLM4AD 框架、多类 LLM 主干以及覆盖组合优化与连续黑盒优化的多个领域上进行评估。在相同评估预算下，AdaEva 相比固定部分评估策略能更可靠地在候选间分配评估资源，取得更强的搜索效率与 anytime 性能，并提升留出集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 实例级部分评估结合自适应淘汰机制是降低 LLM4AD 评估开销的有效途径，AdaEva 以最小侵入方式显著提升评估预算利用率与搜索表现。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for automated algorithm design. However the computational cost of evaluating the generated algorithms can be excessive. We consider the common LLM-driven automated algorithm design (LLM4AD) setting in which a candidate algorithm is evaluated by aggregating its performance over a shared set of training instances. This instance-wise structure raises a natural question: must every candidate be evaluated on the entire instance set before deciding whether it remains competitive? Taking inspiration from algorithm configuration, we introduce AdaEva, a drop-in adaptive partial-evaluation framework that progressively evaluates candidates on larger subsets of the same instance pool and eliminates unpromising candidates as evidence accumulates. Importantly, AdaEva leaves the underlying LLM4AD procedure and per-instance evaluator unchanged and requires no prior knowledge about instance difficulty. We instantiate this idea using successive halving (AdaEva-S) and statistical racing (AdaEva-R), and evaluate both mechanisms across three representative LLM4AD frameworks, multiple LLM backbones, and optimization domains spanning combinatorial and continuous black-box optimization. Under matched evaluation budgets, AdaEva more reliably balances evaluation effort across candidates than fixed partial-evaluation strategies, yielding strong search efficiency and anytime performance together with improved held-out generalization across the evaluated settings.

</details>


### [274] [COVER: Learning to Accept More in Selective Sleep Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03911)
*Yukai Song,Yangfan Deng,Jijun Yin,Zhi-Hong Mao,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出COVER方法，通过辅助信息驱动的初级错误学习与固定尺度评分细化，在给定风险约束下最大化睡眠分期级联第一阶段的覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统睡眠分期对所有EEG epoch统一使用同一模型，计算开销浪费在小模型即可可靠处理的epoch上，因此需要级联结构：由初级分类器保留可靠预测、将其余样本交由更强模型处理。本文聚焦级联第一阶段，即在规定接受风险目标下最大化固定初级预测的覆盖率。

Method: COVER (COVerage-oriented Error Ranking) 包含两项创新：(i) 辅助信息驱动的初级错误学习，用学习得到的错误分数替代最大softmax概率(MSP)，同时保留初级标签；(ii) 固定尺度评分器细化，基于该分数在经验接受风险约束下直接最大化覆盖率，而非优化所有epoch上的错误预测准确率。

Result: 在Sleep-EDF-20数据集上以5%接受风险为目标、被试完全留出进行拟合与选择。辅助信息错误学习将平均被试覆盖率从MSP的31.5%提升至48.8%（被试等风险相近）；在相同接受量下（每被试接受epoch数与学习评分器一致，共20,639个），错误数由1,467降至867。固定尺度细化在嵌套开发中相较其初始化额外提升1.7个百分点覆盖率。COVER在八种评分器中取得最高平均覆盖率50.4%（被试等风险4.5%），优于选择性排序基线SELE(49.3%)与概率融合对比方法DuoF(43.6%)。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个将辅助信息驱动的初级错误学习与固定尺度覆盖率细化相结合用于选择性睡眠分期的工作，为级联睡眠分期中可靠性感知的计算分配提供了基础。

Abstract: Traditional sleep-staging methods apply the same model to every EEG epoch. Such uniform deployment expends computation on epochs that a smaller model could handle reliably, motivating cascades in which a primary classifier accepts its reliable predictions and defers the remainder to a more capable model. In this paper, we study the first stage of such a cascade: maximizing the coverage of fixed primary predictions subject to a prescribed accepted-risk target. We propose COVER (COVerage-oriented Error Ranking), which integrates two key innovations: (i) auxiliary-informed primary-error learning, which replaces maximum softmax probability (MSP) with a learned error score while preserving the primary labels, and (ii) fixed-scale scorer refinement, which builds on this score to directly maximize coverage under an empirical accepted-risk constraint rather than error-prediction accuracy over all epochs. We evaluate COVER on Sleep-EDF-20 at a 5% accepted-risk target, with subjects held out from all fitting and selection. Auxiliary-informed error learning raises mean subject coverage from 31.5% for MSP to 48.8% at similar subject-equal risk. At equal acceptance volume, with MSP accepting the same number of epochs as the learned scorer in each subject (20,639 in total), errors fall from 1,467 to 867. Fixed-scale refinement then adds 1.7 percentage points of coverage over its initialization in nested development, and COVER attains the highest mean coverage among eight evaluated scorers, 50.4% at 4.5% subject-equal risk, above the selective-ranking baseline SELE (49.3%) and the probability-fusion comparator DuoF (43.6%). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to combine auxiliary-informed primary-error learning with fixed-scale coverage refinement for selective sleep staging, offering a basis for reliability-aware allocation of computation in cascaded sleep staging.

</details>


### [275] [TreeWalker: Partial Evaluation for Grouped Tree-Ensemble Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03939)
*Durmus Karatay,Richard Newman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TreeWalker 将部分求值引入分组树集成推理：把组内常量特征视为静态、变化特征视为动态，对每棵树按组仅遍历一次，并在变化分裂处对行位掩码进行划分、跳过空子树；训练不变且兼容标准 LightGBM/XGBoost 模型。作者给出结构性工作量分解，并在 Intel/Arm 上取得显著加速，同时数值精度匹配或优于现有实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多推理负载在共享特征值的行组上重复计算：离散时间生存模型将每个患者展开为 G 个时间步、CTR 模型对同一搜索会话的每个item打分、情景分析仅变动少量输入。主流推理按行独立处理，导致共享工作被重复 G 次，存在大量冗余计算。

Method: 提出 TreeWalker，将分组推理视为部分求值：常量特征为静态、变化特征为动态。每组每树只走一次，在动态分裂处对行位掩码做划分，并跳过空子树。训练流程不变，直接读取标准 LightGBM 与 XGBoost 模型。理论上给出每树工作量分解为常量投影子树规模 |T_c|、G 次叶子写入与谓词掩码供给开销 Q；对 trace 求值器，当 G→∞ 时每行工作量趋于行独立遍历的 (d_v+1)/(d+1)。

Result: 在 Intel 上，参考配置（T=500, L=8）下比行独立遍历快 2.5-3.2×，生存数据集 G=128 时快 6.8-7.8×，Arm 上收益更大；情景分析基准在两种架构的 16 种配置下均更快。数值方面，f64 模型输出与 treelite GTIL 在求和顺序范围内一致；f32 模型上，f64 累加在 99.98% 的行上比原生 f32 更接近 Kahan 补偿参考，且从不更差。

Conclusion: TreeWalker 通过部分求值消除分组推理中的共享冗余，在保持训练不变与标准模型兼容的前提下，于多种架构和负载上显著加速，同时保持或提升数值精度，适合实际部署的树集成分组推理场景。

Abstract: Many inference workloads evaluate a trained tree ensemble on row groups that share feature values: discrete-time survival models expand each patient into $G$ time steps, click-through-rate models score every item in a search session, and scenario analyses vary a few inputs while holding the rest fixed. Standard inference treats each row independently and repeats the shared work $G$ times.
  We present TreeWalker, which applies partial evaluation to grouped inference: constant features are static, varying features dynamic. It walks each tree once per group, partitions a row bitmask at varying splits, and skips empty subtrees. Training is unchanged: TreeWalker reads standard LightGBM and XGBoost models.
  We prove a structural work decomposition: per-tree work splits into the constant-projected subtree size $|T_c|$, $G$ leaf writes, and a predicate-mask provisioning cost $Q$. For the trace evaluator, per-row work approaches a $(d_v+1)/(d+1)$ fraction of a row-independent walk as $G \to \infty$.
  On Intel, TreeWalker is 2.5-3.2$\times$ faster than a row-independent traversal at the reference configuration ($T=500$, $L=8$) and 6.8-7.8$\times$ faster at $G=128$ on the survival datasets, with larger gains on Arm. On a scenario-analysis benchmark it is faster in all 16 configurations on both architectures. For f64 models, outputs match treelite's GTIL up to summation order; for f32 models, f64 accumulation is closer to a Kahan-compensated reference than native f32 on 99.98% of rows and never farther.

</details>


### [276] [DePICT: Decision-Preserving Interface for Constrained Downstream Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03945)
*Utkarsh Grover,Ravi Ranjan,Agoritsa Polyzou,Wyatt T. Mackey,J. Morris Chang,Leonardo Bobadilla,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DePICT，一种通过按优化器解敏感性对上下文方向排序并跨运行区间聚合，从而构造“决策保持接口”的方法，在高维约束优化设定下刻画并剔除不影响最终决策的上下文方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束优化问题的目标与活跃约束中可能包含参数，但最终决策对这些参数的小扰动可能不敏感，因此需要回答：决策系统真正依赖哪些输入？目标是识别并保留对最优解有实质影响的上下文方向。

Method: 面向高维设定，原始上下文参数化一个约束任务，下游智能体仅观测被选中的上下文方向子集。对局部正则约束程序，基于 KKT 条件推导“上下文方向与优化器相关”的判定准则，据此用解敏感性对上下文方向排序，并在一个运行区间上聚合，构造决策保持接口 DePICT。

Result: 分析表明，出现在活跃优化问题中并不必然意味着该变量影响最终决策：某些上下文方向会改变 KKT 条件，但由于其影响被对偶变量吸收，最优解不变；DePICT 正是用于剔除这些方向。在受控诊断实验中，DePICT 精确恢复决策相关接口，并将线性预测器后悔降至 0.009，而最强竞争基线为 0.475。

Conclusion: 决策相关性不能由“是否出现在活跃问题中”判定，需要基于 KKT 的解敏感性分析；DePICT 能有效识别并移除被对偶变量吸收的方向，实现决策保持接口，显著优于已有基线。

Abstract: A constrained optimization problem may involve a parameter in its objective and active constraints, yet the final decision may remain insensitive to small changes in that parameter. This raises a fundamental question: which inputs does a decision making system truly depend on? Building on this question, we introduce DePICT, a procedure for constructing decision preserving interfaces by ranking context directions according to the optimizer's solution sensitivity and aggregating them across an operating regime. We study this problem in a high dimensional setting where primitive context parameterizes a constrained task and the downstream agent observes only a selected subset of context directions. For locally regular constrained programs, we derive a Karush Kuhn Tucker (KKT) based characterization of when a context direction is optimizer relevant. Our analysis shows that appearing in the active optimization problem does not necessarily imply that a variable affects the final decision. Some context directions can alter the KKT conditions while leaving the optimal solution unchanged because their effect is absorbed by the dual variables. DePICT is designed to remove exactly these directions. In a controlled diagnosis, it recovers the decision relevant interface exactly and reduces linear predictor regret to 0.009, compared with 0.475 for the strongest competing baseline.

</details>


### [277] [Synthesizing Physics Formulae with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03947)
*Shuwei Wang,Vadim Bulitko,Michael Youngblood,Ramon Lawrence,William Yeoh,Shinichi Nakagawa,Matthew R. G. Brown,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过设计训练数据中的公式集合并对目标值加噪微调，提升了符号回归Transformer的外推能力和抗噪性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归旨在从输入-输出对中找到紧凑公式并预测未见输入。基于搜索的方法直接探索公式空间，而预训练Transformer更快但易过拟合，所生成公式难以外推到训练时未见过的输入范围。作者希望解决该外推性差的瓶颈。

Method: 通过塑造（shaping）用于训练Transformer的公式集合来改善外推；并进一步用带噪声污染目标值的数据微调Transformer，使合成公式对观测噪声具有鲁棒性。

Result: 在SRBench和LLM-SRBench上，该Transformer约十秒即可合成一个公式，在相当预算下外推表现优于所有被评估方法；基于搜索的方法只有在多出一到三个数量级时间时才在精度上超过它。

Conclusion: 合理设计训练公式分布并结合噪声微调，可显著提升Transformer符号回归的外推能力和鲁棒性，在效率与性能上取得优势。

Abstract: Finding a compact formula that fits a set of input-output pairs and predicts outputs on unseen inputs is a fundamental problem in science. Symbolic regression automates the search for such formulae: search-based methods explore the space of possible formulae directly, while transformers pre-trained on synthetic data produce formulae of comparable quality substantially faster. Existing transformers, however, are prone to overfitting --- they find formulae that fit the training data well but do not extrapolate to input ranges unseen during training. We address this by shaping the set of formulae used to train a transformer, and show that the resulting formulae extrapolate substantially better. Fine-tuning the transformer on data with noise-corrupted target values further makes the synthesized formulae robust to noise in the observations. On SRBench and LLM-SRBench our transformer synthesizes a formula in about ten seconds and extrapolates better than all evaluated methods at a comparable budget. Search-based methods surpass our accuracy only when given one to three orders of magnitude more time.

</details>


### [278] [Evolving LLM-Generated Features for Interpretable Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03951)
*Jack Butler,Zainab Afolabi,Nikita Kozodoi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出进化式框架，用LLM迭代发现自然语言特征定义（评分规则），训练透明分类器（如逻辑回归），在三个基准（含信用风险）上超越单次生成规则并优于零样本LLM分类，且提供可审计决策逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为分类器日益普及，但其决策不透明，难以在信用评分、医疗诊断等受监管领域使用，需要可解释的分类方案。

Method: 一、LLM生成候选二值特征；二、对每个样本评估特征得到特征向量，用于训练透明分类器（如逻辑回归）；三、特征集在多轮迭代中进化，依据分类误差、各类激活率与特征消融得分指导。对比单次LLM评分规则、进化评分规则与零样本LLM直接分类。

Result: 进化特征较单次评分规则平均提升+2.9个百分点，在三个任务中的两个上超过零样本LLM分类，并提供完全可审计的决策逻辑。信用风险任务中，零样本LLM接近随机水平（50.7%）且强烈偏向单一类别，而进化特征实现平衡且可解释的预测；该失败在总体准确率上不可见，只有在逐类审计时才暴露。

Conclusion: 当标签边界无法仅从类别名称推断，或LLM缺乏可靠领域推理能力时，进化方法最有效；逐类审计对发现隐藏偏差至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as classifiers, yet they operate as opaque systems whose decisions are difficult to interpret, which complicates their use in regulated domains such as credit scoring or medical diagnosis. We propose an evolutionary framework that iteratively discovers natural language feature definitions (rubrics) for interpretable classification. An LLM generates candidate binary features, evaluates each sample against them, and the resulting vectors can be used to train a transparent classifier such as logistic regression. The feature set evolves over multiple iterations guided by classification errors, per-class activation rates, and feature ablation scores. We evaluate across three benchmarks, including a credit risk dataset representative of regulated domains, comparing single-shot LLM rubrics, evolved rubrics, and direct zero-shot LLM classification. Evolved features improve over single-shot rubrics by +2.9 pp on average and outperform zero-shot LLM classification on two of three tasks, while providing fully auditable decision logic. On the credit risk task, the zero-shot LLM performs at chance (50.7%) with a strong bias toward a single class, whereas evolved features achieve balanced, interpretable predictions. Crucially, this failure is invisible in aggregate accuracy and surfaces only under per-class auditing. Analysis reveals that evolution is most effective when label boundaries cannot be inferred from category names alone or when the LLM lacks reliable domain-specific reasoning.

</details>


### [279] [One-Step Curvature Probes Miss the Fitting Operator: Retained Capacity and Terminal Null-Space Correction for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03952)
*Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Ibne Farabi Shihab,Md Najmus Swaqeeb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"终端"视角分析持续学习遗忘：旧任务损失由保留拟合能力、端点方向曲率与优化选中的零空间分量共同决定，并据此设计零空间修正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步曲率探针只评估初始方向，而持续学习者的性能是在达到可比新任务拟合后判定的，二者判据不一致，需建立刻画"终端"行为的理论框架。

Method: 在过参数化线性化设定下推导投影梯度下降的收敛解Δ_P=PJ^T(JPJ^T)^{-1}r，将终端旧任务二次型比值分解为保留拟合容量c_P(r)与端点曲率因子G_end(P,r)的乘积，并进一步分离出曲率最优拟合下界与算法相关的零空间超额，据此提出在Jacobian批次上保持新任务输出的终端零空间修正。

Result: rank-one情形下G_end等于一步探针增益，多输出时两者可不同；受控实验验证局部二次近似，投影大幅降低匹配范数曲率却几乎不改变终端遗忘；73/99匹配对中投影带来更大带符号旧任务损失变化；Permuted MNIST与Split CIFAR-100上166/180组方法-数据集-种子对在修正后旧任务损失下降（描述性、受测试条件约束）。

Conclusion: 终端代价由保留拟合容量、端点方向曲率及优化所选零空间分量共同决定；匹配探针增益不足以预测遗忘，固定秩比较与终端零空间修正可区分并缓解遗忘。

Abstract: A one-step curvature probe evaluates an initial direction, whereas continual learners are judged after reaching comparable new-task fit. In an overparameterized linearization, projected gradient descent converges to $Δ_P=PJ^\top(JPJ^\top)^{-1}r$, and its squared-displacement inflation is exactly the reciprocal of the retained fitting capacity $c_P(r)$. More generally, the terminal old-task quadratic ratio factorizes as $1/[c_P(r)G_{\rm end}(P,r)]$, where $G_{\rm end}$ compares curvature along endpoint directions. In the rank-one case, $G_{\rm end}$ equals the one-step probe gain; for multiple outputs, the two gains can differ. The terminal quadratic also separates into a curvature-optimal fitting floor and an algorithm-dependent null-space excess, motivating terminal null-space correction, which preserves linearized new-task outputs on its Jacobian batch. Controlled checks validate the local quadratic and show that projection can greatly reduce matched-norm curvature while barely changing terminal forgetting. Across common-threshold configurations, projection yields the larger signed old-task loss change in 73/99 matched pairs, retained fitting capacity falls with rank, and fixed-rank comparisons separate terminal forgetting even when probe gain is approximately matched. On Permuted MNIST and Split CIFAR-100, terminal correction decreases signed old-task loss in 166/180 method--dataset--seed pairs under the all-seed intention-to-correct analysis; because acceptance and outcome reporting use the same held-out split, this result is descriptive and test-conditioned. Overall, terminal cost depends jointly on retained fitting capacity, endpoint-direction curvature, and the null-space component selected by optimization.

</details>


### [280] [Probabilistic Algorithms for Ising Machines from Optimization to Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03972)
*Corentin Delacour,Xiuqi Zhang,Abdelrahman S. Abdelrahman,Saleh Bunaiyan,Kyle Lee,Shuvro Chowdhury,Kerem Y. Camsari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，主张以"算法-硬件协同设计"的自顶向下视角审视Ising机上的概率算法，强调有理论保证的方法，并展望协同设计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ising机的实际影响力日益取决于算法与硬件的协同设计：算法需求驱动新架构，新硬件能力又激发全新算法。作者希望为下一代Ising机的能力提升与推广提供协同设计框架。

Method: 综述面向多种Ising平台可移植的概率算法：涵盖模拟退火、并行回火等基础方法；包括可原生编码硬约束的二维扩展；以及从聚类平均场方法到变分采样器等扩大可解问题规模的方法；并重点讨论PAOA（QAOA的经典类比）及生成式AI与Ising机的相互促进机制。

Result: 梳理了概率算法的谱系与可移植性特征，指出PAOA直接源于概率硬件发展；提出学习模型可提供全局移动以加速优化，而概率技术可改进大语言模型推断，形成双向增益。

Conclusion: 正如量子计算中算法与硬件共同演化，概率与Ising计算正处于类似拐点；作者提出协同设计框架以加速下一代Ising机的能力与应用普及。

Abstract: Ising machines have emerged as promising hardware accelerators for intractable optimization and sampling problems, yet their practical impact increasingly hinges on the co-design of algorithms and hardware, where algorithmic demands shape new architectures and new hardware capabilities inspire entirely new algorithms. In this Review, we survey probabilistic algorithms designed for portability across diverse Ising platforms, advocating a top-down perspective that prioritizes principled methods with provable guarantees. We cover foundational methods such as simulated annealing and parallel tempering, including two-dimensional extensions that natively encode hard constraints, and examine approaches that expand the scale of solvable problems from cluster mean-field methods to variational samplers. We highlight the Probabilistic Approximate Optimization Algorithm (PAOA), a classical analog of QAOA that emerged directly from probabilistic hardware development, and explore how generative AI and Ising machines might reinforce each other: learned models propose global moves to accelerate optimization, while probabilistic techniques improve inference in large language models. Much as quantum computing has seen algorithms co-evolve with hardware, probabilistic and Ising computing stand at a similar inflection point. We outline a co-design framework for accelerating the capabilities and adoption of next-generation Ising machines.

</details>


### [281] [Learning Latent Protein Languages for Autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03978)
*Mahdi Pourmirzaei,Farzaneh Esmaili,Amir Ziashahabi,Mohammadreza Pourmirzaei,Dong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出两种学习的潜在蛋白质语言（PLL 和 SLL），将序列与结构映射为离散的上下文词元，并用自回归 Transformer（PLLM、SLLM）在潜在词元上预训练。结果表明，潜在表示显著改善自回归生成的计算扩展效率、样本质量、结构预测困惑度与采样速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 Transformer 在蛋白质序列与结构生成上表现相对较弱。作者关注"目标表示"这一因素：氨基酸词元仅编码残基身份，缺乏显式上下文语义；而骨架坐标在自回归框架中又需要离散化表示。因此需要一种兼具语义上下文与可离散建模的表示方式。

Method: 1) 提出 PLL（Protein Latent Language）：基于冻结的 ESM-2 编码器，将序列映射为每残基一个词元、共 4,096 状态的上下文词表；2) 提出 SLL（Structure Latent Language）：在 GCP-VQVAE Lite 基础上加入序列与置信度辅助监督，并保留到骨架坐标的解码能力；3) 分别在 PLL 与 SLL 词元上用下一词元预测预训练自回归 Transformer，得到 PLLM 与 SLLM；4) 在匹配的下游序列训练、无条件序列生成、序列到结构预测、长蛋白采样效率以及骨架生成等任务上评估。

Result: - 匹配下游序列训练下，PLLM 拟合的计算扩展指数为 0.038，而氨基酸自回归模型为 0.020；- 无条件序列生成中，相对氨基酸模型，PLLM 在采样温度范围内将低于启发式 1.5-bit 残基组成熵阈值的样本比例降低 54%；- 序列到结构预测中，用 SLL 替换原 GCP-VQVAE Lite 分词器，在匹配训练下最佳验证困惑度降低 34%；- 长蛋白上，潜在词元采样比基于 MSA 的 AlphaFold2 快约 1,000 倍；- 骨架生成中，SLLM 在多样性与新颖性上可与其他生成模型相媲美；- 观察到使用 SLLM 内部词元置信度进行推理时采样，可能超越单次解码样本的序列到结构预测质量。

Conclusion: 学习的潜在蛋白质语言是一种有前景的底层表示，可用于自回归 Transformer 的扩展与蛋白质生成中的推理时采样。

Abstract: Autoregressive transformers remain comparatively weak for protein sequence and structure generation. We study the role of target representation: amino acid tokens encode residue identities without explicit contextual semantics, while backbone coordinates require a discrete representation in our framework. We introduce two learned latent protein languages. Protein Latent Language (PLL) maps sequences to a 4,096-state contextual alphabet built on a frozen ESM-2 encoder, with one token per residue. Structure Latent Language (SLL) adapts GCP-VQVAE Lite with auxiliary sequence and confidence supervision while retaining decoding to backbone coordinates. We separately pretrain autoregressive transformer models on PLL and SLL tokens using next-token prediction, yielding PLLM and SLLM. Under matched downstream sequence training, PLLM has a fitted compute-scaling exponent of 0.038 versus 0.020 for the amino acid autoregressive model. In unconditional sequence generation, PLLM reduces the fraction of samples below a heuristic 1.5-bit residue-composition entropy threshold by 54% relative to the amino acid model across sampling temperatures. For sequence-to-structure prediction, replacing the original GCP-VQVAE Lite tokenizer with SLL reduces best validation perplexity by 34% under matched training. For long proteins, latent-token sampling is approximately 1,000 times faster than MSA-based AlphaFold2 in our measurements. In backbone generation, SLLM compares favorably with other generative models on diversity and novelty. We also observe early signs that using SLLM's internal token confidence for inference-time sampling can improve sequence-to-structure prediction quality beyond a single decoded sample. These results position learned latent protein languages as a promising substrate for autoregressive transformer scaling and inference-time sampling in protein generation.

</details>


### [282] [$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ Regret and Polylogarithmic Constraint Violation for COCO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03983)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Abhishek Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带约束的在线凸优化（COCO），提出一种新策略：在保持 O(√(T log T)) 遗憾的同时，将累积约束违反（CCV）从 Õ(√T) 降低到 O(log² T)，实现从多项式到多对数的突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 COCO 算法虽能达到 O(√T) 遗憾与 Õ(√T) 的累积约束违反，但 CCV 仍随 T 多项式增长，约束满足程度不足。论文旨在显著降低 CCV，同时保持近似最优的遗憾界。

Method: 结合连续 Hedge 与在收缩可行集上的消除机制。核心观察：当 Hedge 分布的均值违反约束时，由 Grünbaum 不等式可保证常数比例的概率质量被消除。采用自适应学习率调度，并构造耦合剩余体积与学习率的势函数，将概率质量削减转化为 CCV 界。

Result: 所提出的在线策略实现 O(√(T log T)) 遗憾和 O(log² T) 累积约束违反，将 CCV 从多项式级降至多对数级，同时保持近最优遗憾。

Conclusion: 通过连续 Hedge 与收缩可行集消除机制及势函数分析，可在近最优遗憾下大幅降低 COCO 的约束违反，为约束在线优化提供更优的遗憾-违反权衡。

Abstract: We study constrained online convex optimization with adversarial convex losses and constraints ($\mathsf{COCO}$). At each round \(t\in[T]\), a learner selects \(x_t\) from a \(d\)-dimensional convex decision set \(\mathcal X\), after which an adaptive adversary reveals a convex cost function \(f_t\) and constraint function \(g_t\). Consequently, the learner incurs cost \(f_t(x_t)\) and constraint violation \(\max\{0,g_t(x_t)\}\), and aims to simultaneously minimize regret and cumulative constraint violation ($\mathsf{CCV}$) over the entire horizon. Existing algorithms achieve \(O(\sqrt{T})\) regret and \(\widetilde O(\sqrt{T})\) $\mathsf{CCV}$. We show that an online policy can achieve \(O(\sqrt{T\log T})\) regret and \(O(\log^2 T)\) $\mathsf{CCV}$, reducing the $\mathsf{CCV}$ from polynomial to polylogarithmic while retaining near-optimal regret. Our approach combines continuous Hedge with elimination on shrinking feasible sets. The key observation is that whenever the mean of the Hedge distribution violates a constraint, Grünbaum's inequality guarantees that a constant fraction of the Hedge probability mass is eliminated. We use an adaptive learning-rate schedule and a potential function coupling the surviving volume with the learning rate to convert this probability-mass reduction into a bound of \(O(\log^2 T)\) on the $\mathsf{CCV}$.

</details>


### [283] [Selective Backpropagation for Efficient Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04003)
*Eeham Khan,Abdulmoumen Al-Atrash,Ali Ayub*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出选择性反向传播（SBP），一种确定性参数预算框架，通过将梯度更新限制在预分配参数子集上，在少样本类增量学习中实现稳定性、可塑性与效率的良好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FSCIL方法面临困难权衡：简单微调计算高效但存在灾难性遗忘；回放方法缓解遗忘但计算与内存开销大；无样本方法常冻结大部分骨干以减少遗忘，效率提升但可塑性受损。需一种兼顾三者的方案。

Method: SBP为确定性参数预算框架，将梯度更新限制在预先分配的网络参数子集上，冻结历史知识并为未来学习保留无偏容量，无需昂贵的掩码优化即可实现快速适应。

Result: 在标准FSCIL基准上性能强劲，训练时间接近朴素微调、显著低于先前SOTA。实验揭示标准FSCIL评估的局限：短程、分布一致基准上的表现不能预测分布偏移或更长学习周期的行为。在跨域设置及80会话ImageNet-1K流上SBP依然保持强劲且训练成本低。

Conclusion: SBP在稳定性-可塑性-效率之间取得有利权衡，并呼吁在跨域与长周期场景下更全面地评估FSCIL方法。代码已开源。

Abstract: Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning (FSCIL) requires models to continuously learn new classes from limited samples while retaining prior knowledge, under strict constraints on compute and memory. Existing approaches lie along a difficult trade-off: simple fine-tuning is computationally efficient but suffers from catastrophic forgetting, replay-based methods mitigate forgetting at the cost of substantial compute and memory, and exemplar-free methods often reduce forgetting by freezing most of the backbone, improving efficiency at the expense of adaptability. We propose Selective Backpropagation (SBP), a deterministic parameter budgeting framework that bridges this gap. SBP restricts gradient updates to a pre-allocated subset of network parameters, freezing past knowledge and preserving unbiased capacity for future learning, enabling rapid adaptation without costly mask optimization. We show that SBP achieves strong performance across standard FSCIL benchmarks while requiring training time close to that of naive fine-tuning and substantially lower training time than prior SOTA methods. Crucially, our experiments expose a limitation of standard FSCIL evaluation: performance on short, distribution-consistent benchmarks does not necessarily predict behavior under distribution shift or over substantially longer learning horizons. We therefore evaluate FSCIL methods in cross-domain settings and over an 80-session ImageNet-1K stream. SBP remains strong across these regimes while maintaining low training cost, providing a favorable stability-plasticity-efficiency trade-off. Our code is available at https://github.com/PaInt-Lab/sbp-main-public.

</details>


### [284] [Slaying the Hydra: Interaction-Aware Circuit Discovery in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04017)
*Sankaran Vaidyanathan,Rafal Urbaniak,Emily Bunnapradist,Michelangelo Naim,Daniel Waxman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WISE因果估计量与JuntaLearner电路发现方法，通过集合级交互建模与见证机制，在可扩展性下提升电路识别的忠实性、必要性和任务特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机械可解释性研究逐组件打分定位语言模型行为，忽略上下文依赖效应：主组件会抑制备份组件激活，导致组件排名失准。实际因果论用witness（见证）刻画交互结构，但估计需组合枚举，实践中不可行。

Method: 提出witness-integrated set effect (WISE)，一类因果估计量，在原因集合与见证集合上取期望，使计算可行；基于此提出JuntaLearner，一种基于梯度的电路发现方法，学习在不同规模组件集合与见证集合中对组件因果影响排序。同时引入必要性、任务特异性指标及电路识别分数CRS，在强调小电路效应的同时汇总各规模下的指标。

Result: 在多种任务与不断增大的模型上，JuntaLearner在所有指标上的平均CRS均高于归因基线；其成本不随候选组件数量增长，可扩展到大模型，同时考虑集合级交互并避免一阶近似。

Conclusion: WISE与JuntaLearner为考虑集合级交互的电路发现提供了可行且可扩展的框架，并通过CRS等指标更有效地评估小规模电路，优于归因基线。

Abstract: Localizing behavior to individual components of a language model is a central goal of mechanistic interpretability. However, scoring components one at a time misses context-dependent effects: a primary component can inhibit the activation of a backup, leading to issues with ranking components. Actual causality studies the structure of such interactions via witnesses: variables that provide contextual information to resolve interaction terms. However, estimation with witnesses typically requires combinatorial enumeration and is infeasible in practice. We introduce the witness-integrated set effect (WISE), a family of causal estimands that build on the witness mechanism while taking expectations over sets of causes and witnesses to remain computationally feasible. Building on this approach, we introduce JuntaLearner, a gradient-based circuit discovery method that learns to rank components by their causal impact across varying-sized sets of components and witnesses. Alongside faithfulness metrics, we introduce measures of necessity and task specificity, and the circuit recognition score (CRS) to summarize each metric across circuit sizes while emphasizing effects achieved by small circuits. Across tasks and models of increasing size, JuntaLearner achieves higher mean CRS compared to attribution baselines on all metrics. Since its cost does not grow with the number of candidate components, JuntaLearner scales to large models while accounting for set-level interactions and avoiding first-order approximations.

</details>


### [285] [LD-EnFF: Latent-Dynamics Ensemble Flow Filtering for Data Assimilation with Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04034)
*Ziyu Tian,Kaichen Shen,Wenbo Hao,Phillip Si,Peng Chen,Wei Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LD-EnFF，一种在紧凑潜空间中执行预测传播与滤波更新的序贯贝叶斯滤波框架，结合潜动力学代理模型与VAE观测模型，用流匹配集合滤波生成后验样本，在多个复杂动力学稀疏观测基准上大幅优于现有数据同化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据同化需融合模型预测与稀疏含噪观测以估计动力系统状态，但现有方法面临双重挑战：高维非线性动力学导致重复前向模拟计算昂贵；稀疏观测对完整状态提供的直接信息有限。

Method: LD-EnFF在紧凑潜空间中同时完成预测传播与滤波更新：用潜动力学代理模型进行集合传播，用基于变分自编码器（VAE）的观测模型在潜空间评估状态依赖的观测似然；每个同化步基于流匹配的集合滤波更新，利用预测集合与似然生成后验样本，联合更新潜状态与不确定参数，从而避免预测阶段的重复全状态模拟与似然评估阶段的全场重构。

Result: 在涵盖Kolmogorov流、海啸传播与大气建模等具有复杂动力学及稀疏含噪观测的基准上，LD-EnFF大幅优于多种数据同化算法。

Conclusion: 在潜空间统一进行传播与同化可有效缓解高维非线性模拟成本与稀疏观测信息不足的问题，实现高效且高精度的序贯状态与参数联合估计。

Abstract: Data assimilation combines model forecasts with noisy, incomplete observations to estimate the evolving state of a dynamical system. Existing methods face two compounding challenges: high-dimensional nonlinear dynamics make repeated forward simulation computationally expensive, while sparse observations provide limited direct information about the full state. To address these challenges, we propose the Latent-Dynamics Ensemble Flow Filter (LD-EnFF), a sequential Bayesian filtering framework that performs both forecast propagation and filtering updates in a compact latent space. LD-EnFF combines a latent dynamics surrogate for ensemble propagation with a variational autoencoder (VAE)-based observation model that evaluates a state-dependent observation likelihood in latent space. At each assimilation step, an ensemble filtering update based on flow matching uses the forecast ensemble and this likelihood to generate posterior samples, jointly updating latent states and uncertain parameters. This design avoids repeated full-state simulation during forecasting and full-field reconstruction during likelihood evaluation. LD-EnFF substantially outperforms a broad range of data assimilation algorithms on benchmarks spanning Kolmogorov flow, tsunami propagation, and atmospheric modeling, all featuring complex dynamics and sparse, noisy observations.

</details>


### [286] [Protecting Sensitive Data in Image Synthesis via PAC-Private Adaptation for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04038)
*Boming Miao,Tao Zhang,Netanel Raviv,Murat Kantarcioglu,Bradley A. Malin,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种PAC私有的扩散模型适配方法，通过直接面向后验优势（posterior advantage）实现重建隐私，避免DP-SGD反复梯度裁剪与加噪带来的效用损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据常被用作敏感记录的替代品，但基于敏感数据训练或适配的扩散模型仍易受重建攻击。DP-SGD类方法虽可提供可证明隐私，但其隐私定义（输出对单条记录变化的敏感度）与重建隐私（限制后验分布与先验的差异）之间的关系是间接的，这种"间接性"是效用损失的重要来源。

Method: 提出面向重建隐私的PAC私有扩散模型适配：先使用LoRA或Textual Inversion学习紧凑的、依赖数据的扩散模型组件，再根据重复机制输出的协方差校准各向异性高斯噪声。与DP-SGD不同，该方法在优化完成后仅对学习到的组件扰动一次，从而避免跨梯度更新的隐私组合。

Result: 在少样本概念个性化与全数据集图像合成任务上评估，所提方法在达到与DP相同重建隐私水平的同时，更好地保持了主体身份、生成质量以及下游分类准确率。

Conclusion: 直接以PAC隐私刻画重建隐私，并在高维空间中实现可扩展的私有化，是一种在隐私与效用间取得更优权衡的扩散模型适配方案。

Abstract: Synthetic data are increasingly used as an alternative to sharing sensitive records. However, synthetic data generation does not guarantee privacy, as diffusion models trained or adapted on sensitive data remain susceptible to reconstruction attacks. Moreover, while approaches that use differential privacy (DP), such as DP-SGD, achieve provably private diffusion model training, the repeated gradient clipping and noise injection they require result in significant utility loss. An important limitation of DP-based privacy is that, although it has a provable relationship to reconstruction privacy (RP), that relationship is indirect. RP is defined in terms of limiting how much an adversary's posterior distribution over sensitive data differs from the prior, whereas DP provides guarantees by bounding the sensitivity of outputs to changes in individual records. This indirection is an important source of the utility loss. To address this, we propose a PAC-private diffusion model adaptation to achieve reconstruction privacy. Since PAC-privacy is defined directly with respect to posterior advantage over the prior, it directly implicates RP. To obtain scalable PAC privatization in high dimensions, we first learn a compact data-dependent diffusion model component using LoRA or Textual Inversion, and then calibrate anisotropic Gaussian noise from the covariance of repeated mechanism outputs. Unlike DP-SGD, our method perturbs the learned component only once after optimization, thereby avoiding privacy composition across gradient updates. We evaluate the framework on few-shot concept personalization and full-dataset image synthesis, and show that the proposed approach better preserves subject identity, generation quality, and downstream classification accuracy than DP while achieving the same reconstruction privacy.

</details>


### [287] [On architectural choices for interpretability and thermodynamic consistency in Physically Recurrent Neural Networks in the low-data regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04067)
*M. A. Maia,K. A. Meyer,A. M. C. M. van Gils,I. B. C. M. Rocha,F. P. van der Meer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究不同解码器架构对物理循环神经网络（Physically Recurrent Neural Network）潜空间可解释性的影响，提出一种新的权重归一化约束以改善低数据训练，并通过伴随编码器-解码器结构保证热力学一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升物理循环神经网络潜空间的可解释性，并在低数据条件下实现稳健训练，同时借助合理的架构选择提高数据效率与物理一致性。

Method: 分析多种解码器架构对潜空间的影响；引入新的权重归一化约束作为正则化手段；采用多任务方法从微观尺度提取信息而不增加参数或训练集；通过强制伴随编码器-解码器结构与正标量贡献，实现能量一致与耗散非负的热力学一致表述。

Result: 可视化显示架构改变对潜空间的影响，虚构应力可在未显式训练下与RVE真实状态对齐；多任务方法无需额外参数或更大训练集即可引入微观信息；特定架构自然导出热力学一致公式，保证跨尺度能量一致与非负耗散，且进一步降低训练需求。

Conclusion: 研究表明，植根于底层物理的谨慎架构选择可同时提升可解释性、归纳偏置、热力学一致性与数据效率。

Abstract: In this paper, we unravel the effect of different decoder architectures on the interpretability of the latent space of the Physically Recurrent Neural Network. Particular emphasis is given to a new weight normalization constraint, which acts as a regularization technique and enables robust training in the low-data regime. A brief visual exploration illustrates how these changes impact the latent space and how the fictitious stress can align with the true state of the RVE without explicit training. Reaping the benefits of a meaningful latent space, a case study illustrates how information from the microscopic level can be retrieved and incorporated into a multi-task approach that does not require extra parameters or larger training sets. Another key contribution shows that a specific architectural choice can naturally lead to a thermodynamically consistent formulation. By enforcing an adjoint encoder-decoder structure with positive scalar contributions, this modification ensures energy consistency across scales and non-negative dissipation, leading to even lower training requirements. This alternative completes the study on interpretability, inductive bias, and thermodynamic consistency, and demonstrates that data efficiency can be improved with careful architectural choices rooted in the underlying physics.

</details>


### [288] [Articulatory Entrainment and Coordination Complexity in Spontaneous Autistic and Non-autistic Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04071)
*Thanushi Withanage,Carol Espy-Wilson,Elizabeth Redcay,Desi Jones,Noah Sasson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种说话人无关框架，结合声学到发音反演与协调复杂度指标，量化自闭症与非自闭症成人自发对话中的发音同步，发现非自闭症配对同步随时间增强，混合配对最弱，且发音同步与对话成功感正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发音同步（发音器官协调随互动而适应）在自发对话中研究不足，尤其针对自闭症说话者；既有研究多依赖任务化、音素层面分析和侵入式测量，缺乏生态效度与说话人无关的量化手段。

Method: 提出说话人无关框架：对自闭症与非自闭症成人双人自发对话录音，采用声学到发音反演（acoustic-to-articulatory inversion）获取发音运动信息，并用协调复杂度指标刻画发音协调，考察互动过程中发音协调的时间变化。

Result: 非自闭症配对表现出随时间增强的协调复杂度与更强的发音同步；自闭症配对效应中等；混合配对（自闭症-非自闭症）对齐程度最低。发音同步越强，被试自评的对话成功度越高。

Conclusion: 协调复杂度的提升可作为有效社会互动的标志；发音同步可用于量化自发对话中的互动质量，并为自闭症社交沟通研究提供非侵入、说话人无关的分析路径。

Abstract: Articulatory entrainment, the adaptation of vocal tract coordination to facilitate interaction remains underexplored in spontaneous dialogue, particularly among autistic speakers. Many prior studies have utilized task-based, phoneme-level analyses with invasive measurement techniques. Here, we introduce a speaker-independent framework to quantify articulatory entrainment in spontaneous dyadic conversations among autistic and non-autistic adults using acoustic-to-articulatory inversion and coordination complexity metrics. We examine temporal changes in articulatory coordination across interaction. Non-autistic dyads exhibit increasing coordination complexity and stronger entrainment over time, autistic dyads show moderate effects, and mixed dyads demonstrate the least alignment. Greater articulatory entrainment correlates with higher self-reported conversational success, indicating increase in coordination complexity as a marker of effective social interaction.

</details>


### [289] [DUET: Co-Evolving Solver and Grader Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04087)
*Fengyu Gao,Sourav Pal,Austin Z. Henley,Arjun Radhakrishna,Gustavo Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DUET 框架，将求解器智能体（solver）与评估器智能体（grader）置于同一优化循环中联合迭代训练，使评估能力随智能体行为演进而自适应更新，在四个智能体基准上同时提升求解器与评估器性能，并优于固定评估器的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体工作流在技术、金融、企业运营等领域广泛部署，持续改进智能体成为迫切需求。但智能体演化面临两个相互耦合的挑战：其一，演化需要有效评估来度量结果并提供优化反馈；其二，随智能体行为与失败模式变化，固定评估器会逐渐失效。因此"智能体应如何演化"与"如何评估演化中的智能体"本质耦合——行为变化暴露评估器局限，更强评估器又为智能体改进提供更有效反馈。现有做法多将评估器视为固定反馈源，无法适应这一动态过程。

Method: 提出 DUET 框架，联合优化 solver agent 与 grader agent。流程为迭代式：选择训练任务→由 solver 执行→由 grader 评估结果→通过工具调用式（tool-using）更新模块修订 solver 与 grader，并在多轮间交替优化二者。核心设计是将 grader 纳入优化循环，使评估从固定的反馈来源转变为与 solver 协同适应的一等优化目标。

Result: 在四个智能体基准（agent benchmarks）上的实验表明，DUET 同时提升了 solver 与 grader 的性能，并持续优于"固定 grader 优化 solver"的基线方法。

Conclusion: 求解器与评估器的协同演化是提升智能体系统的有效路径；将评估器作为可优化对象纳入训练闭环，能获得比固定评估更优的智能体表现，为智能体的持续自我改进提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Agentic workflows are increasingly used across domains such as technology, finance, and enterprise operations. As these agents become more widely deployed, continually improving them becomes increasingly important. This raises an immediate challenge: How should the agent evolve? This evolution requires effective evaluation that can assess outcomes and provide useful feedback for optimization. As the agent evolves, its behaviors and failure modes may also change, making a fixed evaluator increasingly inadequate. Another fundamental question: How should we evaluate an evolving agent? These two challenges are inherently coupled; changes in agent behavior can expose limitations of the current evaluator, while a stronger evaluator provides more informative feedback for improving the agent. Motivated by this interaction, we introduce DUET, a framework that jointly optimizes a solver agent and a grader agent to improve both. DUET iteratively selects training tasks, executes them with the solver, evaluates the resulting outcomes with the grader, and uses a tool-using update module to revise the solver and the grader, alternating between the two across rounds. By updating the grader within the optimization loop, DUET turns evaluation from a fixed source of feedback into a first-class optimization objective that adapts alongside the solver. Experiments across four agent benchmarks show that DUET improves both solver and grader performance and consistently outperforms baselines that optimize the solver with a fixed grader.

</details>


### [290] [Pareto-Dominant Clarification: Post-Training Coding LLMs via PPO-Lagrangian Budget Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04089)
*Abhinav Rajput,Acey Vogelstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将澄清行为建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），用PPO-Lagrangian对Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct后训练，在显式问题预算约束下优化代码正确率，发现预算受限策略能同时提升准确率并降低提问率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在指令模糊时需决定是否提问澄清，而每次交互都带来用户与系统成本，形成准确率与效率的权衡。现有研究仅观察澄清行为，未在可强制执行的澄清预算下训练策略；基于惩罚的方法需针对每个预算水平单独调参并反复扫描，代价高昂。

Method: 将澄清问题形式化为CMDP，以期望提问预算为约束、代码准确率为优化目标，采用PPO-Lagrangian对Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct进行后训练；在HumanEvalComm数据集上以GPT-4o-mini作为oracle模拟器进行评估。

Result: 未调优的澄清行为呈帕累托低效：受预算约束的策略可同时获得更高准确率与更低澄清率。不同预算水平下呈现对数型帕累托前沿，澄清收益递减。性能提升并非源于整体提问更多，而是来自提问定位更准以及在模糊条件下更强的代码生成；策略在无显式监督下学会非均匀分配预算，在更难（多重退化）任务上提问更频繁。

Conclusion: 无约束的交互式LLM系统可能系统性地低效使用澄清。显式预算约束下的策略优化可同时改善准确率与交互效率，说明将澄清纳入约束优化框架是提升交互式编码智能体表现的有效途径。

Abstract: Coding agents operating under ambiguous instructions or user prompts must decide whether to ask clarifying questions or attempt a solution directly. While clarification from the user may improve the correctness of the agent's solution, each back-and-forth interaction incurs user and system costs, forming an explicit accuracy vs. efficiency tradeoff. Existing works study clarification behavior but do not train policies under enforceable clarification budgets; penalty-based approaches typically require separate coefficient tuning swept across all clarification budget levels. We formulate clarification as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP) and post-train Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct with PPO-Lagrangian to optimize coding accuracy, subject to an expected question-budget constraint. Evaluated on HumanEvalComm with a GPT-4o-mini oracle simulator, the resulting policies reveal that untuned clarification behavior is Pareto-inefficient: budget-constrained policies can simultaneously achieve higher accuracy and lower clarification rates than the baseline model. Across budget levels, we observe a log-shaped Pareto frontier with diminishing returns to additional clarification. Gains arise not from simply asking more questions overall, but from improved question targeting and better code generation under ambiguity. Without explicit supervision, trained policies learn to allocate clarification budget non-uniformly, asking more frequently on tougher (multi-degradation) tasks. These results suggest that unconstrained interactive LLM systems may systematically use clarification inefficiently.

</details>


### [291] [Robust blind unmixing: A geometric approach to overcoming basis variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04091)
*Dumitru Mirauta,Vladimir V. Gusev,Michael W. Gaultois,Matthew J. Rosseinsky,Yannis Goulermas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于度量空间视角的几何化解混方法，利用最优传输距离刻画基函数变化，通过最小化候选解类内方差进行优化，并在一维情形下高效实现，在随机高斯混合、模拟粉末X射线衍射及实验室高光谱成像任务中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信号分离（解混）在科学中普遍存在，如衍射或光谱识别混合物组分。最简单情形下测量信号为基础模式的线性组合。核心挑战在于基函数因噪声和测量物理过程而在不同混合物间发生变异，现有基于模型和参数化的方法依赖特定假设，应用领域受限。因此需要一种不依赖特定参数模型、更具通用性的解混框架。

Method: 提出新的几何解混方法，以度量空间视角看待测量数据生成过程，将关注点从参数化模型转向基变换与对应几何之间的通用关系。采用最优传输距离捕捉常见的基函数变化，并通过最小化候选解的类内方差来驱动优化。特别关注一维情形，因其实际重要性及已有高效的距离与传输映射算法。

Result: 在随机高斯混合模型、模拟粉末X射线衍射以及实验室高光谱成像数据集上的一系列解混任务中，所提方法均表现出有效性。

Conclusion: 该几何化度量空间方法突破了传统参数化模型在应用域上的限制，为解混问题提供了一种通用且有效的途径，并在多种实际与模拟任务中得到验证。

Abstract: Signal separation problems are common in science. A prominent example of this occurs during the use of diffraction or spectroscopy to identify the individual components of a mixture by measuring it. In the simplest case, the measured signal is a linear combination of basis patterns corresponding to the constituent parts. The unmixing problem is to infer all or some of these basis patterns and abundances of components from measurements of distinct mixtures. One of the core challenges of this task is the variation of the basis from mixture to mixture due to noise and the exact physics of the measurement process. This is usually addressed with tailored model-based and parametric methods that are then limited in use to specific application domains by the nature of the assumptions made. We propose a novel geometric approach to unmixing problems which views the generation of data during measurement through a metric space lens, thereby shifting the focus from parametrised models to a general relationship between basis transformations and the corresponding geometry. We take advantage of the optimal transport distances to capture commonly occurring basis variations, and use minimisation of in-class variance of candidate solutions to drive the optimisation. We pay special attention to the one-dimensional case due to its practical importance and availability of efficient distance and transport map routines. The effectiveness of our approach is demonstrated on a range of unmixing tasks using random Gaussian mixture models, simulated powder X-ray diffraction, and laboratory hyperspectral imaging datasets.

</details>


### [292] [Beyond Masked Sparsity: SNACK Enables Truly Sparse Neural Networks on GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04093)
*Jafar Badour,Maurice van Keulen,Elena Mocanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SNACK：一种真正稀疏的GPU层及配套COO格式SpMM CUDA内核SNACK-COO，仅存储与计算非零连接，在大模型训练与单流推理的高稀疏小批量场景下显著加速并降低内存与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络参数量持续增长，GPU训练与推理开销上升。稀疏神经网络与动态稀疏训练（DST）理论上可降本，但多数实现基于稠密张量上的二值掩码（mask over dense tensors），几乎无法兑现理论上的计算、内存和能耗节省。

Method: 提出SNACK——真正稀疏的GPU层，仅存储和计算非零连接；提供简洁的PyTorch API，在稀疏范式下完成连接重构与梯度反传；并实现SNACK-COO，一个COO格式的SpMM CUDA内核，采用batch-to-Streaming-Multiprocessor映射，面向大模型训练与单流推理典型的小批量、高稀疏场景进行调优。

Result: 内核层面：在95%稀疏度下，SNACK比掩码稠密基线（Dense+Mask）快至多7倍，并与cuSPARSE、Sputnik、FlashSparse具有竞争力。90%稀疏度下，单层SNACK训练相比Dense+Mask和全稠密层分别加速8倍与3.7倍，推理分别加速4倍与2倍，内存比稠密减少72%，能耗亦大幅降低。端到端：在99%稀疏度下，将GPT-2峰值训练内存最多降低40%，图式推理延迟相比Dense+Mask降低4.8倍。

Conclusion: SNACK通过端到端的真稀疏实现（存储、计算、API与定制CUDA内核协同设计），在保持竞争力的同时将稀疏性的理论收益转化为实际的计算、内存与能耗节省，可用于大模型训练与推理。

Abstract: Deep neural networks continue to grow in parameter count, driving up training and inference cost on GPUs. Sparse neural networks and Dynamic Sparse Training (DST) promise to reduce these costs, but most implementations rely on binary masks over dense tensors and recover little of the theoretical compute, memory, or energy savings. We propose SNACK, a truly sparse GPU layer that stores and computes only non-zero connections. SNACK exposes a simple PyTorch API for restructuring connections and backpropagating gradients entirely in the sparse paradigm, and ships SNACK-COO, a custom COO-format SpMM CUDA kernel with a batch-to-Streaming-Multiprocessor mapping tuned for the small-batch, high-sparsity regime typical of large-model training and single-stream inference. At the kernel level, SNACK is up to 7x faster than the masked dense baseline (Dense+Mask) and competitive with cuSPARSE, Sputnik, and FlashSparse at 95% sparsity. At 90% sparsity, a single SNACK layer accelerates training by 8x and 3.7x, and inference by 4x and 2x, over Dense+Mask and fully dense layers, respectively, while using 72% less memory than dense and substantially less energy. End-to-end, SNACK reduces GPT-2 peak training memory by up to 40% and graph-style inference latency by 4.8x over Dense+Mask at 99% sparsity.

</details>


### [293] [Progressive Multi-Ancestor Bit-Depth Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04100)
*Adil Mubashir Chaudhry,Osama Ahmad,Zubair Khalid,Murtaza Taj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一框架PMABD，通过渐进式多祖先位宽蒸馏稳定超低位量化压缩，性能超越现有压缩框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法同时进行FP32到INT4的转换与蒸馏，导致训练不稳定且预测性能大幅下降，需解决超低位量化的稳定性问题。

Method: PMABD渐进压缩网络，生成一系列中间教师模型，每个教师从所有更高精度祖先学习，并共同监督最终学生模型；多祖先多阶段设计降低量化噪声、稳定量化；引入基于饱和的停止准则。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100（ResNet-20/32/18）和Tiny-ImageNet（MobileNetV2）上超越SOTA压缩框架，W2A2（ResNet-18/CIFAR-100）学生模型性能提升1.06%；基于饱和的停止准则提升最终学生性能。

Conclusion: PMABD通过渐进多祖先位宽蒸馏有效稳定超低位量化，提升压缩模型预测性能，优于现有压缩框架。

Abstract: Model compression strategies are widely employed to reduce memory footprint and network complexity, particularly for devices with constrained computational, memory, and energy resources. Prior works that rely on simultaneous conversion from floating-point high-precision (FP32) to integer low-precision (INT4) representations and distillation into smaller models suffer from unstable training and drastic degradation of prediction performance. To address these limitations, we propose a unified framework, known as \textbf{P}rogressive \textbf{M}ulti-\textbf{A}ncestor \textbf{B}it-depth \textbf{D}istillation (PMABD), that progressively compresses the network while transferring knowledge through a growing pool of higher-precision ancestor teachers. PMABD generates a sequence of intermediate teachers that each learn from all higher-precision ancestors and jointly supervise the final target student. This multi-ancestor, multi-stage design stabilizes ultra-low-bit quantization by lowering quantization noise profiles across training and ensuring stable quantization. Experiments on CIFAR-10/100 with ResNet-20/32/18, and Tiny-ImageNet with MobileNetV2 show that PMABD outperforms state-of-the-art compression frameworks, results in 1.06$\%$ increase in performance of W2A2 (ResNet-18/CIFAR-100) student model. We show that a saturation-based stopping criterion contributes to improve the performance of our final student.

</details>


### [294] [Physics is the Best Teacher: Consistency Learning for Time-Invariant Operators of Chaotic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04108)
*Lufang Chiang,Jiachen Yao,Thomas Y. L. Lin,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将混沌系统长期行为预测建模为学习时间不变演化算子，通过一致性方程（含微分与复合目标）实现单次大跨度状态跳转，在五个混沌系统上提升了短期轨迹与长期统计精度，评估次数仅为自回归方法的十分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌系统长期行为预测在科学计算中至关重要，但现有方法依赖数值求解器或自回归模型，只能逐步小步推进，导致长时程预测计算代价高昂。

Method: 将问题重构为学习系统的时间不变演化算子，使其在一次评估中跨越大时间跨度跳转状态；推导该算子必须满足的一致性方程，包含物理时间上的微分目标与复合目标；该方程同时将学习算子与物理给定的瞬时动力学相联系，从而在一致性学习中嵌入物理约束。

Result: 在五个混沌系统上，物理蒸馏一致性使短期轨迹和长期统计均更准确；所学算子可经受时间外推，且所需评估次数仅为自回归推演的十分之一。

Conclusion: 基于时间不变算子与一致性学习的方法为混沌动力学长期模拟提供了高效路径，兼顾精度与计算效率。

Abstract: Accelerating the prediction of long-term behavior in chaotic systems is crucial in scientific computing. However, existing methods rely on numerical solvers or autoregressive models that advance one small step at a time, which makes long horizons expensive. We instead view this problem as learning the system's time-invariant evolution operator, which jumps the state across a large time span in a single evaluation. To this end, we derive the consistency equations a time-invariant operator must satisfy, with differential and compositional objectives in physical time. These equations also connect the learned operator to the physics-prescribed instant dynamics, enabling physics embedding in consistency learning. Across five chaotic systems, we find that physics-distilled consistency makes both short-term trajectories and long-term statistics more accurate. The learned operator survives temporal extrapolation and requires one-tenth as many evaluations as autoregressive rollout, offering an efficient route to long-term simulation of chaotic dynamics.

</details>


### [295] [SEER: Self-Evolving Event Reasoning and Retrieval for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04109)
*Mingtian Tan,Palash Goyal,Mihir Parmar,Sarkar Snigdha Sarathi Das,Chun-Liang Li,Nanyun Peng,Thomas Hartvigsen,Jinsung Yoon,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SEER闭环框架，通过自进化的事件推理与检索优化事件条件，提升时间序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时间序列常受外生事件和结构性变化驱动，仅依赖历史数值观测的传统预测方法不够；语言模型虽可检索外部新闻，但标准检索增强方法面临高噪声、信号缺失以及无法对事件影响进行因果推理的问题。

Method: SEER将预测误差转化为两种解耦的反馈机制：(i) 反思性检索记忆，用于精炼后续搜索查询并过滤虚假噪声；(ii) 持久化因果知识库，用于蒸馏可迁移的领域动态。并在事件检索和反思中强制执行严格的时间边界，避免前视偏差和数据泄漏。

Result: 在六个高波动时间序列基准上，SEER持续优于最先进的时间序列基础模型和语言模型基线。

Conclusion: SEER作为一种自进化事件推理与检索的闭环框架，有效提升了事件条件化时间序列预测性能，并规避信息泄漏风险。

Abstract: Real-world time series are frequently driven by exogenous events and structural shifts, rendering conventional forecasting based solely on historical numerical observations insufficient. While language models can retrieve external news, standard retrieval-augmented approaches struggle with high noise, missing signals, and an inability to reason causally about event impacts. We propose SEER (Self-Evolving Event Reasoning and Retrieval), a closed-loop framework that dynamically optimizes event conditioning for time series forecasting. SEER translates prediction errors into two decoupled feedback mechanisms: (i) a reflective retrieval memory that refines subsequent search queries and filters spurious noise, and (ii) a persistent causal knowledge base that distills transferable domain dynamics. SEER enforces strict chronological boundaries across both event retrieval and reflection, preventing look-ahead bias and data leakage. Across six volatile time-series benchmarks, SEER consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time series foundation models and language model baselines.

</details>


### [296] [Sharp Convergence and Sample Complexity of Policy Mirror Descent for Average-Reward MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04117)
*Enes Arda,Atilla Eryilmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对遍历平均奖励MDP中的策略镜像下降（PMD）进行了有限时间、有限样本分析，基于单一主递推给出线性及超线性收敛率，并得到 $t_{\mathrm{mix}}^3/\varepsilon^2$ 的端到端样本复杂度，证明其不可改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PMD在折扣MDP中已有成熟的有限时间理论，但在平均奖励这一更自然的控制目标下理论尚不完善。

Method: 围绕一个支配任意critic收敛性的单一主递推（无需外部正则化）展开分析，并针对精确、非精确表格及线性函数逼近（LFA）更新进行特化；结合信息论下界进行匹配。

Result: 特化后得到精确PMD的超线性收敛、其余情形的线性收敛；端到端样本复杂度在表格（依赖 $|S||A|$）与LFA（依赖 $d$）下均为 $t_{\mathrm{mix}}^3/\varepsilon^2$，将LFA此前最优的 $t_{\mathrm{mix}}^5$ 改进为 $t_{\mathrm{mix}}^3$，且下界表明该复杂度不可改进。

Conclusion: 在遍历平均奖励MDP中建立了PMD的统一有限时间/有限样本理论，给出最优样本复杂度，并证明了critic在该复杂度上的不可改进性。

Abstract: Policy mirror descent (PMD) has a mature finite-time theory in discounted Markov decision processes (MDPs), but less is known in the average-reward setting, a more natural objective for many control applications. We give a finite-time, finite-sample analysis of PMD in ergodic average-reward MDPs built around a single master recursion that governs convergence for any critic, without external regularization. Its specializations yield linear rates for exact, inexact-tabular, and linear function approximation (LFA) updates, with a superlinear regime for exact PMD. We complement these convergence results with end-to-end sample complexities of order $t_{\mathrm{mix}}^3/\varepsilon^2$ in both tabular ($|S||A|$-dependent) and LFA ($d$-dependent) settings. Our LFA sample complexity sharpens the prior best $t_{\mathrm{mix}}^5$ mixing dependence to $t_{\mathrm{mix}}^3$, and matching information-theoretic lower bounds establish that the critic's $t_{\mathrm{mix}}^3/\varepsilon^2$ sample complexity is unimprovable in both settings.

</details>


### [297] [Dynamic Harness Search: Building Multi-Agent Systems Per-Query via Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04137)
*Som Sagar,Shasha Li,Hejie Cui,Ransalu Senanayake,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHIFT 将执行移出每查询搜索循环，用本地 LLM 学习 harness 构建策略与效用价值函数，并以 MCTS 为每个查询即时构造 harness，在六个基准上取得领先精度并降低执行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent harness 决定了角色、指令、工具与通信结构，且最优配置取决于具体查询。由于各设计选择的价值只能通过实际执行观测，为每个查询定制 harness 原先要么在推理时执行多种备选方案，要么依赖昂贵的人工设计，因此需要一种避免在推理循环内反复执行的方法。

Method: 提出 SHIFT：由本地 LLM 架构师从搜索中学习 harness 构建动作的策略，并学习一个价值函数，从已测量的执行中预测在精度与执行成本之间权衡的效用；对每个查询，用蒙特卡洛树搜索结合这些预测来构造 harness。

Result: 在六个基准共 9,193 个任务（涵盖数学、文档与通用助手任务）上，配合 Gemini 3.5 Flash 执行器，SHIFT 取得约 80% 的最高平均准确率，优于覆盖提示、提示优化与工作流搜索的 17 个基线，超出最强基线 7.2 个百分点。SHIFT 的低成本模式在平均准确率上仍优于所有基线，同时比最强基线少用 32% 的执行 token。联合选择结构、指令与工具比仅选指令或仅选工具最高提升 9.1 个百分点；学习到的价值选择能从候选池中识别出更准确且执行成本更低的 harness。

Conclusion: SHIFT 通过将执行移出每查询搜索循环，实现了高效的查询自适应 harness 构造，并证明结构、指令与工具的联合优化以及学习式价值选择对精度与成本均有关键收益。

Abstract: Agent harnesses specify the roles, instructions, tools, and communication structure used to solve a task, and the right harness depends on the query. Because the value of each design choice is observable only through execution, tailoring a harness to each query has required either executing alternatives at inference time or costly manual design. We introduce SHIFT, which moves execution out of the per-query search loop. A local LLM architect learns a policy over harness-building actions from search, and a value function that predicts, from measured executions, a utility balancing accuracy against execution cost. For each query, Monte Carlo tree search uses these predictions to construct a harness. Across 9,193 tasks in six benchmarks, from math to document and general-assistant tasks, with a Gemini 3.5 Flash executor, SHIFT attains the highest mean accuracy, about 80%, outperforming 17 baselines that span prompting, prompt optimization, and workflow search, and exceeding the strongest baseline by 7.2 percentage points. A cheaper mode of SHIFT also attains a higher mean accuracy than every baseline while using 32% fewer execution tokens than the strongest baseline.
