We further show that choosing structure, instructions, and tools jointly beats choosing only instructions or only tools by up to 9.1 percentage points, and that learned value selection identifies more accurate harnesses with lower execution cost from candidate pools.

</details>


### [298] [Dual-Scale Relational Graph Transformers for Ecosystem-Aware Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04138)
*Mohsen Nayebi Kerdabadi,Xinrou Li,Yao Xiao,Zijun Yao,Xin Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERMES 是一种双尺度图 Transformer 编码器，联合建模会话级局部异构图结构与欺诈生态全局状态，用于数字银行账户接管（ATO）欺诈检测。基于超 1.3 亿高风险交易会话的评估中，相较生产系统实现客户摩擦相对降低 44.44%、欺诈召回率相对提升 24.66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 账户接管欺诈对数字银行威胁日益严重，检测需在降低合法客户摩擦与提升召回之间取得平衡。现有生产系统主要依赖孤立评分会话的表格模型，丢弃了交互网络的关系结构；已有图模型虽利用会话与网络实体的关系，但多局限于局部邻域推理，仅能刻画欺诈风险的一部分。作者认为欺诈风险同时取决于会话周围的局部关系结构与欺诈生态的动态全局状态。

Method: 提出 HERMES（面向高风险会话的异质关系微观—宏观图 Transformer 编码器），采用双尺度架构：Micro-GT 通过结构化、关系感知的注意力机制，在时间安全的会话邻域上建模局部异质图结构；Macro-GT 建模生态系统级上下文，使用非前瞻性"气候"token 汇总欺诈动态、平台转移与基础设施复用信息，并结合自适应类别原型随时间跟踪代表性欺诈与正常会话模式。

Result: 在美国某领先金融机构超 1.3 亿条高风险交易会话上评估，HERMES 稳定优于生产系统与强图基线，客户摩擦相对降低 44.44%，欺诈召回相对提升 24.66%。消融实验与时间稳定性分析表明，局部关系建模与全局生态上下文在不同欺诈机制下均带来互补增益。

Conclusion: 联合建模会话级局部关系结构与全局欺诈生态状态的双尺度架构，可显著提升 ATO 欺诈检测效果，并在欺诈模式演变下保持稳定，超越现有生产系统与主流图方法。

Abstract: Account takeover (ATO) fraud is a growing threat to digital banking, requiring effective detection while minimizing friction for legitimate customers. Production systems predominantly rely on tabular models that score sessions in isolation, discarding the relational structure of the underlying interaction network. Although graph-based models exploit relationships among sessions and network entities, they primarily reason over local neighborhoods and therefore capture only part of the problem: fraud risk depends jointly on the local relational structure surrounding a session and the evolving global state of the fraud ecosystem. We present HERMES (HEterogeneous Relational Micro--macro graph transformer Encoder for high-risk Sessions), a dual-scale architecture that jointly models these complementary scales of information. Micro-GT captures local heterogeneous graph structure through structured, relation-aware attention over a temporally safe session neighborhood. Complementing this local representation, Macro-GT models ecosystem-level context using non-anticipative climate tokens that summarize fraud dynamics, platform shifts, and infrastructure reuse, together with adaptive class prototypes that track representative fraud and benign session patterns over time. Evaluated on more than 130 million high-risk transaction sessions from a leading U.S. financial institution, HERMES consistently outperforms production and strong graph-based baselines, achieving a 44.44% relative reduction in customer friction and a 24.66% relative improvement in fraud recall over the production system. Ablation and temporal-stability analyses further demonstrate complementary gains from local relational modeling and global ecosystem context across changing fraud regimes.

</details>


### [299] [ExpertMuon-Compass: Alignment-Guided Step Sizes for Mixture-of-Experts Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04140)
*Omatharv Bharat Vaidya,Ashwin Vinod,Pedram Akbarian,Aditya Sai Ellendula,Connor T. Jerzak,Nhat Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ExpertMuon-Compass（Compass），在 Muon 优化器中为每个专家引入"家族因子"（层内专家间正交化更新与梯度余弦的对比）和"标量半径"（将更新与梯度对应行的对齐度聚合为步长乘子），在不改变更新方向和动量缓冲的前提下自适应调整各专家的步长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 语言模型将每个 token 路由到少量专家，各专家训练数据不同且随训练变化；在共享学习率下，Muon 对同形状的专家矩阵施加近似相同大小的更新，即使某专家的更新与其当前梯度对齐度很差，导致优化不够自适应。

Method: Compass 将每个专家的 Muon 步乘以两个因子：(1) 家族因子——比较该专家正交化更新与梯度的余弦，与同层其他专家相同余弦的对比；(2) 标量半径——把更新与梯度对应行之间的对齐度聚合成单一的步长乘子。方法保留 Muon 的更新方向与动量缓冲，并给出两个因子的扰动界证明。

Result: 在 FineWeb-Edu 预训练中，对所有矩阵使用 Nesterov 动量的 Compass 表现与 Muon、NorMuon 及其他优化器相当或更优（长程训练中权重衰减与 NorMuon 匹配）；将因子加入 NorMuon 后损失不高于 NorMuon。当各专家所见数据在训练中变化时（如多语料按语言分块到达）效果最显著。使用 Compass 后专家负载保持均衡，路由器对专家的分配更果断。

Conclusion: Compass 通过层内专家间的对齐度比较与行级对齐聚合，为 MoE 中的每个专家提供更自适应的步长缩放，在保持 Muon 更新方向与动量机制的同时提升预训练性能，尤其适用于专家数据分布动态变化的场景。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models send each token to a few experts, so each expert is trained on a different part of the data, and this part changes during training. With a shared learning rate, Muon applies updates of roughly the same size to expert matrices of the same shape, even when an expert's update is poorly aligned with its current gradient. We here propose ExpertMuon-Compass (Compass), which multiplies the Muon step of each expert by two factors. A family factor compares the cosine between the expert's orthogonalized update and its gradient with the same cosine for the other experts in its layer. A scalar radius aggregates the alignment between corresponding rows of the update and gradient into one step-length multiplier. Compass keeps the update direction and the momentum buffer of Muon. In pretraining on FineWeb-Edu, Compass with Nesterov momentum on all matrices performs as well as or better than Muon, NorMuon, and other optimizers, with weight decay matched to NorMuon in the longer runs. Adding its factors to NorMuon gives the same or a lower loss than NorMuon. Compass is the most effective when the data seen by each expert varies during training, for example, as when the languages of a multilingual corpus arrive in separate blocks. With Compass, the expert load stays balanced, and the router assigns tokens to experts more decisively. We also prove a perturbation bound for the two factors.

</details>


### [300] [Ideal Paths for Approximating Logistic Gradient Descent Trajectories at Large Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04142)
*Junjie Xiao,Huiwen Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过几何近似刻画了大尺度初始化下全批量逻辑回归梯度下降的完整训练轨迹，将其分解为负间隔修正与最小间隔增长两个阶段，并给出收敛性、误差界及峰值/累积损失的渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代训练常从预训练模型出发而非从零开始，因此初始化如何影响后续训练轨迹成为关键问题。经典隐式偏置结果只刻画长时间训练所选的方向（终点方向），无法提供训练中途的中间行为信息，故需要一个能描述整个轨迹的定量理论。

Method: 在严格线性可分数据上研究全批量逻辑回归梯度下降，初始尺度为 R。对任意归一化初始位置，利用最小范数投影规则构造由有限多条线性段组成的唯一连续理想路径，路径分为负间隔修正与最小间隔增长两阶段。证明经显式两阶段时间重参数化后，除以 R 的定步长 GD 轨迹在每个固定参数区间上随 R→∞ 一致收敛到该路径，并给出考虑初始化扰动与阶段切换的定量误差界。

Result: 得到峰值评估损失与累积训练损失的渐近公式。特别地，即使两端点损失都趋于零，峰值评估损失仍可随 R 线性增长；修正阶段与间隔增长阶段的累积损失分别以 R^2 和 R 归一化后收敛到显式极限。受控几何与固定图像特征上的实验支持了理论结论。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了大初始化下逻辑回归 GD 轨迹的统一几何极限描述，揭示了两阶段结构及中间过程的损失峰值现象，弥补了仅依赖隐式偏置方向的不足。

Abstract: Modern training on a new task often starts from a previously trained model rather than from scratch, raising the question of how this initialization affects the subsequent training trajectory. Classical implicit-bias results characterize the direction selected by prolonged training, but this direction alone does not provide information regarding the intermediate behavior. We address this question through a geometric approximation of full-batch logistic gradient descent (GD) trajectories on strictly linearly separable data, with large initialization of scale $R$ motivated by prior training. From any limiting normalized initial position, we use minimum-norm projection rules to construct a unique continuous ideal path consisting of finitely many linear segments. The path has two stages: negative-margin correction followed by minimum-margin growth. We prove that, after an explicit two-stage time reparameterization, the fixed-step GD trajectory divided by $R$ converges uniformly to this path on every fixed parameter interval as $R\to\infty$. Further, our quantitative error bounds account for initialization perturbations and the transition between stages. This approximation provides asymptotic formulas for peak evaluation loss and cumulative training loss. In particular, peak evaluation loss can grow linearly in $R$ even when both endpoint losses tend to zero. The cumulative losses in the correction and margin-growth stages, normalized by $R^2$ and $R$, respectively, converge to explicit limits. Experiments on controlled geometries and fixed image features complement our theoretical results.

</details>


### [301] [Consideration Circuits: Depth Separation and Universality Beyond a Single Softmax](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04143)
*Junjie Xiao,Huiwen Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出「考虑电路」（consideration circuits, CC），一种基于有向无环图、由多个MNL单元组成的多阶段特征化选择模型，并从理论上刻画其深度—范数分离，实验上验证其以更少参数取得更优NLL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典与深度特征化选择模型通常先对物品打分再接单一softmax，单阶段结构在菜单无关的随机效用模型（RUM）假设下表达能力受限；在固定非共线特征的三物品折衷任务中，该类模型（含单MNL单元）存在有下界的误差，无法任意逼近确定性选择表。因此需要一个更具表达力的多阶段选择建模框架。

Method: 将选择过程表示为有向无环图（DAG），节点为多项Logit（MNL）单元。源单元对菜单项分配概率，内部单元以前驱分布的「概率加权特征摘要」计算MNL权重并组合前驱分布。路由偏置可设为菜单无关。通过深度与最大味向量范数的关系进行理论分析，并给出逼近任意确定性选择表的充要几何条件。

Result: 在深度—范数分离上：深度由2增至3，达到误差ε所需最优最大味向量范数从Θ(log(1/ε)/ε)降至Θ(log(1/ε))；深度2下界对任意宽度和菜单无关路由偏置均成立，而一个零路由偏置的五节点深度3电路即可达到对数率。一般情形下，刻画了在有限菜单家族上逼近任意确定性选择表的两条必要充分几何条件，满足时深度3足够，深度4在含非单元素菜单时可达最优对数范数缩放。实验中，独立树形电路在少于600个参数下，在四个固定池基准和Expedia时间划分上取得最低平均测试负对数似然；作为输出头时，CC推广线性MNL读出，并在Expedia与Trivago上为每个测试编码器降低平均测试NLL。

Conclusion: 多阶段、图结构的MNL组合（考虑电路）突破了菜单无关RUM的单阶段表达瓶颈，展现出清晰的深度—范数分离：深度3即可实现对数级范数率，深度4达到最优缩放。模型参数高效，在多个基准与作为通用输出头时均优于现有基线，表明其作为特征化选择模型与读出层的通用价值。

Abstract: Most feature-based choice models, classical and deep, score items and apply a single softmax. We introduce consideration circuits (CC), feature-based models of multi-stage choice defined by directed acyclic graphs of multinomial logit (MNL) units. Source units assign probabilities to menu items, and internal units combine predecessor distributions using MNL weights computed from their probability-weighted feature summaries. On a three-item compromise task with fixed non-collinear features, menu-independent random-utility models (RUM), including a single MNL unit, suffer an error bounded away from zero. For CC, in contrast, we establish a sharp depth--norm separation: increasing depth from $2$ to $3$ reduces the optimal maximum taste-vector norm for error $ε$ from $Θ(\log(1/ε)/ε)$ to $Θ(\log(1/ε))$. The depth-$2$ lower bound holds for arbitrary width and menu-independent routing biases, while a five-node depth-$3$ circuit with zero routing biases attains the logarithmic rate. More generally, we characterize two geometric conditions that are necessary and sufficient for approximating arbitrary deterministic choice tables on finite menu families. Under these conditions, depth $3$ suffices, while depth $4$ achieves optimal logarithmic norm scaling whenever the family contains a non-singleton menu. In experiments, standalone tree circuits with fewer than $600$ parameters attain the lowest mean test negative log-likelihood (NLL) among the evaluated models on four fixed-pool benchmarks and the Expedia temporal split. As output heads, CC generalize the linear MNL readout and lower mean test NLL for every tested encoder on Expedia and Trivago.

</details>


### [302] [You May Be Running the Wrong Inception Crop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04147)
*Jason Chuan-Chih Chou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现Inception crop在不同框架中的采样分布不一致（TensorFlow/JAX使用f(A)∝1/√A，PyTorch遵循原始均匀采样），通过522个ViT-S/16模型实验系统研究crop scale分布对训练的影响，并基于发现重新评估Beta crop。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Inception crop已成为视觉模型训练的标准数据增强方法，其均匀采样crop scale和aspect ratio的做法被广泛采用。然而TensorFlow/JAX生态的标准实现意外地使用f(A)∝1/√A采样crop scale，与PyTorch实现不一致。这一发现促使作者系统研究crop scale分布如何影响模型训练。

Method: 在ImageNet-1k数据集上训练522个ViT-S/16模型，覆盖不同训练预算和crop scale分布。使用AdamW和Scion两种优化器进行实验验证。基于发现进一步评估Beta crop的性能表现。

Result: 90 epochs训练预算下达到78.78±0.09 top-1验证准确率。主要发现：1.更高的训练预算需要更强的增强；2.crop scale分布的下尾决定Inception crop的增强强度；3.高训练预算训练的模型表现出更稀疏的显著性，与crop scale分布和权重衰减无关。Beta crop因其更柔和的截断能在不同训练预算下以更少妥协优化模型性能。

Conclusion: crop scale分布的下尾是决定增强强度的关键因素。Beta crop能够跨训练预算优化性能。使用Scion优化器复现了发现1和3，表明结果具有普遍性。

Abstract: A decade after its inception, Inception crop has become the standard crop-based data augmentation method for training deep vision models. Not only is its practice of uniformly sampling crop scale and aspect ratio widely adopted, but also its lower and upper bounds, with the scale lower bound being the sole exception that is sometimes tuned. It is therefore surprising that the standard implementation in the TensorFlow / JAX ecosystem samples crop scale with probability density function $f(A) \propto \frac{1}{\sqrt{A}}$ unlike the PyTorch counterpart, which follows the original description. Motivated by this discovery, we train 522 ViT-S/16 models on the ImageNet-1k dataset with various training budgets and crop scale distributions. We reach $78.78\pm0.09$ top-1 val. accuracy with 90 epochs of training budget and find that 1. Higher training budget requires stronger augmentation; 2. Lower tail of the distribution of the crop scale determines the augmentation strength of Inception crop; 3. Models trained with higher training budget exhibit sparser saliency, regardless of the crop scale distribution or weight decay. Based on 2. we revisit the performance of Beta crop, whose softer cutoff allows it to optimize model performance across training budgets with less compromise. We replicate 1. and 3. with Scion optimizer in addition to AdamW, suggesting that the results may be general.

</details>


### [303] [How RL Reshapes LLM Reasoning: Transferability, Coverage, and Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04158)
*Ziheng Cheng,Yixiao Huang,Hanlin Zhu,Somayeh Sojoudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨RL究竟扩展还是重排LLM的推理空间：通过跨Qwen/Gemma的实证与两阶段自回归策略模型，证明RL主要改变策略选择偏好，从而解释跨域增益、遗忘、覆盖率变化及算力scaling律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期RL训练LLM的报告存在看似矛盾的现象：数学训练能提升其他领域表现，但Pass@1上升可能伴随Pass@N下降。核心问题是RL到底是扩展了LLM的推理边界，还是仅在其既有推理空间内重新加权。作者希望统一解释跨域增益、遗忘、覆盖率变化与算力scaling。

Method: 作者在Qwen和Gemma模型族上复现这些现象，验证跨域增益与遗忘，并观察大采样预算下覆盖率有增有减。通过对比RL前后解题轨迹，识别推理策略的转变，进而提出两阶段自回归策略模型，将“策略选择”与“问题特定执行”分离；在此框架内理论分析RL隐式偏置如何重塑策略偏好，并推导log-sigmoid与log-linear算力scaling律及其预测力。

Result: 实验显示跨域增益与遗忘并存；大采样预算下部分任务覆盖率上升、部分下降。轨迹分析表明RL后模型的推理策略发生转移。理论证明：RL的隐式偏置会改变策略偏好，导致某些任务获益而抑制其他任务所需策略；即使策略支持集未扩展，也可在给定采样预算下扩大或缩小覆盖率。所推导的log-sigmoid与log-linear算力scaling律具备可评估的预测能力。

Conclusion: RL并不必然扩展LLM的推理边界，而主要通过隐式偏置重塑策略选择偏好，这统一解释了跨域迁移、推理覆盖率变化与算力scaling现象；两阶段策略模型为理解与预测RL对LLM推理的影响提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Recent studies on reinforcement learning (RL) report seemingly conflicting evidence about large language model (LLM) reasoning. Training on mathematics can improve performance in other domains, yet gains in Pass@1 can coincide with lower Pass@$N$ than the base model. This raises a fundamental question: does RL expand an LLM's reasoning boundary, or merely reweight its existing reasoning space? We revisit these phenomena across Qwen and Gemma model families, showing both cross-domain gains and forgetting, while coverage at large sampling budgets increases on some tasks and decreases on others. Detailed analysis of solution traces before and after RL indicates a shift in the reasoning strategies the model employs, motivating a two-stage autoregressive policy model that separates \emph{strategy selection} from problem-specific execution. Within this framework, we prove how RL's implicit bias reshapes strategy preferences, allowing gains on some tasks while suppressing strategies required by others. This mechanism can also broaden or narrow coverage at a given sampling budget even without expanding strategy support. We further provide theoretical justifications for log-sigmoid and log-linear scaling laws in RL compute, and evaluate their predictive power. Together, these results connect changes in strategy selection to cross-domain transfer, reasoning coverage, and compute scaling.

</details>


### [304] [Principled Top-$k$ Selection for Language Models with Hybrid Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04162)
*Xuchen Gong,Junfei Sun,Tian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对从m个候选中选出最佳k项的top-k选择问题（如RAG文档选择、MoE专家路由），提出一个有原则的训练目标，其梯度自然包含监督梯度与策略梯度的混合信号。理论证明算法收敛率为O(1/√T)，并在合成回归、RAG、MoE任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型系统（如RAG的文档选择、MoE的专家路由）需要从m个候选中选出最佳k项，但选择模块的训练面临梯度信号弱、探索-利用权衡不佳的问题，且已有方法多依赖启发式，缺乏对top-k选择问题显式建模与求解的原则性目标。

Method: 提出一个有原则的top-k选择训练目标，其梯度自然以混合形式提供更丰富的训练信号，包含监督梯度分量与策略梯度分量；并从理论上分析问题难度随m增大而增加，证明算法收敛率为O(1/√T)，通过在偏差与方差间取得平衡达到最优上界。

Result: 在合成回归问题、检索增强生成（RAG）与混合专家（MoE）系统等top-k选择任务上验证方法，实验表明该方法在下一词预测困惑度与问答准确率上均优于基线。

Conclusion: 该工作为选择模块训练提供了原则性目标与理论保证，其混合梯度机制在多种top-k选择任务上取得更优表现，为RAG与MoE等系统的选择模块训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Selecting the best $k$ items out of $m$ candidates is a critical component of modern large language model systems, such as document selection in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and expert routing in Mixture-of-Experts (MoEs). However, training these selection modules remains challenging due to weak gradient signals and suboptimal exploration-exploitation tradeoffs. Furthermore, prior works often rely on heuristics, lacking principled objectives and approaches that explicitly model and solve the top-$k$ selection problem. In this work, we propose a principled objective for training selection modules, whose gradient naturally provides richer training signals in a hybrid form---containing a supervised-gradient component and a policy-gradient component. We show that the selection problem becomes harder as $m$ increases, and our algorithm converges at rate $O(1/\sqrt{T})$, with the optimal upper bound achieved by balancing between bias and variance. Practically, we apply our method to a set of tasks involving top-$k$ selection, including synthetic regression problems, RAG, and MoE systems, showing that our method outperforms the baselines in next-token prediction perplexity and QA accuracy.

</details>


### [305] [Clean: Second-order LLM Training at Linear Memory Cost via Nyström Sketching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04204)
*Beheshteh T. Rakhshan,Sahar Rajabi,Maziar Sargordi Shikai Fang,Guillaume Rabusseau,Sirisha Rambhatla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Clean 利用随机 Nystrom 方法近似 SOAP 的左右预条件器，将优化器内存复杂度从二次降为线性，并可通过 Q-Clean 低精度变体进一步压缩优化器状态，在保持竞争力的同时显著降低内存、加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型训练存在内存与收敛速度的根本权衡：Adam 等内存高效优化器丢弃跨参数曲率信息，而 SOAP 等全曲率方法虽能加速收敛，却带来过高的内存开销。

Method: 提出 Clean：用随机 Nystrom 方法精确近似 SOAP 的左右预条件器，使内存复杂度由关于模型维度的二次降为线性；随后重新整合离低秩子空间分量，以极低内存代价保留丰富曲率信息。并进一步提出 Q-Clean，一种激进压缩优化器状态的低精度变体。

Result: 在 LLaMA-1.3B 预训练中，Q-Clean 相比 Muon 减少超过 50% 的优化器内存；Clean 的优化器状态占用小于标准 AdamW，却以墙钟时间快 26% 达到 AdamW 的最终性能；并首次在单张 80GB GPU 上实现 13B 参数模型的预训练。

Conclusion: Clean 与 Q-Clean 兼顾内存高效与全曲率优化，提供了可扩展、高效且易用的大规模模型优化方案。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) entails a fundamental trade-off: memory-efficient optimizers such as Adam discard cross-parameter curvature, whereas full-curvature methods such as SOAP can accelerate convergence at prohibitive memory costs. We introduce Clean, a memory-efficient and full-curvature optimizer designed to resolve this bottleneck. Clean leverages the randomized Nystrom method to accurately approximate the left and right preconditioners in SOAP, and to reduce the optimizer's memory complexity from quadratic to linear in terms of model dimensions. We subsequently reintegrate the off-subspace components to capture curvature information beyond the low-rank approximation, preserving rich curvature at minimal memory cost. We further propose Q-Clean, a low-precision variant that aggressively compresses optimizer states. Q-Clean reduces optimizer memory consumption by \textbf{over 50\%} compared to Muon when pre-training a LLaMA-1.3B architecture, all while maintaining strong and competitive predictive performance. Notably, Clean operates with a smaller optimizer-state footprint than standard AdamW while reaching AdamW's final performance \textbf{26\% faster} in wall-clock time. Furthermore, our methods uniquely enable the pre-training of a 13B-parameter model on a single 80GB GPU, providing a scalable, efficient, and accessible approach to large-scale model optimization.

</details>


### [306] [FTD-GNO: Memory-Efficient Graph Neural Operators through Functional Tensor Decomposition of the Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04212)
*Xiaomin Zhang,Boyue Wang,Junbin Gao,Yongli Hu anbd Baocai Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FTD-GNO，将GNO积分核分解为低维模态函数，降低内存与训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准图神经算子（GNO）用单一神经网络参数化积分核并在图边上评估核交互，在高分辨率或大邻域下产生显著的计算与内存开销。

Method: FTD-GNO框架将高维连续积分核解耦为低维模态函数，采用CP、张量列（Tensor-Train）、Tucker等经典张量分解格式实例化核，从而在无需显式物化完整边wise核张量的情况下代数重建积分算子。

Result: 理论复杂度分析表明FTD-GNO显著降低高维核构建相关的参数与激活内存成本；实验显示峰值内存低于对应的未分解图积分基线且训练时间更短；傅里叶-图实验表明FTD可提升混合算子中图积分层的效率并具良好可扩展性。

Conclusion: FTD-GNO通过张量分解实现内存高效的算子学习，在保留GNO连续算子学习结构的同时降低内存与计算开销。

Abstract: Graph Neural Operators (GNOs) provide flexible surrogate models for learning solution operators of partial differential equations (PDEs). However, standard GNOs typically parameterize the integral kernel with a monolithic neural network and evaluate kernel interactions over graph edges, leading to substantial computational and memory overhead at high resolutions or with large neighborhoods. To address these limitations, we propose Functional Tensor Decomposition Graph Neural Operator (FTD-GNO), a memory-efficient GNO framework that decouples the high-dimensional continuous integral kernel into low-dimensional mode-wise functions. By instantiating the kernel with classical tensor decomposition formats, including CP, Tensor-Train, and Tucker decompositions, FTD-GNO enables algebraic reconstruction of the integral operator without explicitly materializing full edge-wise kernel tensors. This factorized formulation reduces the memory footprint of kernel evaluation and aggregation while retaining the continuous operator-learning structure of GNOs. Theoretical complexity analysis shows that FTD-GNO substantially lowers parameter and activation-memory costs associated with high-dimensional kernel construction. Experiments show lower peak memory than the corresponding unfactorized graph-integral baselines, with shorter recorded training times. Fourier-graph experiments further demonstrate that FTD can improve the efficiency of a graph-integral layer within a hybrid operator and has good scalability.

</details>


### [307] [PaLoRA: Paced Low-Rank Adaptation for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04226)
*Yuxuan Li,Fanhu Zeng,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示LoRA持续学习中固定小学习率启发式的局限，提出最优梯度缩放应随历史知识有效秩自适应增长，导出pacing law（s*=√(R/c)）并据此构建PaLoRA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LoRA的持续学习方法普遍依赖小学习率作为启发式手段来抑制梯度缩放幅度，以防止灾难性遗忘。然而这种固定启发式缺乏理论指导，无法说明随任务累积其约束强度应如何演变。作者指出即使存在零空间投影等方向性约束，有限精度更新仍会沿多个方向泄漏到历史知识子空间，而随着历史知识有效秩增长，固定小学习率无法阻止遗忘累积加剧。

Method: 在一种各向异性泄漏模型下推导出梯度步最优缩放的pacing law：s*=√(R/c)，其中R为历史更新的有效秩，表征最优幅度约束应随有效秩自适应增大以平衡稳定性与可塑性。基于该洞察提出PaLoRA，包含三部分：通过自适应SVD截断压缩历史知识；将梯度投影到先前任务的零空间；施加秩感知的自适应pacing。

Result: 实验表明PaLoRA相较先前的持续学习方法取得一致性提升，在长时程（long-horizon）场景下尤其显著，在具有挑战性的50任务ImageNet-A与ImageNet-R基准上取得约4%的准确率增益。

Conclusion: 研究证明梯度幅度约束不应固定不变，而应随历史知识有效秩自适应演化，pacing law为LoRA持续学习提供了理论指导，PaLoRA验证了该理论在长任务序列中的有效性。

Abstract: LoRA-based continual learning methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting through various mechanisms, yet nearly all complement these with small learning rates as a heuristic to restrict gradient scaling magnitude. Such fixed heuristics lack theoretical guidance on how the strength of this restriction should evolve as tasks accumulate. We reveal that even under directional constraints such as nullspace projection, finite-precision updates inevitably leak into the subspace of accumulated prior knowledge along multiple directions. While small learning rates attenuate such leakage, they cannot prevent the accumulated forgetting from intensifying as the effective rank of historical knowledge grows. We show that the optimal magnitude restriction should adaptively increase with this effective rank to balance stability and plasticity, i.e., preservation of previous knowledge and acquisition of new task information. Under an anisotropic leakage model, we derive a pacing law $s^*=\sqrt{R/c}$ that characterizes the optimal scaling of gradient steps, i.e., the magnitude restriction itself, where $R$ is the effective rank of past updates. Based on this insight, we propose PaLoRA, which compresses historical knowledge via adaptive SVD truncation, projects gradients onto the nullspace of prior tasks, and applies rank-aware adaptive pacing. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements over prior methods, with particularly strong performance in long-horizon settings, achieving substantial gains of 4% accuracy on challenging 50-task ImageNet-A and ImageNet-R benchmarks.

</details>


### [308] [Stochastic Adaptive Fourier Decomposition for Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04241)
*Pengqing Shi,Liming Zhang,Tao Qian,Stephen Tierney,Jie Yin,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAFDNO：用输入自适应的Takenaka-Malmquist正交系替代固定傅里叶基，通过神经极点预测器摊销随机极点选择，在九个PDE基准上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO依赖固定傅里叶基与硬频率截断，难以建模非周期、局部化及细尺度解结构。

Method: 以Stochastic Adaptive Fourier Decomposition理论导出的自适应TM正交系替代预定义傅里叶模态；用神经极点预测器摊销经典SAFD中昂贵的贪心极点搜索，从隐特征直接构造TM系统；在Hardy空间中对解析分支系数做可学习滤波，保留谱算子的全局感受野。

Result: 在九个PDE基准中，六个规则网格问题全部最优；在Darcy、Burgers、Navier-Stokes上提升显著；零样本超分辨性能更强，在更细离散化上性能退化更小。

Conclusion: 输入自适应的TM系统是固定谱表示的有前景替代方案，可用于神经算子学习。

Abstract: Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs) offer an efficient paradigm for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). However, FNOs rely on a fixed Fourier basis and hard frequency truncation, which inherently limit their ability to model non-periodic, localized, and fine-scale solution structures. We propose the Stochastic Adaptive Fourier Decomposition Neural Operator (SAFDNO), a spectral neural operator that replaces predefined Fourier modes with an adaptive Takenaka-Malmquist (TM) orthonormal system derived from the theory of Stochastic Adaptive Fourier Decomposition (SAFD). Instead of performing an expensive greedy pole search in classical SAFD, SAFDNO amortizes stochastic pole selection through a neural pole predictor and constructs the adaptive TM system directly from latent features. The resulting operator performs learned filtering on coefficients of analytic branches in the Hardy space with respect to the input-adaptive TM system, preserving the global receptive field of spectral operators while providing a more flexible representation than using fixed spectral bases. Across nine PDE benchmark problems, SAFDNO achieves the best performance on all six regular-grid problems among strong neural operator baselines, with especially notable gains on Darcy, Burgers, and Navier-Stokes, where fixed Fourier modes are often less effective at modeling localized oscillations, sharp transitions, and multiscale structures. SAFDNO also exhibits stronger zero-shot super-resolution performance and shows less performance degradation when deployed on finer discretizations. These results suggest that the input-adaptive TM system provides a promising alternative to fixed spectral representations for neural operator learning.

</details>


### [309] [Optimizer Geometry Sets the Pace: Spectral Learning Dynamics in Matrix Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04249)
*Mahalakshmi Sabanayagam,Simon Lucey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一的奇异模动力学框架，研究优化器几何与深度如何共同决定深度矩阵分解中的谱偏置，推导了GD、SignGD、Adam、Muon、Shampoo、K-FAC的诞生/饱和/衰减规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵与曲率型优化器的成功引发对更新几何如何塑造学习的关注：这类方法通过归一化或预条件改变各分量训练进度。在深度矩阵分解中，减缓梯度下降的几何会延迟小奇异模出现，从而偏向低秩解；但当归一化或曲率校正削弱该减速时，这种谱偏置是否仍存在尚不清楚。作者希望厘清优化器几何与深度对奇异模动力学的联合影响。

Method: 在显式平衡与对齐假设下，建立一个统一的奇异模动力学分析框架，针对欧氏GD、SignGD与瞬时Adam近似的逐坐标更新、Muon与累积Shampoo的谱更新，以及K-FAC的块曲率模型，分别推导各模的诞生、饱和与衰减定律，据此比较不同优化器几何对低秩偏置的作用。

Result: 结果并非从强到弱的简单低秩偏置排序：SignGD与理想Muon消除发散的诞生壁垒，使无支撑模在有限时间内归零；累积Shampoo初期保留GD的深度相关壁垒，随后累积梯度产生追赶阶段，同时使原本活跃的模越来越持久；无阻尼K-FAC抵消分解诱导的减速但保留目标奇异值次序，而正阻尼引入谱阈值，阈值以下的慢GD相律重新出现。

Conclusion: 归一化、累积状态、阻尼与深度可被直接解释为控制模在训练中何时诞生、持续与消失的机制，为理解不同优化器几何如何塑造学习动力学提供了统一视角。

Abstract: Recent successes of matrix- and curvature-based optimizers have renewed interest in how update geometry shapes learning. These methods normalize or precondition updates, changing how different components progress during training. In deep matrix factorization, the geometry that slows gradient descent (GD) favors low-rank solutions by delaying the emergence of small singular modes. This raises the question of what remains of that spectral bias when normalization or curvature correction weakens or removes the slowdown. We study how optimizer geometry and depth jointly govern this behavior through a common framework for singular mode dynamics. Under explicit balance and alignment assumptions, we derive birth, saturation, and decay laws for Euclidean GD, coordinate-wise updates of SignGD and an instantaneous Adam approximation, spectral updates of Muon and cumulative Shampoo, and a block curvature model of K-FAC. The resulting picture is not a simple ordering from stronger to weaker low-rank bias. To highlight, SignGD and ideal Muon eliminate the divergent birth barrier and drive unsupported modes to zero in finite time. Cumulative Shampoo initially retains GD's depth-dependent barrier, then accumulated gradients produce a catch-up phase while making previously active modes increasingly persistent. Undamped K-FAC cancels the factorization-induced slowdown while preserving the ordering of the target singular values, whereas positive damping introduces a spectral threshold below which the slow GD phase laws reappear. These results give normalization, accumulated state, damping, and depth a direct interpretation as controls determining when modes emerge, persist, and disappear during training.

</details>


### [310] [A Hand-Checkable Proof That Two Hidden ReLU Layers Compute the Maximum of Six Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04256)
*Dimitrios Myrisiotis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为六个实输入的最大值函数给出显式"六边形恒等式"，用纯手算分析证明两层隐藏层的充分性，并构造出隐层宽度为17和41、零偏置、有理权重的显式网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算最大值函数是研究ReLU网络深度的标准测试问题。已知通过计算机辅助构造，最多十二个输入可用两层隐藏层实现，但这些构造依赖程序验证，缺乏可自包含、可手工检验的解析证明。本文旨在为六个实输入提供显式且可人工验证的证明。

Method: 通过计算机辅助搜索发现一个针对六个实输入的"六边形恒等式"，其局部结构导出深度上界。作者给出显式抵消步骤，使证明完全可手工检查，无需运行验证程序。

Result: 给出六输入最大值函数的两层隐藏层深度界的一个自包含解析证明；该恒等式进一步导出显式网络，隐层宽度为17和41，偏置为零，权重为有理数。

Conclusion: 该工作将依赖计算机验证的深度结果转化为可人工核验的解析证明，并提供了具体、显式的网络参数，增强了结果的可信度与可复现性。

Abstract: Exactly computing the maximum function is a standard test case for studying depth in ReLU networks. Two hidden layers are known to suffice for up to twelve inputs through computer-assisted constructions. For six real inputs, we give an explicit hexagon identity whose local structure yields a self-contained analytical proof of this depth bound. The identity was found by computer-assisted search; we prove it through explicit cancellations that can be checked entirely by hand, without executing a verification program. The identity also yields an explicit network with hidden widths $17$ and $41$, zero biases, and rational weights.

</details>


### [311] [Cross-Trait Transfer in Subliminal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04260)
*Xingyu Zhao,Yiqiao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究"阈下学习"中的跨特质迁移现象，提出有向特质迁移矩阵量化一个教师特质如何影响学生对其他特质的偏好，发现迁移矩阵呈现相关特质聚类结构，并揭示学习动态从广泛共享偏移向特质特异迁移演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阈下学习中，学生模型仅通过训练语义无关的输出即可习得教师的行为特质，这是一种微妙的统计现象，特质传递依赖生成数据中微弱的统计模式。为理解师生配对间的特质传递机制，作者需要刻画不同特质之间的相互作用，因此研究跨特质迁移。

Method: 引入有向特质迁移矩阵（directed trait-transfer matrix），利用学生答案的对数概率增益（log-probability gains）量化一种教师特质对学生在其他特质上偏好的影响；并用输出分布度量与表示度量解释跨特质结构；进一步分析特质发展与交互，包括学习动态和多特质实验。

Result: 迁移矩阵揭示了相关特质的聚类结构，学生有时会发展出与教师特质相似但不完全相同的新偏好；这种跨特质结构可被输出分布度量和表示度量部分捕获。学习动态显示从广泛的共享偏移逐步走向更特质特异的迁移；多特质实验表明对立特质可增强这种分化。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了特质迁移与竞争中的显著统计结构，为理解阈下学习中隐藏偏好如何被传递提供了更广阔的视角。

Abstract: Subliminal learning is a phenomenon where a student language model acquires a teacher model's behavioral traits by training on semantically unrelated outputs. It is a subtle statistical phenomenon as trait transmission relies on weak statistical patterns in the generated data. To understand trait transmission between teacher-student pairs, we study cross-trait transfer: how data generated under one teacher trait changes the student's preferences of other traits. To this end, we introduce a directed trait-transfer matrix that quantifies these effects using log-probability gains for student answers. We find that the trait-transfer matrix reveals clusters of related traits, with students sometimes developing preferences for traits similar, but not identical, to the teacher's trait. Such cross-trait structure can be partially captured by output distribution metrics and representation-based metrics. Further, we analyze trait development and interaction: learning dynamics shows a progression from broad shared shifts toward more trait-specific transfer, and multi-trait experiments suggest that opposed traits can enhance such differentiation. Together, our findings reveal salient statistical structures over trait transfer and competition, thus providing a broader view of how hidden preferences are transmitted in subliminal learning.

</details>


### [312] [Adaptive Bregman Alternating Projections for Feasible Gromov-Wasserstein Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04264)
*Aoran Zhang,César A. Uribe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应KL-BAPG（A-KL-BAPG）算法，通过固定惩罚预烧阶段与受保护递增惩罚阶段结合，消除Gromov-Wasserstein问题的可行性差距，并给出收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gromov-Wasserstein（GW）问题无需共享特征空间或已知对应关系即可比较结构化分布，但其非凸目标与耦合边际约束导致计算困难。Bregman交替投影梯度（BAPG）虽采用低成本的行列交替更新，但固定惩罚松弛会留下持续的可行性差距。

Method: A-KL-BAPG结合有限固定惩罚的预烧阶段与带保护的递增惩罚阶段。在尾部每次迭代中，复用BAPG的交替更新，并回溯延迟幂步长，直到满足受Sinkhorn启发的投影直径保护条件。

Result: 证明每次迭代回溯有限终止且可行性差距渐近消失；建立加权平方校正残差的最佳迭代O(1/log N)界，并在支撑正则条件下证明原GW问题存在平稳聚点。实验表明，在合成与真实图对齐问题上，A-KL-BAPG精度与目标值接近固定惩罚KL-BAPG，同时将边际可行性差距降低62-99%、投影平稳残差降低28-98%；异构域适应实验也呈现类似模式。

Conclusion: A-KL-BAPG在精度、目标值、可行性与平稳性之间取得良好平衡，优于BAPG变体、基于投影的方法及任务特定基线，且其平稳性保证针对原GW问题而非松弛问题，区别于固定惩罚BAPG。

Abstract: The Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) problem compares structured distributions without requiring a shared feature space or known correspondences, but its nonconvex objective and coupled marginal constraints make computation challenging. Bregman alternating projected gradient (BAPG) uses inexpensive alternating row and column updates, yet its fixed-penalty relaxation leaves a persistent feasibility gap. We propose Adaptive KL-BAPG (A-KL-BAPG), which combines a finite fixed-penalty burn-in with a guarded increasing-penalty phase. At each tail iteration, the method reuses BAPG's alternating updates and backtracks a delayed-power step until a Sinkhorn-inspired projective-diameter safeguard is satisfied. We prove finite termination of the backtracking at each iteration and show that the feasibility gap vanishes asymptotically. We further establish a best-iterate $O(1/\log N)$ bound for the weighted squared corrected residual and, under a support regularity condition, the existence of a stationary accumulation point for the original GW problem. This distinguishes A-KL-BAPG from fixed-penalty BAPG, whose stationarity guarantees are given for the relaxed problem. Experiments show that A-KL-BAPG achieves a favorable balance of accuracy, objective value, feasibility, and stationarity relative to BAPG variants, projection-based methods, and task-specific baselines. For synthetic and real graph alignment problems, it closely matches the accuracy and objective value of fixed-penalty KL-BAPG while reducing the marginal feasibility gap by 62-99% and the projected stationarity residual by 28-98%. Heterogeneous domain adaptation experiments show a similar pattern: A-KL-BAPG maintains comparable target accuracy and objective values while achieving better feasibility and stationarity than fixed-penalty KL-BAPG.

</details>


### [313] [Self-Reflection Fine-Tuning: Enhancing Agent Security against Prompt Injection Attacks from Failure Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04269)
*Zixuan Wang,Hao Li,Fengyu Gao,G. Edward Suh,Yi Zeng,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik,Ning Zhang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自反思微调（SRFT）框架，让LLM代理从自身对抗失败经历中学习，构建SR-Agent，显著降低提示注入攻击成功率并保持任务性能，展现自适应攻击下的强泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在工具增强环境中广泛部署，但依赖外部输入使其易受提示注入攻击而劫持任务目标。现有安全对齐方法依赖静态专家轨迹或偏好优化，难以泛化到自适应攻击模式。

Method: SRFT框架：不被动模仿专家行为，而是让代理暴露于注入攻击构造的受损轨迹中，利用专家模型生成结构化自反思推理，对比不安全行为与最优行为。这种反思性监督教导代理识别恶意指令、推理其后果并保持与原始用户意图对齐。在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen3-8B上实例化为SR-Agent。

Result: 在静态和自适应提示注入基准上评估，SRFT大幅降低攻击成功率，同时保持任务性能，并在自适应攻击下展现强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过自反思从失败中学习是构建鲁棒且安全的LLM代理的有前景方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in tool-augmented environments, but their reliance on external inputs makes them highly vulnerable to prompt injection attacks that can hijack task objectives. Existing safety alignment methods rely on static expert trajectories or preference optimization, limiting their ability to generalize to adaptive attack patterns. In this work, we propose Self-Reflection Fine-Tuning (SRFT), a training framework that enables agents to improve robustness by learning from their own failure experiences under adversarial conditions. Instead of passively imitating expert behaviors, SRFT exposes the agent to compromised trajectories constructed via injected attacks, and leverages an expert model to generate structured self-reflection reasoning that contrasts unsafe and optimal actions. This reflective supervision teaches the agent to identify malicious instructions, reason about their consequences, and maintain alignment with the original user intent. We instantiate this framework in SR-Agent, built on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen3-8B, and evaluate it on both static and adaptive prompt injection benchmarks. Experimental results show that SRFT substantially reduces attack success rates while preserving task performance, and demonstrates strong generalization under adaptive attacks. These findings suggest that learning from failure via self-reflection is a promising direction for building robust and secure LLM agents. Our code is released at https://github.com/Eden-Wang1710/srft-repo.

</details>


### [314] [Rethinking Self-Distillation for Multi-Teacher Capability Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04272)
*Roy Xie,Dan Friedman,Feng Nan,Yukun Huang,Zhichao Xu,Chengjiu Zhang,Jun Xu,Manaal Faruqui,Vivek Rathod,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）相比离线策略方法（SFT、软标签蒸馏）的精度优势主要来自训练设计与超参调优，而非算法本身；四类方法精度几乎相同，但MOPD训练GPU时长为SFT的14.8–23.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型后训练中，组合来自同一基座检查点的多个专家模型能力日益普遍，近期趋势认为多教师在线策略蒸馏优于传统离线策略方法。但MOPD带来更高的推理与环境交互成本，其报告的精度增益来源存疑，需要受控实验厘清算法本身的贡献。

Method: 开展受控自蒸馏研究，覆盖两种多教师设置、四个模型、十一个基准，对比离线策略方法（监督微调SFT、软标签蒸馏）与混合教师前缀蒸馏、MOPD；并重新审视四篇已发表的在线/离线策略蒸馏对比工作，在SFT基线使用拒绝采样教师轨迹并独立调参的条件下重新评估；同时测试免训练的权重合并方法。

Result: 四种方法取得几乎相同的精度，但MOPD训练GPU时长为SFT的14.8–23.1倍；此前报告的在策略增益在修正SFT基线后大幅缩小；简单权重合并可在数分钟CPU时间内恢复专家能力，但随模型规模减小和任务干扰增加，精度下降。

Conclusion: 研究结果质疑近期归因于MOPD的增益，认为更高效的离线策略基线经仔细调优是可行替代方案，权重合并可作为免训练补充手段。

Abstract: Combining capabilities of multiple expert models trained starting from the same base checkpoint has become increasingly common in frontier language-model post-training. Recent trends suggest that multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) outperforms conventional off-policy methods. However, despite the higher inference and environment interaction costs incurred by MOPD, we find that much of its reported accuracy gain is due to certain training design choices and hyperparameter optimization, rather than the algorithm itself. We conduct a controlled self-distillation study across two multi-teacher settings, four models, and eleven benchmarks, comparing off-policy methods, namely supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and soft-label distillation, to hybrid teacher-prefix distillation and MOPD. We found that all four methods achieve \textit{nearly identical} accuracy. However, MOPD uses $14.8$--$23.1\times$ SFT's training GPU-hours. We also revisit four recently published comparisons between on-policy and off-policy distillation and find that the reported on-policy gains shrink substantially when SFT baselines are trained on rejection-sampled teacher trajectories and use independently tuned hyperparameters. As a training-free alternative, we also find that simple weight-merging methods can recover expert capabilities with minutes of CPU merging time, although their accuracy degrades as model size decreases and task interference increases. Overall, our results question recent gains reported due to MOPD and suggest careful tuning of more efficient off-policy baselines as a viable alternative.

</details>


### [315] [Integrated Imputation-Classification for Supervised Learning with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04273)
*Yue Liu,Ben Liang,Ali Tizghadam,Ilijc Albanese*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IICN，将插补器与(n+1)类判别器对抗联合训练于单一分类目标，证明全局最优时实现缺失坐标边缘化并得到贝叶斯最优分类器，在多个数据集上超越传统与生成式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缺失特征值下的监督分类方法通常将插补与分类解耦，产生的插补虽看似合理但对预测缺乏信息量，导致分类性能受限。

Method: 提出集成插补与分类网络IICN，联合训练插补器与一个(n+1)类判别器：判别器需区分n个真实类别及一个额外的"已插补"类，二者以单一类别监督分类目标进行对抗训练。

Result: 理论上证明在全局最优处，插补器与判别器共同实现缺失坐标上的边缘化，从而得到贝叶斯最优分类器；在FashionMNIST、CIFAR-10及天然存在缺失的表格数据集上，IICN优于经典"先插补后分类"流程及近期生成式基线，在困难设置下表现稳健且准确。

Conclusion: IICN通过对抗式联合训练将插补与分类整合到统一目标中，具备贝叶斯最优性理论保证，并在多类数据上验证了其鲁棒性与精度优势。

Abstract: We study supervised classification problems with missing feature values. Existing approaches often decouple imputation from classification, producing imputations that may be plausible but uninformative for prediction. Instead, we propose the Integrated Imputation and Classification Network (IICN), which jointly trains an imputer and an ${(n{+}1)}$-classdiscriminator adversarially with a single class supervised classification objective, where the discriminator learns to distinguish among the $n$ true classes and an additional ``imputed" class. We prove that at the global optimum, the imputer and discriminator together implement marginalization over missing coordinates and yield a Bayes-optimal classifier. We evaluate IICN on FashionMNIST, CIFAR-10, and tabular datasets with naturally occurring missingness. IICN outperforms classical impute-then-classify pipelines and recent generative baselines, showing strong robustness and accuracy in challenging settings.

</details>


### [316] [Do RUL explanations hold up? Faithfulness and stability of attributions on C-MAPSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04278)
*Manh Hien Nguyen,Ngoc Thanh Nguyen,Isabella Mendoza Cortes,Tam Khuat,Thanh Pham,Nhat Quang Tran,Ushik Shrestha Khwakhali,Loan Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 深度学习RUL模型（1D CNN、LSTM、小型Transformer）在NASA C-MAPSS上训练后，附加三种归因图（IG、遮挡、末层注意力）并按faithfulness、噪声稳定性、跨种子一致性、bin内cosine一致性进行评估；结论推荐在面向检修工程师的报告中采用遮挡或IG，注意力权重仅作可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C-MAPSS上深度RUL模型与传感器-时间步热力图已成常规，但"看起来像机械图"的热力图不等于工程师可据以行动的可靠解释；XAI-for-PdM文献对faithfulness、稳定性、跨种子一致性等检验仍报告不足，缺乏对归因方法可靠性的系统评估。

Method: 在官方FD001与FD003划分上训练三种标准架构（1D CNN、LSTM、小型Transformer），采用分段RUL上限125周期与官方PHM08非对称评分；挂接三种归因图（Integrated Gradients、遮挡、末层注意力），并执行删除/插入faithfulness、传感器尺度噪声下Spearman稳定性、跨训练种子一致性、RUL分箱内cosine一致性等检验；另在AI4I 2020故障数据集上对树模型重要性做表格化对照验证。

Result: 预测误差被作为前提而非主张；核心发现是哪类解释方法在移除其顶部单元时能真正推动RUL输出、哪类图能经受5%输入扰动。LSTM上IG与遮挡faithfulness相近；Transformer注意力计算廉价且时间上平滑，但faithfulness较弱。三种图在5%输入噪声下几乎不变，然而IG/遮挡在两个LSTM种子间仅中度一致——对传感器抖动的稳定性不等于对重训练的稳定性。AI4I 2020上树模型重要性呈相同的删除模式。

Conclusion: 面向检修工程师阅读的C-MAPSS类报告建议采用遮挡或IG，原始注意力权重仅作可视化用途；同时强调需区分输入扰动稳定性与重训练稳定性，呼吁在PdM的XAI评估中补齐faithfulness与跨种子一致性等被低估的检验。

Abstract: Deep remaining-useful-life (RUL) models on NASA C-MAPSS are now routine, and so are heatmaps that colour sensors and timesteps. A heatmap that looks mechanical is not the same as an explanation an engineer can act on. We train three standard architectures - a 1D CNN, an LSTM, and a small Transformer encoder - on the official FD001 and FD003 splits with the piecewise RUL cap of 125 cycles and the official PHM08 asymmetric score. We then attach three attribution maps (Integrated Gradients, occlusion, last-layer attention) and evaluate them with the checks the XAI-for-PdM literature still under-reports: deletion/insertion faithfulness, Spearman stability under sensor-scale noise, agreement across training seeds, and cosine consistency inside RUL bins. Prediction error is a prerequisite, not the claim. The headline is which explanation method moves the RUL output when its top cells are removed, and which map survives a 5% input perturbation. Integrated Gradients and occlusion are similarly faithful on the LSTM; Transformer attention is cheap and temporally smooth but weakly faithful. All three maps are almost unchanged under 5% input noise, yet IG/occlusion agree only moderately across two LSTM seeds - stability to sensor jitter is not the same as stability to retraining. A secondary tabular check on the AI4I 2020 failure dataset shows the same deletion pattern for tree importances. We recommend occlusion or IG for any C-MAPSS-style report that will be read by a maintenance engineer, and we treat raw attention weights as a visualisation only.

</details>


### [317] [First-Order Steering: Translating Weight Adaptation into Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04283)
*Sri Pranav Kunda,Alexander Kurz,Tomas Dominik,Uri Maoz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 First-Order Steering，将激活引导形式化为权重更新矩阵的一阶近似，并给出近似误差理论界；进而提出 HeRD-Merging 模型合并方法以最小化一阶近似误差，从而生成可组合、更准确的引导向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导可借助残差流中的可解释方向在推理时操控模型行为，但现有方法难以将多个目标行为的引导向量组合同时施加。已有模型合并研究表明，通过学习到的权重适配表示的目标行为可高精度组合。因此，若能建立权重适配到激活引导向量的转换，就可把模型合并的优势扩展到可组合的推理时行为控制。

Method: 提出 First-Order Steering：将激活引导表述为由引导强度向量参数化的权重更新矩阵的一阶近似，并建立一阶引导近似误差的理论界。在此基础上提出 HeRD-Merging 模型合并流程，通过最小化一阶近似误差项来提升一阶引导的精度，使产生的权重适配能导出更准确的引导向量。

Result: 所生成的引导向量在单一行为与组合行为的控制上均比现有激活引导方法更准确；HeRD-Merging 在性能上与传统模型合并基线相当，同时其权重适配可支持更准确的一阶引导向量。

Conclusion: 将激活引导理解为权重更新的一阶近似，并通过误差最小的模型合并来构造权重适配，可同时实现可组合的推理时行为控制与不劣于基线的模型合并性能，为激活引导与模型合并两个方向的结合提供了理论依据与方法框架。

Abstract: Activation steering exploits interpretable directions in the residual stream to enable inference-time manipulation of model behavior. Composing steering vectors to apply multiple target behaviors simultaneously is important in various fields-including AI alignment and safety-but remains a challenge for existing activation steering methods. In contrast, prior work in model merging shows that target behaviors represented by learned weight adaptations can be combined with high accuracy. A method that translates weight adaptations into activation steering vectors could therefore extend prior work in model merging to generate composable steering vectors that better enable simultaneous inference-time behavioral control. For this, we introduce First-Order Steering, a formulation of activation steering as a first-order approximation of weight update matrices parameterized by a vector of steering strengths, and establish theoretical bounds on the approximation error of first-order steering. We then develop a novel model merging procedure, HeRD-Merging, which minimizes the first-order approximation error terms to enable higher first-order steering accuracy. Together, our method produces steering vectors that control both individual and composed behaviors more accurately than existing activation steering methods. Furthermore, HeRD-Merging matches the performance of conventional model-merging baselines, while producing weight adaptations that admit more accurate first-order steering vectors.

</details>


### [318] [Questioning the Questions: Sustaining Self-Evolution in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04299)
*Jinyuan Li,Chengsong Huang,Langlin Huang,Donghong Cai,Shiping Gao,Yuyi Yang,Jiaxin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析自进化推理模型在多轮自训练中性能崩溃的原因，提出R-Quest方法，通过问题有效性与新颖性反馈引导自进化，在多个基准上取得最优性能并保持十轮稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化推理模型通过自生成问题学习，但重复自训练会导致性能崩溃。作者希望探究性能随轮次下降的机理，并找到维持自进化可持续性的方法。

Method: 分析发现自生成问题存在两类质量问题：无效问题随轮次增多，且答案一致性过滤会进一步抬高其在训练数据中的比例；基于词汇相似度的多样性控制会漏掉数学等价但表述不同的问题，导致后期问题多样性崩溃。据此提出R-Quest：先用问题有效性与新颖性反馈引导自进化；训练求解器识别并拒绝无效问题，再用其判断引导提问者奖励并过滤求解器训练数据；使用冻结基模型比较采样问题对以提供新颖性反馈，避免问题重复。

Result: 在两个模型家族的数学推理、通用领域推理和代码生成共12个基准上，R-Quest持续取得最高平均性能；在十轮自进化中保持稳定增益，最终轮达到峰值，并比R-Zero高出17.32分。

Conclusion: 自进化的性能崩溃主要源于无效问题累积和数学等价问题导致的多样性崩溃；引入有效性与新颖性反馈可有效缓解这两类问题，实现长期稳定的自进化提升。

Abstract: Self-evolving reasoning models learn from their own generated questions, yet repeated self-training can lead to performance collapse. In this paper, we investigate why performance deteriorates over successive rounds and how to sustain self-evolution. Our analysis identifies two recurring quality problems in self-generated questions: invalid questions and repeated variants of the same mathematical questions. First, invalid questions become more prevalent across rounds, and answer-consistency filtering further increases their proportion in training data. Second, existing question diversity controls based on lexical similarity can miss mathematically equivalent questions expressed in different ways, which leads to question diversity collapse in later training rounds. Building on these findings, we introduce R-Quest, which uses question validity and novelty feedback to guide self-evolution. We first train the solver to recognize and reject invalid questions, then use its judgments to guide questioner rewards and filter solver training data. To avoid question repetition, we use a frozen base model to compare sampled question pairs and provide novelty feedback. Empirically, our method consistently achieves the highest average performance on 12 benchmarks in mathematical reasoning, general-domain reasoning, and code generation across two model families. Additionally, R-Quest maintains stable performance gains over ten rounds of self-evolution, peaking in the final round and outperforming R-Zero by 17.32 points.

</details>


### [319] [What to Preserve in Recursive Computation: A Local Predictive Sufficiency Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04303)
*Peilin Wang,Feng Shiyang,Hongfu Gao,Cencheng Zhao,Di Yuan,Hui Chen,Guiguang Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"递归预测闭包"原则，将局部预测充分性转化为可训练的投影约束，通过在参数更新时保持预测信息来提升递归压缩模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归计算反复压缩或复用中间状态，导致一个矛盾：需要在较长递归路径上保持有用的信息，同时也面临更多在到达最终预测前丢失的机会。现有的重建或局部预测目标虽提供可处理监督，但无法保证保留的信息对后续递归计算仍然充分。

Method: 作者将局部预测充分性等同于"递归预测闭包"（控制各接口处的局部预测缺陷即可控制根节点的差异）。从变分刻画出发，推导出有限预测检验和经验预测缺陷，用以衡量压缩中保留的预测价值。其预测敏感度定义了参数更新的边缘松弛半空间约束，仅当预测保持会被违反时，才将宿主优化器提议的更新投影到这些约束的交集上。该任务无关原则通过宿主兼容的干预方式在四类任务中实例化。

Result: 在时序图、语言记忆、视觉-语言-动作控制、递归自我改进四类任务上，在匹配压缩预算下，该方法与对应宿主模型相当或更优，且在更重递归或记忆需求下增益更大，同时更好地在连续变换中保持预测信息。

Conclusion: 研究确立了递归接口处的预测保持作为递归压缩的通用训练原则，且保持端点任务、骨干网络和评估协议不变。

Abstract: Recursive computation repeatedly compresses or reuses intermediate states, creating a simple tension: information that must remain useful across longer recursive paths is also exposed to more opportunities for loss before reaching the final prediction. Existing reconstruction or local-prediction objectives provide tractable supervision, but do not ensure that the retained information remains sufficient for subsequent recursive computation. We identify local predictive sufficiency with recursive predictive closure: controlling local predictive deficiencies at individual interfaces controls the resulting discrepancy at the root. We then turn this principle into a tractable training procedure. Starting from a variational characterization, we derive finite predictive tests and an empirical predictive deficiency that measures predictive value retained across compression. Its predictive sensitivities define margin-relaxed half-space constraints on parameter updates, and we project the host optimizer's proposed update onto their intersection only when predictive preservation would otherwise be violated. Across temporal graphs, language memory, vision-language-action control, and recursive self-improvement, the method matches or improves the corresponding host models under matched compression budgets, with larger gains under heavier recursive or memory demands, while better preserving predictive information across successive transformations. Crucially, the same task-agnostic predictive-preservation principle is instantiated across all four settings through host-compatible interventions while keeping the endpoint task, backbone, and evaluation protocol fixed. These results establish predictive preservation at recursive interfaces as a general training principle for recursive compression.

</details>


### [320] [Modeling Deletion Requests in Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04310)
*Christian Cianfarani,Aloni Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究用户行使"被遗忘权"请求删除数据时如何影响模型行为，定义自适应性和集体性两类用户行为，通过随机优化理论揭示行为差异带来的理论差距，并利用数据估值技术设计删除请求策略以显著改变模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘被视为在AI模型中实现"被遗忘权"的有前景方法。既有研究多关注遗忘算法本身，而本文关注一个新角度：用户在行使删除权时，其行为方式（如何组织删除请求）会如何影响模型的最终行为，以及不同用户行为之间是否存在实质性的效果差异。

Method: 1) 定义用户在请求删除数据时可能采用的两种行为：自适应性（adaptivity，用户根据模型反馈调整请求）和集体性（collectivity，用户联合发起请求）；2) 建立用户目标与随机优化结果之间的联系，进行理论分析；3) 借助数据估值技术设计"删除请求者"（deletion requesters），以在现实场景中显著改变模型行为；4) 在计算机视觉任务上开展实验。

Result: 理论分析证明了具有与不具有自适应性、集体性行为的用户群体在潜在影响上存在差距。实验表明不同用户行为模型对模型产生差异化影响，并尝试分离自适应性（adaptivity）和集体性（collectivity）各自的贡献。

Conclusion: 用户的行为方式（自适应性与集体性）是决定删除请求效果的关键因素，通过数据估值技术设计的删除请求者可显著改变模型行为，表明"被遗忘权"的实际影响高度依赖于用户如何组织其删除请求。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is seen as a promising approach to enable users to exercise the "right to erasure" in the context of AI models. We ask how users might influence the behavior of models when exercising this right. We define two types of behaviors that users might adopt when requesting the deletion of their data: adaptivity and collectivity. Drawing connections between the goals of users in this context and results in stochastic optimization, we demonstrate theoretical gaps between the potential effects of groups of users who do and do not display these behaviors. We then show how techniques from data valuation might be used to design deletion requesters that can significantly alter model behavior in realistic settings. In experiments on computer vision tasks, we demonstrate the differential effects of different models of user behavior and attempt to isolate the impacts of adaptivity and collectivity.

</details>


### [321] [LyapuFlow: Controlling Generative Flows with Lyapunov Feedback for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04326)
*Minseon Gwak,Hans Hao-Hsun Hsu,Danielle C. Maddix,N. Benjamin Erichson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LyapuFlow 是一种基于李雅普诺夫反馈控制的预训练流模型测试时约束方法，通过预测终端样本并施加最小范数控制来满足约束，在科学机器学习和图像逆问题中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练流模型广泛用作科学与视觉领域的生成先验，推理时引导可在无需重训练的情况下施加测试时约束。现有方法依赖投影、后验采样或对生成轨迹的迭代优化，存在局限性，需要一种替代机制。

Method: LyapuFlow 在每一步采样中：预测当前流所趋向的终端样本，并在该预测上评估约束违反程度；然后计算满足预设李雅普诺夫下降条件的最小范数控制。当无控动力学已能以规定速率降低约束违反时，控制保持不激活；否则在反馈信赖域内提供修正更新，防止控制主导预训练动力学。

Result: 在数据空间和潜空间均进行了验证，在科学机器学习和图像逆问题的测试时约束施加任务上，性能优于采用不同机制的替代方法。

Conclusion: 基于李雅普诺夫反馈控制的测试时引导是预训练流模型约束施加的有效替代方案，可在不重训练的前提下兼顾约束满足与保留预训练动力学特性。

Abstract: Pretrained flow models are now widely used as generative priors in science and vision, where inference-time guidance enables test-time constraints without retraining. Existing methods use projection, posterior sampling, or iterative optimization of the generative trajectory. We propose LyapuFlow, an alternative based on Lyapunov feedback control. At each sampling step, LyapuFlow predicts the terminal sample towards which the current flow is evolving, and evaluates the constraint violation on this prediction. Then, we compute the minimum-norm control that satisfies a prescribed Lyapunov decrease condition. The resulting control remains inactive when the uncontrolled dynamics already reduce the constraint violation at the prescribed rate. Otherwise, it provides a corrective update within a feedback trust region that prevents the control from dominating the pretrained dynamics. We demonstrate LyapuFlow in both data and latent spaces, outperforming alternatives spanning different mechanisms for test-time constraint enforcement in scientific machine learning and image inverse problems.

</details>


### [322] [S$^3$N: A Spherical Spiral Scanning Network for Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04338)
*Fan Yan,Chen Hui,Weisi Lin,Haiqi Zhu,Feng Jiang,Sun-Yuan Kung,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出球面螺旋扫描网络 S³N，通过 L2Proj 实现 LL 与 HEALPix 网格间的双向映射，并用 AQSS 块沿四条极到极螺旋路径进行状态空间扫描，从而解决 HP 网格方法的重构误差与跨面通信问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 HEALPix 网格的机器学习天气预报可避免传统经纬度网格在极区的面积畸变，但现有 HP 方法多采用逐点映射并在独立基面或局部窗口内处理像素，导致映射重构误差，且跨面通信依赖手工边界处理或移位窗口。

Method: 提出 S³N。其一，L2Proj 通过连续有限元表示的 L² 投影，实现大气场在 LL 与 HP 网格间的双向映射。其二，Attention-Guided Quad-Spiral State-Space Scanning（AQSS）块利用跨纬度注意力引导选择性状态空间更新，沿四条全局极到极螺旋路径传播信息。

Result: 实验表明，S³N 在 4 天、7 天和 10 天预报时效上取得更优结果，并在长程预报中表现出更慢的误差增长。

Conclusion: S³N 同时缓解了 HP 网格的映射误差与跨面通信问题，无需额外边界处理机制即可实现跨基面的连续信息传播，在中期与长程天气预报中均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Machine learning-based weather prediction (MLWP) has achieved strong performance in global weather forecasting. Recent Hierarchical Equal Area isoLatitude Pixelation (HEALPix)-based methods use the HEALPix (HP) grid to avoid area distortion near the poles of conventional latitude-longitude (LL) grids. However, existing HP-based approaches often use pointwise mapping methods and process HP pixels within separate base faces or local windows. Consequently, the mapping may introduce reconstruction errors and cross-face communication depends on handcrafted boundary handling or shifted windows. We propose the Spherical Spiral Scanning Network (S$^3$N) to address both limitations. First, L2Proj provides a bidirectional method for mapping atmospheric fields between the LL and HP grids through an $L^2$ projection of their continuous finite-element representations. Second, the Attention-Guided Quad-Spiral State-Space Scanning (AQSS) block uses cross-latitude attention to guide selective state-space updates along four global pole-to-pole spiral paths. This design enables continuous information propagation across HP base-face boundaries without additional boundary-processing mechanisms. Experiments show that S$^3$N achieves better results at 4-, 7-, and 10-day lead times, and exhibits slower error growth in long-range forecasting.

</details>


### [323] [Hierarchical Credit Assignment for RLVR on Fused Gromov-Wasserstein Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04344)
*Qi Yu,Ruizhong Qiu,Zhichen Zeng,Xuying Ning,Yanjun Zhao,Dongqi Fu,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HarA，一种面向组式RLVR的分层信用分配方法，通过Fused Gromov-Wasserstein重心衡量推理行为的全局语义新颖性，并重加权token级优势以增强LLM探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组式RLVR方法（如GRPO）对同一结果的所有rollout内所有token赋予统一优势，难以区分贡献。已有工作仅基于token位置或熵等局部信号改进信用分配，无法捕捉推理行为相对于当前策略的全局语义新颖性。

Method: HarA将每个采样rollout表示为token隐状态与位置的分布，计算同一结果下所有rollout的Fused Gromov-Wasserstein（FGW）重心，以刻画当前策略下的潜在推理模式；用当前rollout到重心的FGW距离贡献度量推理元素的语义新颖性。为降低FGW求解开销，引入锚点引导的线性化，将其转化为可用Sinkhorn算法高效求解的Wasserstein形式，并据此对组式RLVR的token级优势重加权，在灵活粒度上突出新颖推理行为。

Result: 在三种组式RLVR方法上的大量实验表明，该即插即用方法有效增强LLM探索能力，在多样推理基准上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: HarA通过全局语义新颖性驱动的分层信用分配，弥补了局部信号方法的不足，可即插即用地提升组式RLVR的探索效果与推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has been shown to improve the reasoning capability of large language models (LLMs) across diverse reasoning tasks. However, group-based RLVR methods, such as GRPO, assign a uniform advantage to all tokens within rollouts of the same outcome. While existing works refine credit assignment of GRPO based on local signals such as token locations or entropy, they often fail to capture the global semantic novelty of a reasoning behavior relative to the current policy. In this work, we propose a hierarchical credit assignment approach for group-based RLVR methods, called HarA, which identifies and encourages semantically novel reasoning behaviors during RLVR. HarA represents each sampled rollout as a distribution over the hidden states and locations of tokens, and computes the Fused Gromov-Wasserstein (FGW) barycenters of all rollouts with the same outcome, capturing the internal reasoning patterns in the latent space under the current policy. The semantic novelty of a reasoning element can then be measured by its contribution to the FGW distance between the current rollout and the barycenter. While solving the FGW formulation is expensive, we introduce an anchor-guided linearization that turns it into a Wasserstein formulation solvable via the Sinkhorn algorithm efficiently. By reweighing token-level advantage of group-based RLVR methods based on the novelty signals, HarA highlights novel reasoning behaviors at flexible granularities to encourage fine-grained LLM exploration. Extensive experiments across three group-based RLVR methods show that our plug-and-play method effectively enhances the exploration of LLMs, outperforming existing methods across diverse reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [324] [Checkable NTK Positivity and Finite-Width Gradient Descent for Scalar- and Vector-Valued PINNs with Strong-Form, Weak-Form, and Nonlocal Linear Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04357)
*Zifan Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为带线性约束的物理信息神经网络（PINNs）提供了可验证的正定性判据与有限宽度梯度下降保证：对强形式微分行、弱形式残差/边界泛函、有限测度非局部观测等各类约束，极限NTK正定等价于固定设计系数/泛函/矩矩阵的秩条件，该条件可在训练前计算并检测结构零模；给定证书后，显式宽度与步长条件保证经验NTK保留至少一半极限间隙，且全批量梯度下降每步几何式降低训练损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs 的训练收敛性缺乏理论保证，尤其在含微分、初始/边界及非局部等线性约束时，极限 NTK 可能因结构零模而退化，导致梯度下降失效。作者希望提供训练前可计算的证书，用以判定 NTK 正定性并给出有限宽度下的收敛保证。

Method: 研究对象为带线性约束的标量或向量值两层 PINN，激活函数为光滑非多项式且有界对称初始化（如均匀初始化的 tanh）。约束涵盖任意固定有限阶强形式微分行（含耦合系统）、任意线性初始/边界条件、弱形式残差与边界泛函，以及有限测度非局部观测（积分、非局部扩散、Dirac 数据行），维度任意。方法上，将极限 NTK 的正定性等价转化为固定设计的有限系数矩阵、泛函矩阵或矩矩阵的秩条件，并由此导出有限宽度与步长条件。

Result: 极限 NTK 正定性与固定设计系数/泛函/矩矩阵的秩条件等价，该证书可在训练前计算以检测结构零模。在给定证书下，显式宽度与步长条件保证：以高概率经验 NTK 至少保留极限间隙的一半，且全批量梯度下降每次迭代均几何式降低训练损失。受控实验验证了证书与有限宽度机制。

Conclusion: 论文为带线性约束的 PINNs 建立了统一的正定性判据与收敛理论：通过可提前计算的秩条件证书判断极限 NTK 是否正定，并在有限宽度下保证梯度下降的几何收敛，适用于强/弱形式及非局部观测等多类约束。

Abstract: We give checkable positive-definiteness criteria for the limiting neural tangent kernel (NTK) and high-probability finite-width gradient-descent guarantees for scalar- or vector-valued physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) with linear constraints. The constraints may be strong-form differential rows of any fixed finite order, including coupled systems, with any linear initial or boundary conditions; weak-form residual and boundary functionals; or finite-measure nonlocal observations such as integral, nonlocal-diffusion, and Dirac-data rows, all in any dimension. For each class, positive definiteness of the limiting NTK is equivalent to a rank condition on a finite coefficient, functional, or moment matrix of the fixed design: a certificate computed before training that detects structural zero modes. The model hypotheses are those of an ordinary two-layer PINN, a smooth nonpolynomial activation with bounded symmetric initialization, and are met by standard choices such as $\tanh$ with uniform initialization. Given a certificate, explicit width and step-size conditions ensure, with high probability, that the empirical NTK retains at least half the limiting gap and that full-batch gradient descent decreases the training loss geometrically at every iteration. Controlled experiments check the certificates and the finite-width mechanism.

</details>


### [325] [A Bird's-Eye View of Iterative Reward Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04364)
*Logan Mondal Bhamidipaty,Lauren Robson,Linda Petrini,Shengrui Lyu,Kamal Ndousse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出BIRD基准，将已有的LLM迭代奖励设计方法统一到相同的配置与评估空间，从而可直接比较算法、消融设计选择并在匹配反馈条件下原型化新组件；在MuJoCo、Meta-World、Assistax、HumanoidBench上发现少量简单设计选择即可稳定提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL中设计有效奖励函数通常依赖大量专业知识与试错。近期基于LLM的系统可利用策略反馈生成并迭代改进奖励代码，但这些方法在实现细节、反馈假设和评估环境上各不相同，难以直接比较，也阻碍了对设计选择的系统理解与复用。

Method: 提出BIRD（Benchmark for Iterative Reward Design）基准，将现有方法表达在统一的配置与评估空间中，使得可以直接比较算法、逐项消融设计选择，并在匹配的反馈条件与策略训练预算下原型化新组件；在MuJoCo、Meta-World、Assistax和HumanoidBench上进行实验。

Result: 在四个环境中识别出一小组简单设计选择能够持续提升性能；将这些选择组合后，性能显著优于所评估的先前方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明简单基线具有较强竞争力，并提示需要进一步研究在何种情况下增加算法复杂度才能真正改进迭代奖励设计；同时提供开源代码以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Designing effective reward functions in RL typically requires substantial expertise and trial and error. Recent work automates this process with LLM-based systems that generate and iteratively improve reward code using policy feedback. However, these methods are often hard to compare because they differ in implementation details, feedback assumptions, and evaluation environments. To address this, we introduce a Benchmark for Iterative Reward Design (BIRD) that expresses existing methods in a unified configuration and evaluation space. This lets us compare algorithms directly, ablate individual design choices, and prototype new components under matched feedback conditions and policy-training budgets. Across MuJoCo, Meta-World, Assistax, and HumanoidBench, we identify a small set of simple design choices that consistently improve performance. Combining these choices yields significantly better performance than the evaluated methods from prior work. Our results highlight the strength of simple baselines and motivate further study of when additional algorithmic complexity improves iterative reward design. Code is available at https://github.com/safety-research/bird.

</details>


### [326] [A multi-stage probabilistic framework to estimate gas-fired generator performance during extreme winter weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04368)
*Sajjad Uddin Mahmud,Anamika Dubey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用公开气象与电力需求数据，结合NERC匿名机组故障记录，构建三阶段贝叶斯概率框架，量化冬季极端天气下发电机组停运概率、可用容量损失与事件持续时间；以纽约州2013—2022年为案例，揭示了低温与高需求对机组可靠性的显著负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端冬季天气反复扰乱美国燃气发电，但系统量化停运风险所需的机组级数据多为专有数据，难以获取，导致可靠性评估缺乏可迁移的基线方法。

Method: 采用公开气象与电力需求数据，结合NERC匿名发电机故障（contingency）记录，建立三阶段贝叶斯概率框架，依次估计：机组故障事件的逐时概率、事件发生条件下的期望净可用容量、以及事件持续时间。以纽约州2013—2022年为应用案例。

Result: 气温越低、电力需求越高，故障概率越高、保留容量越低、事件持续时间越长。在最严重的观测压力条件下，估计的逐时事件平均概率达24%，期望平均净可用容量降至铭牌容量的13%。完全停运事件持续时间中位数为12.7小时；部分降额事件持续时间随容量损失严重程度从2.4小时增至7.1小时。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个可迁移的基线，拥有机组级记录的电力公司可直接扩展使用，从而获得更精确的可靠性估计，服务于运行规划与资源充裕性评估。

Abstract: Extreme winter weather has repeatedly disrupted gas-fired power generation in the United States, yet the plant-level data needed to systematically quantify outage risk remain proprietary. Using publicly available weather and electricity demand data together with anonymized generator contingency records from the North American Electric Reliability Corporation (NERC), we develop a three-stage Bayesian probabilistic framework for estimating winter-driven generator performance. Applied to New York State (2013--2022), the framework sequentially estimates: the hourly probability of a generator contingency event, the expected net available capacity conditioned on an event occurring, and the event duration. Colder conditions and higher electricity demand are associated with higher failure probability, lower retained capacity, and longer event duration. Under the most severe observed stress conditions, estimated mean hourly event probability reaches 24\% , while expected mean net available capacity falls to 13\% of nameplate rating. Full outage events have a median duration of 12.7 hours, while partial derating event duration increases from 2.4 to 7.1 hours with capacity loss severity. The proposed framework establishes a transferable baseline that utilities with access to plant-level records can directly extend to obtain more precise reliability estimates for operational planning and resource adequacy assessment.

</details>


### [327] [CEENs: Causality-enforced evolutional networks for solving time-dependent partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04405)
*Jeahan Jung,Heechang Kim,Hyomin Shin,Minseok Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将时间因果性融入PINN训练的方法，通过将时间域划分为不重叠子区间、每个子区间使用独立神经网络、基于PDE积分形式构造损失函数并顺序训练，显著提升长时间PDE模拟精度，同时降低计算与内存成本，并给出并行化算法进一步加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始PINN公式缺乏对时间因果性的考虑，导致在尚未学好初始条件时便偏向满足较晚时刻的控制方程，从而在PDE长时间积分中产生错误解、精度受限。因此需要一种能够将时间因果性显式融入训练过程的方案。

Method: 受经典数值方法中体现时间因果性的启发，将时间域划分为互不重叠的子区间，为每个子区间分配一个独立的神经网络，并基于PDE在这些子区间上的积分形式构造损失函数；网络从初始时间步开始进行顺序训练。此外，提出并行化算法以提升计算效率。

Result: 在多种PDE问题的长时间模拟中，当原始PINN方法失效时，该方法在精度上取得显著提升，且相比PINN方法需要更少的计算成本和内存；并行化算法在求解时间相关PDE时展现出显著的加速效果。

Conclusion: 通过将时间因果性无缝集成到训练中，可有效克服PINN在长时间PDE积分中的局限，在精度、计算成本和内存方面均优于原始PINN，并可通过并行化进一步加速，为时间相关PDE求解提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Despite the growing popularity of physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), their applicability in the long-time integration of partial differential equations (PDEs) remains constrained. We argue that this problem stems from the lack of consideration of temporal causality in the original PINN formulation, resulting in a bias towards satisfying governing equations at later times before learning the initial condition and hence leading to erroneous solutions. To this end, we propose a novel method that seamlessly integrates temporal causality into the training process. Drawing inspiration from classical numerical methods where the temporal causality is reflected, we divide the time domain into nonoverlapping subintervals, assign a unique neural network to each subinterval, and construct a loss function founded on the integral form of PDEs within these subintervals. The proposed networks undergo sequential training, beginning with the initial time step. Our method demonstrates significant improvement in accuracy for long-time simulations of various PDE problems where the original PINN method fails while it requires less computational cost and memory compared to the PINN method. A parallelization algorithm is provided to further enhance the computational efficiency, showing a significant speedup for solving time-dependent PDEs.

</details>


### [328] [Specific Algorithmic Interpretability of Neural Networks: A Case Study on Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04413)
*Yanglin Zhang,Anneke von Seeger,Gilad Lerman,Ron Levie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个构造可解释神经网络的原则性框架：先用算法初始化参数使网络精确实现某个统计估计算法，再在控制偏离初始化程度的前提下微调，从而在泛化与可解释性之间取得平衡，并以纹理分类为例进行了实例化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有受算法启发的网络架构虽能解释计算结构，但标准训练后的参数往往不再与原始算法保持清晰关系，导致可解释性丢失。作者希望弥合这一鸿沟，使训练后的网络仍保留算法层面的显式解释。

Method: 首先将每个数据点建模为依赖类别的随机过程样本，并假设其统计量可由单样本估计且足以区分类别；据此构造初始参数恰好实现该统计量估计算法的神经网络，使其完全可解释。为应对理想模型与真实数据的偏差，在限制参数偏离算法初始化的前提下进行微调。通过PAC-Bayes分析给出泛化界，其复杂度项随微调半径增长。最后用散射变换估计判别性统计量，将该框架实例化于纹理分类。

Result: 微调后的网络大致保留初始网络的算法解释；PAC-Bayes分析给出了泛化界，其复杂度项随微调半径缩放，为方法提供了统计动机；框架成功实例化于基于散射变换的纹理分类任务。

Conclusion: 通过在算法初始化附近受限微调，可在获得适应真实数据能力的同时保持网络的可解释性，并以泛化理论支持微调半径的选取，为构造兼具可解释性与泛化性的神经网络提供了可推广的原则性范式。

Abstract: We develop a principled framework for constructing neural networks whose specific parameter realizations admit an explicit algorithmic interpretation. Existing algorithm-inspired architectures can explain the computational structure of a network, yet after standard training the learned parameters need not retain a clear relation to the motivating algorithm. We address this gap as follows. First, we model each data point as a sample of a class-dependent stochastic process and assume that statistics of this process can be estimated from a single sample and these statistics are sufficient to distinguish the classes. We then construct a neural network whose initial parameters exactly implement an algorithm for estimating these statistics, making the network fully interpretable. To account for mismatch between the idealized model and real data, we fine-tune this network while controlling its deviation from the algorithmic initialization. The trained network hence roughly retains the interpretation of the initial network. A PAC-Bayesian analysis yields a uniform generalization bound whose complexity term scales with the fine-tuning radius, providing a statistical motivation for our approach. We instantiate the framework for texture classification using the scattering transform to estimate the discriminative statistics.

</details>


### [329] [MaDeL: Manifold-Decomposed Feature Losses for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04419)
*Beomsu Kim,Jong Chul Ye,Kwanyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出流形分解特征损失MaDeL，无需显式估计流形即可将生成目标分解为流形切向变化与法向偏离两部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向同性目标（如MSE）对集中于低维流形附近的数据，会将流形内部的有效变化与偏离流形的无效位移混为一谈。在稀疏、强约束领域，这种环境空间回归易导致偏离流形的插值。

Method: MaDeL从被破坏的观测中学习互补表示：一个表示重建干净样本，另一个重建所施加的破坏。在特征瓶颈下，二者Jacobian分别与切空间和法空间对齐（线性流形上精确成立，光滑流形上局部成立），由此构成各向异性的目标函数，分别度量内在变化与偏离流形的偏差。

Result: 在合成数据、地球与气候科学、扭转角基准上，MaDeL在单步采样下提升了支撑恢复与平均角度W1距离；在蛋白质骨架上，减少了单步与少步采样预算下的空间位阻冲突。

Conclusion: 研究表明，生成目标可从数据中直接区分流形平行变化与正交偏离，而无需显式估计流形，从而改善强约束生成任务的采样质量。

Abstract: Generative models are often trained with isotropic objectives such as mean-squared error. For data concentrated near a low-dimensional manifold, however, such losses conflate displacement along the manifold, which may represent valid variation, with displacement away from it, which produces invalid samples. This mismatch is especially problematic in sparse, highly constrained domains, where ambient-space regression can encourage off-manifold interpolation. We ask whether a generative objective can distinguish manifold-parallel variation from manifold-orthogonal deviation directly from data, without explicitly estimating the manifold. We introduce a manifold-decomposed feature loss (MaDeL) that learns complementary representations from corrupted observations: one is trained to recover the clean sample, while the other is trained to recover the corruption. We show that, under a feature bottleneck, their Jacobians align with the tangent and normal spaces, exactly for linear manifolds and locally for smooth manifolds. Together, these representations define an anisotropic objective that separately measures intrinsic variation and off-manifold deviation. Across synthetic, Earth and climate science, and torsion-angle benchmarks, MaDeL improves support recovery and average angular $W_1$ under single-step sampling; on protein backbones, it reduces steric clashes across one- and few-step sampling budgets.

</details>


### [330] [Measuring Effective Data Resolution in Guided Diffusion Posteriors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04422)
*Ridham Patel,Defu Cao,Jiacheng Pang,Yan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出“有效数据分辨率”指标，通过比较逆问题应得分辨率 dof_ref 与采样器实际实现分辨率 dof_samp，量化黑盒生成后验中数据对重建结果的真实约束程度，并据此揭示引导扩散采样器信息传递的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引导扩散采样器广泛用于从稀疏观测重建物理场，但现有诊断指标无法说明重建结果在多大程度上真正由数据决定，即缺乏对数据信息实际利用程度的量化手段。

Method: 提出有效数据分辨率概念，即逆问题所应保证的分辨率 dof_ref 与采样器实际实现的分辨率 dof_samp 之比较；设计扰动估计器，仅通过查询采样器即可测得 dof_samp 及空间映射 R(x,x)，并与精确参考进行验证。

Result: 测量结果表明：引导权重可显著改变表观信息传递；即使先验和得分函数精确，均值、离散度与分辨率也无法由单一权重联合校正；且分辨率保真度并不能可靠地由引导规则表面上的原理性所推断。

Conclusion: 数据实际约束重建的程度应被显式度量，仅凭引导规则的理论优雅性或单权重调参不足以保证分辨率保真，有效数据分辨率为评估生成式反问题采样器提供了可操作的诊断工具。

Abstract: Guided diffusion samplers are increasingly used to reconstruct physical fields from sparse observations, but standard diagnostics do not say how much of the reconstruction was actually determined by the data. We introduce effective data resolution for black-box generative posteriors: a comparison between the resolution warranted by the inverse problem, $\mathrm{dof}_{\mathrm{ref}}$, and the resolution realised by the sampler, $\mathrm{dof}_{\mathrm{samp}}$. A perturbation estimator measures $\mathrm{dof}_{\mathrm{samp}}$ and the spatial map $R(x,x)$ from sampler queries alone. We validate the estimator against exact references and use it to study guided diffusion. The resulting measurements show that guidance weight can strongly alter apparent information transfer, that mean, spread and resolution are not jointly corrected by one weight even with an exact prior and score, and that resolution fidelity does not follow reliably from the apparent principledness of a guidance rule.

</details>


### [331] [On the Trade-off Between Information Loss and Generalization in Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04424)
*Zhongqi Fan,Zheng Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对稀疏注意力机制进行了系统的理论分析：通过JS散度刻画稀疏化带来的近似误差，并通过Rademacher复杂度与互信息方法推导了依赖于稀疏度的泛化界，揭示了信息保真度与泛化能力之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力虽在缓解Transformer二次复杂度瓶颈上取得广泛经验成功，但其理论理解仍较零散，两个基本问题尚不清晰：(1) 稀疏化如何影响注意力机制的信息保真度；(2) 这种信息损失如何与模型泛化行为相互作用。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 首先通过JS散度刻画近似误差：假设注意力分数为独立同分布的亚高斯随机变量（参数为sigma），基于截断质量alpha的次序统计量集中分析，得到全注意力分布与稀疏注意力分布之间JS散度的闭式解 log2 + ((1-alpha)/2)log(1-alpha) - ((2-alpha)/2)log(2-alpha)。随后通过Rademacher复杂度推导泛化界；并基于互信息泛化界，结合稀疏假设类的熵与覆盖数分析，得到依赖于稀疏度的泛化界。

Result: Rademacher复杂度泛化界为 O(gamma * sqrt(M/n) * (sqrt(log(3eL/M)) + sqrt(pi)/2))；稀疏度相关的泛化界为 O(sqrt((M/(2n)) * (log(eL/M) + log(1 + 2/epsilon))))。

Conclusion: 分析表明，稀疏化在降低所考虑假设类复杂度的同时，会引入可由JS散度量化的近似误差。这些发现为稀疏Transformer架构中信息保真度与泛化能力之间的权衡提供了理论刻画。

Abstract: To mitigate the quadratic complexity bottleneck of the Transformer, sparse attention has emerged as a pivotal technology. Despite the extensive empirical success of sparse Transformers, the theoretical understanding of sparse attention remains fragmented. In particular, two fundamental questions remain unclear: (1) How does sparsification affect the information fidelity of attention mechanisms? (2) How does this information loss interact with the generalization behavior of the model? To bridge this gap, this paper proposes a systematic analysis of the Jensen-Shannon (JS) divergence and of the generalization gap of sparse attention mechanisms. Specifically, we first characterize the approximation error via the JS divergence. Through an order-statistics-based concentration analysis of the truncation mass alpha --- where the attention scores are assumed to be independent and identically distributed sub-Gaussian random variables with parameter sigma --- the JS divergence between the full attention distribution and the sparse attention distribution is shown to admit the closed form log 2 + ((1 - alpha)/2) log(1 - alpha) - ((2 - alpha)/2) log(2 - alpha). Subsequently, we derive a generalization bound through Rademacher complexity, quantified by O(gamma * sqrt(M/n) * (sqrt(log(3eL/M)) + sqrt(pi)/2)). Furthermore, building on a mutual-information-based generalization bound together with an entropy and covering-number analysis of the sparse hypothesis class, we obtain the sparsity-dependent generalization bound O(sqrt((M/(2n)) * (log(eL/M) + log(1 + 2/epsilon)))). Our analysis shows that sparsity reduces the complexity of the considered hypothesis class while introducing approximation error that can be quantified by the JS divergence. These findings provide a theoretical characterization of the trade-off between information fidelity and generalization in sparse Transformer architectures.

</details>


### [332] [UnAct: Gradient-Free Unlearning via Targeted Activation Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04426)
*Saeed Abdul Muizz,Aayat Rafiq,Iqra Altaf Gillani,Janibul Bashir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UnAct：一种仅需前向传播的无梯度类别遗忘方法，用遗忘图像的响应衰减末层单元连接，在遗忘数据稀缺时优于SSD/LFSSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免重训方法（如SSD、LFSSD）虽避免全量重训，但仍需在完整数据集上进行反向传播与参数重要性计算。实际中删除请求可能只带来少量待遗忘类别的图像，此时SSD/LFSSD会失效甚至使网络崩溃，因此需要一个低资源、数据稀缺下仍稳定的类别遗忘方案。

Method: 提出UnAct：无梯度、无标签、无保留数据的类别遗忘方法。仅对遗忘图像做前向传播，依据响应为末层单元打分，衰减响应最强的连接，最多重复20轮。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/20/100训练的ResNet-18上：遗忘整类时与SSD、LFSSD相当，且遗忘数据稀缺时不崩溃；各类规模下保留精度与重训差距≤2.5点，而SSD/LFSSD在全类操作点最多损失86点。CIFAR-10仅5张遗忘图像时，UnAct与重训距离0.21点，LFSSD为67、SSD为90，即使按规模用oracle重选SSD阈值也无法弥合差距。初步迁移到ViT-B/16：UnAct距重训11.5，SSD为33.7；在请求时计算重要性时，UnAct比SSD快19倍。

Conclusion: UnAct以纯前向、多轮衰减的方式实现了数据稀缺场景下稳健的类别遗忘，在ResNet与ViT上均优于SSD/LFSSD，且效率显著更高，代码已开源。

Abstract: Machine unlearning seeks to remove the influence of designated training data from a trained model without retraining from scratch. Retrain-free methods such as Selective Synaptic Dampening (SSD) and its label-free variant LFSSD avoid full retraining but still require backpropagation and parameter importance computed over the entire dataset.
