We ask: what happens when a deletion request arrives with only a few images of the class to be forgotten? To answer this question, we introduce UnAct, a gradient-free class-unlearning method that needs only forward passes over the forget images. UnAct scores late-layer units by their responses, attenuates the most responsive connections, and repeats this for up to 20 rounds using no gradients, no labels, and no retained data. On ResNet-18 trained with CIFAR-10, CIFAR-20, and CIFAR-100, UnAct is competitive with SSD and LFSSD when forgetting entire classes and, unlike them, never collapses the network when forget data is scarce. On ResNet-18, across all tested sizes, UnAct's retain accuracy stays within 2.5 points of retraining, while SSD and LFSSD, at their full-class operating points, lose up to 86 points on some classes. With five forget images on CIFAR-10, UnAct's distance to retraining is 0.21 points, against 67 for LFSSD and 90 for SSD, and re-selecting SSD's threshold at each size with an oracle does not close the gap. In preliminary transfer to ViT-B/16, UnAct's distance to retraining is 11.5 against 33.7 for SSD, and a request is 19x faster than SSD when SSD computes its importance at request time. The code is available at https://github.com/abdulmuizz0903/UnAct

</details>


### [333] [COSMOS: Soft Mechanism Mixtures with Verifiable Routing for Long-Horizon PDE Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04427)
*Anupam Rawat,Manikandan Padmanaban,Jagabondhu Hazra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出软机制混合神经算子COSMOS,四个过程偏置专家持续激活并由学习门控连续混合,配合零初始化全分辨率残差;同时给出已知混合权重的算子分裂组合基准。实验表明稠密软路由优于硬top-1,但小种子结果不稳定,且路由标签与专家机制不对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数神经算子针对单一方程族与离散化学习整体映射,而真实系统具有组合性:输运、扩散、波动与反应过程可同时作用。现有专家混合算子多采用稀疏top-K路由,但并发物理本质上是混合而非离散选择,因此需要软机制混合的算子框架。

Method: COSMOS含四个过程偏置专家,每步全部激活,由学习门控连续混合,特征经小网络融合;专家共享粗潜网格,零初始化全分辨率残差恢复瓶颈损失细节。另构建算子分裂组合基准,每条轨迹有已知混合权重w*。

Result: 在20步展开下对比族调FNO:3种子初评显示扩散-反应有增益、Navier-Stokes持平、浅水较差;11种子复核表明扩散-反应增益不稳定,无法给出可靠精度胜出结论。消融显示均匀路由或移除专家混合显著降低稳定区展开表现。带标签基准上稠密软路由误差较同融合的硬top-1低2.2倍,用生成权重w*推理可将展开误差降至0.034,揭示学习门控的局限。

Conclusion: COSMOS支持组合精度,但路由标签与专家预期机制不对齐,即模型学到的是组合加权而非机制身份辨识;结果也提示小种子展开比较需谨慎。

Abstract: Neural operators offer an efficient alternative to classical PDE solvers, but most learn a monolithic map per equation family and discretization. Real systems are compositional: transport, diffusion, wave, and reaction processes can act simultaneously. Existing mixture-of-experts operators typically use sparse top-$K$ routing, although concurrent physics is naturally a blend rather than a discrete choice.
  We propose COSMOS (Cooperative Operator Specialists with Mechanism-level Operator Soft-routing), a soft mechanism-mixture neural operator. Four process-biased specialists remain active at every step and are continuously mixed by a learned gate, with their features fused by a small network. Specialists share a coarse latent grid, while a zero-initialized full-resolution residual restores detail lost through the bottleneck. We also introduce an operator-splitting compositional benchmark with known mixture weights $w^\star$ per trajectory.
  Against family-tuned FNO under 20-step rollouts, an initial 3-seed evaluation suggested gains on diffusion--reaction, parity on Navier--Stokes, and weaker shallow-water performance. An 11-seed audit showed that the diffusion--reaction gain was unstable, motivating caution in small-seed rollout comparisons and precluding a reliable accuracy-win claim on these families. Ablations show that uniform routing or removing the specialist mixture substantially degrades stable-regime rollouts. On the labeled benchmark, dense soft routing yields $2.2\times$ lower error than hard top-1 routing at identical fusion; using generator weights $w^\star$ at inference further lowers rollout error to $0.034$, diagnosing limitations of the learned gate. However, routing labels do not align with the specialists' intended mechanisms: COSMOS supports compositional accuracy, not mechanism identity.

</details>


### [334] [LocusRL: Diagnosing LLM Reward and Policy Interventions in Competitive Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04441)
*Chengyu Luan,Bo Xin,Songyan Guo,Yuxiang Zuo,Ahmed Yazdan,Jiahang Li,Yicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LocusRL是一个诊断框架，将受控的奖励-策略比较与对奖励判断、信号传递、优化目标和执行动作的审计相结合，用于揭示LLM干预强化学习背后的真实机制，而非仅凭聚合性能判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可通过奖励设计和动作选择干预强化学习，但聚合性能无法完整反映这些干预的实际作用：相似回报可能掩盖不同学习机制，看似合理的奖励可能诱导致不良行为。因此需要从机制层面追踪干预如何转化为学习信号与动作。

Method: 提出LocusRL诊断框架，将受控的奖励-策略比较与四类审计（奖励判断、信号传递、优化目标、执行动作）相连接，将性能差异追溯到可检验的解释，并通过可执行规则与反事实回放检验针对性修正。评估涵盖两批共十个Connect Four训练种子、一个三种子奖励方向匹配实验，并在Leduc（不完全信息）和Goofspiel（奖励验证）中扩展。

Result: 发现干预效应存在种子依赖性反转；追踪实际更新后，历史Qwen训练被解释为通过奖励加权的教师动作似然进行学习。在终局奖励固定下，符号反转的稠密oracle仅得2.8%聚合胜率，而仅终局训练为57.2%、正向稠密oracle为46.7%，表明奖励可强烈影响学习却不提升性能。决策层面，反事实回放验证了对已诊断动作错误的一次获胜修正。Leduc与Goofspiel实验进一步揭示参考标签定义与验证数据暴露会影响干预评估。

Conclusion: 评估LLM干预必须追踪其输出如何转化为学习信号与动作；LocusRL将聚合结果转化为可操作的诊断与可验证的修正。

Abstract: Large language models can intervene in reinforcement learning through both reward design and action selection, yet aggregate performance offers an incomplete account of what these interventions actually do. Similar returns can conceal different learning mechanisms, while plausible rewards can induce undesirable behavior. We introduce LocusRL, a diagnostic framework that connects controlled reward-policy comparisons with audits of reward judgments, signal delivery, optimization objectives, and executed actions. The framework traces performance differences to testable explanations and checks targeted corrections through executable rules and counterfactual replay.
  Across two evaluation batches covering ten Connect Four training seeds, we uncover seed-dependent reversals in intervention effects and show how tracing actual updates changes their interpretation: historical Qwen training operates through reward-weighted teacher-action likelihood. A separate matched three-seed reward-direction experiment distinguishes sensitivity to a learning signal from its usefulness. With terminal rewards held fixed, a sign-reversed dense oracle yields a 2.8% aggregate win rate, compared with 57.2% for terminal-only training and 46.7% for the positive dense oracle. Thus, a reward can strongly influence learning without improving performance. At the decision level, counterfactual replay verifies a winning correction to a diagnosed action error. Complementary experiments in Leduc and reward-validation studies in Goofspiel extend the analysis to imperfect-information settings, revealing how reference-label definitions and validation-data exposure affect intervention assessment.
  Together, these findings show why evaluating LLM interventions requires tracing how their outputs become learning signals and actions. LocusRL turns aggregate outcomes into actionable diagnoses and verifiable corrections.

</details>


### [335] [One-Step Generation via Riemannian Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04454)
*David Li,Chanhyuk Lee,Jaehoon Yoo,Nikita Gushchin,Floor Eijkelboom,Eric Moulines,Maxim Panov,Alexander Korotin,Seunghoon Hong,Jinwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在统一框架下推导并比较多种几何感知的漂移场，用于约束空间上的一步生成器，实验表明不同数据结构下最优场各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漂移模型与Wasserstein梯度流主要针对连续欧氏域（如图像空间）设计，粒子可进行无约束加性更新；但在约束空间上，这类更新可能越出有效域或忽略其几何结构，导致不适合作训练目标。已有针对约束空间的工作多局限于特定领域且缺乏充分实验对比。

Method: 在面向一步生成器的统一训练框架内，推导并比较若干几何感知的漂移场（geometry-aware fields），将迭代分布精炼过程迁移至训练阶段并摊销到生成器中，使更新尊重目标空间的几何与约束。

Result: 在具有不同结构的数据上测试，各设定下均取得有竞争力的一步生成结果；最优场随任务而变化。

Conclusion: 目标函数（漂移场）的选择在实践中至关重要，不存在适用于所有约束空间的通用最优场，需按任务几何结构进行选择。

Abstract: Recently, Drifting Models and Wasserstein Gradient Flows have attracted substantial attention because they move iterative distributional refinement to training and amortize it into a generator, enabling fast inference. However, existing formulations have been developed largely for continuous Euclidean domains, such as image spaces, where particles admit unconstrained additive updates. On constrained spaces, these updates can leave the valid domain or ignore its geometry, making them unsuitable targets for training. Recent work has adapted updates to these spaces, but has focused on particular fields or offered limited empirical comparison. We derive and compare several geometry-aware fields within a common training framework for one-step generators. We test the method on data with different structures and obtain competitive one-step results in each setting. The best-performing field varies by task, showing why the choice of objective matters in practice.

</details>


### [336] [Target-free Latent Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04467)
*Luoyu Chen,Weiqi Wang,Chenhan Zhang,Zhiyi Tian,Yuxian Huang,Shui Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无目标对抗训练框架，通过放大并多样化模型隐空间中行为层面的漂移，以无监督方式生成语义多样的对抗样本，缓解对抗样本行为同质化问题，提升越狱攻击鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗训练防御方法构造对抗样本时，要么鼓励固定的有害目标补全，要么基于固定良性-有害数据对进行目标化激活消融，导致生成的对抗样本诱导的有害行为高度同质化，无法反映真实越狱攻击的行为多样性，这种行为层面的狭窄性从根本上限制了防御鲁棒性。

Method: 提出无目标（target-free）对抗训练框架，以无监督方式生成对抗样本：在模型隐空间中放大并多样化行为层面的漂移，从而产生语义多样的对抗样本，诱导范围广泛的有害行为。同时引入语义熵作为输出层面的对抗行为多样性度量指标。

Result: 在目标模型上成功诱导出多样化的有害行为，显著缓解了行为狭窄性问题，并提升了对越狱攻击的鲁棒性；语义熵能够有效量化对抗行为的多样性。

Conclusion: 行为层面的多样性是提升安全对齐鲁棒性的关键；无目标、无监督的对抗样本生成可扩展行为覆盖范围，暴露更多样的失效模式，从而改善安全对齐效果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain highly vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that induce harmful behaviors and circumvent safety alignment. To defend against such attacks, adversarial training paradigms have been proposed to first simulate failure modes and then train the model to correct them, yielding promising improvements in safety alignment. However, these methods typically construct adversarial samples either by encouraging fixed harmful target completions or by performing targeted activation ablation derived from fixed benign--harmful data pairs. As a result, the generated adversarial samples tend to induce homogeneous harmful behaviors that poorly reflect the diversity of behaviors elicited by real-world jailbreak attacks. This behavior-level narrowness fundamentally limits their robustness. To address this issue, we propose a target-free adversarial training framework that generates adversarial samples in an unsupervised manner. By amplifying and diversifying behavior-level shifts in the model's latent space, our approach produces semantically diverse adversarial samples that induce a wide range of harmful behaviors. This expanded behavioral coverage exposes more diverse failure modes and thereby improves safety alignment. To quantify this effect, we use semantic entropy as an output-level measure of adversarial behavioral diversity. Empirically, our method elicits diverse harmful behaviors in the target model, substantially mitigating behavioral narrowness and improving robustness to jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [337] [BARQ: Balanced Codebook Refinement for Low-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04490)
*Chenhang Cui,Xu Xie,Linrui Xu,Xiaohao Liu,Xingyu Zhu,Fei Shen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BARQ，通过熵正则化最优传输实现平衡分配并细化码本，提升 LLM 权重量化质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 参数规模增长导致模型存储与参数访存成为部署瓶颈；基于码本的权重量化可缓解，但拟合过程中最近码字分配不均衡，部分码字更新不足，限制码本利用率。

Method: 先通过带均匀边缘分布的熵正则化最优传输，结合曲率加权重建代价，计算权重块与码字的联合软分配，保证每个码字在精确解中获得相等的正向拟合质量；再用分配加权重心更新细化码字，证明其对固定分配最小化拟合目标；有限 Sinkhorn 迭代下，只要所有码字质量超过分母下限，该更新仍保持最优性；最后丢弃软分配，用细化后的码本进行标准硬最近码字编码。

Result: 在多个 LLM 上，相同比特预算下 BARQ 相比被测基线取得更低困惑度和更高平均零样本准确率；分析给出了降低硬量化失真与评估损失的充分条件。

Conclusion: BARQ 通过平衡分配细化现有码本，有效提升码本利用率与量化质量，在多种 LLM 上验证了性能优势。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) grow in parameter count, model storage and parameter memory traffic have become major bottlenecks to efficient deployment. Codebook-based weight quantization reduces these costs, but imbalanced nearest-codeword assignments during fitting can leave some codewords insufficiently updated, limiting effective codebook utilization. To address this limitation, we propose Balanced Assignment Refinement for Quantization (BARQ), which improves quantization quality through balanced fitting of existing codebooks. Specifically, we first compute joint soft assignments between weight blocks and codewords through entropically regularized optimal transport with uniform marginals and curvature-weighted reconstruction costs, ensuring equal positive fitting mass for every codeword in the exact solution. We then refine the codewords through an assignment-weighted barycentric update, which we prove minimizes the fitting objective for fixed assignments. For finite Sinkhorn iterations, the implemented update retains this optimality provided all codeword masses exceed the denominator floor. Finally, we discard the soft assignments and use the refined codebook for standard hard nearest-codeword encoding, with our analysis establishing sufficient conditions for reducing hard-quantization distortion and evaluation loss. Across multiple LLMs, BARQ achieves lower perplexity and higher mean zero-shot accuracy than the evaluated baselines at comparable bit budgets. The code is available at https://github.com/chenhangcuisg-code/BARQ.

</details>


### [338] [Understanding Clustering in Slot Attention via Particle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04493)
*Vasudev Joy,Rajat Rasal,Avinash Kori,Anthea Monod,Ben Glocker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将粒子动力学视角扩展到Slot Attention，提出无参数的简化版SSA，其动力学与软k-means聚类相关联，并能为物体中心表征的出现提供机制性解释；在Pascal VOC上取得与Slot Attention相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作通过相互作用粒子动力学研究注意力，揭示了token聚类的涌现机制。但Slot Attention中学习到的组件（参数化的QKV等）掩盖了聚类行为在多大程度上源于注意力本身的内在动力学，缺乏机制性理解。

Method: 提出简化Slot Attention（SSA）：一种无参数（parameter-free）变体，去除学习到的神经网络组件，将其动力学与软k-means聚类建立联系，从而给出物体中心表征涌现的直观机制解释。

Result: 在Pascal VOC数据集上，SSA达到与Slot Attention相当的分割性能，表明无需学习到的神经网络组件也能实现有竞争力的物体中心分割。

Conclusion: 物体中心表征的涌现可归因于注意力动力学本身（与软k-means等价的迭代机制），而非必须依赖可学习参数；这为Slot Attention的机制理解提供了简化、可解释的基线。

Abstract: Studying attention through the lens of interacting particle dynamics has shown how token clustering can emerge from the underlying dynamics. We extend this perspective to slot attention, a method for object-centric image segmentation and representation learning in which learned components obscure how much of the clustering behaviour is intrinsic to the attention dynamics. We therefore introduce simplified slot attention (SSA), a parameter-free variant whose dynamics are connected to soft $k$-means clustering and which provides a straightforward mechanistic explanation for the emergence of object-centric representations. On the Pascal VOC dataset, SSA achieves performance comparable to that of slot attention, demonstrating that competitive object-centric segmentation can be achieved without learned neural-network components.

</details>


### [339] [LoRA's Second Descent Extends Beyond Parameter Parity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04507)
*Yueran Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过"模块匹配的秩扫描"(MMRS)系统研究了LoRA适配器秩与双下降现象的关系，发现LoRA表现出第二次下降，但这种下降只有在失去参数优势后才达到稠密微调的风险水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双下降现象已引发广泛关注，且已有工作将其与数据、模型和训练配置联系起来。然而在实际微调中常用的LoRA（低秩适配）方法中，决定其容量的关键超参数——适配器秩——与双下降的关系尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出"模块匹配的秩扫描"(MMRS)：在四个视觉骨干网络和一个7B语言模型上，于标签噪声条件下进行实验。MMRS扩展至超过全秩的范围，将每个秩与相同模块的稠密微调进行配对比较（按随机种子配对）。

Result: 在DeiT-Tiny上，风险在秩为1时最低，随着适配器能够拟合噪声标签而急剧上升，形成"插值悬崖"。越过峰值后风险再次下降，但所有仍节省参数的峰后秩，其风险依然高于稠密微调。秩为1的LoRA在四个视觉骨干网络中的三个上优于稠密微调，体现了小秩下的强正则化效果。

Conclusion: LoRA确实表现出第二次下降，但只有在丧失参数优势后才与稠密微调的风险持平——首次在DeiT-Tiny上出现于秩达到稠密投影权重四倍时。因此LoRA的双下降并未带来"既省参数又低风险"的理想结果。

Abstract: Double descent has sparked considerable interest, with recent work relating it to the data, the model and the learning configuration. Practical fine-tuning commonly involves training a small adapter on top of frozen pretrained weights, as in low-rank adaptation (LoRA). The adapter's rank is the hyperparameter that sets its capacity, yet how this rank relates to double descent has not been well explored. We quantify this relation under label noise on four vision backbones and a 7B language model with a module-matched rank sweep (MMRS), which extends past full rank and compares every rank with dense fine-tuning of the same modules, paired by seed. On DeiT-Tiny, risk is lowest at rank one and rises sharply as the adapter becomes able to fit the noisy labels, forming an interpolation cliff. Past the peak, risk falls again, but every tested post-peak rank that still saves parameters remains above dense risk. Rank-one LoRA outperforms dense fine-tuning on three of the four vision backbones, consistent with strong regularization at small rank. LoRA thus exhibits a second descent, but matches dense risk only after losing its parameter advantage, first on DeiT-Tiny at four times dense's projection weights. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/lora-second-descent-C7B5.

</details>


### [340] [Length Generalization Needs Proper Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04518)
*Pavlo Vasylenko,Matthias Lindemann,André F. T. Martins,Marcos Treviso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出长度泛化不仅取决于架构（位置编码、注意力机制），也与训练过程本身有关；权重衰减会阻碍外推，而合理放置的 dropout 能改善外推，并提出 VPAD 新策略进一步降低激活方差失配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长度泛化通常被归因于架构设计（如位置编码、注意力机制），但训练过程（尤其是正则化）对其影响尚未被系统研究。作者希望厘清权重衰减与 dropout 等正则手段对长度外推的作用机制。

Method: 系统研究正则化对长度泛化的影响：分析 dropout 在架构中的放置位置，指出标准做法（layer normalization 前接 dropout）引入系统性的分布失配，改为在 linear projection 前施加 dropout 可缓解；提出 Variance-Preserving Affine Dropout（VPAD），显式减小各层 pre-activation 的方差失配。

Result: 权重衰减会阻碍外推；重定位 dropout 可提升外推。改造后的 SmolLM3（sliding window attention）经 dropout 继续预训练后，在 Needle-in-a-Haystack 上可完美外推至 64×，在 RULER 与 HELMET 上远超预训练上下文长度；Mamba2 同样受益，表明问题具有架构无关性。VPAD 进一步降低 pre-activation 方差失配，并在 transformer 上带来额外外推提升。

Conclusion: 长度泛化是训练过程与架构共同作用的结果，而非仅由架构决定；通过合理放置 dropout 及采用 VPAD 可显著提升多种架构的长上下文外推能力。

Abstract: Length generalization is the ability of sequential models to perform well on context lengths unseen during training. In this work, we show that the challenge of achieving length generalization is related not only to architectural choices such as positional encoding and the attention mechanism but also to the training procedure itself. We study how regularization affects length generalization and find that weight decay can hinder extrapolation. In contrast, dropout improves extrapolation when its placement within the architecture is reconsidered. In that regard, we show that the standard placement of dropout before layer normalization introduces a systematic distributional mismatch, and that applying dropout just before the linear projection resolves this issue. For example, a modified SmolLM3 with sliding window attention, continually pre-trained with dropout, can extrapolate perfectly to 64$\times$ on Needle-in-a-Haystack and far beyond the pre-training context size on RULER and HELMET. Mamba2 also benefits from dropout, suggesting an architecture-agnostic nature of the problem. We further propose Variance-Preserving Affine Dropout (VPAD), a new dropout strategy that substantially reduces the resulting pre-activation variance mismatch, leading to further extrapolation improvements in transformers.

</details>


### [341] [Proximal Causal Learning under Unmeasured Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04519)
*Ying Tang,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PCL-U框架，无需预先指定代理变量，直接从观测协变量中学习代理变量以处理未测量混杂，在基准数据集上匹配或超越现有方法且估计稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据估计处理效应通常依赖无未测量混杂假设（NUCA），但该假设在实际中很少成立。近邻因果学习（PCL）通过代理变量处理未测量混杂，但现有方法要求代理变量预先指定，限制了其适用性。

Method: PCL-U使用神经网络编码器将协变量分解为处理诱导型、结果诱导型和共享型代理变量，并以极小极大互信息目标进行引导，再通过实用的基于矩的风险函数获得因果估计。

Result: 在基准数据集上，PCL-U匹配或优于现有基线方法；在两类不同维度和混杂强度的合成数据集上，方法保持了稳定的估计精度。

Conclusion: PCL-U实现了从观测协变量中自动学习代理变量，缓解了对预指定代理变量的依赖，展现出与现有方法相当或更优的因果估计性能及稳定性。

Abstract: Estimating treatment effects from observational data typically relies on the No Unmeasured Confounding Assumption (NUCA), which rarely holds in practice. Proximal causal learning (PCL) addresses unmeasured confounding via proxy variables, yet existing methods require the proxy variables to be pre-specified. Thus, we propose PCL-U, a framework that learns proxy variables directly from observed covariates. PCL-U uses neural encoders to decompose covariates into treatment-inducing, outcome-inducing, and shared proxies, guided by minimax mutual information objectives, and obtains causal estimates through a practical moment-based risk function. Experiments on benchmarks show that PCL-U matches or outperforms existing baselines. Besides, there are two types of synthetic datasets with varying dimensions and confounding strengths that illustrate that our method maintains stable estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [342] [Weight Decay and Neuron Condensation: A Three-Stage Analysis of Two-Layer ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04533)
*Cheng Xu,Pengxiao Lin,Zhangchen Zhou,Zhi-Qin John Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从NTK初始化出发，将权重衰减下的训练动力学刻画为三个阶段：快速拟合、幅度压缩与神经元凝聚，揭示权重衰减如何促进参数方向对齐（凝聚）表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重衰减虽广泛用作正则化，但其在神经元凝聚（参数方向对齐）中的作用尚不清晰，本文旨在给出其动力学机制解释。

Method: 以两层ReLU网络为对象，从NTK regime的参数初始化出发，引入并分析控制神经元幅度与方向的"残差相关场"，分三阶段追踪训练动力学。

Result: 快速拟合阶段残差趋于准静态平衡且切核近似不变；幅度压缩早期核衰减放大残差相关场，其孤立吸引极值给出凝聚方向候选；幅度稳定后证明吸引极值漂移有界并证明神经元方向向其收缩。

Conclusion: 该分阶段分析从动力学角度解释了权重衰减如何促成凝聚表征，而不仅是缩减参数范数。

Abstract: Weight decay is widely used as a regularization technique in neural network training, yet its role in neuron condensation (parameter direction alignment) remains unclear. Starting from a parameter initialization in the neural tangent kernel regime, we characterize training dynamics under weight decay through three stages: rapid fitting, amplitude compression, and neuron condensation. Using a two-layer ReLU network, we analyze a residual correlation field that governs both neuron amplitudes and directions. During rapid fitting, the residual approaches a quasi-static equilibrium maintained by weight decay while the tangent kernel remains nearly unchanged. In the early stage of amplitude compression, kernel decay amplifies the residual correlation field, whose isolated attracting extrema provide candidates for condensation directions. As neuron amplitudes stabilize, we bound the drift of attracting extrema and demonstrate contraction of neuron directions around them, leading to neuron condensation. This staged analysis provides a dynamical understanding of how weight decay promotes a condensed representation, beyond reducing parameter norms.

</details>


### [343] [PhaseGate: Phase-Aware CPU Retrieval Scheduling for On-Device LLMs on Unified Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04537)
*Seoyoon Yum,Sehoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PHASEGATE利用LLM推理阶段（prefill/decode）作为CPU检索的准入信号，分别标定并发上限，在保障LLM延迟约束的同时将检索吞吐提升至最优固定策略的2.0倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在统一内存系统上，设备端助手需并行运行GPU的LLM推理与CPU检索。饱和检索负载下，4个并发检索worker使decode阶段p95延迟上升60-61%，而prefill仅上升5.7-6.9%，说明两阶段对CPU争用敏感度差异显著，需按阶段区分准入控制。

Method: 提出PHASEGATE，以LLM阶段（prefill/decode）为独立CPU检索的准入信号，分别标定两阶段并发上限（在基础M4配置上为4和1）。引入阶段盲对照TimeGate，使用相同两个上限但按标定推导的时间表调度，不观测LLM阶段。

Result: 在积压队列下，PHASEGATE达到最佳可行固定策略聚合检索吞吐的2.0倍，且在全部7次留出运行中两项p95延迟指标均不超过无检索基线的1.25倍。TimeGate吞吐相近但在每次运行中都违反输出token延迟上限。M2与M2 Pro Mac mini复现该策略排序；输出长度扫描显示，随着decode占比增加，优势缩小。

Conclusion: LLM推理阶段是有效的检索准入信号，阶段感知的并发控制可在统一内存设备上同时满足LLM延迟约束与高检索吞吐，优于阶段盲调度，且结论在多种Apple Silicon配置上成立，但在长输出场景下收益递减。

Abstract: On-device assistants run GPU-based LLM inference alongside CPU retrieval on unified-memory systems. Under a saturated local-retrieval workload, four concurrent retrieval workers raise 95th-percentile (p95) decode latency by 60-61% on two M4 systems, whereas prefill latency rises by only 5.7-6.9%. We study LLM phase as an admission signal for independent CPU retrieval under controlled LLM workloads. PHASEGATE calibrates separate concurrency limits for prefill and decode, selecting four and one on our base-M4 configuration. Under a backlogged queue, it achieves 2.0 times the aggregate retrieval throughput of the best tested feasible fixed policy, with both p95 LLM latency metrics within 1.25 times their no-retrieval baselines in all seven held-out runs. A phase-blind control, TimeGate, uses the same two limits on a calibration-derived schedule without observing LLM phase. It achieves similar retrieval throughput but violates the output-token latency limit in every run. M2 and M2 Pro Mac minis reproduce the policy ordering, while output-length sweeps show that the advantage narrows as decode occupies more of each request.

</details>


### [344] [TMAML: Temporal Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning for Cold-Start Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04547)
*Wannes Janssens,Matthias Bogaert,Dirk Van den Poel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将冷启动预测形式化为少窗口学习问题，提出时序模型无关元学习（TMAML），将MAML适配到深度时序预测，在TFT上实例化并在三类冷启动场景中进行初步实证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷启动预测（历史数据极少或没有）是常见挑战，需要能从少量数据快速学习并借助相关序列信息（如静态协变量）泛化的方法。现有多序列全局预测模型虽可生成冷启动预测，但未针对训练集外泛化或短历史自适应进行优化。

Method: 将冷启动预测建模为few-window learning问题；提出TMAML，把为神经网络少样本自适应设计的MAML改造用于深度时序预测；构造时间一致的support-query窗口作为元任务，配合时序元训练与元测试流程，使模型显式面向冷启动优化；在Temporal Fusion Transformer（TFT）上实例化TMAML。

Result: 在三种冷启动场景下做了预测精度与校准的初步实证：TMAML持续优于或持平于标准ERM训练的TFT；在三个场景中的两个上比naive基线校准更好；但在概率预测精度上未持续优于naive基线。

Conclusion: TMAML为冷启动时序预测提供了显式优化的元学习框架，在点预测上相对ERM有稳定收益、在校准上部分场景更优，但概率预测精度尚无一致优势，需进一步研究。

Abstract: Cold-start forecasting, the task of forecasting a time series with little to no historical data, is a common challenge. Addressing it requires approaches that learn quickly from few datapoints and leverage information from related series, typically through static covariates, to generalize well to unseen series. While some global forecasting models can generate cold-start predictions by leveraging information shared across multiple series, they are not optimized for out-of-train-set generalization or adaptation from short histories. In this work, we formulate cold-start forecasting as a few-window learning problem and introduce Temporal Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning (TMAML), which tailors the model-agnostic meta-learning algorithm, originally developed for few-shot adaptation of neural networks, to deep time series forecasting. TMAML constructs meta-tasks as temporally consistent support-query windows and pairs them with a temporal meta-training and meta-testing procedure, yielding forecasting models that are explicitly optimized for cold-start forecasting. We instantiate TMAML on the Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) and present an initial empirical analysis of forecast accuracy and calibration across three cold-start scenarios: TMAML consistently outperforms or matches a standard ERM-trained TFT, yields better-calibrated forecasts than naive on two of the three scenarios, but does not consistently outperform naive on probabilistic forecast accuracy.

</details>


### [345] [Coupling Noisy Pairwise Knowledge to the DAG Posterior for Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04559)
*Guoliang Xu,James E Corter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HB-NoisyKG贝叶斯框架，将观测数据与LLM等来源的重复因果报告结合，通过特征条件Beta先验和共享误差矩阵建模报告可靠性，交替推断提升结构学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部因果报告能改善有限观测下的结构学习，但不同来源和变量对的报告可靠性不一致，需要一种能建模并自适应估计报告可靠性的方法。

Method: HB-NoisyKG：每条报告视为某个DAG所隐含的直接配对状态的噪声观测；用特征条件Beta先验汇集配对可靠性信息，用共享误差矩阵捕捉系统性错误；采用交替推断，用图后验细化可靠性估计并影响后续图更新。报告似然仅依赖图配对状态边际，使同一观测层支持离散与连续似然（graph-only与joint inference两种模式）。

Result: 在五个离散基准上，相较80次重启的no-KG基线（最多80次重启），HB平均SHD从22.39降至16.06；在随机变量ID并保留描述的物理光隧道上，SHD从39.00降至27.30；在连续Sachs上，graph-only BGe较no-KG Top-K将AUROC提高0.121；受控合成实验中，持续更新相比一次性估计降低了平均可靠性估计误差和留出报告的对数损失。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模报告可靠性并交替推断，HB-NoisyKG能有效融合噪声外部因果报告，在离散与连续、graph-only与joint inference场景下均提升结构学习性能，且持续更新可靠性估计优于一次性估计。

Abstract: External causal reports can improve structure learning from limited observations, but their reliability varies across sources and variable pairs. We introduce HB-NoisyKG, a Bayesian framework that combines observational data with repeated causal reports from sources such as large language models. Each report is a noisy observation of a direct pair state implied by one DAG. A feature-conditioned Beta prior pools information about pair reliability, and a shared error matrix captures systematic mistakes. Alternating inference uses the graph posterior to refine reliability estimates, which determine how reports influence subsequent graph updates. The report likelihood uses only graph pair-state marginals, so the same observation layer supports discrete and continuous likelihoods in graph-only and joint inference. Against an 80-restart no-KG baseline, HB uses at most 80 total restarts and lowers mean SHD from 22.39 to 16.06 on five discrete benchmarks. On a physical light tunnel with random variable IDs and retained descriptions, HB lowers SHD from 39.00 for no-KG to 27.30. On continuous Sachs, graph-only BGe raises AUROC by 0.121 over no-KG Top-K. In a controlled synthetic study, continued updating also reduces mean reliability estimation error and held-out report log loss compared with one-time estimation.

</details>


### [346] [Only Project Once: Projection-Adaptive Loss for Exact Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04572)
*Tim Aebersold,Soheyl Massoudi,Mark Fuge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Projection-Adaptive Loss（PAL），证明训练中仅需单步分离（detached）投影即可实现精确约束满足，在多个基准上匹配或超越现有方法并大幅提升训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在诸多领域部署学习模型需要精确满足约束，现有方法在训练中展开多步修复并对残余违反做软惩罚，导致计算与内存开销大、修复不收敛时鲁棒性差，且将大部分约束满足工作交给修复过程。作者质疑这一常规做法，探索能否用更轻量的训练方式达到同等甚至更好的约束满足。

Method: 提出 Projection-Adaptive Loss（PAL）：在训练中对原始神经预测执行单步且分离梯度的投影，利用该单步投影后的约束残差自适应地为原始预测上的约束惩罚加权，从而无需多步展开修复即可完成训练。

Result: 在极端非线性约束上，PAL 是唯一保持近乎完美可行性的方法；在合成与工程基准上匹配或超越现有方法；在 ACOPF 基准上训练速度比经典修复方法 DC3 快 2.5 倍；在约束评估昂贵（如使用神经网络代理）的场景下仍可训练，而现有展开方法在此内存不可行。

Conclusion: 单步分离投影足以在训练中实现精确约束满足，PAL 以更低计算和内存成本达到更优的可行性与性能，为约束满足的学习模型提供更高效、更鲁棒的训练范式。

Abstract: Precise constraint satisfaction is a prerequisite to deploying learned models in many areas, motivating methods that repair raw neural predictions with a repair procedure. Current methods unroll multiple repair steps in training and softly penalize constraint violations that remain after the unroll. This is compute- and memory-intensive, lacks robustness when the repair fails to converge, and surrenders most of the constraint satisfaction work to the repair. Our central finding is that, contrary to common practice, a single detached projection step suffices in training. We accomplish this with a Projection-Adaptive Loss (PAL), which uses the constraint residual after this single step to adaptively weigh constraint penalties on the raw prediction. In experiments, PAL is the only method that retains virtually perfect feasibility on extremely nonlinear constraints, and matches or outperforms current methods on synthetic and engineering benchmarks. Because it only requires a single detached projection step, PAL trains 2.5x faster than the canonical repair-based method (DC3) on its own ACOPF benchmark. PAL can also be trained when constraints are expensive to evaluate (e.g., via neural surrogates), a setting where current unrolled methods are memory-intractable.

</details>


### [347] [DiffGate: Difficulty-Gated Teacher Guidance for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04596)
*Karn Tiwari,Varnith Chordia,Prathosh A P*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DiffGate，一种结果门控（outcome-gated）的蒸馏目标，将 GRPO 与选择性、有界教师指导结合，仅在失败轨迹上施加按组难度缩放并平滑限幅的教师监督，在四个模型-领域设置上提升了 pass@8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 on-policy distillation（OPD）目标多为 token-local 且 outcome-agnostic，只在每个前缀上对齐师生分布，而推理质量由轨迹级别决定；RLVR/GRPO 提供结果级监督但存在奖励稀疏、信用分配粗糙、全失败组梯度消失等问题。作者指出 OPD 与 RLVR 存在互补盲点：教师信号密集但与 rollout 正确性弱对齐，组相对奖励捕捉任务成功但 token 级信用粗糙。

Method: 提出 DiffGate：将 GRPO 与选择性、有界的教师指导结合。教师监督仅施加于失败轨迹，并按组难度缩放，同时平滑限幅以防止极端师生差异主导优化。验证器决定哪些轨迹获得教师指导，教师在这些轨迹内提供密集的 token 级更新方向。

Result: 在 Qwen3-0.6B 与 Qwen3-1.7B 学生模型上，DiffGate 相较匹配 GRPO 在代码任务上 avg@8 分别提升 +1.7 与 +1.8 分，pass@8 提升 +1.6 与 +5.7 分；在数学任务上 avg@8 与 GRPO 相差 0.5 分以内，pass@8 分别提升 +1.1 与 +3.9 分。总体在全部四个模型-领域设置上提升 pass@8。

Conclusion: DiffGate 通过结果门控选择性融合教师密集指导与 GRPO 结果级奖励，在评测协议下改善了解决方案覆盖率（pass@8），验证了 OPD 与 RLVR 互补结合的有效性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as a widely used paradigm for post-training large language models, reducing the train--test mismatch of conventional distillation by supervising the student on its own generated trajectories. However, existing OPD objectives remain largely token-local and outcome-agnostic, optimizing teacher--student agreement at each prefix despite reasoning quality being determined at the trajectory level. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), particularly Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), provides complementary outcome-level supervision but suffers from sparse rewards and coarse credit assignment. We show that OPD and RLVR exhibit complementary blind spots: teacher signals provide dense local guidance but are weakly aligned with rollout correctness, whereas group-relative rewards capture task success but provide coarse token-level credit and vanish on all-failure groups. We introduce DiffGate, an outcome-gated objective that combines GRPO with selective, bounded teacher guidance. Teacher supervision is applied only to failed trajectories, scaled by group difficulty, and smoothly bounded to prevent extreme teacher--student discrepancies from dominating optimization. The verifier therefore determines \emph{which trajectories} receive teacher guidance, while the teacher provides dense token-level update directions within those trajectories. Across Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3-1.7B students, DiffGate improves code avg@8 over matched GRPO by $+1.7$ and $+1.8$ points and pass@8 by $+1.6$ and $+5.7$ points, respectively. On mathematics, avg@8 remains within $0.5$ points of GRPO while pass@8 improves by $+1.1$ and $+3.9$ points. Overall, DiffGate improves pass@8 across all four model--domain settings, demonstrating improved solution coverage under our evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [348] [Asymptotically Optimal Best Arm Identification with Fixed-Budget under Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04600)
*Keqin Chen,Jie Bian,Yulian Wu,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究纯ε-差分隐私下的固定预算最优臂识别问题，证明误差概率的最优指数衰减率上界由隐私感知运输指数刻画，并提出达到该基准的自适应算法AO-Pri-BAI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私下的最优臂识别需同时兼顾统计效率与隐私保护。以往固定置信度分析（Jourdan和Azize, 2025）中的运输指数刻画的是停止时间，而固定预算场景下误差概率的最优衰减率尚未被刻画，因此需要建立相应的隐私感知理论基准并设计可达该基准的算法。

Method: 采用纯ε-差分隐私模型，研究固定预算最优臂识别；证明误差概率的最优指数衰减率上界由实例相关的隐私感知运输指数决定；基于该指数提出自适应算法AO-Pri-BAI，使用Laplace树机制维护私有运行估计，并通过困难备择与臂分配之间的min–max交互学习采样设计。

Result: 证明了AO-Pri-BAI满足纯ε-差分隐私；其失败概率指数与隐私感知基准相匹配；数值实验表明即便在非渐近情形下，AO-Pri-BAI在多种实例上均优于基准算法。

Conclusion: 固定预算差分隐私最优臂识别的误差衰减由隐私感知运输指数刻画，AO-Pri-BAI在理论上达到该最优基准并在实验中验证了有效性，完善了隐私约束下的最优臂识别理论。

Abstract: Best arm identification under differential privacy is a pure-exploration problem in which both statistical efficiency and privacy protection must be achieved simultaneously. We study fixed-budget best arm identification for bandits under pure $ε$-differential privacy, where the learner must recommend an arm after a prescribed sampling budget while protecting the full transcript. We prove that the optimal exponential decay rate of the error probability is upper bounded by an instance-dependent privacy-aware transportation exponent that differs from the analogous quantity used to characterize the stopping time in fixed-confidence analysis by Jourdan and Azize [2025]. Guided by this exponent, we propose AO-Pri-BAI, an adaptive algorithm that maintains private running estimates through Laplace-tree mechanisms and learns a sampling design through a min--max interaction between hard alternatives and arm allocations. We prove that AO-Pri-BAI satisfies pure $ε$-differential privacy. We also establish that the exponent of the failure probability of AO-Pri-BAI matches the privacy-aware benchmark. Numerical studies show that even in the non-asymptotic setting, AO-Pri-BAI outperforms benchmark algorithms on various instances, complementing the theoretical analyses.

</details>


### [349] [Anticipating the Consequences of Curriculum Decisions with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04604)
*Octavio Pappalardo,Nathan Herr,Tim Rocktäschel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将自动课程学习建模为序贯决策问题，指出常用局部学习信号与课程决策真实价值之间存在信息鸿沟，并引入LLM估计任务的潜在下游收益与当前可学习性，结合在线学习进度估计构建课程方法，在Craftax的256个文本目标基准上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动课程学习能通过选择训练经验提升强化学习效果，但预测课程决策的后果很困难。作者指出：决定课程决策价值的量（如任务对未来任务的迁移收益）与常用于指导课程的局部学习信号（如即时学习进度）之间存在不匹配，因此需要利用更丰富的问题信息来预见决策后果。

Method: 将自动课程学习形式化为序贯决策问题；提出一种方法，把在线学习进度估计与LLM提供的两类估计相结合：(i) 在每个任务上学习的潜在下游收益，(ii) 当前直接训练该任务是否可能产生进展。在Craftax中构建包含256个文本目标的自定义基准，并在不同课程目标下进行评估。

Result: 在优化单个目标任务时获得最强增益；在优化整个任务集合时，收益因学习者的跨任务迁移机制而异，从学习速度的适度提升到持续到训练结束的更大增益不等。

Conclusion: LLM可提供关于学习问题的更丰富信息，从而更好地预判课程决策的后果；相较于仅依赖局部学习信号，该方法在特定课程目标下更有效，但其收益依赖于迁移机制与优化目标。

Abstract: Automatic curriculum learning can improve the effectiveness of reinforcement learning by selecting the training experiences presented to the agent over time. Predicting the consequences of such decisions can, however, be difficult. We analyze automatic curriculum learning as a sequential decision-making problem, highlighting a gap between the quantities that determine the value of curriculum decisions and the information captured by local learning signals commonly used to guide them. We then investigate whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can exploit richer information about the learning problem to better anticipate the consequences of curriculum decisions. We introduce a method that combines online learning-progress estimates with LLM-informed estimates of (i) the potential downstream benefits of learning on each task and (ii) whether direct training on a task is currently likely to produce progress. We evaluate the approach on a custom benchmark of 256 textual goals in Craftax under different curriculum objectives. We observe the strongest gains when optimizing for individual target tasks. When optimizing across the full task set, the benefits vary across learners with different mechanisms for cross-task transfer, ranging from modest improvements in learning speed to larger gains that persist through the end of training.

</details>
